--[[
    SILENCE ADMIN PANEL  -  Client-Side Executor Script
    Paste into any Roblox executor and run.
    Re-execute = auto reload (cleans old instance).

    Hotkeys (customizable in GUI):
      RightShift   - toggle GUI
      RightControl - minimize / restore
      E (while emoting) - stop emote

    Chat commands (prefix ;):
      ;fly 80   ;ws 100   ;jp 200   ;esp   ;chams
      ;tracers  ;aimbot   ;freecam  ;fov 90   ;disco ;slowmo 0.5
      ;trail    ;bighead  ;goto <plr>
      ;noclip   ;antifling  ;antivoid  ;hop
      ;ghost    ;invis    ;headless  ;acc  ;scale 2  ;face <id>
      ;slot <n>  ;slotid <n> <id>  ;slothide <n>  ;slots
      ;walk     ;walkid <id>
      ;wp <name>  ;wps   ;wpgo <n>
      ;ai       ;autoclick  ;autorejoin  ;itemesp
      ;streamer ;chatlog    ;uiscale 1.2  ;uicolor ff00ff
      ;save     ;load
      ;defaultdance  ;floss  ;scenario  ;orangejustice  ;stopemote

    Admin: type /silenceadmin in chat, then enter password.
]]

print("[Silence] script starting - if you don't see 'loaded' below, something errored")

-- SERVICES

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- CONFIG: replace with YOUR Roblox GamePass ID for script-user
-- detection. Create a cheap GamePass in any of your Roblox places,
-- then put the GamePass ID here. When users inject, they'll be
-- prompted to "Buy" once. After purchase, they're auto-detected as
-- Silence users by everyone else's script in the same Roblox server.
-- Set to 0 to disable detection (admin panel will show all players).
----------------------------------------------------------------
local SCRIPT_PASS_ID = 1836957867
-- try a different pass without touching the file:
--   getgenv().SilencePassId = 123456789
pcall(function()
    local g = getgenv()
    if g and tonumber(g.SilencePassId) then SCRIPT_PASS_ID = tonumber(g.SilencePassId) end
end)

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- LOCAL IMAGES
-- Put your PNGs in a folder called  Silence  on your Desktop:
--     C:\Users\<you>\Desktop\Silence\discord-icon.png
--     C:\Users\<you>\Desktop\Silence\settings-icon.png
--
-- Finding that folder is the hard part. Roblox's sandboxed os library has no
-- getenv, so the Windows account name cannot simply be read - and most
-- executors refuse listfiles("C:/Users") outright, which is why relying on
-- that alone left the folder undiscovered.
--
-- What does work on nearly every executor: listfiles on its OWN workspace is
-- always allowed, and the paths it returns are ABSOLUTE. Slicing the
-- ...\Users\<account>\ prefix off one of them gives the home folder without
-- ever touching the environment. Both the plain Desktop and the OneDrive-
-- redirected Desktop are then probed.
--
-- Order: workspace/Silence, then the discovered Desktop folders, then the
-- copy a previous run cached, then the GitHub raw URL.
-- Override everything with:  getgenv().SilenceIconDir = "C:/full/path"
----------------------------------------------------------------
local ICON_DIRS = { src = {} }   -- probed directories + where each icon came
                                 -- from, both surfaced on the Logs tab

local function loadLocalImage(filenames, remoteURL)
    -- Most executors hand back a content string even when the file does not
    -- exist, so a caller would get a truthy-but-broken asset and never draw
    -- its fallback. Only trust it when the file is really there.
    local function fromWorkspace(fname)
        local exists = true
        if type(isfile) == "function" then
            local okI, r = pcall(function() return isfile(fname) end)
            exists = okI and r and true or false
        end
        if not exists then return nil end
        local ok, asset = pcall(function() return getcustomasset(fname) end)
        if ok and type(asset) == "string" and asset ~= "" then return asset end
        return nil
    end

    -- 1. a Silence folder inside the executor's own workspace
    for _, fname in ipairs(filenames) do
        local asset = fromWorkspace("Silence/" .. fname)
        if asset then
            print("[Silence] image '" .. fname .. "' loaded from workspace/Silence")
            ICON_DIRS.src[filenames[1]] = "executor workspace /Silence"
            return asset
        end
    end

    -- Work out the candidate directories once, then reuse them.
    if not ICON_DIRS.done then
        ICON_DIRS.done = true
        local seen = {}
        local function addDir(d)
            if type(d) ~= "string" or d == "" then return end
            d = d:gsub("[/\\]+$", "")
            local key = string.lower(d)
            if not seen[key] then
                seen[key] = true
                table.insert(ICON_DIRS, d)
            end
        end
        local function addHome(home)
            home = tostring(home):gsub("\\", "/"):gsub("/+$", "")
            addDir(home .. "/Desktop/Silence")
            addDir(home .. "/OneDrive/Desktop/Silence")
        end
        -- pull  <drive>:/Users/<account>  out of any text at all
        local function harvest(text)
            if type(text) ~= "string" then return end
            local home = text:match("([A-Za-z]:[/\\][Uu][Ss][Ee][Rr][Ss][/\\][^/\\%s]+)")
            if home then addHome(home) end
        end

        -- an explicit override always wins
        pcall(function()
            local g = getgenv()
            if g then addDir(g.SilenceIconDir) end
        end)

        -- (a) executors that hand out their own directory
        for _, getter in ipairs({ "getworkspacedirectory", "getexecutordirectory",
                                  "getworkingdirectory" }) do
            pcall(function()
                local fn = rawget(getfenv(), getter)
                if type(fn) == "function" then harvest(tostring(fn())) end
            end)
        end
        pcall(function()
            if syn and syn.get_workspace_directory then
                harvest(tostring(syn.get_workspace_directory()))
            end
        end)

        -- (b) our own workspace listing. Some executors return absolute paths
        -- here, which is all we need; others return paths relative to the
        -- workspace, in which case this finds nothing and (c) takes over.
        pcall(function()
            if type(listfiles) ~= "function" then
                ICON_DIRS.note = "listfiles: not available"
                return
            end
            local list
            for _, root in ipairs({ "", "." }) do
                local okL, r = pcall(function() return listfiles(root) end)
                if okL and type(r) == "table" and #r > 0 then list = r break end
            end
            if not list then
                -- empty workspace: leave one file behind so there is a path
                pcall(function() writefile("silence_probe.txt", "silence") end)
                local okL, r = pcall(function() return listfiles("") end)
                if okL then list = r end
                pcall(function()
                    if type(delfile) == "function" then delfile("silence_probe.txt") end
                end)
            end
            if type(list) ~= "table" or #list == 0 then
                ICON_DIRS.note = "listfiles(''): nothing returned"
            else
                ICON_DIRS.note = "listfiles(''): " .. #list .. " entries, 1st = "
                                 .. tostring(list[1])
                for _, entry in ipairs(list) do harvest(tostring(entry)) end
            end
        end)

        -- (c) a deliberately bogus file operation. The error message an
        -- executor raises almost always spells out the path it resolved to,
        -- which is where the account folder comes from when listfiles only
        -- ever answers with relative names.
        pcall(function()
            local probes = {
                function() return readfile("silence__probe__.dat") end,
                function() return loadfile("silence__probe__.dat") end,
                function() return listfiles("silence__probe__dir") end,
                function() return getcustomasset("silence__probe__.dat") end,
            }
            for _, fn in ipairs(probes) do
                local okP, errP = pcall(fn)
                if not okP then harvest(tostring(errP)) end
            end
        end)

        -- (d) the direct listing, for the executors that do allow it
        for _, root in ipairs({ "C:/Users", "C:\\Users" }) do
            pcall(function()
                if type(listfiles) ~= "function" then return end
                for _, entry in ipairs(listfiles(root)) do
                    local name = tostring(entry):match("[^/\\]+$")
                    if name then addHome("C:/Users/" .. name) end
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    -- 2. the Desktop folder itself. Re-read every inject, so swapping the PNG
    -- on the Desktop takes effect instead of an old cached copy winning.
    for _, dir in ipairs(ICON_DIRS) do
        for _, fname in ipairs(filenames) do
            local full = dir .. "/" .. fname
            local okRead, content = pcall(function() return readfile(full) end)
            if okRead and type(content) == "string" and #content > 100 then
                -- cache it in the workspace: getcustomasset only serves files
                -- the executor itself owns
                if pcall(function() writefile(fname, content) end) then
                    local asset = fromWorkspace(fname)
                    if asset then
                        print("[Silence] copied " .. full .. " -> " .. fname)
                        ICON_DIRS.src[filenames[1]] = full
                        return asset
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- 3. whatever a previous inject already cached in the workspace root
    for _, fname in ipairs(filenames) do
        local asset = fromWorkspace(fname)
        if asset then
            print("[Silence] image '" .. fname .. "' loaded from the workspace cache")
            ICON_DIRS.src[filenames[1]] = "executor workspace (cached earlier)"
            return asset
        end
    end

    if remoteURL and writefile and getcustomasset then
        local okGet, body = pcall(function() return game:HttpGet(remoteURL, true) end)
        if okGet and type(body) == "string" and #body > 100 then
            local target = filenames[1]
            if pcall(function() writefile(target, body) end) then
                local asset = fromWorkspace(target)
                if asset then
                    print("[Silence] downloaded " .. remoteURL .. " -> " .. target)
                    ICON_DIRS.src[filenames[1]] = "downloaded from GitHub"
                    return asset
                end
            end
        else
            print("[Silence] HttpGet failed for: " .. remoteURL)
        end
    end
    return nil
end

-- POINT THIS AT YOUR OWN REPO
-- Everything the script downloads hangs off this one line. Change the user
-- and repo name and both icons follow; nothing else needs editing.
-- Must end with a slash.
----------------------------------------------------------------
local ICON_BASE = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Tyler-Daddy666/silence/main/"

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- RANK SYNC (optional)
-- A client-side script has no way to tell another client anything: Roblox
-- gives it no channel. So for ranks to be visible to everyone, one small
-- endpoint has to hold them.
--   SYNC_BASE  the worker URL, ending with a slash. Empty disables sync and
--              everything stays local to your own machine.
--   SYNC_KEY   write password. Only the person setting ranks needs a real
--              one; leaving it blank still lets this client READ ranks.
-- Reading is public, writing needs the key.
----------------------------------------------------------------
local SYNC_BASE = "https://silence.tag-sync.workers.dev/"
local SYNC_KEY  = ""
-- Never put the write key in the file you upload: the loader is public and
-- anyone could then set ranks. Set it only on your own machine, on the line
-- above the loader:   getgenv().SilenceSyncKey = "your-key"
pcall(function()
    local g = getgenv()
    if g and type(g.SilenceSyncKey) == "string" and g.SilenceSyncKey ~= "" then
        SYNC_KEY = g.SilenceSyncKey
    end
end)

-- The two icon files. Name them exactly like this, both in Desktop\Silence
-- and in the repo. Save them WHITE on transparent: the panel is black, so a
-- black icon would be invisible on it.
local DISCORD_IMG  = loadLocalImage(
    { "discord-icon.png", "silence_discord.png", "discord.png" },
    ICON_BASE .. "discord-icon.png"
)
local SETTINGS_IMG = loadLocalImage(
    { "settings-icon.png", "silence_settings.png", "gear.png" },
    ICON_BASE .. "settings-icon.png"
)
print("[Silence] SETTINGS_IMG=", tostring(SETTINGS_IMG))
if not DISCORD_IMG or not SETTINGS_IMG then
    print("[Silence] icon PNGs missing - drawn glyphs will be used instead.")
    print("[Silence] wanted: discord-icon.png + settings-icon.png")
    print("[Silence] save them WHITE on transparent (the panel is black).")
    if #ICON_DIRS == 0 then
        print("[Silence] this executor would not reveal any absolute path, so")
        print("[Silence] the Desktop cannot be reached. Put the 'Silence'")
        print("[Silence] folder inside the executor's own workspace folder,")
        print("[Silence] or set getgenv().SilenceIconDir = 'C:/full/path'")
        print("[Silence] on the line above the script.")
    else
        print("[Silence] folders that were checked:")
        for _, d in ipairs(ICON_DIRS) do print("[Silence]   " .. d) end
    end
end

print("[Silence] DISCORD_IMG=", tostring(DISCORD_IMG))

local Players              = game:GetService("Players")
local MarketplaceService   = game:GetService("MarketplaceService")
local AvatarEditorService  = game:GetService("AvatarEditorService")
local RunService           = game:GetService("RunService")

local TeleportService  = game:GetService("TeleportService")
local StarterGui       = game:GetService("StarterGui")
local CoreGui          = game:GetService("CoreGui")

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- EXECUTOR COMPAT
----------------------------------------------------------------
local env = (typeof(getgenv) == "function") and getgenv() or _G
if env.__SilenceAdminPanel then
    print("[Silence] previous instance found, cleaning up...")
    pcall(function() env.__SilenceAdminPanel.cleanup() end)
    env.__SilenceAdminPanel = nil
end
-- Nuke a leftover blur from a previous load. It lives in Lighting rather
-- than under the ScreenGui, so a failed cleanup would leave the world
-- permanently out of focus with nothing left to switch it off.
pcall(function()
    for _, e in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
        if e.Name == "__SIL_Blur" then pcall(function() e:Destroy() end) end
    end
end)
-- Nuke leftover GUIs from a previous load.
--
-- This used to scan CoreGui and PlayerGui only - but pickGuiParent() puts
-- the panel under gethui() whenever the executor offers it, and that is
-- NEITHER of those. So a panel from an earlier run sat in a container this
-- sweep could not see, survived every reload, and kept drawing over the new
-- one. That is how you end up staring at an old version number after
-- re-executing: the file was fine, the old window was simply still there.
--
-- It also matters when getgenv() is reset between executions, because then
-- env.__SilenceAdminPanel is nil, cleanup() never runs, and this sweep is
-- the only thing standing between you and two stacked panels.
pcall(function()
    local containers = { CoreGui, LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") }
    local okH, hui = pcall(function() return gethui and gethui() end)
    if okH and hui then table.insert(containers, 1, hui) end

    for _, container in ipairs(containers) do
        if container then
            for _, c in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                local nm = typeof(c.Name) == "string" and c.Name or ""
                if string.sub(nm, 1, 16) == "SilenceAdminPane"
                or string.sub(nm, 1, 6)  == "__SIL_"
                or nm == "NotifyHolder"
                or nm == "FloatBtn" then
                    pcall(function() c:Destroy() end)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)
local SilenceAPI = {}
env.__SilenceAdminPanel = SilenceAPI

-- The version belongs to THIS FILE, not to the ?v= in the loader URL. The
-- URL number only busts a cache: load ?v=99 after forgetting to upload and
-- you still get the old script, with no way to tell. What the panel shows
-- comes from the source that is actually running, so a mismatch between this
-- and what you expect means the upload did not happen.
-- Lives on SilenceAPI rather than in a local: the main chunk is at 198 of
-- Luau's 200 locals.
SilenceAPI._version = "v55"
SilenceAPI._built   = "2026-08-18"
-- Printed before anything can go wrong, so the console always tells you
-- which build actually executed. If this says v26 and the panel says v22,
-- an old window survived. If this says v22, you are being served an old
-- file and no amount of reloading the panel will change that.
print("[Silence] ======== " .. SilenceAPI._version
      .. "  built " .. SilenceAPI._built
      .. "  loaded " .. os.date("%H:%M:%S") .. " ========")
-- true while any Silence window is open. Anything that lives outside the
-- ScreenGui stack - the overhead tag, world click handlers - asks this
-- before acting on a click.
function SilenceAPI._modalOpen()
    for _, w in ipairs(SilenceAPI._modalWindows or {}) do
        if w and w.Parent and w.Visible then return true end
    end
    return false
end

-- Registering a window here does three things: it counts towards
-- _modalOpen, it tucks the main panel away while the window is up, and it
-- brings the panel back when the LAST window closes. Whether the panel was
-- even open is remembered, so opening the editor from a hidden panel does
-- not conjure the panel back afterwards.
function SilenceAPI._modalWatch(w)
    if not w then return end
    SilenceAPI._modalWindows = SilenceAPI._modalWindows or {}
    table.insert(SilenceAPI._modalWindows, w)
    w:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
        if w.Visible then
            if SilenceAPI._mainWasOpen == nil then
                SilenceAPI._mainWasOpen = SilenceAPI._mainVisible
                                      and SilenceAPI._mainVisible() or false
            end
            if SilenceAPI._setMainVisible then SilenceAPI._setMainVisible(false) end
        else
            -- another window may still be up; only the last one restores
            if SilenceAPI._modalOpen() then return end
            if SilenceAPI._mainWasOpen and SilenceAPI._setMainVisible then
                SilenceAPI._setMainVisible(true)
            end
            SilenceAPI._mainWasOpen = nil
        end
    end)
end

-- closes the topmost open window; returns true if it closed something
function SilenceAPI._closeTopModal()
    local list = SilenceAPI._modalWindows or {}
    for i = #list, 1, -1 do
        local w = list[i]
        if w and w.Parent and w.Visible then w.Visible = false return true end
    end
    return false
end

print("[Silence] env initialized, building GUI...")

-- DIAGNOSTIC: catch any uncaught error and display in StarterGui so the
-- user sees what halted the script without needing to open F9.
local function _silenceFatal(stage, err)
    warn("[Silence] FATAL at " .. stage .. ": " .. tostring(err))
    pcall(function()
        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
            Title = "Silence FATAL",
            Text  = stage .. ": " .. tostring(err):sub(1, 220),
            Duration = 10,
        })
    end)
end

local Connections = {}
local function track(c) table.insert(Connections, c); return c end

local function pickGuiParent()
    local ok, hui = pcall(function() return gethui and gethui() end)
    if ok and hui then return hui end
    if pcall(function() local _ = CoreGui:GetChildren() end) then return CoreGui end
    return LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
end
local function protect(g)
    if syn and syn.protect_gui then pcall(syn.protect_gui, g) end
    if protect_gui then pcall(protect_gui, g) end
end


----------------------------------------------------------------
-- THEME - black + purple + white
----------------------------------------------------------------
-- Original black + white look. The extra keys (bg4/border/accentDim/good/
-- warn/bad) stay because ~50 places that used to hardcode a grey now read a
-- token instead -- that indirection is what lets the UI COLORS section in
-- Misc recolour the panel live.
local Theme = {
    bg        = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),        -- pure black
    bg2       = Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 12),     -- panel sections
    bg3       = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 22),     -- inputs
    bg4       = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40),     -- hover / pressed
    border    = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45),

    accent    = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),  -- white
    accent2   = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220),  -- soft white
    accentDim = Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 90),

    text      = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
    textDim   = Color3.fromRGB(170, 170, 170),

    on        = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
    off       = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40),
    good      = Color3.fromRGB(80, 220, 130),
    warn      = Color3.fromRGB(255, 180, 80),
    bad       = Color3.fromRGB(200, 60, 60),
}

-- Recolour registry. Each entry remembers WHICH token an instance is painted
-- from, decided once at creation. The previous version matched live colours by
-- equality, but white is accent AND text AND on by default, so dragging a
-- picker through a shared value fused those roles and they recoloured together
-- from then on. Storing the role removes the guesswork entirely.
SilenceAPI._themed     = {}   -- { inst, prop, token }
SilenceAPI._themedGrads = {}  -- UIGradients painted from the accent

-- Widget registries. Declared up here rather than beside the widget
-- factories because the HUD and the shell header are built long before
-- those and register into them as well.
SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers = {}   -- re-read state -> repaint; run after a
                                    -- config load, a keybind or a recolour
SilenceAPI._searchRows       = {}   -- { {inst=, label=, tab=} } for the search
SilenceAPI._searchExpanders  = {}   -- fn(searching) for groups that collapse
SilenceAPI._currentTabName   = nil  -- set by addTab while a page is filled
function SilenceAPI._theme(inst, prop, token)
    table.insert(SilenceAPI._themed, { inst = inst, prop = prop, token = token })
    pcall(function() inst[prop] = Theme[token] end)
    return inst
end

    jumpSound = "",           -- optional rbxassetid, only if you have no mp3
    acBlocked = false,        -- true after a scan finds critical anti-cheat hits
    acRiskyOverride = false,  -- user-set: ignore acBlocked and use features anyway
    acHits = 0,               -- last scan hit count (for banner display)
    floatVisible = true,      -- left-side floating S icon visibility (Misc toggle)
    menuBlur = true,          -- blur the world behind the panel while it is up
    -- Hotkey overrides (user-customizable)
    hotkeys = {
        toggleGUI = Enum.KeyCode.RightShift,
        minimize  = Enum.KeyCode.RightControl,
        stopEmote = Enum.KeyCode.E,
    },
    colors = {
        espOutline = Color3.fromRGB(170,90,255),
        espFill    = Color3.fromRGB(170,90,255),
        espNeutral = Color3.fromRGB(200,200,200),
        trail      = Color3.fromRGB(170,90,255),
        tracers    = Color3.fromRGB(170,90,255),
        nametag    = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255),
        espFriendly = Color3.fromRGB(80,200,255),
        espEnemy    = Color3.fromRGB(255,80,80),
        -- panel chrome, editable under Misc > UI COLORS
        uiAccent    = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255),
        uiAccent2   = Color3.fromRGB(220,220,220),
        uiText      = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255),
        uiTextDim   = Color3.fromRGB(170,170,170),
        uiBg        = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0),
        uiBg2       = Color3.fromRGB(12,12,12),
        uiBg3       = Color3.fromRGB(22,22,22),
    },
    freecamSpeed = 50,
}

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- UTILS
----------------------------------------------------------------
-- Custom notifications: black/purple, white text, slide-in from right,
-- hover to pin, click to dismiss. Forward decl; built once GUI is up.
local NotifyStack
SilenceAPI._notifHistory = {}
local function notify(title, text, dur)
    -- keep a rolling record so the Logs tab can show what scrolled past
    local h = SilenceAPI._notifHistory
    table.insert(h, { t = os.date("%H:%M:%S"), title = tostring(title), text = tostring(text) })
    if #h > 120 then table.remove(h, 1) end
    if NotifyStack then NotifyStack:push(title, text, dur) end
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- JUMPSCARE
-- Delivered through the same endpoint the ranks use, so it can only reach an
-- account that is running Silence. The receiver can switch it off in Misc - a
-- prank nobody can opt out of stops being a prank.
--
-- ON VIDEO: Roblox cannot play one. VideoFrame exists but only accepts video
-- assets uploaded to Roblox, and that upload is not open to normal accounts.
-- An mp4 on GitHub has no decoder to reach. What DOES work is a numbered run
-- of stills played back fast next to the extracted audio, which is a short
-- video in everything but name.
--
-- Every asset comes through loadLocalImage, which is misnamed: it checks the
-- Silence folder, falls back to the repo, writes the download to disk and
-- hands back a getcustomasset id. Nothing in it cares whether the bytes are
-- a PNG or an MP3.
--
--   jumpscare.png                  single still, used when there are no frames
--   jumpscare1.png .. jumpscareN   played at 12fps - the video
--   jumpscare.mp3                  the sound, and it decides the length
--
-- Repo layout: a jumpscare/ subfolder is tried first, then the repo root.
----------------------------------------------------------------
do
    local function jumpAsset(names, file)
        return loadLocalImage(names, ICON_BASE .. "jumpscare/" .. file)
            or loadLocalImage(names, ICON_BASE .. file)
    end

    SilenceAPI._jumpFrames = nil
    SilenceAPI._jumpReady  = false

    function SilenceAPI._loadJumpFrames()
        if SilenceAPI._jumpFrames and #SilenceAPI._jumpFrames > 0 then
            return SilenceAPI._jumpFrames
        end
        local out = {}
        for i = 1, 64 do
            local f = jumpAsset({ "jumpscare" .. i .. ".png" },
                                "jumpscare" .. i .. ".png")
            if not f then break end
            out[i] = f
        end
        -- Only a result that found something is kept. Caching an empty table
        -- poisoned the whole session: {} is truthy in Lua, so one attempt
        -- made before the files were uploaded meant every later call returned
        -- that empty table without retrying and went straight to the
        -- placeholder.
        if #out > 0 then SilenceAPI._jumpFrames = out end
        return out
    end

    -- Fetched in the background shortly after load, so the first jumpscare
    -- already has everything. Fetching at the moment of the scare is exactly
    -- what put the placeholder on screen first: 34 downloads take seconds and
    -- the effect had already begun.
    task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(3)
        SilenceAPI._jumpImg = jumpAsset(
            { "jumpscare.png", "jump.png", "scare.png" }, "jumpscare.png")
        SilenceAPI._jumpSnd = jumpAsset(
            { "jumpscare.mp3", "jumpscare.ogg", "jumpscare.wav" }, "jumpscare.mp3")
        SilenceAPI._loadJumpFrames()
        SilenceAPI._jumpReady = true
        print("[Silence] jumpscare ready: frames="
              .. tostring(#(SilenceAPI._jumpFrames or {}))
              .. "  sound=" .. tostring(SilenceAPI._jumpSnd ~= nil))
    end)

    function SilenceAPI._jumpscare(fromName)
        if State.allowJumpscare == false then return end

        task.spawn(function()
            -- Wait for the assets rather than drawing a placeholder over
            -- them. Nothing is on screen yet, so waiting costs nobody
            -- anything and the real thing starts on its very first frame.
            local t0 = tick()
            while not SilenceAPI._jumpReady and tick() - t0 < 8 do
                task.wait(0.1)
            end

            local frames = SilenceAPI._loadJumpFrames()
            local still  = SilenceAPI._jumpImg

            local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
            sg.Name = "__SIL_Jump"
            sg.IgnoreGuiInset = true
            sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
            sg.DisplayOrder = 2000000
            protect(sg)
            sg.Parent = pickGuiParent()

            -- One closer, called from several places. Whichever fires first
            -- wins and the rest are no-ops, so no path can leave a black
            -- screen with no way back.
            local closed = false
            local function close()
                if closed then return end
                closed = true
                pcall(function() sg:Destroy() end)
            end

            local back = Instance.new("Frame", sg)
            back.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            back.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            back.BorderSizePixel = 0
            back.ZIndex = 1

            if #frames > 0 or still then
                local img = Instance.new("ImageLabel", sg)
                img.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                img.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                img.Image = frames[1] or still
                img.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
                img.ZIndex = 2
                if #frames > 1 then
                    task.spawn(function()
                        local i = 1
                        while sg.Parent and not closed do
                            i = i % #frames + 1
                            img.Image = frames[i]
                            task.wait(1 / 12)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            else
                -- genuinely nothing on disk and nothing downloadable, so the
                -- fallback has to carry it alone
                local t = Instance.new("TextLabel", sg)
                t.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                t.Text = "BOO"
                t.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                t.TextScaled = true
                t.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 30, 30)
                t.ZIndex = 2
                task.spawn(function()
                    while sg.Parent and not closed do
                        back.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(math.random(0, 60), 0, 0)
                        t.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, math.random(0, 60), math.random(0, 60))
                        task.wait(0.05)
                    end
                end)
            end

            local sndId = SilenceAPI._jumpSnd or tostring(State.jumpSound or "")
            local snd
            if sndId ~= "" then pcall(function()
                snd = Instance.new("Sound")
                snd.SoundId = sndId
                -- 10 is the ceiling Roblox allows, and a Sound under a
                -- ScreenGui has no distance falloff, so this is as loud as
                -- the engine can be made to play it
                snd.Volume = 10
                snd.Parent = sg
                snd:Play()
            end) end

            -- The sound decides how long this lasts. TimeLength reads 0 until
            -- the asset has loaded, so it is polled briefly rather than
            -- trusted straight away, and Ended closes it either way.
            if snd then
                pcall(function() snd.Ended:Connect(close) end)
                task.spawn(function()
                    local t1 = tick()
                    while tick() - t1 < 5 and not closed do
                        local len = 0
                        pcall(function() len = snd.TimeLength end)
                        if type(len) == "number" and len > 0.1 then
                            task.delay(len + 0.3, close)
                            return
                        end
                        task.wait(0.1)
                    end
                    task.delay(2, close)
                end)
            else
                task.delay(1.7, close)
            end
            -- nothing may stay on screen longer than this, whatever happens
            task.delay(20, close)

            task.spawn(function()
                local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
                local t2 = tick()
                while tick() - t2 < 1.1 and not closed do
                    pcall(function()
                        cam.CFrame = cam.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(
                            math.rad(math.random(-4, 4)),
                            math.rad(math.random(-4, 4)), 0)
                    end)
                    task.wait()
                end
            end)

            if fromName and fromName ~= "test" then
                task.delay(0.2, function()
                    notify("Silence", tostring(fromName) .. " sent you that.", 6)
                end)
            end
        end)
    end
end

-- one place decides how a player's name is shown, so streamer mode can
-- blank it out everywhere at once
function SilenceAPI._safeName(p)
    if not p then return "?" end
    if not State.streamerMode then return p.DisplayName end
    local id = 0
    for i, q in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if q == p then id = i break end end
    return "Player " .. id
end
local function getChar() return LocalPlayer.Character end
local function getHRP() local c=getChar(); return c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") end
local function getHum() local c=getChar(); return c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") end

local function findPlayer(name)
    if not name or name == "" then return nil end
    name = name:lower()
    if name == "me" then return LocalPlayer end
    if name == "random" then
        local list = {}
        for _,p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= LocalPlayer then table.insert(list,p) end
        end
        return list[math.random(1, math.max(1,#list))]
    end
    for _,p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p.Name:lower():sub(1,#name)==name
        or p.DisplayName:lower():sub(1,#name)==name then return p end
    end
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- FLY
----------------------------------------------------------------
local flyConn
local flyKeys = { W=false,A=false,S=false,D=false,Space=false,LeftControl=false,LeftShift=false }
local function stopFly()
    State.flying=false
    if flyConn then flyConn:Disconnect() flyConn=nil end
    local hum=getHum(); if hum then hum.PlatformStand=false end
    local hrp=getHRP()
    if hrp then
        for _,n in ipairs({"__FlyBV","__FlyBG"}) do
            local x=hrp:FindFirstChild(n); if x then x:Destroy() end
        end
    end
end
local function startFly(speed)
    stopFly()
    local hrp,hum=getHRP(),getHum()
    if not hrp or not hum then return end
    State.flying=true; State.flySpeed=speed or State.flySpeed
    hum.PlatformStand=true
    local bv=Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
    bv.Name="__FlyBV"; bv.MaxForce=Vector3.new(9e9,9e9,9e9)  -- math.huge ersetzt
    bv.Velocity=Vector3.zero; bv.Parent=hrp
    local bg=Instance.new("BodyGyro")
    bg.Name="__FlyBG"; bg.MaxTorque=Vector3.new(9e9,9e9,9e9)
    bg.P=9e4; bg.D=1000; bg.CFrame=hrp.CFrame; bg.Parent=hrp
    flyConn=RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not State.flying then return end
        hrp=getHRP(); if not hrp then return end
        local cam=Workspace.CurrentCamera
        local dir=Vector3.zero
        if flyKeys.W then dir=dir+cam.CFrame.LookVector end
        if flyKeys.S then dir=dir-cam.CFrame.LookVector end
        if flyKeys.A then dir=dir-cam.CFrame.RightVector end
        if flyKeys.D then dir=dir+cam.CFrame.RightVector end
        if flyKeys.Space then dir=dir+Vector3.new(0,1,0) end
        if flyKeys.LeftControl then dir=dir-Vector3.new(0,1,0) end
        -- Stealth: cap speed + shift multiplier so server-side speed checks don't trip
        local stealth = SilenceAPI._acStealth and SilenceAPI._acStealth()
        local mult = flyKeys.LeftShift and (stealth and 1.4 or 2.5) or 1
        local maxSpeed = stealth and math.min(State.flySpeed, 50) or State.flySpeed
        bv.Velocity = dir.Magnitude>0 and dir.Unit*maxSpeed*mult or Vector3.zero
        bg.CFrame = cam.CFrame
    end)
end
track(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(i,gp)
    if gp then return end
    local k=i.KeyCode.Name; if flyKeys[k]~=nil then flyKeys[k]=true end
end))
track(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
    local k=i.KeyCode.Name; if flyKeys[k]~=nil then flyKeys[k]=false end
end))

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- INF JUMP
----------------------------------------------------------------
track(UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
    if State.infJump then
        local hum=getHum(); if hum then hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end
    end
end))

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- ESP / CHAMS
----------------------------------------------------------------
local function clearEspFor(plr)
    local set=State.espObjects[plr]
    if set then
        for _,o in pairs(set) do pcall(function() o:Destroy() end) end
        State.espObjects[plr]=nil
    end
end
local function clearAllEsp()
    for plr,_ in pairs(State.espObjects) do clearEspFor(plr) end
end
local function espColorFor(plr)
    if State.espTeamCheck and plr.Team and LocalPlayer.Team then
        if plr.Team == LocalPlayer.Team then
            return State.colors.espFriendly, State.colors.espFriendly
        else
            return State.colors.espEnemy, State.colors.espEnemy
        end
    end
    if State.espTeamCheck and (not plr.Team or not LocalPlayer.Team) then
        return State.colors.espNeutral, State.colors.espNeutral
    end
    return State.colors.espOutline, State.colors.espFill
end
local function buildEspFor(plr)
    if plr==LocalPlayer or not plr.Character then return end
    clearEspFor(plr)
    local set={}; State.espObjects[plr]=set
    local hl=Instance.new("Highlight")
    hl.Name="__SIL_HL"
    hl.FillTransparency = State.chamsOn and 0.35 or 1
    hl.OutlineTransparency=0
    local oc, fc = espColorFor(plr)
    hl.FillColor=fc; hl.OutlineColor=oc
    hl.DepthMode=Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
    hl.Adornee=plr.Character; hl.Parent=plr.Character
    set.hl=hl
    local bb=Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    bb.Name="__SIL_BB"; bb.AlwaysOnTop=true
    bb.Size=UDim2.new(0,200,0,50); bb.StudsOffset=Vector3.new(0,3,0)
    bb.LightInfluence=0
    local lbl=Instance.new("TextLabel",bb)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency=1; lbl.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
    lbl.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; lbl.TextSize=14
    lbl.TextColor3=Theme.text; lbl.TextStrokeTransparency=1
    lbl.Text=plr.Name
    bb.Parent=plr.Character
    set.bb,set.lbl=bb,lbl
    local h=plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head"); if h then bb.Adornee=h end

    -- BOX + HEALTH BAR. BillboardGui Size scale is measured in studs, so the
    -- box shrinks with distance on its own instead of needing a per-frame
    -- WorldToViewportPoint projection.
    local hrp0 = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if hrp0 then
        local box=Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        box.Name="__SIL_BOX"; box.AlwaysOnTop=true; box.LightInfluence=0
        box.Size=UDim2.new(4.5,0,6.5,0); box.Adornee=hrp0
        box.Enabled=false
        box.Parent=plr.Character

        local frame=Instance.new("Frame",box)
        frame.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0); frame.BackgroundTransparency=1
        frame.BorderSizePixel=0
        local bstroke=Instance.new("UIStroke",frame)
        bstroke.Thickness=2; bstroke.Color=oc
        bstroke.ApplyStrokeMode=Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border

        local hbBg=Instance.new("Frame",box)
        hbBg.Size=UDim2.new(0,4,1,0); hbBg.Position=UDim2.new(0,-7,0,0)
        hbBg.BackgroundColor3=Theme.bg2; hbBg.BorderSizePixel=0
        hbBg.Visible=false
        local hbFill=Instance.new("Frame",hbBg)
        hbFill.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0,1)
        hbFill.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,1,0); hbFill.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
        hbFill.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(80,220,90); hbFill.BorderSizePixel=0

        set.box,set.boxStroke,set.hbBg,set.hbFill = box,bstroke,hbBg,hbFill
    end
end
local espUpdateConn
local espFrameCounter = 0
local function setEsp(on)
    State.espOn=on
    if espUpdateConn then espUpdateConn:Disconnect() espUpdateConn=nil end
    -- Tracers render from their own ScreenGui and never touch State.espObjects,
    -- so they are no reason to keep the highlights and the RenderStepped loop
    -- alive. Including them here made ESP impossible to switch back off.
    if not on and not State.chamsOn then clearAllEsp() return end
    for _,p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p~=LocalPlayer and p.Character then buildEspFor(p) end
    end
    espUpdateConn=RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        espFrameCounter = espFrameCounter + 1
        if espFrameCounter % 2 == 0 then return end  -- nur jeden 2. Frame
        local myHrp=getHRP()
        for _,p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p~=LocalPlayer then
                if p.Character and not State.espObjects[p] then buildEspFor(p) end
                local set=State.espObjects[p]
                if set and p.Character then
                    local oc, fc = espColorFor(p)
                    if set.hl then
                        -- was hard-coded to true, which pinned every outline on
                        set.hl.Enabled = State.espOn or State.chamsOn
                        set.hl.FillTransparency = State.chamsOn and 0.35 or 1
                        set.hl.OutlineTransparency = State.espOn and 0 or 1
                        set.hl.OutlineColor = oc
                        set.hl.FillColor = fc
                    end
                    if set.bb then set.bb.Enabled = State.espOn and State.nametagsOn end
                    local hum=p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    local hrp=p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if set.box then
                        set.box.Enabled = State.espOn and State.espBox
                        if set.boxStroke then set.boxStroke.Color = oc end
                        if set.box.Adornee ~= hrp then set.box.Adornee = hrp end
                    end
                    if set.hbBg then
                        set.hbBg.Visible = State.espOn and State.espHealthBar
                        if set.hbFill and hum and hum.MaxHealth > 0 then
                            local r = math.clamp(hum.Health / hum.MaxHealth, 0, 1)
                            set.hbFill.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, r, 0)
                            set.hbFill.BackgroundColor3 =
                                Color3.fromRGB(math.floor(245*(1-r)), math.floor(215*r)+30, 60)
                        end
                    end
                    if set.lbl and hum and hrp and myHrp then
                        local d=(hrp.Position-myHrp.Position).Magnitude
                        set.lbl.Text=string.format("%s\n[%d hp] [%d studs]",
                            SilenceAPI._safeName(p), math.floor(hum.Health), math.floor(d))
                        set.lbl.TextColor3 = State.colors.nametag
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        for plr,_ in pairs(State.espObjects) do
            if not plr.Parent or not plr.Character then clearEspFor(plr) end
        end
    end)
end
local function setChams(on)
    State.chamsOn=on
    if on and not espUpdateConn then setEsp(true); State.espOn=false end
    for _,set in pairs(State.espObjects) do
        if set.hl then
            set.hl.FillTransparency = on and 0.35 or 1
            set.hl.OutlineTransparency = (State.espOn or on) and 0 or 1
            set.hl.Enabled = State.espOn or on
        end
        if set.bb then set.bb.Enabled = State.espOn and State.nametagsOn end
    end
    if not State.espOn and not on then
        if espUpdateConn then espUpdateConn:Disconnect() espUpdateConn = nil end
        clearAllEsp()
    end
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- TRACERS
----------------------------------------------------------------
local tracerConn,tracerGui
local tracerFrames={}
local function clearTracers()
    for _,f in pairs(tracerFrames) do f:Destroy() end
    tracerFrames={}
    if tracerGui then tracerGui:Destroy() tracerGui=nil end
end
local function setTracers(on)
    State.tracersOn=on
    if tracerConn then tracerConn:Disconnect() tracerConn=nil end
    clearTracers()
    if not on then return end
    tracerGui=Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    tracerGui.Name="__SIL_Tracers"; tracerGui.IgnoreGuiInset=true
    tracerGui.ResetOnSpawn=false
    protect(tracerGui); tracerGui.Parent=pickGuiParent()
    local tcFrame = 0
    tracerConn=RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        tcFrame = tcFrame + 1
        if tcFrame % 2 == 0 then return end
        for _,p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p~=LocalPlayer and p.Character then
                local hrp=p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if hrp then
                    local pos,vis=Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(hrp.Position)
                    local f=tracerFrames[p]
                    if not f then
                        f=Instance.new("Frame")
                        f.BorderSizePixel=0; f.BackgroundColor3=State.colors.tracers
                        f.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5); f.Parent=tracerGui
                        tracerFrames[p]=f
                    end
                    if vis then
                        local s=Camera.ViewportSize
                        local fx,fy=s.X/2,s.Y
                        local dx,dy=pos.X-fx,pos.Y-fy
                        local len=math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy)
                        local ang=math.deg(math.atan2(dy,dx))-90
                        f.Visible=true
                        f.Size=UDim2.new(0,1,0,len)
                        f.Position=UDim2.new(0,(fx+pos.X)/2,0,(fy+pos.Y)/2)
                        f.Rotation=ang
                    else f.Visible=false end
                end
            end
        end
        for p,f in pairs(tracerFrames) do
            if not p.Parent then f:Destroy() tracerFrames[p]=nil end
        end
    end)
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- LIGHTING
----------------------------------------------------------------
local function setFullbright(on)
    State.fullbright=on
    if on then
        State.lightingBackup={
            Ambient=Lighting.Ambient, OutdoorAmbient=Lighting.OutdoorAmbient,
            Brightness=Lighting.Brightness, FogEnd=Lighting.FogEnd,
            ClockTime=Lighting.ClockTime, GlobalShadows=Lighting.GlobalShadows,
        }
        Lighting.Ambient=Color3.new(1,1,1); Lighting.OutdoorAmbient=Color3.new(1,1,1)
        Lighting.Brightness=3; Lighting.FogEnd=9e9
        Lighting.ClockTime=14; Lighting.GlobalShadows=false
    elseif State.lightingBackup then
        for k,v in pairs(State.lightingBackup) do Lighting[k]=v end
    end
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- DISCO
----------------------------------------------------------------
local discoConn
local function setDisco(on)
    State.disco=on
    if discoConn then discoConn:Disconnect() discoConn=nil end
    if not on then
        Lighting.Ambient=Color3.fromRGB(70,70,70)
        Lighting.ColorShift_Top=Color3.new(0,0,0)
        return
    end
    local t=0
    discoConn=RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        t=t+dt*2
        Lighting.Ambient=Color3.fromHSV(t%1,1,1)
        Lighting.ColorShift_Top=Color3.fromHSV((t+0.5)%1,1,0.5)
    end)
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- FREECAM
----------------------------------------------------------------
local freecamConn
local freecamPos,freecamRot
local _freecamControls = nil
local fcKeys={W=false,A=false,S=false,D=false,E=false,Q=false,LeftShift=false,LeftControl=false}
local function setFreecamControls(disable)
    pcall(function()
        if not _freecamControls then
            local ps = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
            if not ps then
                ps = game:GetService("StarterPlayer"):FindFirstChild("StarterPlayerScripts")
            end
            if ps then
                local pm = ps:FindFirstChild("PlayerModule")
                if pm then
                    _freecamControls = require(pm):GetControls()
                end
            end
        end
        if _freecamControls then
            if disable then _freecamControls:Disable() else _freecamControls:Enable() end
        end
    end)
end
local function setFreecam(on)
    State.freecam=on
    if freecamConn then freecamConn:Disconnect() freecamConn=nil end
    if on then
        Camera.CameraType=Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
        freecamPos=Camera.CFrame.Position; freecamRot=Vector2.new(0,0)
        UserInputService.MouseBehavior=Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCurrentPosition
        setFreecamControls(true)
        freecamConn=RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
            local dx=UserInputService:GetMouseDelta()
            freecamRot=freecamRot+Vector2.new(-dx.Y*0.4,-dx.X*0.4)
            freecamRot=Vector2.new(math.clamp(freecamRot.X,-89,89),freecamRot.Y)
            local cf=CFrame.new(freecamPos)*CFrame.Angles(0,math.rad(freecamRot.Y),0)
                *CFrame.Angles(math.rad(freecamRot.X),0,0)
            local mv=Vector3.zero
            if fcKeys.W then mv=mv+cf.LookVector end
            if fcKeys.S then mv=mv-cf.LookVector end
            if fcKeys.A then mv=mv-cf.RightVector end
            if fcKeys.D then mv=mv+cf.RightVector end
            if fcKeys.E then mv=mv+Vector3.new(0,1,0) end
            if fcKeys.Q then mv=mv-Vector3.new(0,1,0) end
            local sp = State.freecamSpeed or 50
            if fcKeys.LeftShift then sp=sp*4 end
            if fcKeys.LeftControl then sp=sp/4 end
            freecamPos=freecamPos+mv*sp*dt
            Camera.CFrame=cf
        end)
    else
        UserInputService.MouseBehavior=Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
        Camera.CameraType=Enum.CameraType.Custom
        Camera.CameraSubject=getHum()
        setFreecamControls(false)
    end
    if SilenceAPI._setMenuBlur then SilenceAPI._setMenuBlur() end
end
track(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(i,gp)
    if gp then return end
    local k=i.KeyCode.Name; if fcKeys[k]~=nil then fcKeys[k]=true end
end))
track(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
    local k=i.KeyCode.Name; if fcKeys[k]~=nil then fcKeys[k]=false end
end))

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- SPECTATE / ANTI-AFK / HITBOX / INVIS / TRAIL / BIGHEAD / GOD / SLOWMO / CRASHFX / AIMBOT
----------------------------------------------------------------
local function setSpectate(p)
    State.spectateTgt=p
    if p and p.Character then
        Camera.CameraSubject=p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or p.Character
    else Camera.CameraSubject=getHum() end
end

local afkConn
local function setAntiAfk(on)
    State.antiAfk=on
    if afkConn then afkConn:Disconnect() afkConn=nil end
    if on then
        local vu=game:GetService("VirtualUser")
        afkConn=LocalPlayer.Idled:Connect(function()
            vu:CaptureController(); vu:ClickButton2(Vector2.new())
        end)
    end
end
SilenceAPI._setAntiAfk = setAntiAfk

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- VC BYPASS - OHNE Metatable-Patch (nur regelmaessige Re-Joins)
----------------------------------------------------------------
local vcBypassConns = {}
local function _vcClearConns()
    for _, c in ipairs(vcBypassConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    vcBypassConns = {}
end

local function setVcBypass(on)
    State.vcBypass = on
    _vcClearConns()
    if SilenceAPI._vcAutoOff then pcall(SilenceAPI._vcAutoOff) end
    if not on then return end

    -- Only the timer lives here. Detecting a dead session and rebuilding one
    -- both need the method names that were found by probing, and those live
    -- in the Voice tab block (SilenceAPI._vcState / _vcHardReset).
    --
    -- Ten seconds between checks, and the reset holds its own cooldown on
    -- top. The version this replaces re-handshaked every 1.5 seconds whether
    -- or not anything was wrong, and cycling the microphone permission that
    -- often is by itself enough to make voice unusable.
    -- The watcher itself lives in the Voice tab block, next to the method
    -- names that were confirmed by probing this build. This is only the switch.
    if SilenceAPI._vcAutoOn then pcall(SilenceAPI._vcAutoOn) end
end
SilenceAPI._setVcBypass = setVcBypass

local hitboxConn,hitboxOriginal=nil,{}
local function setHitbox(size)
    State.hitboxSize = size
    if hitboxConn then hitboxConn:Disconnect() hitboxConn=nil end
    if size == nil then
        for p,info in pairs(hitboxOriginal) do
            if p.Character then
                local hrp=p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if hrp then hrp.Size=info.size; hrp.Transparency=info.trans end
            end
        end
        hitboxOriginal={}; return
    end
    if SilenceAPI._acStealth and SilenceAPI._acStealth() then
        notify("Hitbox", "Skipped - too risky under detected AC.", 4)
        State.hitboxSize = nil
        return
    end
    hitboxConn=RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        for _,p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p~=LocalPlayer and p.Character then
                local hrp=p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if hrp then
                    if not hitboxOriginal[p] then
                        hitboxOriginal[p]={size=hrp.Size,trans=hrp.Transparency}
                    end
                    hrp.Size=Vector3.new(size,size,size); hrp.Transparency=0.6
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end


local origHeadSize
local function setBigHead(scale)
    State.bighead = scale and scale>0
    local c=getChar(); if not c then return end
    local h=c:FindFirstChild("Head"); if not h then return end
    if not origHeadSize then origHeadSize=h.Size end
    h.Size = (scale and scale>0) and (origHeadSize*scale) or origHeadSize
end

local trailObj
local function setTrail(on)
    State.trail=on
    local c=getChar(); if not c then return end
    local hrp=c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"); if not hrp then return end
    if trailObj then trailObj:Destroy() trailObj=nil end
    if not on then return end
    local a0=Instance.new("Attachment",hrp); a0.Position=Vector3.new(0,2,0); a0.Name="__SIL_A0"
    local a1=Instance.new("Attachment",hrp); a1.Position=Vector3.new(0,-2,0); a1.Name="__SIL_A1"
    local t=Instance.new("Trail")
    t.Name="__SIL_Trail"; t.Attachment0=a0; t.Attachment1=a1
    t.Lifetime=1.2; t.MinLength=0
    t.WidthScale=NumberSequence.new(1,0)
    local tc = State.colors.trail
    t.Color=ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,    tc),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,  Color3.new(
            math.min(1,tc.R+0.2), math.min(1,tc.G+0.2), math.min(1,tc.B+0.2))),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,    Color3.new(1,1,1)),
    })
    t.Parent=hrp; trailObj=t
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- CUSTOM ACCESSORIES  (client-side, visual only - other players
-- don't see these). IDs live in State.accHead / State.accTorso;
-- getgenv().Head and getgenv().Torso are still honoured so an
-- existing config keeps working without changes.
----------------------------------------------------------------
-- This chunk sits at 199 of Luau's 200-local ceiling, so the whole module
-- lives inside a do-block and is published on SilenceAPI instead of adding
-- main-chunk locals (same convention as _refreshAcLocks / _destroyTags).
do
local activeAccessories  = {}
-- how each mounted item attached: "weld" | "charmesh" | "limbmesh".
-- A mesh swap repaints the limb in place, so blanking that limb would erase
-- the very thing we just applied. _slotHiddenParts reads this.
local mountKindByKey     = {}
local headlessConnection = nil
local accAddedConn, accRemovingConn = nil, nil

-- accepts a table of ids or a "123, 456" string, returns clean numbers
local function parseIdList(v)
    local out = {}
    if type(v) == "table" then
        for _, id in ipairs(v) do
            local n = tonumber(id)
            if n and n ~= 0 then table.insert(out, n) end
        end
    elseif type(v) == "string" then
        for word in v:gmatch("%d+") do
            local n = tonumber(word)
            if n and n ~= 0 then table.insert(out, n) end
        end
    end
    return out
end

-- executors give us game:GetObjects; InsertService is the fallback
local function loadAssetRoot(assetId)
    local url = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(assetId)
    local ok, objs = pcall(function() return game:GetObjects(url) end)
    if ok and type(objs) == "table" and objs[1] then return objs[1] end
    local ok2, model = pcall(function()
        return game:GetService("InsertService"):LoadAsset(assetId)
    end)
    if ok2 and model then return model end
    return nil
end

local function addAccessoryToCharacter(accessoryId, attachPart, character, keyOverride, r6Limb)
    if not accessoryId or accessoryId == 0 then return end
    if not attachPart or not attachPart.Parent then return end
    if not character or not character.Parent then return end

    -- a named slot passes its own key so switching it off removes exactly
    -- that accessory, even if two slots share the same asset id
    local key = keyOverride or (tostring(accessoryId) .. "@" .. attachPart.Name)
    local existing = activeAccessories[key]
    if existing and existing.Parent then return end

    local root = loadAssetRoot(accessoryId)
    if not root then return end

    -- Most catalog items load as an Accessory. Body-part bundles like Korblox
    -- do not: they come back as a CharacterMesh (R6) or a bare MeshPart (R15),
    -- and the old code threw those away, which is why a leg id did nothing.
    local acc = root:IsA("Accessory") and root or root:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Accessory", true)
    local handle
    if acc then
        handle = acc:FindFirstChild("Handle")
    else
        local cm = root:IsA("CharacterMesh") and root
                or root:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("CharacterMesh", true)
        if cm then
            -- R6 body meshes simply live inside the character
            local clone = cm:Clone()
            clone.Name = "__SIL_Mesh_" .. tostring(accessoryId)
            clone.Parent = character
            activeAccessories[key] = clone
            mountKindByKey[key] = "charmesh"
            pcall(function() root:Destroy() end)
            return
        end

        -- R6 limb target: the asset is shaped for an R15 rig, where a leg is
        -- three separate parts. Welding that onto a single R6 "Right Leg"
        -- leaves a third-length stub floating mid-thigh, which is exactly the
        -- "Korblox hangs in the air" symptom. Repaint the limb instead.
        if r6Limb then
            local src = root:IsA("BasePart") and root
                     or root:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
            local meshId, texId
            if src then
                if src:IsA("MeshPart") then
                    meshId, texId = src.MeshId, src.TextureID
                else
                    local sm = src:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("DataModelMesh")
                    if sm and sm:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
                        meshId, texId = sm.MeshId, sm.TextureId
                    end
                end
            end
            pcall(function() root:Destroy() end)
            if not meshId or meshId == "" then return end
            for _, d in ipairs(r6Limb:GetChildren()) do
                if d.Name == "__SIL_LimbMesh" then pcall(function() d:Destroy() end) end
            end
            local m = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
            m.Name     = "__SIL_LimbMesh"
            m.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
            m.MeshId   = meshId
            if texId and texId ~= "" then m.TextureId = texId end
            m.Parent = r6Limb
            activeAccessories[key] = m
            mountKindByKey[key] = "limbmesh"
            return
        end
        -- a loose mesh: wrap it so the welding path below still applies
        local part = root:IsA("BasePart") and root
                  or root:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
        if part then
            local wrapper, oldRoot = Instance.new("Accessory"), root
            part.Name = "Handle"
            part.Parent = wrapper
            if oldRoot ~= part and oldRoot.Parent then
                pcall(function() oldRoot:Destroy() end)
            end
            -- root == acc from here so the tail cleanup leaves it alone
            acc, handle, root = wrapper, part, wrapper
        end
    end
    if not acc or not handle then pcall(function() root:Destroy() end) return end

    -- character may have died/respawned while the asset was downloading
    if not character.Parent or not attachPart.Parent then
        pcall(function() root:Destroy() end) return
    end

    for _, d in ipairs(acc:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("BaseScript") then pcall(function() d:Destroy() end) end
    end
    handle.CanCollide = false
    handle.Anchored   = false
    pcall(function() handle.Massless = true end)

    -- attachment-to-attachment gives the correct offset; fall back to a
    -- plain weld on the part itself when the names don't line up
    local accAtt, partAtt
    for _, a in ipairs(handle:GetChildren()) do
        if a:IsA("Attachment") then
            local match = attachPart:FindFirstChild(a.Name)
            if match and match:IsA("Attachment") then
                accAtt, partAtt = a, match
                break
            end
            accAtt = accAtt or a
        end
    end

    for _, old in ipairs(handle:GetChildren()) do
        if old:IsA("Weld") or old:IsA("Motor6D") or old:IsA("WeldConstraint") then
            pcall(function() old:Destroy() end)
        end
    end

    local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
    weld.Name  = "__SIL_AccWeld"
    weld.Part0 = attachPart
    weld.Part1 = handle
    if accAtt and partAtt then
        weld.C0 = partAtt.CFrame
        weld.C1 = accAtt.CFrame
    else
        weld.C0 = CFrame.new()
        weld.C1 = CFrame.new()
    end
    weld.Parent = handle

    acc.Name   = "__SIL_Acc_" .. tostring(accessoryId)
    acc.Parent = character
    activeAccessories[key] = acc
    mountKindByKey[key] = "weld"

    -- LoadAsset hands back a wrapper Model; drop it once the accessory is out
    if root ~= acc then pcall(function() root:Destroy() end) end
end

-- includeSlots=false leaves the named slots mounted: turning the bulk list
-- off should not silently remove the horns you toggled on separately.
local function clearAccessories(includeSlots)
    for key, accessory in pairs(activeAccessories) do
        local k = tostring(key)
        local isSlot = k:sub(1, 4) == "slot"
        -- Never touch another player's synced accessories. They live in the
        -- same registry but belong to a different character entirely, and
        -- clearing them here is not this function's business.
        local isSynced = k:sub(1, 5) == "sil_p"
        if isSynced then
            -- skip
        elseif includeSlots or not isSlot then
            if accessory and accessory.Parent then
                pcall(function() accessory:Destroy() end)
            end
            activeAccessories[key] = nil
            mountKindByKey[key] = nil
        end
    end
end

local function slotKey(i) return "slot" .. i end

-- every body part a slot target covers, across R6 and R15 naming
local PART_GROUPS = {
    Head     = { "Head" },
    Torso    = { "Torso", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso" },
    RightLeg = { "Right Leg", "RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot" },
    LeftLeg  = { "Left Leg",  "LeftUpperLeg",  "LeftLowerLeg",  "LeftFoot"  },
}
SilenceAPI._partGroups = PART_GROUPS

local function attachPartFor(character, part)
    if part == "Torso" then
        return character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
            or character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
            or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    elseif part == "RightLeg" then
        return character:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")
            or character:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
    elseif part == "LeftLeg" then
        return character:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperLeg")
            or character:FindFirstChild("Left Leg")
    end
    return character:FindFirstChild("Head")
end

-- Which body parts must stay invisible right now. Ghost / Invisible Limbs
-- own Transparency as well, so they read this instead of fighting over it.
function SilenceAPI._slotHiddenParts()
    local set = {}
    for i, sl in ipairs(State.accSlots) do
        local kind = mountKindByKey["slot" .. i]
        -- charmesh / limbmesh replace the limb in place: hiding it would
        -- hide the swapped mesh along with it
        if sl.on and sl.hide and kind ~= "charmesh" and kind ~= "limbmesh" then
            for _, n in ipairs(PART_GROUPS[sl.part] or {}) do set[n] = true end
        end
    end
    return set
end

local function unmountSlot(i)
    local a = activeAccessories[slotKey(i)]
    if a and a.Parent then pcall(function() a:Destroy() end) end
    activeAccessories[slotKey(i)] = nil
    mountKindByKey[slotKey(i)] = nil
end

local function mountSlotInto(character, i)
    local sl = State.accSlots[i]
    if not sl or not sl.on then return end
    local id = tonumber(tostring(sl.id or ""):match("%d+") or "")
    if not id then return end
    if not character or not character.Parent then return end
    local part = attachPartFor(character, sl.part)
    if not part then return end
    -- an R6 rig has one part per limb; tell the loader so it repaints that
    -- part instead of welding an R15-shaped segment onto it
    local hum = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local isLimb = (sl.part == "RightLeg" or sl.part == "LeftLeg")
    local r6Limb = nil
    if isLimb and hum and hum.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
        r6Limb = part
    end
    unmountSlot(i)
    task.spawn(function()
        addAccessoryToCharacter(id, part, character, slotKey(i), r6Limb)
        if SilenceAPI._applyBodyVisuals then pcall(SilenceAPI._applyBodyVisuals) end
    end)
end

local function anySlotOn()
    for _, sl in ipairs(State.accSlots) do if sl.on then return true end end
    return false
end

local function applyHeadless(character)
    if headlessConnection then
        headlessConnection:Disconnect()
        headlessConnection = nil
    end
    local head = character and character:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if not head then return end

    local function hide()
        pcall(function() head.Transparency = 1 end)
        for _, d in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
            if d:IsA("Decal") then pcall(function() d.Transparency = 1 end) end
        end
    end
    hide()
    -- the game re-shows the head on respawn/reset FX - put it back
    headlessConnection = head:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Transparency"):Connect(function()
        if State.headless and head.Transparency < 1 then hide() end
    end)
end

local function unapplyHeadless(character)
    if headlessConnection then
        headlessConnection:Disconnect()
        headlessConnection = nil
    end
    local head = character and character:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if not head then return end
    pcall(function() head.Transparency = 0 end)
    for _, d in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
        if d:IsA("Decal") then pcall(function() d.Transparency = 0 end) end
    end
    -- ghost mode also owns head transparency; let it have the last word
    if SilenceAPI._applyBodyVisuals then pcall(SilenceAPI._applyBodyVisuals) end
end

local function onCharacterAdded(character)
    if not (State.accessoriesOn or State.headless or anySlotOn()) then return end

    local head = character:WaitForChild("Head", 10)
    if not head then return end

    if State.headless then applyHeadless(character) end

    -- named slots are independent of the bulk lists below
    for i = 1, #State.accSlots do mountSlotInto(character, i) end
    -- a hide=true slot blanks its limb; repaint once they are all mounted
    if SilenceAPI._applyBodyVisuals then
        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(0.2)
            pcall(SilenceAPI._applyBodyVisuals)
        end)
    end

    if not State.accessoriesOn then return end

    local torso = character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
                or character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    for _, accessoryId in ipairs(State.accHead) do
        if accessoryId and accessoryId ~= 0 then
            task.spawn(function()
                addAccessoryToCharacter(accessoryId, head, character)
            end)
        end
    end

    if torso then
        for _, accessoryId in ipairs(State.accTorso) do
            if accessoryId and accessoryId ~= 0 then
                task.spawn(function()
                    addAccessoryToCharacter(accessoryId, torso, character)
                end)
            end
        end
    end
end

-- respawn hooks stay alive while either feature is on; not track()'d
-- because cleanup() calls setAccessories(false)/setHeadless(false) first
local function syncCharConns()
    if State.accessoriesOn or State.headless or anySlotOn() then
        if accAddedConn then return end
        accAddedConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
            task.spawn(function() onCharacterAdded(char) end)
        end)
        accRemovingConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
            if headlessConnection then
                headlessConnection:Disconnect()
                headlessConnection = nil
            end
            clearAccessories(true)
        end)
    else
        if accAddedConn    then accAddedConn:Disconnect();    accAddedConn = nil end
        if accRemovingConn then accRemovingConn:Disconnect(); accRemovingConn = nil end
    end
end

local function setAccessories(on)
    State.accessoriesOn = on

    if not on then
        clearAccessories(false)
        syncCharConns()
        notify("Accessories", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end

    -- pull getgenv().Head / .Torso in if the GUI lists are still empty
    if #State.accHead  == 0 then State.accHead  = parseIdList(env.Head)  end
    if #State.accTorso == 0 then State.accTorso = parseIdList(env.Torso) end

    if #State.accHead == 0 and #State.accTorso == 0 then
        State.accessoriesOn = false
        syncCharConns()
        notify("Accessories", "No IDs set - fill in Head/Torso IDs first.", 4)
        return
    end

    syncCharConns()
    if LocalPlayer.Character then
        task.spawn(function() onCharacterAdded(LocalPlayer.Character) end)
    end
    notify("Accessories", "ON - " .. #State.accHead .. " head, " .. #State.accTorso .. " torso", 3)
end

local function setHeadless(on)
    State.headless = on
    syncCharConns()
    local c = getChar()
    if on then applyHeadless(c) else unapplyHeadless(c) end
end

-- re-mount with the current ID lists (after editing the inputs)
local function reapplyAccessories()
    if not State.accessoriesOn then return end
    clearAccessories(false)
    if LocalPlayer.Character then
        task.spawn(function() onCharacterAdded(LocalPlayer.Character) end)
    end
end

local function setSlot(i, on)
    local sl = State.accSlots[i]; if not sl then return end
    sl.on = on and true or false
    syncCharConns()
    if SilenceAPI._pushStyle then SilenceAPI._pushStyle() end
    if sl.on then
        if tostring(sl.id or ""):match("%d+") then
            mountSlotInto(getChar(), i)
        else
            notify(sl.name, "No asset ID set for this slot", 3)
        end
    else
        unmountSlot(i)
    end
    -- show / restore the limb this slot covers
    if SilenceAPI._applyBodyVisuals then pcall(SilenceAPI._applyBodyVisuals) end
end

local function setSlotHide(i, on)
    local sl = State.accSlots[i]; if not sl then return end
    sl.hide = on and true or false
    if SilenceAPI._applyBodyVisuals then pcall(SilenceAPI._applyBodyVisuals) end
end

local function setSlotId(i, v)
    local sl = State.accSlots[i]; if not sl then return end
    sl.id = tostring(v or ""):match("%d+") or ""
    if sl.on then unmountSlot(i); mountSlotInto(getChar(), i) end
end

local SLOT_PART_ORDER = { "Head", "Torso", "RightLeg", "LeftLeg" }
SilenceAPI._slotPartOrder = SLOT_PART_ORDER

local function setSlotPart(i, part)
    local sl = State.accSlots[i]; if not sl then return end
    local valid = false
    for _, p in ipairs(SLOT_PART_ORDER) do if p == part then valid = true break end end
    sl.part = valid and part or "Head"
    if sl.on then unmountSlot(i); mountSlotInto(getChar(), i) end
    if SilenceAPI._applyBodyVisuals then pcall(SilenceAPI._applyBodyVisuals) end
end

local function slotsOff()
    for i = 1, #State.accSlots do
        State.accSlots[i].on = false
        unmountSlot(i)
    end
    syncCharConns()
    if SilenceAPI._applyBodyVisuals then pcall(SilenceAPI._applyBodyVisuals) end
end

-- Copying a skin strips the character down; the Skins code calls this
-- afterwards so headless and the accessory slots come straight back.
SilenceAPI._remountAfterSkin = function()
    if not (State.accessoriesOn or State.headless or anySlotOn()) then return end
    if LocalPlayer.Character then
        task.spawn(function() onCharacterAdded(LocalPlayer.Character) end)
    end
end
SilenceAPI._setSlot     = setSlot
SilenceAPI._setSlotId   = setSlotId
SilenceAPI._setSlotPart = setSlotPart
SilenceAPI._setSlotHide = setSlotHide
SilenceAPI._slotsOff    = slotsOff
SilenceAPI._parseIdList        = parseIdList
SilenceAPI._setFullbright      = setFullbright
SilenceAPI._setDisco           = setDisco
SilenceAPI._setAccessories     = function(v)
    setAccessories(v)
    if SilenceAPI._pushStyle then SilenceAPI._pushStyle() end
end
SilenceAPI._setHeadless        = function(v)
    setHeadless(v)
    if SilenceAPI._pushStyle then SilenceAPI._pushStyle() end
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- LARP APPEARANCE FOR OTHER PLAYERS
-- The local versions of these cannot simply be pointed at somebody else.
-- applyHeadless stores ONE headlessConnection in an upvalue, and the slot
-- mounts key off a slot index that only exists for you - so aiming either at
-- a second character makes the two fight over the same state, and your own
-- horns disappear the moment somebody else gets theirs.
--
-- This keeps one record per player and reuses only the primitives that
-- already take a character: addAccessoryToCharacter and attachPartFor.
-- Accessory keys carry the wearer's user id, so two people in the same
-- costume do not delete each other's copy out of activeAccessories.
--
-- An IIFE, not do..end: a do..end shares the chunk's registers and this file
-- runs at 198 of Luau's 200.
----------------------------------------------------------------
;(function()
    local worn = {}   -- [player] = { keys = {...}, conn = ..., head = ... }
    -- what each mount actually came back as, so a failure is visible instead
    -- of being guessed at from an empty leg
    local larpLog = {}
    SilenceAPI._larpLog = larpLog
    -- Players whose costume you personally do not want to see. Local and
    -- one-sided: they keep wearing it, you just stop drawing it.
    local larpHidden = {}
    SilenceAPI._isLarpHidden = function(p) return larpHidden[p] and true or false end

    local function clearFor(player)
        local rec = worn[player]
        if not rec then return end
        for _, k in ipairs(rec.keys) do
            local a = activeAccessories[k]
            if a and a.Parent then pcall(function() a:Destroy() end) end
            activeAccessories[k] = nil
            mountKindByKey[k] = nil
        end
        if rec.conn then pcall(function() rec.conn:Disconnect() end) end
        if rec.head and rec.head.Parent then
            pcall(function() rec.head.Transparency = 0 end)
            for _, d in ipairs(rec.head:GetChildren()) do
                if d:IsA("Decal") then pcall(function() d.Transparency = 0 end) end
            end
        end
        -- limbs this player's slots hid go back to whatever they were
        for _, h in ipairs(rec.hidden or {}) do
            if h.part and h.part.Parent then
                pcall(function() h.part.Transparency = h.t end)
            end
        end
        worn[player] = nil
    end

    local function applyFor(player, cfg)
        clearFor(player)
        larpLog[player] = nil
        if not player or player == LocalPlayer or not cfg then return end
        if larpHidden[player] then return end
        local char = player.Character
        if not char or not char.Parent then return end

        local rec = { keys = {} }
        worn[player] = rec

        if cfg.headless then
            local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
            if head then
                rec.head = head
                local function hide()
                    pcall(function() head.Transparency = 1 end)
                    for _, d in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
                        if d:IsA("Decal") then pcall(function() d.Transparency = 1 end) end
                    end
                end
                hide()
                -- games put the head back during respawn effects, so this
                -- has to keep watching rather than fire once
                rec.conn = head.ChildAdded:Connect(function()
                    task.wait(0.05); hide()
                end)
            end
        end

        local function mount(ids, partName, tag)
            if type(ids) ~= "table" then return end
            local part = attachPartFor(char, partName)
            if not part then return end
            for i, id in ipairs(ids) do
                local n = tonumber(tostring(id):match("%d+") or "")
                if n then
                    local key = "sil_p" .. tostring(player.UserId) .. "_" .. tag .. i
                    rec.keys[#rec.keys + 1] = key
                    task.spawn(function()
                        addAccessoryToCharacter(n, part, char, key, nil)
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
        mount(cfg.head,  "Head",  "h")
        mount(cfg.torso, "Torso", "t")

        -- The numbered slots, each with its own target limb. An R6 rig has
        -- one part per limb, so the loader has to be told to repaint that
        -- part instead of welding an R15-shaped segment onto it - the same
        -- distinction mountSlotInto makes for your own character.
        if type(cfg.slots) == "table" then
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local groups = SilenceAPI._partGroups or {}
            for i, sl in ipairs(cfg.slots) do
