                local n = tonumber(tostring(sl.id or ""):match("%d+") or "")
                local part = n and attachPartFor(char, sl.part)
                if part then
                    -- A limb bundle is shaped for whatever rig it came from.
                    -- On a single-part R6 limb the loader must repaint that
                    -- part; welding an R15-shaped segment on leaves the
                    -- third-length stub floating mid-thigh.
                    local isLimb = (sl.part == "RightLeg" or sl.part == "LeftLeg")
                    local r6 = nil
                    if isLimb and hum
                       and hum.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
                        r6 = part
                    end
                    local key = "sil_p" .. tostring(player.UserId) .. "_s" .. i
                    rec.keys[#rec.keys + 1] = key

                    task.spawn(function()
                        addAccessoryToCharacter(n, part, char, key, r6)
                        local kind = mountKindByKey[key]
                        larpLog[player] = larpLog[player] or {}
                        larpLog[player][#larpLog[player] + 1] =
                            tostring(sl.part) .. ":" .. tostring(n) .. "=" ..
                            tostring(kind or "FAILED")

                        -- The hide happens AFTER the mount, and only if
                        -- something actually arrived. Hiding first meant a
                        -- failed asset load left an invisible leg with
                        -- nothing on it - which looks exactly like "Korblox
                        -- does not show".
                        --
                        -- charmesh and limbmesh replace the limb in place, so
                        -- hiding it would take the new mesh with it. Same
                        -- rule _slotHiddenParts applies to your own body.
                        if sl.hide and activeAccessories[key]
                           and kind ~= "charmesh" and kind ~= "limbmesh" then
                            rec.hidden = rec.hidden or {}
                            for _, nm in ipairs(groups[sl.part] or {}) do
                                local bp = char:FindFirstChild(nm)
                                if bp and bp:IsA("BasePart") then
                                    rec.hidden[#rec.hidden + 1] =
                                        { part = bp, t = bp.Transparency }
                                    pcall(function() bp.Transparency = 1 end)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end

        -- Local only. The server keeps its own numbers for this player, so
        -- nobody else sees the change and it reverts on their next respawn -
        -- which the respawn hook re-applies.
        local sc = tonumber(cfg.scale)
        if sc and sc ~= 1 then
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then
                for _, n in ipairs({ "BodyDepthScale", "BodyWidthScale",
                                     "BodyHeightScale", "HeadScale" }) do
                    local v = hum:FindFirstChild(n)
                    if v then pcall(function() v.Value = sc end) end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    SilenceAPI._applyLarpFor = function(player, cfg) pcall(applyFor, player, cfg) end
    SilenceAPI._setLarpHidden = function(player, hidden)
        if not player then return end
        larpHidden[player] = hidden and true or nil
        if hidden then
            pcall(clearFor, player)
        elseif SilenceAPI._larpOf then
            -- put it straight back rather than waiting for the next poll
            pcall(applyFor, player, SilenceAPI._larpOf(player))
        end
    end
    SilenceAPI._clearLarpFor = function(player) pcall(clearFor, player) end

    -- what travels to everyone else
    SilenceAPI._myLarpPayload = function()
        -- Only the slots that are switched on and actually carry an id. An
        -- empty slot in the middle must not shift the ones after it, so this
        -- rebuilds a dense list rather than copying indices across.
        local slots = {}
        for _, sl in ipairs(State.accSlots or {}) do
            local id = sl.on and tostring(sl.id or ""):match("%d+")
            if id then
                slots[#slots + 1] = { id = id, part = sl.part,
                                      hide = sl.hide and true or false }
            end
        end
        return {
            headless = State.headless and true or false,
            head  = State.accHead  or {},
            torso = State.accTorso or {},
            slots = slots,
            scale = tonumber(State.bodyScale) or 1,
        }
    end
end)()
SilenceAPI._reapplyAccessories = reapplyAccessories
end -- CUSTOM ACCESSORIES

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- MOVEMENT / SAFETY / LARP BODY
-- Same do-block trick: everything scoped, entry points on SilenceAPI.
----------------------------------------------------------------
do
local noclipConn, antiFlingConn, antiVoidConn, larpCharConn, walkConn
local lastSafeCF, lastSafeAt, lastFlingSweep = nil, 0, 0

----------------------------------------------------------- NOCLIP
local function setNoclip(on)
    State.noclip = on
    if noclipConn then noclipConn:Disconnect(); noclipConn = nil end
    if not on then
        local c = getChar()
        if c then
            -- direct children only: accessory Handles are descendants too and
            -- must stay non-collidable, or your hat starts shoving you around
            for _, part in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                    pcall(function() part.CanCollide = true end)
                end
            end
        end
        return
    end
    noclipConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
        local c = getChar(); if not c then return end
        for _, part in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.CanCollide then
                part.CanCollide = false
            end
        end
    end)
end

------------------------------------------------------- ANTI-FLING
-- Two halves: kill our own runaway spin every frame, and (throttled)
-- make everyone else's parts massless so they can't transfer momentum.
local function setAntiFling(on)
    State.antiFling = on
    if antiFlingConn then antiFlingConn:Disconnect(); antiFlingConn = nil end
    if not on then
        -- hand everyone else their collisions back, otherwise players stay
        -- walk-through-able for the rest of the session
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= LocalPlayer and p.Character then
                for _, part in ipairs(p.Character:GetChildren()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                        pcall(function()
                            part.CanCollide = true
                            part.Massless   = false
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return
    end
    antiFlingConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local hrp = getHRP(); if not hrp then return end
        if hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity.Magnitude > 40 then
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        end
        if hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude > 350 then
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        end
        if tick() - lastFlingSweep < 0.5 then return end
        lastFlingSweep = tick()
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= LocalPlayer and p.Character then
                for _, part in ipairs(p.Character:GetDescendants()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                        pcall(function()
                            part.CanCollide = false
                            part.Massless   = true
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

-------------------------------------------------------- ANTI-VOID
local function setAntiVoid(on)
    State.antiVoid = on
    if antiVoidConn then antiVoidConn:Disconnect(); antiVoidConn = nil end
    lastSafeCF, lastSafeAt = nil, 0
    if not on then return end
    antiVoidConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local hrp = getHRP(); if not hrp then return end
        local floor = Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight + 60
        if hrp.Position.Y > floor then
            local hum = getHum()
            -- only bank a spot while actually standing on something
            if hum and hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air and tick() - lastSafeAt > 1 then
                lastSafeCF, lastSafeAt = hrp.CFrame, tick()
            end
        elseif lastSafeCF then
            hrp.CFrame = lastSafeCF + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            notify("Anti-Void", "Caught you falling - teleported back.", 2)
        end
    end)
end

------------------------------------------------------- LARP: BODY
local LIMBS_R6  = { "Torso","Left Arm","Right Arm","Left Leg","Right Leg" }
local LIMBS_R15 = {
    "UpperTorso","LowerTorso",
    "LeftUpperArm","LeftLowerArm","LeftHand",
    "RightUpperArm","RightLowerArm","RightHand",
    "LeftUpperLeg","LeftLowerLeg","LeftFoot",
    "RightUpperLeg","RightLowerLeg","RightFoot",
}

local function eachLimb(char, fn)
    for _, list in ipairs({ LIMBS_R6, LIMBS_R15 }) do
        for _, n in ipairs(list) do
            local p = char:FindFirstChild(n)
            if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then fn(p) end
        end
    end
end

-- ghost + invisible-limbs + headless all fight over Transparency, so one
-- function owns it and the individual toggles just re-run this.
local function applyBodyVisuals()
    local char = getChar(); if not char then return end
    local limbT = State.invisParts and 1 or (State.ghost and 0.6 or 0)
    -- a slot with hide=true (Korblox) owns its limb; do not paint over it
    local hidden = SilenceAPI._slotHiddenParts and SilenceAPI._slotHiddenParts() or {}
    eachLimb(char, function(p)
        pcall(function() p.Transparency = hidden[p.Name] and 1 or limbT end)
    end)

    local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if head and hidden[head.Name] then
        pcall(function() head.Transparency = 1 end)
    elseif head and not State.headless then
        pcall(function() head.Transparency = State.ghost and 0.6 or 0 end)
        for _, d in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
            if d:IsA("Decal") then
                pcall(function() d.Transparency = State.ghost and 0.6 or 0 end)
            end
        end
    end

    for _, acc in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if acc:IsA("Accessory") then
            local h = acc:FindFirstChild("Handle")
            if h then pcall(function() h.Transparency = State.ghost and 0.6 or 0 end) end
        end
    end
end

local function setGhost(on)      State.ghost = on;      applyBodyVisuals() end
local function setInvisParts(on) State.invisParts = on; applyBodyVisuals() end

-- remembers each Humanoid's untouched HipHeight so scaling stays reversible
local baseHip = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

local function applyBodyScale()
    local char = getChar(); if not char then return end
    local hum  = getHum()
    local s = tonumber(State.bodyScale) or 1
    if s <= 0 then s = 1 end
    if hum and baseHip[hum] == nil then
        local okH, h = pcall(function() return hum.HipHeight end)
        baseHip[hum] = okH and h or 0
    end

    -- Model:ScaleTo scales parts AND joints and works on R6 and R15 alike.
    -- The Humanoid *Scale NumberValues used before only exist on R15, which
    -- is why this silently did nothing on an R6 rig.
    local okScale = pcall(function() char:ScaleTo(s) end)
    if okScale then
        -- ScaleTo moves the parts but leaves HipHeight alone, so without this
        -- a scaled character sinks into the floor or hovers above it.
        if hum and baseHip[hum] and baseHip[hum] > 0 then
            pcall(function() hum.HipHeight = baseHip[hum] * s end)
        end
        return
    end

    if not hum then return end
    for _, n in ipairs({ "BodyDepthScale","BodyHeightScale","BodyWidthScale","HeadScale" }) do
        local v = hum:FindFirstChild(n)
        if not v then
            pcall(function()
                local nv = Instance.new("NumberValue")
                nv.Name = n
                nv.Parent = hum
                v = nv
            end)
        end
        if v and v:IsA("NumberValue") then pcall(function() v.Value = s end) end
    end
end

local function setBodyScale(v)
    State.bodyScale = tonumber(v) or 1
    applyBodyScale()
end

-- remembers the real face per decal so clearing the box restores it
local origFaceTex = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

local function applyFace()
    local char = getChar(); if not char then return end
    local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head"); if not head then return end
    local id = tostring(State.faceId or ""):match("%d+")
    for _, d in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
        if d:IsA("Decal") then
            if origFaceTex[d] == nil then
                local okT, t = pcall(function() return d.Texture end)
                origFaceTex[d] = okT and t or ""
            end
            pcall(function()
                d.Texture = id and ("rbxassetid://" .. id) or origFaceTex[d]
            end)
        end
    end
end

local function setFace(v)
    State.faceId = tostring(v or "")
    applyFace()
end

-------------------------------------------------- WALK ANIMATION
-- Roblox's default Animate script keeps the ids in Animate.walk.WalkAnim and
-- Animate.run.RunAnim. Swapping those and bouncing the script is what makes
-- the character actually walk with a different animation.
-- Driving the track ourselves instead of rewriting Animate.walk.WalkAnim.
-- Swapping those ids only works if the game's Animate script reloads AND the
-- asset resolves; when it does not you end up sliding around with no walk
-- cycle at all, which is exactly what happened. Playing our own track at
-- Action priority overrides the default walk without touching Animate, so
-- idle and jump keep working, and emotes (Action4) still win over it.
local walkTrack = nil

local function stopWalkAnim()
    if walkConn then walkConn:Disconnect(); walkConn = nil end
    if walkTrack then pcall(function() walkTrack:Stop(0.15) end) end
    walkTrack = nil
end

local function applyWalkAnim()
    stopWalkAnim()
    if not State.walkAnim then return true end

    local id = tostring(State.walkAnimId or ""):match("%d+")
    if not id then return false end
    local hum = getHum(); if not hum then return false end
    local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
    if not animator then
        local ok = pcall(function() animator = Instance.new("Animator", hum) end)
        if not ok or not animator then return false end
    end

    local a = Instance.new("Animation")
    a.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. id
    local ok, trk = pcall(function() return animator:LoadAnimation(a) end)
    if not ok or not trk then return false end

    trk.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
    trk.Looped   = true
    walkTrack = trk

    -- Length stays 0 when Roblox refuses the asset (wrong type, or an
    -- animation this experience is not allowed to load). Say so instead of
    -- leaving the user with a silently animation-less character.
    task.spawn(function()
        local waited = 0
        while waited < 2.5 and (trk.Length == nil or trk.Length == 0) do
            task.wait(0.1); waited = waited + 0.1
        end
        if State.walkAnim and walkTrack == trk and trk.Length == 0 then
            notify("Walk Animation",
                   "ID " .. id .. " will not load - wrong asset type, or this game blocks it.", 6)
        end
    end)

    walkConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not State.walkAnim or not walkTrack then return end
        local h = getHum(); if not h then return end
        local moving   = h.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0.1
        local grounded = h.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air
        if moving and grounded and not State.currentEmote then
            if not walkTrack.IsPlaying then pcall(function() walkTrack:Play(0.15) end) end
        elseif walkTrack.IsPlaying then
            pcall(function() walkTrack:Stop(0.15) end)
        end
    end)
    return true
end

local function setWalkAnim(on)
    State.walkAnim = on
    if on and not tostring(State.walkAnimId or ""):match("%d+") then
        State.walkAnim = false
        notify("Walk Animation", "No animation ID set", 3)
        return
    end
    if not applyWalkAnim() and on then
        notify("Walk Animation", "Could not load the animation on your character.", 4)
    end
end

local function setWalkAnimId(v)
    State.walkAnimId = tostring(v or ""):match("%d+") or ""
    if State.walkAnim then applyWalkAnim() end
end

-- everything above has to survive a respawn
local function syncLarpCharConn()
    local want = State.noclip or State.ghost or State.invisParts or State.walkAnim
              or (State.bodyScale and State.bodyScale ~= 1)
              or (State.faceId ~= nil and State.faceId ~= "")
    if want then
        if larpCharConn then return end
        larpCharConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
            char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
            task.wait(0.3)
            applyBodyVisuals(); applyBodyScale(); applyFace()
            if State.noclip then setNoclip(true) end
            if State.walkAnim then applyWalkAnim() end
        end)
    elseif larpCharConn then
        larpCharConn:Disconnect(); larpCharConn = nil
    end
end

------------------------------------------------------- WAYPOINTS
local function saveWaypoint(name)
    local hrp = getHRP()
    if not hrp then notify("Waypoints", "No character", 2); return false end
    name = (name and name ~= "") and name or ("Point " .. (#State.waypoints + 1))
    local p = hrp.Position
    table.insert(State.waypoints, { name = name, x = p.X, y = p.Y, z = p.Z })
    notify("Waypoints", 'Saved "' .. name .. '"', 2)
    return true
end

local function gotoWaypoint(i)
    local w = State.waypoints[i]
    local hrp = getHRP()
    if not w or not hrp then return end
    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(w.x, w.y + 3, w.z)
    notify("Waypoints", "-> " .. w.name, 2)
end

local function deleteWaypoint(i)
    if State.waypoints[i] then table.remove(State.waypoints, i) end
end

------------------------------------------------------ SERVER HOP
-- shared by the Settings popup button and the Movement tab
local function serverHop()
    notify("Server Hop", "Searching new server...", 2)
    local HS = game:GetService("HttpService")
    local ok, body = pcall(function()
        return game:HttpGet("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/"..game.PlaceId.."/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100")
    end)
    if not ok or not body then notify("Server Hop", "HTTP unavailable in this executor", 3); return end
    local ok2, data = pcall(function() return HS:JSONDecode(body) end)
    if not ok2 or not data or not data.data then notify("Server Hop", "Parse error", 3); return end
    for _, srv in ipairs(data.data) do
        if srv.playing and srv.maxPlayers and srv.playing < srv.maxPlayers and srv.id ~= game.JobId then
            TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, srv.id, LocalPlayer)
            return
        end
    end
    notify("Server Hop", "No open server found", 3)
end

local function larpCleanup()
    State.walkAnim = false; pcall(applyWalkAnim)
    setNoclip(false); setAntiFling(false); setAntiVoid(false)
    State.ghost, State.invisParts = false, false
    State.bodyScale, State.faceId = 1, ""
    pcall(applyBodyVisuals); pcall(applyBodyScale)
    if larpCharConn then larpCharConn:Disconnect(); larpCharConn = nil end
end

SilenceAPI._setNoclip       = function(v) setNoclip(v);       syncLarpCharConn() end
SilenceAPI._setAntiFling    = setAntiFling
SilenceAPI._setAntiVoid     = setAntiVoid
SilenceAPI._setGhost        = function(v) setGhost(v);        syncLarpCharConn() end
-- Each of these changes what everyone else should be drawing on you, so the
-- published style goes out again. _pushStyle debounces, so holding a slider
-- still results in one request.
SilenceAPI._setInvisParts   = function(v) setInvisParts(v);   syncLarpCharConn() end
SilenceAPI._setBodyScale    = function(v)
    setBodyScale(v); syncLarpCharConn()
    if SilenceAPI._pushStyle then SilenceAPI._pushStyle() end
end
SilenceAPI._setFace         = function(v) setFace(v);         syncLarpCharConn() end
SilenceAPI._setWalkAnim     = function(v) setWalkAnim(v);     syncLarpCharConn() end
SilenceAPI._setWalkAnimId   = setWalkAnimId
SilenceAPI._applyBodyVisuals= applyBodyVisuals
SilenceAPI._saveWaypoint    = saveWaypoint
SilenceAPI._gotoWaypoint    = gotoWaypoint
SilenceAPI._deleteWaypoint  = deleteWaypoint
SilenceAPI._serverHop       = serverHop
SilenceAPI._larpCleanup     = larpCleanup
end -- MOVEMENT / SAFETY / LARP BODY

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- WORLD INTERACTION / SESSION / LOGGING
----------------------------------------------------------------
do
local promptConn, clickConns, clickList = nil, {}, {}
local rejoinConns = {}
local itemEspObjs = {}
local chatConns = {}

SilenceAPI._chatLog   = {}
SilenceAPI._remoteLog = {}

local function pushLog(list, entry, cap)
    table.insert(list, entry)
    if #list > (cap or 200) then table.remove(list, 1) end
end

------------------------------------------------------ AUTO-INTERACT
-- ProximityPromptService.PromptShown only fires for prompts the client can
-- actually see, so there is no polling and no Workspace sweep here.
local function setAutoInteract(on)
    State.autoInteract = on
    if promptConn then promptConn:Disconnect(); promptConn = nil end
    if not on then return end
    local okSvc, PPS = pcall(function() return game:GetService("ProximityPromptService") end)
    if not okSvc or not PPS then
        State.autoInteract = false
        notify("Auto-Interact", "ProximityPromptService unavailable here", 3)
        return
    end
    promptConn = PPS.PromptShown:Connect(function(prompt)
        if not State.autoInteract then return end
        task.spawn(function()
            pcall(function()
                prompt:InputHoldBegin()
                if prompt.HoldDuration and prompt.HoldDuration > 0 then
                    task.wait(prompt.HoldDuration + 0.06)
                end
                prompt:InputHoldEnd()
            end)
        end)
    end)
end

-------------------------------------------------------- AUTO-CLICK
-- ClickDetectors have no "shown" signal, so the list is built once and kept
-- current incrementally instead of re-walking Workspace on every tick.
local function rebuildClickList()
    clickList = {}
    pcall(function()
        for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("ClickDetector") then table.insert(clickList, d) end
        end
    end)
end

local function setAutoClick(on)
    State.autoClick = on
    for _, c in ipairs(clickConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    clickConns = {}
    if not on then clickList = {}; return end
    if type(fireclickdetector) ~= "function" then
        State.autoClick = false
        notify("Auto-Click", "Your executor has no fireclickdetector", 4)
        return
    end
    rebuildClickList()
    table.insert(clickConns, Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
        if d:IsA("ClickDetector") then table.insert(clickList, d) end
    end))
    table.insert(clickConns, Workspace.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(d)
        if not d:IsA("ClickDetector") then return end
        for i, c in ipairs(clickList) do
            if c == d then table.remove(clickList, i) break end
        end
    end))
    task.spawn(function()
        while State.autoClick do
            local hrp = getHRP()
            if hrp then
                local range = tonumber(State.autoInteractRange) or 24
                for _, cd in ipairs(clickList) do
                    if not State.autoClick then break end
                    local part = cd.Parent
                    if part and part:IsA("BasePart")
                       and (part.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude <= range then
                        pcall(function() fireclickdetector(cd) end)
                    end
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
    end)
end

-------------------------------------------------------- AUTO-REJOIN
local function setAutoRejoin(on)
    State.autoRejoin = on
    for _, c in ipairs(rejoinConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    rejoinConns = {}
    if not on then return end

    local function rejoin(why)
        if not State.autoRejoin then return end
        notify("Auto-Rejoin", tostring(why or "disconnected") .. " -- rejoining...", 4)
        task.wait(1)
        pcall(function()
            if #Players:GetPlayers() <= 1 then
                TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, LocalPlayer)
            else
                TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, game.JobId, LocalPlayer)
            end
        end)
    end

    local okG, GuiService = pcall(function() return game:GetService("GuiService") end)
    if okG and GuiService then
        table.insert(rejoinConns, GuiService.ErrorMessageChanged:Connect(function()
            local msg
            pcall(function() msg = GuiService:GetErrorMessage() end)
            if msg and msg ~= "" then rejoin(msg) end
        end))
    end
    -- the kick dialog is a CoreGui prompt; catching it is more reliable than
    -- the error signal in a lot of games
    pcall(function()
        local prompt = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxPromptGui")
        local overlay = prompt and prompt:FindFirstChild("promptOverlay")
        if overlay then
            table.insert(rejoinConns, overlay.ChildAdded:Connect(function(c)
                if c.Name == "ErrorPrompt" then rejoin("kicked") end
            end))
        end
    end)
end

---------------------------------------------------------- ITEM ESP
local function clearItemEsp()
    for _, o in pairs(itemEspObjs) do pcall(function() o:Destroy() end) end
    itemEspObjs = {}
end

local function setItemEsp(on)
    State.itemEsp = on
    if not on then clearItemEsp(); return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while State.itemEsp do
            local seen = {}
            local hrp = getHRP()
            pcall(function()
                for _, t in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
                    if t:IsA("Tool") then
                        local handle = t:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                                    or t:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                        if handle then
                            seen[t] = true
                            local bb = itemEspObjs[t]
                            if not bb or not bb.Parent then
                                bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
                                bb.Name = "__SIL_ITEM"
                                bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
                                bb.LightInfluence = 0
                                bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 140, 0, 18)
                                bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 1.6, 0)
                                bb.Adornee = handle
                                bb.Parent = handle
                                local lb = Instance.new("TextLabel", bb)
                                lb.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                                lb.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                                lb.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                                lb.TextSize = 12
                                lb.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4
                                lb.TextColor3 = Theme.accent2
                                itemEspObjs[t] = bb
                            end
                            local lb = bb:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
                            if lb then
                                local d = hrp and math.floor((handle.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude) or 0
                                lb.Text = t.Name .. "  [" .. d .. "m]"
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            for t, o in pairs(itemEspObjs) do
                if not seen[t] then pcall(function() o:Destroy() end); itemEspObjs[t] = nil end
            end
            task.wait(0.6)
        end
        clearItemEsp()
    end)
end

--------------------------------------------------------- CHAT LOG
local function setChatLog(on)
    State.chatLog = on
    for _, c in ipairs(chatConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    chatConns = {}
    if not on then return end

    local function hook(p)
        table.insert(chatConns, p.Chatted:Connect(function(msg)
            if not State.chatLog then return end
            pushLog(SilenceAPI._chatLog,
                { t = os.date("%H:%M:%S"), who = SilenceAPI._safeName(p), msg = msg })
        end))
    end
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do hook(p) end
    table.insert(chatConns, Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(hook))

    -- modern games route through TextChatService instead of Player.Chatted
    pcall(function()
        local TCS = game:GetService("TextChatService")
        if TCS and TCS.MessageReceived then
            table.insert(chatConns, TCS.MessageReceived:Connect(function(m)
                if not State.chatLog then return end
                local who = "?"
                pcall(function()
                    local src = m.TextSource
                    local p = src and Players:GetPlayerByUserId(src.UserId)
                    who = p and SilenceAPI._safeName(p) or "system"
                end)
                pushLog(SilenceAPI._chatLog,
                    { t = os.date("%H:%M:%S"), who = who, msg = m.Text })
            end))
        end
    end)
end

-------------------------------------------------------- REMOTE SPY
-- The __namecall hook installed further up calls this on every remote call,
-- so the spy costs nothing at all while it is switched off.
function SilenceAPI._spyRecord(remote, method, args)
    if not State.remoteSpy then return end
    local okName, name = pcall(function() return remote:GetFullName() end)
    local parts = {}
    for i = 1, math.min(#args, 6) do
        local v = args[i]
        local tv = typeof(v)
        if tv == "Instance" then
            parts[#parts+1] = v.ClassName .. "(" .. v.Name .. ")"
        elseif tv == "table" then
            parts[#parts+1] = "table"
        else
            parts[#parts+1] = string.sub(tostring(v), 1, 40)
        end
    end
    if #args > 6 then parts[#parts+1] = "... +" .. (#args - 6) end
    pushLog(SilenceAPI._remoteLog, {
        t = os.date("%H:%M:%S"),
        method = tostring(method),
        name = okName and name or "?",
        args = table.concat(parts, ", "),
    }, 150)
end

local function worldCleanup()
    setAutoInteract(false); setAutoClick(false)
    setAutoRejoin(false); setItemEsp(false); setChatLog(false)
    State.remoteSpy = false
end

SilenceAPI._setAutoInteract = setAutoInteract
SilenceAPI._setAutoClick    = setAutoClick
SilenceAPI._setAutoRejoin   = setAutoRejoin
SilenceAPI._setItemEsp      = setItemEsp
SilenceAPI._setChatLog      = setChatLog
SilenceAPI._worldCleanup    = worldCleanup
end -- WORLD INTERACTION / SESSION / LOGGING


local origGravity=Workspace.Gravity
local function setSlowmo(factor)
    State.slowmo=factor
    Workspace.Gravity=origGravity*factor
    local hum=getHum(); if hum then hum.WalkSpeed=16*factor end
end

local aimbotConn
local currentTarget = nil  -- fuer Aimbot-Tracking

local function isTargetVisible(targetHead)
    if not targetHead or not targetHead.Parent then return false end
    local origin = Camera.CFrame.Position
    local diff = targetHead.Position - origin
    local dist = diff.Magnitude
    if dist < 0.1 then return true end
    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    local exclude = { LocalPlayer.Character }
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = exclude
    params.IgnoreWater = true
    local result = Workspace:Raycast(origin, diff.Unit * dist, params)
    if not result then return true end
    local char = targetHead.Parent
    if char and result.Instance:IsDescendantOf(char) then return true end
    return false
end

local function getClosestPlayer()
    local center=Camera.ViewportSize/2
    local best,bestDist=nil,math.huge
    -- Behaelte aktuelles Ziel, wenn noch im FOV
    if currentTarget and currentTarget.Parent then
        local pos, vis = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(currentTarget.Position)
        if vis then
            local d = (Vector2.new(pos.X,pos.Y)-center).Magnitude
            if d < State.aimbotFov then
                best = currentTarget
                bestDist = d
            end
        end
    end
    for _,p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p~=LocalPlayer and p.Character then
            local h=p.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
            local hum=p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if h and hum and hum.Health>0 then
                local isFriendly = State.aimbotCustomTeam[p.Name:lower()]
                    or (State.aimbotTeamcheck and p.Team and p.Team==LocalPlayer.Team)
                if not isFriendly then
                    local pos,vis=Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(h.Position)
                    if vis then
                        local d=(Vector2.new(pos.X,pos.Y)-center).Magnitude
                        if d<State.aimbotFov and d<bestDist then
                            if not State.aimbotVisibilityCheck or isTargetVisible(h) then
                                bestDist=d; best=h
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    currentTarget = best
    return best
end
local function setAimbot(on)
    State.aimbot=on
    if aimbotConn then aimbotConn:Disconnect() aimbotConn=nil end
    if not on then currentTarget=nil return end
    aimbotConn=RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2) then return end
        local h=getClosestPlayer()
        if not h then return end
        if (SilenceAPI._acStealth and SilenceAPI._acStealth()) then
            local desired = CFrame.new(Camera.CFrame.Position, h.Position)
            Camera.CFrame = Camera.CFrame:Lerp(desired, 0.25)
        else
            Camera.CFrame = CFrame.new(Camera.CFrame.Position, h.Position)
        end
    end)
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- SILENT AIM + MAGIC BULLETS (wie bisher, aber mit Tracking-Erweiterung)
----------------------------------------------------------------
print("[Silence] defining silent aim functions...")
local _silentAimHookInstalled = false
local _silentOldNamecall

local function getClosestPlayerNoFov()
    local best, bestDist = nil, math.huge
    for _,p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= LocalPlayer and p.Character then
            local h = p.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
            local hum = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if h and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                local isFriendly = State.aimbotCustomTeam[p.Name:lower()]
                    or (State.aimbotTeamcheck and p.Team and p.Team == LocalPlayer.Team)
                if not isFriendly then
                    local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if hrp then
                        local d = (hrp.Position - h.Position).Magnitude
                        if d < bestDist then bestDist = d; best = h end
                    else
                        return h
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return best
end

local function _silenceRewriteArgs(method, target, args)
    if method ~= "FireServer" and method ~= "InvokeServer" then return false end
    if not target or not target.Parent then return false end
    local replaced = false
    local targetCharacter = target.Parent
    local stealth = SilenceAPI._acStealth and SilenceAPI._acStealth()
    local function nudge(pos)
        if not stealth then return pos end
        return pos + Vector3.new(
            (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.6,
            (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.4,
            (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.6
        )
    end
    for i, v in ipairs(args) do
        local t = typeof(v)
        if t == "Vector3" then
            args[i] = nudge(target.Position); replaced = true
        elseif t == "CFrame" then
            args[i] = CFrame.new(v.Position, nudge(target.Position)); replaced = true
        elseif t == "Instance" then
            if v:IsA("Player") and v ~= LocalPlayer then
                local plr = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(targetCharacter)
                if plr then args[i] = plr; replaced = true end
            elseif v:IsA("Model") and v:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
                args[i] = targetCharacter; replaced = true
            elseif v:IsA("BasePart") then
                args[i] = target; replaced = true
            end
        end
    end
    return replaced
end

-- Refreshed on every execution. A __namecall hook installed by an EARLIER run
-- captured that run's State/functions, which cleanup() has since switched off -
-- so it reads them from here instead and keeps working after a reload. Without
-- this, every re-execute stacked another wrapper on top of the old one and the
-- overhead on every RemoteEvent call in the game grew without bound.
env.__SilenceHookCtx = {
    state        = State,
    rewrite      = _silenceRewriteArgs,
    closest      = getClosestPlayer,
    closestNoFov = getClosestPlayerNoFov,
    spy          = SilenceAPI._spyRecord,
}

local function installSilentAimHook()
    if _silentAimHookInstalled then return end
    -- a previous execution already owns the hook; it reads env.__SilenceHookCtx
    if env.__SilenceHookInstalled then
        _silentAimHookInstalled = true
        return
    end
    local hmm = rawget(getfenv(), "hookmetamethod")
    if type(hmm) == "function" then
        local ok, oldFn = pcall(hmm, game, "__namecall", function(self, ...)
            local ctx = env.__SilenceHookCtx
            local method = (getnamecallmethod and getnamecallmethod()) or ""
            if ctx and ctx.spy and ctx.state.remoteSpy
               and (method == "FireServer" or method == "InvokeServer") then
                pcall(ctx.spy, self, method, {...})
            end
            if ctx and (ctx.state.silentAim or ctx.state.magicBullets)
               and (method == "FireServer" or method == "InvokeServer") then
                local target = ctx.state.magicBullets and ctx.closestNoFov() or ctx.closest()
                if target then
                    local args = { ... }
                    if ctx.rewrite(method, target, args) then
                        return _silentOldNamecall(self, table.unpack(args))
                    end
                end
            end
            return _silentOldNamecall(self, ...)
        end)
        if ok and oldFn then
            _silentOldNamecall = oldFn
            _silentAimHookInstalled = true
            env.__SilenceHookInstalled = true
            print("[Silence] silent aim hook installed via hookmetamethod")
            return
        end
    end
    if not getrawmetatable then return end
    local mt
    local okMt = pcall(function() mt = getrawmetatable(game) end)
    if not okMt or not mt then return end
    pcall(function() setreadonly(mt, false) end)
    _silentOldNamecall = mt.__namecall
    mt.__namecall = function(self, ...)
        local ctx = env.__SilenceHookCtx
        local method = (getnamecallmethod and getnamecallmethod()) or ""
        if ctx and ctx.spy and ctx.state.remoteSpy
           and (method == "FireServer" or method == "InvokeServer") then
            pcall(ctx.spy, self, method, {...})
        end
        if ctx and (ctx.state.silentAim or ctx.state.magicBullets)
           and (method == "FireServer" or method == "InvokeServer") then
            local target = ctx.state.magicBullets and ctx.closestNoFov() or ctx.closest()
            if target then
                local args = { ... }
                if ctx.rewrite(method, target, args) then
                    return _silentOldNamecall(self, table.unpack(args))
                end
            end
        end
        return _silentOldNamecall(self, ...)
    end
    pcall(function() setreadonly(mt, true) end)
    _silentAimHookInstalled = true
    env.__SilenceHookInstalled = true
    print("[Silence] silent aim hook installed via getrawmetatable")
end

local function setSilentAim(on)
    State.silentAim = on
    if on then installSilentAimHook() end
end
local function setMagicBullets(on)
    State.magicBullets = on
    if on then installSilentAimHook() end
end
print("[Silence] silent aim functions defined")

local crashAttach
local function setCrashFx(on)
    State.crashFx=on
    if crashAttach then crashAttach:Destroy() crashAttach=nil end
    if not on then return end
    local hrp=getHRP(); if not hrp then return end
    local a=Instance.new("Attachment",hrp); a.Name="__SIL_FX"
    local fire=Instance.new("ParticleEmitter",a)
    fire.Texture="rbxassetid://243660364"; fire.Rate=200
    fire.Lifetime=NumberRange.new(0.6,1.2)
    fire.Speed=NumberRange.new(8,14); fire.SpreadAngle=Vector2.new(180,180)
    fire.Color=ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,Color3.fromRGB(255,80,0)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,Color3.fromRGB(170,90,255)),
    })
    fire.Size=NumberSequence.new(1.5,0); fire.LightEmission=1; fire.LightInfluence=0
    crashAttach=a
end

local godConn, godSpawnConn
local function applyGod(h)
    if not h then return end
    pcall(function()
        h.MaxHealth = 9e9
        h.Health = 9e9
        h.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
        h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
        h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
        h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false)
        h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics, false)
    end)
end
local function setGod(on)
    State.godmode = on
    if godConn      then godConn:Disconnect()      godConn = nil end
    if godSpawnConn then godSpawnConn:Disconnect() godSpawnConn = nil end
    if not on then
        local h = getHum()
        if h then
            pcall(function()
                h.MaxHealth = 100
                h.Health = 100
                h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, true)
                h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, true)
                h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, true)
                h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics, true)
            end)
        end
        return
    end
    applyGod(getHum())
    godConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local h = getHum()
        if h and h.Health < h.MaxHealth then h.Health = h.MaxHealth end
    end)
    godSpawnConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        task.wait(0.4)
        if not State.godmode then return end
        local h = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        applyGod(h)
    end)
end

track(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(i,gp)
    if gp then return end
    if State.clickTpOn and i.KeyCode==Enum.KeyCode.T then
        local hrp=getHRP()
        if hrp and Mouse.Hit then
            hrp.CFrame=CFrame.new(Mouse.Hit.Position+Vector3.new(0,3,0))
        end
    end
end))

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- EMOTE SYSTEM (anim + local sound, walk-while-emoting via Action4)
----------------------------------------------------------------
local Emotes = {}  -- only Custom Emote (paste your own IDs)

local activeTrack
local activeSound
local activeAnimConn
local emoteResumeConn
local emoteEnforceConn

local function stopEmote()
    if emoteResumeConn  then emoteResumeConn:Disconnect()  emoteResumeConn=nil  end
    if activeAnimConn   then activeAnimConn:Disconnect()   activeAnimConn=nil   end
    if emoteEnforceConn then emoteEnforceConn:Disconnect() emoteEnforceConn=nil end
    if activeTrack then pcall(function() activeTrack:Stop() end); activeTrack=nil end
    if activeSound then pcall(function() activeSound:Stop(); activeSound:Destroy() end); activeSound=nil end
    State.currentEmote=nil
    -- Re-enable Animate script
    local c=getChar(); if c then
        local an=c:FindFirstChild("Animate")
        if an then an.Disabled=false end
    end
end

local ContentProvider = game:GetService("ContentProvider")

local function tryLoadAnim(animator, animId)
    local a = Instance.new("Animation")
    a.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://"..tostring(animId)
    pcall(function() ContentProvider:PreloadAsync({a}) end)
    local ok, trk = pcall(function() return animator:LoadAnimation(a) end)
    if not ok or not trk then return nil end
    task.wait(0.1)
    if trk.Length and trk.Length > 0 then return trk end
    pcall(function() trk:Destroy() end)
    return nil
end

local function tryLoadSound(soundId, loop, pitch)
    local s = Instance.new("Sound")
    s.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..tostring(soundId)
    s.Volume = 1.5
    s.Looped = loop and true or false
    s.PlaybackSpeed = pitch or 1
    s.Parent = SoundService
    local elapsed = 0
    while elapsed < 3 and not s.IsLoaded do
        task.wait(0.1); elapsed = elapsed + 0.1
    end
    if s.IsLoaded and s.TimeLength > 0 then return s end
    pcall(function() s:Destroy() end)
    return nil
end

local function playEmote(animId, soundId, loop, pitch)
    stopEmote()
    local hum=getHum(); if not hum then notify("Emote","No humanoid") return end
    local char=getChar()

    print(string.format("[Silence] playEmote anim=%s sound=%s rig=%s",
        tostring(animId), tostring(soundId), tostring(hum.RigType)))

    local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
    if not animator then animator = Instance.new("Animator", hum) end

    -- Nur Animate deaktivieren
    if char then
        local an = char:FindFirstChild("Animate")
        if an and not an.Disabled then an.Disabled = true end
    end

    for _,t in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
        pcall(function() t:Stop(0) end)
    end

    local a = Instance.new("Animation")
    a.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://"..tostring(animId)
    local preloadOk, preloadErr = pcall(function() ContentProvider:PreloadAsync({a}) end)
    if not preloadOk then
        warn("[Silence] PreloadAsync error for "..animId..": "..tostring(preloadErr))
    end

    local trk
    local ok1, err1 = pcall(function() trk = animator:LoadAnimation(a) end)
    if not ok1 or not trk then
        local ok2, err2 = pcall(function() trk = hum:LoadAnimation(a) end)
        if not ok2 or not trk then
            notify("Emote FAIL","Both LoadAnimation methods failed.\nAnim: "..tostring(err1).."\nHum: "..tostring(err2),6)
            warn("[Silence] Humanoid:LoadAnimation also failed: "..tostring(err2))
            if char then local an=char:FindFirstChild("Animate"); if an then an.Disabled=false end end
            return
        end
        print("[Silence] OK loaded via Humanoid:LoadAnimation (fallback)")
    else
        print("[Silence] OK loaded via Animator:LoadAnimation")
    end

    local waited = 0
    while waited < 2 and (trk.Length == nil or trk.Length == 0) do
        task.wait(0.1); waited = waited + 0.1
    end
    if trk.Length == 0 then
        notify("Emote FAIL", "ID "..animId.." has Length=0 - that ID is NOT an animation",6)
        warn("[Silence] Animation "..animId.." Length=0. Wrong asset type or moderated.")
        pcall(function() trk:Destroy() end)
        if char then local an=char:FindFirstChild("Animate"); if an then an.Disabled=false end end
        return
    end

    trk.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
    trk.Looped   = true
    trk:Play(0.1)
    activeTrack = trk
    State.currentEmote = animId
    print(string.format("[Silence] OK anim playing  Length=%.2fs  Looped=%s",
        trk.Length, tostring(trk.Looped)))
    if trk.Length < 0.5 then
        notify("Emote WARN", "Length="..string.format("%.2f",trk.Length).."s - animation has too few keyframes.", 6)
        warn("[Silence] WARNING: animation length is suspiciously short ("..trk.Length.."s)")
    end

    if soundId and soundId > 0 then
        local s = Instance.new("Sound")
        s.Name = "__SIL_EmoteSound"
        s.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..tostring(soundId)
        s.Volume = 1.5
        s.Looped = loop and true or false
        s.PlaybackSpeed = pitch or 1
        s.Parent = SoundService
        s:Play()
        activeSound = s
        task.spawn(function()
            local elapsed = 0
            while elapsed < 3 and not s.IsLoaded do
                task.wait(0.1); elapsed = elapsed + 0.1
            end
            if not s.IsLoaded or s.TimeLength == 0 then
                warn("[Silence] Sound "..soundId.." won't load (moderated / bad ID)")
                notify("Sound FAIL","Sound "..soundId.." won't load",4)
            else
                print(string.format("[Silence] OK sound playing  len=%.2fs", s.TimeLength))
            end
        end)
    end

    -- Enforcer: Nur Animate deaktiviert halten, keine anderen Skripte
    local trackAnimId = animId
    emoteEnforceConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if State.currentEmote ~= trackAnimId then return end
        local c2 = getChar(); if not c2 then return end
        local an = c2:FindFirstChild("Animate")
        if an and not an.Disabled then an.Disabled = true end

        local hum2 = c2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"); if not hum2 then return end
        local anim2 = hum2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator"); if not anim2 then return end

        for _,t in ipairs(anim2:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
            if t ~= activeTrack then
                pcall(function() t:Stop(0) end)
            end
        end

        if not activeTrack or not activeTrack.Parent then
            local newAnim = Instance.new("Animation")
            newAnim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://"..tostring(trackAnimId)
            local rOk, newTrk = pcall(function() return anim2:LoadAnimation(newAnim) end)
            if rOk and newTrk then
                newTrk.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
                newTrk.Looped = true
                newTrk:Play(0)
                activeTrack = newTrk
            end
        elseif not activeTrack.IsPlaying then
            pcall(function() activeTrack:Play(0) end)
        elseif activeTrack.Length > 0 and activeTrack.TimePosition >= activeTrack.Length - 0.05 then
            pcall(function() activeTrack.TimePosition = 0 end)
        end
    end)

    activeAnimConn = trk.Stopped:Connect(function()
        if State.currentEmote == animId and activeTrack then
            pcall(function() activeTrack:Play(0) end)
        end
    end)

    emoteResumeConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        task.wait(0.6)
        if State.currentEmote == animId then
            playEmote(animId, soundId, loop, pitch)
        end
    end)
end

-- Press E while emoting => stop (nutzt custom Hotkey)
track(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(i,gp)
    if gp then return end
    if i.KeyCode == State.hotkeys.stopEmote and State.currentEmote then
        stopEmote()
    end
end))

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- SKINS - copy target's accessories, clothes, body colors, face
-- (client-side, FE-safe; doesn't change your rig)
----------------------------------------------------------------
local originalSnapshot  = nil
local currentSkinPlayer = nil

local SKIN_CLASSES = {
    Accessory=true, Shirt=true, Pants=true,
    ShirtGraphic=true, CharacterMesh=true, BodyColors=true, Hat=true,
}
local R6_PARTS  = { "Head","Torso","Left Arm","Right Arm","Left Leg","Right Leg" }
local R15_PARTS = {
    "Head","UpperTorso","LowerTorso",
    "LeftUpperArm","LeftLowerArm","LeftHand",
    "RightUpperArm","RightLowerArm","RightHand",
    "LeftUpperLeg","LeftLowerLeg","LeftFoot",
    "RightUpperLeg","RightLowerLeg","RightFoot",
}

local SCALE_NAMES = { "BodyDepthScale","BodyHeightScale","BodyWidthScale","HeadScale" }

-- anything the panel itself mounted (accessory slots, Korblox mesh) is named
-- __SIL_*. It must never be captured into a snapshot nor stripped as if it
-- were part of somebody's outfit.
local function isPanelOwned(inst)
    return type(inst.Name) == "string" and inst.Name:sub(1, 6) == "__SIL_"
end

local function snapshotChar(char)
    local snap = { items={}, headDecals={}, headMeshes={}, headSize=nil,
                   bodyColors={}, scales={}, rig=nil }
    local hum0 = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum0 then snap.rig = hum0.RigType end
    for _, it in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if SKIN_CLASSES[it.ClassName] and not isPanelOwned(it) then
            local ok, c = pcall(function() return it:Clone() end)
            if ok and c then table.insert(snap.items, c) end
        end
    end
    local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if head then
        for _, d in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
            if d:IsA("Decal") then
                local ok, c = pcall(function() return d:Clone() end)
                if ok and c then table.insert(snap.headDecals, c) end
            elseif d:IsA("DataModelMesh") then
                local ok, c = pcall(function() return d:Clone() end)
                if ok and c then table.insert(snap.headMeshes, c) end
            end
        end
        snap.headSize = head.Size
    end
    for _, name in ipairs(R6_PARTS) do
        local p = char:FindFirstChild(name)
        if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then snap.bodyColors[name] = p.Color end
    end
    for _, name in ipairs(R15_PARTS) do
        local p = char:FindFirstChild(name)
        if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then snap.bodyColors[name] = p.Color end
    end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        for _, sn in ipairs(SCALE_NAMES) do
            local s = hum:FindFirstChild(sn)
            if s then snap.scales[sn] = s.Value end
        end
    end
    return snap
end

-- stripHeadMesh is only true when both rigs match. Removing your own head
-- mesh while the source has none to give back is what made the head vanish.
local function stripChar(char, stripHeadMesh)
    for _, it in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if SKIN_CLASSES[it.ClassName] and not isPanelOwned(it) then
            pcall(function() it:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
    local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if head then
        for _, d in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
            local drop = d:IsA("Decal") or (stripHeadMesh and d:IsA("DataModelMesh"))
            if drop and not isPanelOwned(d) then
                pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
    end
end

local function paintBody(char, colorMap)
    for name, c in pairs(colorMap) do
        local p = char:FindFirstChild(name)
        if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then pcall(function() p.Color = c end) end
    end
end

local function applySkinSnapshot(char, snap)
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local myRig = hum and hum.RigType
    -- R6 and R15 are different rigs: an R6 head wears a SpecialMesh child, an
    -- R15 head is a MeshPart with none, and CharacterMesh / body scales only
    -- mean something on their own rig. Copying those across is what broke the
    -- head. Clothes, face and body colours transfer either way.
    local sameRig = (snap.rig == nil) or (myRig == nil) or (snap.rig == myRig)

    stripChar(char, sameRig)
    for _, it in ipairs(snap.items) do
        if sameRig or it.ClassName ~= "CharacterMesh" then
            local clone = it:Clone()
            if clone:IsA("Accessory") and hum then
                local ok = pcall(function() hum:AddAccessory(clone) end)
                if not ok then pcall(function() clone.Parent = char end) end
            else
                pcall(function() clone.Parent = char end)
            end
        end
    end
    local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if head then
        if sameRig then
            for _, m in ipairs(snap.headMeshes) do
                local c = m:Clone(); pcall(function() c.Parent = head end)
            end
            if snap.headSize then pcall(function() head.Size = snap.headSize end) end
        end
        -- a face is just a texture, it carries over between rigs
        for _, d in ipairs(snap.headDecals) do
            local c = d:Clone(); pcall(function() c.Parent = head end)
        end
    end
    paintBody(char, snap.bodyColors)
    if hum and sameRig then
        for sn, v in pairs(snap.scales) do
            local s = hum:FindFirstChild(sn)
            if s then pcall(function() s.Value = v end) end
        end
    end
    if not sameRig then
        notify("Skin", "Different rig (R6 vs R15) - clothes, face and colours copied, body meshes skipped.", 5)
    end
    -- the strip wiped the head; put back anything the panel owns
    if SilenceAPI._remountAfterSkin then pcall(SilenceAPI._remountAfterSkin) end
end

local function copyPlayerSkin(target)
    if not target then notify("Skin","No target",3) return end
    local myChar = getChar()
    local tgtChar = target.Character
    if not myChar then notify("Skin","No character",3) return end
    if not tgtChar then notify("Skin",target.Name.." not loaded yet",3) return end

    if not originalSnapshot then
        originalSnapshot = snapshotChar(myChar)
        print("[Silence] Saved original snapshot ("..#originalSnapshot.items.." items, "
            ..#originalSnapshot.headMeshes.." head meshes)")
    end

    local tgtSnap = snapshotChar(tgtChar)
    applySkinSnapshot(myChar, tgtSnap)
    currentSkinPlayer = target
    notify("Skin","Wearing "..target.Name.."'s outfit",3)
    print("[Silence] Skin copied from "..target.Name
        .." ("..#tgtSnap.items.." items, "..#tgtSnap.headMeshes.." head meshes)")
end

local function resetSkin()
    local myChar = getChar()
    if not myChar then return end
    if not originalSnapshot then notify("Skin","No original saved",3) return end
    applySkinSnapshot(myChar, originalSnapshot)
    currentSkinPlayer = nil
    notify("Skin","Reset to original",3)
end

local function saveSkinGlobally(target)
    if not target then notify("Skin","No target",3) return end
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        local desc = Players:GetHumanoidDescriptionFromUserId(target.UserId)
        if not desc then error("could not fetch description") end
        local rigType = Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15
        if target.Character then
            local hum = target.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum and hum.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
                rigType = Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6
            end
        end
        AvatarEditorService:PromptSaveAvatar(desc, rigType)
    end)
    if ok then
        notify("Skin","Save prompt opened - click Save, then equip in Roblox Avatar Editor + rejoin", 8)
    else
        notify("Skin","Save failed: "..tostring(err), 5)
    end
end

track(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p)
    if currentSkinPlayer == p then
        notify("Skin",p.Name.." left - restoring original",3)
        resetSkin()
    end
end))
track(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    task.wait(0.7)
    if currentSkinPlayer and currentSkinPlayer.Character then
        copyPlayerSkin(currentSkinPlayer)
    end
end))

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- TROLLING - target abuse, sit-on-head, orbit, follow, stick, dash
----------------------------------------------------------------
local trollMode    = nil
local trollTarget  = nil
local trollConn
local headstandConn
local spinConn

local function stopTrolling()
    if trollConn then trollConn:Disconnect() trollConn=nil end
    trollMode=nil; trollTarget=nil
    local h=getHum()
    if h then
        h.Sit=false
        h.PlatformStand=false
    end
end

local function startTrolling(mode, target)
    stopTrolling()
    if not target then notify("Trolling","No target set",3) return end
    trollMode   = mode
    trollTarget = target
    local angle = 0
    notify("Trolling", mode.." -> "..target.Name, 3)

    local hum = getHum()
    if hum and (mode == "siton" or mode == "stick" or mode == "orbit") then
        hum.PlatformStand = true
    end

    trollConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        local hrp=getHRP()
        if not hrp or not trollTarget or not trollTarget.Character then return end
        local tHrp  = trollTarget.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local tHead = trollTarget.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if not tHrp then return end

        local tVel
        local okV = pcall(function() tVel = tHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity end)
        if not okV or not tVel then tVel = tHrp.Velocity end
        local lead = tVel * dt
        local _, ry, _ = tHrp.CFrame:ToOrientation()

        if mode == "siton" then
            if tHead then
                local pos = tHead.Position + lead + Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)
                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos) * CFrame.Angles(0, ry, 0)
                pcall(function() hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = tVel end)
                if hum and not hum.Sit then hum.Sit = true end
            end
        elseif mode == "orbit" then
            angle = angle + dt * 3
            local r = 6
            local center = tHrp.Position + lead
            local pos = center + Vector3.new(math.cos(angle)*r, 2, math.sin(angle)*r)
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos, center)
            pcall(function() hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = tVel end)
        elseif mode == "follow" then
            local h=getHum()
            if h then
                local dist = (tHrp.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                if dist > 4 then h:MoveTo(tHrp.Position + lead) end
            end
        elseif mode == "stick" then
            hrp.CFrame = (tHrp.CFrame + lead) * CFrame.new(3, 0, 0)
            pcall(function() hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = tVel end)
        elseif mode == "spam" then
            -- sanfter Wackel
            local offset = Vector3.new(
                (math.random()-0.5)*0.5,
                (math.random()-0.5)*0.3,
                (math.random()-0.5)*0.5
            )
            hrp.CFrame = (tHrp.CFrame + lead) * CFrame.new(offset)
        end
    end)
end

local function setHeadstand(on)
    if headstandConn then headstandConn:Disconnect() headstandConn=nil end
    if not on then return end
    headstandConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local hrp=getHRP(); if not hrp then return end
        local _, y, _ = hrp.CFrame:ToOrientation()
        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position) * CFrame.fromOrientation(math.rad(180), y, 0)
    end)
end

local function setSpin(on)
    if spinConn then spinConn:Disconnect() spinConn=nil end
    if not on then return end
    local angle = 0
    spinConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        local hrp=getHRP(); if not hrp then return end
        angle = angle + dt * 15
        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position) * CFrame.Angles(0, angle, 0)
    end)
end

local function dashAt(player)
    if not player or not player.Character then return end
    local hrp = getHRP()
    local tHrp = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp or not tHrp then return end
    for i = 1, 5 do
        local toTarget = tHrp.Position - hrp.Position
        local dir = toTarget.Magnitude > 0.1 and toTarget.Unit or Vector3.new(1,0,0)
        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(tHrp.Position - dir*2 + Vector3.new(0,1,0))
        task.wait(0.05)
    end
end

-- COMMANDS

local function setWS(v) local h=getHum() if h then h.WalkSpeed=v end end
local function setJP(v)
    local h=getHum(); if not h then return end
    if h.UseJumpPower~=nil then h.UseJumpPower=true end
    if h.JumpPower then h.JumpPower=v end
    h.JumpHeight=v/50*7.2
end
local Commands={}

Commands.jump=Commands.jp
Commands.infjump=function() State.infJump = not State.infJump end
Commands.esp=function() setEsp(not State.espOn) end
Commands.espoff=function() setEsp(false) setChams(false) setTracers(false) clearAllEsp() end
Commands.chams=function() setChams(not State.chamsOn) end
Commands.tracers=function() setTracers(not State.tracersOn) end
Commands.fullbright=function() setFullbright(true) end
Commands.normallight=function() setFullbright(false) end
Commands.disco=function() setDisco(not State.disco) end
Commands.freecam=function() setFreecam(not State.freecam) end
Commands.fc=Commands.freecam
Commands.fov=function(a) Camera.FieldOfView=tonumber(a[1]) or 70 end
Commands.aimbot=function() setAimbot(not State.aimbot); notify("Aimbot",State.aimbot and "ON (hold RMB)" or "OFF") end
Commands.trail=function() setTrail(not State.trail) end
Commands.bighead=function(a) setBigHead(tonumber(a[1]) or 4) end
Commands.smallhead=function() setBigHead(0.4) end

Commands.antifling=function() SilenceAPI._setAntiFling(not State.antiFling) end
Commands.antivoid=function() SilenceAPI._setAntiVoid(not State.antiVoid) end
Commands.hop=function() SilenceAPI._serverHop() end
Commands.serverhop=Commands.hop
-- larp / avatar
Commands.ghost=function() SilenceAPI._setGhost(not State.ghost) end
Commands.invis=function() SilenceAPI._setInvisParts(not State.invisParts) end
Commands.headless=function() SilenceAPI._setHeadless(not State.headless) end
Commands.acc=function() SilenceAPI._setAccessories(not State.accessoriesOn) end
Commands.scale=function(a) SilenceAPI._setBodyScale(tonumber(a[1]) or 1) end
-- ;slot 2        toggles slot 2
-- ;slotid 2 1234 sets its asset id
Commands.slot=function(a)
    local i = tonumber(a[1])
    if not i or not State.accSlots[i] then
        notify("Slots", "Usage: " .. State.prefix .. "slot 1-" .. #State.accSlots, 4)
        return
    end
    SilenceAPI._setSlot(i, not State.accSlots[i].on)
    if SilenceAPI._slotUI and SilenceAPI._slotUI[i] then pcall(SilenceAPI._slotUI[i]) end
    notify(State.accSlots[i].name, State.accSlots[i].on and "ON" or "OFF", 2)
end
Commands.slotid=function(a)
    local i = tonumber(a[1])
    if not i or not State.accSlots[i] then return end
    SilenceAPI._setSlotId(i, a[2] or "")
    if SilenceAPI._slotUI and SilenceAPI._slotUI[i] then pcall(SilenceAPI._slotUI[i]) end
    notify(State.accSlots[i].name, "id = " .. State.accSlots[i].id, 2)
end
-- ;slothide 4   flips whether that slot blanks the limb underneath
Commands.slothide=function(a)
    local i = tonumber(a[1])
    if not i or not State.accSlots[i] then return end
    SilenceAPI._setSlotHide(i, not State.accSlots[i].hide)
    if SilenceAPI._slotUI and SilenceAPI._slotUI[i] then pcall(SilenceAPI._slotUI[i]) end
    notify(State.accSlots[i].name,
           "hide limb: " .. (State.accSlots[i].hide and "ON" or "OFF"), 2)
end
Commands.slots=function()
    local out = {}
    for i, sl in ipairs(State.accSlots) do
        out[#out+1] = i .. ":" .. sl.name .. "(" .. (sl.id ~= "" and sl.id or "-") ..
                      "," .. sl.part .. (sl.hide and ",hide" or "") ..
                      "," .. (sl.on and "on" or "off") .. ")"
    end
    notify("Slots", table.concat(out, "  "), 6)
end

Commands.walk=function() SilenceAPI._setWalkAnim(not State.walkAnim) end

    SilenceAPI._setWalkAnimId(a[1] or "")
    notify("Walk Animation", "id = " .. State.walkAnimId, 2)
end
