Commands.wps=function()
    if #State.waypoints == 0 then notify("Waypoints", "none saved", 2) return end
    local names = {}
    for i, w in ipairs(State.waypoints) do names[#names+1] = i .. ":" .. w.name end
    notify("Waypoints", table.concat(names, "  "), 5)
end
-- automation / logging
Commands.autointeract=function() SilenceAPI._setAutoInteract(not State.autoInteract) end
Commands.ai=Commands.autointeract
Commands.autoclick=function() SilenceAPI._setAutoClick(not State.autoClick) end
Commands.autorejoin=function() SilenceAPI._setAutoRejoin(not State.autoRejoin) end
Commands.itemesp=function() SilenceAPI._setItemEsp(not State.itemEsp) end
Commands.streamer=function()
    State.streamerMode = not State.streamerMode
    notify("Streamer Mode", State.streamerMode and "ON" or "OFF", 2)
end
Commands.chatlog=function() SilenceAPI._setChatLog(not State.chatLog) end
Commands.uiscale=function(a) SilenceAPI._setUiScale(tonumber(a[1]) or 1) end
-- ;uicolor ff0000   (accepts with or without the leading #)
Commands.uicolor=function(a)
    local hex = tostring(a[1] or ""):gsub("#", "")
    if #hex ~= 6 or not tonumber(hex, 16) then
        notify("UI Colors", "Usage: " .. State.prefix .. "uicolor ff00ff", 4)
        return
    end
    State.colors.uiAccent = Color3.fromRGB(
        tonumber(hex:sub(1,2), 16),
        tonumber(hex:sub(3,4), 16),
        tonumber(hex:sub(5,6), 16))
    SilenceAPI._applyUiColors()
    notify("UI Colors", "Accent set to #" .. hex, 3)
end
-- config
Commands.save=function() if SilenceAPI._saveConfig then SilenceAPI._saveConfig() end end
Commands.load=function() if SilenceAPI._loadConfig then SilenceAPI._loadConfig(false) end end
Commands.goto=function(a)
    local p=findPlayer(a[1])
    if p and p.Character then
        local hrp=getHRP()
        local tgt=p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hrp and tgt then hrp.CFrame=tgt.CFrame+Vector3.new(0,3,0) end
    end
end
Commands.tppos=function(a)
    local x,y,z=tonumber(a[1]),tonumber(a[2]),tonumber(a[3])
    local hrp=getHRP(); if hrp and x and y and z then hrp.CFrame=CFrame.new(x,y,z) end
end
Commands.spectate=function(a) local p=findPlayer(a[1]); if p then setSpectate(p) end end
Commands.unspectate=function() setSpectate(nil) end
Commands.antiafk=function() setAntiAfk(not State.antiAfk) end
Commands.hitbox=function(a) local n=tonumber(a[1]); if n and n>=0 then setHitbox(n) else setHitbox(nil) end end
Commands.clickteleport=function() State.clickTpOn=not State.clickTpOn; notify("ClickTP",State.clickTpOn and "Press T" or "Off") end
Commands.sittoggle=function() local h=getHum() if h then h.Sit=not h.Sit end end
Commands.reset=function() local h=getHum() if h then h.Health=0 end end
Commands.rejoin=function() TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, LocalPlayer) end
Commands.prefix=function(a) if a[1] then State.prefix=a[1]:sub(1,1) end end
Commands.stopemote=function() stopEmote() end
Commands.unload=function()
    notify("Silence","Unloading...",1)
    pcall(function() SilenceAPI._container.Visible = false end)
    task.wait(0.05)
    local ok, err = pcall(SilenceAPI.cleanup)
    if not ok then warn("[Silence] cleanup error: "..tostring(err)) end
    env.__SilenceAdminPanel = nil
end
for _,e in ipairs(Emotes) do
    Commands[e[1]:lower():gsub("%s+","")] = function() playEmote(e[2], e[3], e[4], e[5]) end
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- ANTI-CHEAT GATE
----------------------------------------------------------------
local function acAllowed() return (not State.acBlocked) or State.acRiskyOverride end
local function acStealth() return State.acBlocked and State.acRiskyOverride end
SilenceAPI._acAllowed = acAllowed
SilenceAPI._acStealth = acStealth

local function gateSetter(featureName, fn)
    return function(...)
        if not acAllowed() then
            notify("BLOCKED", featureName .. " - AC detected. Toggle RISKY in AntiCheat tab.", 3)
            return
        end
        return fn(...)
    end
end

startFly        = gateSetter("Fly",            startFly)
setEsp          = gateSetter("ESP",            setEsp)
setChams        = gateSetter("Chams",          setChams)
setTracers      = gateSetter("Tracers",        setTracers)
setAimbot       = gateSetter("Aimbot",         setAimbot)
setSilentAim    = gateSetter("Silent Aim",     setSilentAim)
setMagicBullets = gateSetter("Magic Bullets",  setMagicBullets)
setGod          = gateSetter("God Mode",       setGod)
setBigHead      = gateSetter("Big Head",       setBigHead)
setHitbox       = gateSetter("Hitbox",         setHitbox)
setCrashFx      = gateSetter("Crash FX",       setCrashFx)
setFreecam      = gateSetter("Freecam",        setFreecam)
setTrail        = gateSetter("Trail",          setTrail)
setSpectate     = gateSetter("Spectate",       setSpectate)
setHeadstand    = gateSetter("Headstand",      setHeadstand)
setSpin         = gateSetter("Spin",           setSpin)

local function acForceOffAll()
    local prev = State.acRiskyOverride
    State.acRiskyOverride = true
    pcall(stopFly)
    pcall(function() State.silentAim = false; State.magicBullets = false end)
    pcall(setEsp, false); pcall(setChams, false); pcall(setTracers, false)
    pcall(setAimbot, false); pcall(setGod, false)
    pcall(setBigHead, nil)
    pcall(setHitbox, nil); pcall(setCrashFx, false)
    pcall(setFreecam, false); pcall(setTrail, false)
    pcall(setSpectate, nil)
    pcall(setHeadstand, false); pcall(setSpin, false)
    State.acRiskyOverride = prev
end
SilenceAPI._acForceOffAll = acForceOffAll

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- GUI HELPERS
----------------------------------------------------------------
local function corner(p,r) local c=Instance.new("UICorner",p); c.CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,r or 6); return c end
local function stroke(p,col,thick,trans,token)
    local s=Instance.new("UIStroke",p)
    s.Color=col or Theme.bg3; s.Thickness=thick or 1
    s.Transparency=trans or 0
    -- Most strokes are drawn in the accent, so they join the recolour
    -- registry. White is accent AND text by default, which makes a pure
    -- colour comparison ambiguous - pass `token` to say which role it is.
    if token then SilenceAPI._theme(s, "Color", token)
    elseif col and col == Theme.accent then SilenceAPI._theme(s, "Color", "accent") end
    return s
end
local function padding(p,t,b,l,r)
    local u=Instance.new("UIPadding",p)
    u.PaddingTop=UDim.new(0,t or 0); u.PaddingBottom=UDim.new(0,b or 0)
    u.PaddingLeft=UDim.new(0,l or 0); u.PaddingRight=UDim.new(0,r or 0)
    return u
end
local function gradient(p,c1,c2,rotation)
    local g=Instance.new("UIGradient",p)
    g.Color=ColorSequence.new(c1,c2); g.Rotation=rotation or 0
    return g
end

-- Every slider, colour strip and draggable header used to open its own
-- UserInputService.InputChanged connection, so a panel with ~40 sliders held
-- ~40 live listeners that nothing ever disconnected. They all share this one.
SilenceAPI._dragHandlers = {}
function SilenceAPI._onDrag(fn) table.insert(SilenceAPI._dragHandlers, fn) end
track(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
       and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
    for i = #SilenceAPI._dragHandlers, 1, -1 do
        pcall(SilenceAPI._dragHandlers[i], input)
    end
end))

local glowGradients = {}
local function addAnimatedGlow(element, opts)
    opts = opts or {}
    local thickness   = opts.thickness   or 2
    local baseTrans   = opts.transparency or 0.55
    local windowSize  = opts.window      or 0.18
    local color       = opts.color       or Theme.accent

    local s = Instance.new("UIStroke", element)
    s.Color        = color
    s.Thickness    = thickness
    s.Transparency = baseTrans
    s.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border

    local g = Instance.new("UIGradient", s)
    g.Color = ColorSequence.new(color)
    g.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,                       1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(math.max(0, 0.5 - windowSize), 1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,                     0),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(math.min(1, 0.5 + windowSize), 1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,                       1),
    })
    g.Rotation = math.random(0, 360)
    table.insert(glowGradients, g)
    if color == Theme.accent then
        SilenceAPI._theme(s, "Color", "accent")
        table.insert(SilenceAPI._themedGrads, g)
    end
    return s
end

do
    local rot = 0
    track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        rot = (rot + dt * 80) % 360
        -- walk backwards so gradients whose owner is gone (destroyed overhead
        -- tags, closed popups) leave the list; it used to only ever grow
        for i = #glowGradients, 1, -1 do
            local g = glowGradients[i]
            if g and g.Parent then g.Rotation = rot
            else table.remove(glowGradients, i) end
        end
    end))
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- GLASS
-- The surface language the floating bar established: a translucent sheet,
-- an accent edge, a slow travelling glow, and a sheen that runs off the top.
-- Every window goes through here so they cannot drift apart.
--
-- The sheen is added as the FIRST child on purpose. ZIndexBehavior is
-- Sibling, so at equal ZIndex whatever is parented afterwards draws over it,
-- which keeps it behind the window's own contents without having to give
-- every one of them a raised layer.
----------------------------------------------------------------
function SilenceAPI._glass(frame, opts)
    opts = opts or {}
    local radius = opts.radius or 18
    frame.BackgroundTransparency = opts.transparency or 0.25
    corner(frame, radius)
    local edge = stroke(frame, Theme.accent, 1, opts.edge or 0.4)
    if opts.glow ~= false then
        addAnimatedGlow(frame, {
            thickness    = opts.glowThickness or 1.5,
            transparency = 0.42,
            window       = 0.16,
        })
    end
    local sheen = Instance.new("Frame", frame)
    sheen.Name = "__SIL_Sheen"
    sheen.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    sheen.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    sheen.BorderSizePixel = 0
    sheen.ZIndex = frame.ZIndex
    corner(sheen, radius)
    local g = Instance.new("UIGradient", sheen)
    g.Rotation = 90
    g.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.88),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
    })
    return frame, edge
end

-- Any window registered here turns the background blur on while it is open,
-- so a popup on its own gets the same treatment the main shell does.
SilenceAPI._blurSurfaces = {}
function SilenceAPI._blurWatch(inst)
    table.insert(SilenceAPI._blurSurfaces, inst)
    track(inst:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
        if SilenceAPI._setMenuBlur then SilenceAPI._setMenuBlur() end
    end))
    return inst
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- SETTINGS GEAR GLYPH
-- Drawn from primitives: Gotham has no gear character (it renders as an
-- empty box, which is what the old "SET" label was standing in for) and an
-- rbxasset texture path that moves would leave the button blank.
-- Eight teeth sit on a circle, each rotated to point straight outward, and a
-- hollow hub covers their inner ends so nothing pokes into the middle.
----------------------------------------------------------------
SilenceAPI._gearHosts = {}   -- buttons wearing a gear, for a live re-check
function SilenceAPI._gearIcon(b)
    -- ZIndexBehavior is Sibling, so the glyph has to outrank whatever button
    -- holds it; the header button sits at 7 and the HUD button at 31
    local z = (b.ZIndex or 1) + 1
    table.insert(SilenceAPI._gearHosts, b)

    -- a real PNG wins if the loader found one
    if SETTINGS_IMG then
        local img = Instance.new("ImageLabel", b)
        img.Name = "GearImg"
        img.Size = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 20)
        img.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -10, 0.5, -10)
        img.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        img.Image = SETTINGS_IMG
        img.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
        img.ZIndex = z
        return
    end

    local ico = Instance.new("Frame", b)
    ico.Name = "GearVec"
    ico.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 22)
    ico.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -11, 0.5, -11)
    ico.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    ico.BorderSizePixel = 0
    ico.ZIndex = z

    -- What makes this read as a gear rather than a flower: the hub is a
    -- THICK ring around a LARGE hole (the hole is over half the width), and
    -- the teeth are wide, rounded, and clearly clear of the rim. The teeth
    -- sit on a circle of radius 10 about the centre (11,11), each rotated to
    -- its own angle so every one points straight outward.
    local function tooth(cx, cy, rot)
        local f = Instance.new("Frame", ico)
        f.Size = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 5)
        f.Position = UDim2.new(0, cx - 3, 0, cy - 2.5)
        f.Rotation = rot
        f.BorderSizePixel = 0
        f.ZIndex = z
        corner(f, 2)
        SilenceAPI._theme(f, "BackgroundColor3", "text")
    end
    tooth(11,    1,     0);   tooth(18.07,  3.93,  45)
    tooth(21,   11,    90);   tooth(18.07, 18.07, 135)
    tooth(11,   21,     0);   tooth(3.93,  18.07,  45)
    tooth(1,    11,    90);   tooth(3.93,   3.93, 135)

    -- rim: 15px circle with a 3.5px stroke covers radius 5.75 to 9.25, so the
    -- teeth (inner edge 7.5) are buried in it and only their tips show
    local hub = Instance.new("Frame", ico)
    hub.Size = UDim2.new(0, 15, 0, 15)
    hub.Position = UDim2.new(0, 3.5, 0, 3.5)
    hub.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    hub.BorderSizePixel = 0
    hub.ZIndex = z
    local hc = Instance.new("UICorner", hub); hc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    local hs = Instance.new("UIStroke", hub); hs.Thickness = 3.5
    SilenceAPI._theme(hs, "Color", "text")
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- COLOR APPLY (live update existing objects)
----------------------------------------------------------------
local function applyColors()
    for plr,set in pairs(State.espObjects) do
        if set.hl then
            local oc, fc = espColorFor(plr)
            set.hl.OutlineColor = oc
            set.hl.FillColor    = fc
        end
        if set.lbl then
            set.lbl.TextColor3 = State.colors.nametag
        end
    end
    for _,f in pairs(tracerFrames) do
        if f and f.Parent then f.BackgroundColor3 = State.colors.tracers end
    end
    if trailObj then
        local tc = State.colors.trail
        trailObj.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, tc),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.new(
                math.min(1,tc.R+0.2), math.min(1,tc.G+0.2), math.min(1,tc.B+0.2))),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.new(1,1,1)),
        })
    end
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- GUI ROOT
----------------------------------------------------------------
----------------------------------------------------------------
-- HEADER-ONLY DRAG
----------------------------------------------------------------
local function makeHeaderDrag(popup, header)
    local dragging, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil
    header.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            dragStart = input.Position
            startPos = popup.Position
        end
    end)
    header.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = false
        end
    end)
    SilenceAPI._onDrag(function(input)
        if not dragging then return end
        local delta = input.Position - dragStart
        local newX = startPos.X.Offset + delta.X
        local newY = startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
        local screen = Camera.ViewportSize
        local size = popup.AbsoluteSize
        local absX = startPos.X.Scale * screen.X + newX
        local absY = startPos.Y.Scale * screen.Y + newY
        local minX = 0
        local maxX = math.max(0, screen.X - size.X)
        local minY = 0
        local maxY = math.max(0, screen.Y - size.Y)
        absX = math.clamp(absX, minX, maxX)
        absY = math.clamp(absY, minY, maxY)
        newX = absX - startPos.X.Scale * screen.X
        newY = absY - startPos.Y.Scale * screen.Y
        popup.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, newX, startPos.Y.Scale, newY)
    end)
end

local function makeHudBtn(text, xPos, onClick, iconFn)
    local b = Instance.new("TextButton", Hud)
    b.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 28)
    b.Position = UDim2.new(0, xPos, 0.5, -14)
    b.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg3
    b.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
    b.BorderSizePixel = 0
    b.Text = iconFn and "" or text
    b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    b.TextSize = 12
    b.TextColor3 = Theme.text
    b.AutoButtonColor = false
    b.ZIndex = 31
    corner(b, 9)
    local st = stroke(b, Theme.accent, 1, 0.7)
    b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(b,  TweenInfo.new(0.15), { BackgroundTransparency = 0 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(st, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Transparency = 0.15 }):Play()
    end)
    b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(b,  TweenInfo.new(0.18), { BackgroundTransparency = 0.3 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(st, TweenInfo.new(0.18), { Transparency = 0.7 }):Play()
    end)
    b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(onClick or function() end)
    if iconFn then iconFn(b) end
    return b
end

local ulLay = Instance.new("UIListLayout", userList)
ulLay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
ulLay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
local ulPad = Instance.new("UIPadding", userList)
ulPad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6); ulPad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6)
ulPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 6); ulPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6)

local rowByPlayer = {}
local function buildUserRow(p)
    local row = Instance.new("Frame", userList)
    row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
    row.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    row.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg
    row.BorderSizePixel = 0
    row.ZIndex = 62
    row.ClipsDescendants = true
    corner(row, 4)
    local rowLay = Instance.new("UIListLayout", row)
    rowLay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    rowLay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 0)

    local header = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
    header.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
    header.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    header.Text = ""
    header.AutoButtonColor = false
    header.ZIndex = 62
    header.LayoutOrder = 1

    local arrow = Instance.new("TextLabel", header)
    arrow.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 1, 0)
    arrow.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 0)
    arrow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    arrow.Text = ">"
    arrow.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    arrow.TextSize = 11
    arrow.TextColor3 = Theme.textDim
    arrow.ZIndex = 63

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", header)
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -100, 1, 0)
    lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 0)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    local prefix = (p == LocalPlayer) and "* " or ""
    lbl.Text = prefix .. p.DisplayName .. "  (@" .. p.Name .. ")"
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    lbl.TextSize = 11
    lbl.TextColor3 = Theme.text
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    lbl.ZIndex = 63

    local marker = Instance.new("TextLabel", header)
    marker.Size = UDim2.new(0, 70, 0, 16)
    marker.Position = UDim2.new(1, -78, 0.5, -8)
    marker.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(110, 40, 200)
    marker.BorderSizePixel = 0
    marker.Text = "SILENCE"
    marker.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    marker.TextSize = 9
    marker.TextColor3 = Theme.text
    marker.Visible = false
    marker.ZIndex = 64
    corner(marker, 3)

    local info = Instance.new("Frame", row)
    info.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 0)
    info.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 0)
    info.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg2
    info.BorderSizePixel = 0
    info.Visible = false
    info.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    info.ZIndex = 62
    info.LayoutOrder = 2
    corner(info, 4)
    local infoLay = Instance.new("UIListLayout", info)
    infoLay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    infoLay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 2)
    local infoPad = Instance.new("UIPadding", info)
    infoPad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6); infoPad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6)
    infoPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10); infoPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10)

    local function addInfoLine(key, value, color)
        local line = Instance.new("TextLabel", info)
        line.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14)
        line.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        line.Text = string.format("%-14s %s", key, tostring(value))
        line.Font = Enum.Font.Code
        line.TextSize = 11
        line.TextColor3 = color or Theme.text
        line.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        line.ZIndex = 63
        return line
    end

    local populated = false
    local function populate()
        if populated then return end
        populated = true

        local avRow = Instance.new("Frame", info)
        avRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 64)
        avRow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        avRow.LayoutOrder = -1
        avRow.ZIndex = 63
        local av = Instance.new("ImageLabel", avRow)
        av.Size = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, 56)
        av.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 4)
        av.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg
        av.BorderSizePixel = 0
        av.Image = "rbxthumb://type=AvatarHeadShot&id=" .. p.UserId .. "&w=150&h=150"
        av.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
        av.ZIndex = 64
        local avc = Instance.new("UICorner", av); avc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
        local avBody = Instance.new("ImageLabel", avRow)
        avBody.Size = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, 56)
        avBody.Position = UDim2.new(0, 64, 0, 4)
        avBody.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg
        avBody.BorderSizePixel = 0
        avBody.Image = "rbxthumb://type=AvatarBust&id=" .. p.UserId .. "&w=180&h=180"
        avBody.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
        avBody.ZIndex = 64
        local avbc = Instance.new("UICorner", avBody); avbc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

        addInfoLine("Display:", p.DisplayName)
        addInfoLine("Name:",    "@" .. p.Name)
        addInfoLine("UserId:",  p.UserId)
        addInfoLine("AccountAge:", p.AccountAge .. " days")
        local createdEst = "~unknown"
        if p.UserId > 0 then
            local years = 2006 + (p.UserId / 5e9) * 18
            createdEst = string.format("~%d", math.floor(years))
        end
        addInfoLine("CreatedEst:", createdEst .. "  (UserId based)")
        addInfoLine("Membership:", tostring(p.MembershipType):gsub("Enum.MembershipType.",""))
        local teamName = p.Team and p.Team.Name or "(none)"
        addInfoLine("Team:", teamName, p.Team and p.Team.TeamColor and p.Team.TeamColor.Color or Theme.text)
        local frnd = "no"
        pcall(function() if LocalPlayer:IsFriendsWith(p.UserId) then frnd = "yes" end end)
        addInfoLine("Friend:", frnd)

        local hpLine     = addInfoLine("Health:", "-")
        local wsLine     = addInfoLine("WalkSpeed:", "-")
        local jpLine     = addInfoLine("JumpPower:", "-")
        local distLine   = addInfoLine("Distance:", "-")
        local posLine    = addInfoLine("Position:", "-")
        local velLine    = addInfoLine("Speed:", "-")
        local toolLine   = addInfoLine("Tool:", "-")
        local lookLine   = addInfoLine("LookingAtMe:", "-")
        local stateLine  = addInfoLine("State:", "-")
        local backpackLn = addInfoLine("Backpack:", "-")

        local passLine = addInfoLine("SilencePass:", "checking...", Theme.textDim)
        task.spawn(function()
            local owns = false
            if SilenceAPI._ownsScriptPass then
                owns = SilenceAPI._ownsScriptPass(p) and true or false
            end
            if passLine and passLine.Parent then
                passLine.Text = string.format("%-14s %s", "SilencePass:", owns and "yes" or "no")
                passLine.TextColor3 = owns and Color3.fromRGB(180, 120, 255) or Theme.textDim
            end
        end)

        local updConn
        updConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not info.Parent or not info.Visible then return end
            local char = p.Character
            local hum  = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local hrp  = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hum then
                hpLine.Text = string.format("%-14s %d / %d", "Health:", math.floor(hum.Health), math.floor(hum.MaxHealth))
                wsLine.Text = string.format("%-14s %d", "WalkSpeed:", math.floor(hum.WalkSpeed))
                jpLine.Text = string.format("%-14s %d", "JumpPower:", math.floor(hum.JumpPower))
                stateLine.Text = string.format("%-14s %s", "State:", tostring(hum:GetState()):gsub("Enum.HumanoidStateType.",""))
                local heldTool = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
                toolLine.Text = string.format("%-14s %s", "Tool:", heldTool and heldTool.Name or "(none)")
            else
                hpLine.Text = string.format("%-14s %s", "Health:", "(no char)")
                toolLine.Text = string.format("%-14s %s", "Tool:", "(no char)")
            end
            if hrp then
                posLine.Text = string.format("%-14s %d, %d, %d", "Position:",
                    math.floor(hrp.Position.X), math.floor(hrp.Position.Y), math.floor(hrp.Position.Z))
                velLine.Text = string.format("%-14s %d studs/s", "Speed:", math.floor(hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude))
                local myHrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if myHrp then
                    distLine.Text = string.format("%-14s %d studs", "Distance:", math.floor((hrp.Position - myHrp.Position).Magnitude))
                    local toMe = (myHrp.Position - hrp.Position)
                    local lookDot = toMe.Magnitude > 0 and hrp.CFrame.LookVector:Dot(toMe.Unit) or 0
                    lookLine.Text = string.format("%-14s %s (%.2f)", "LookingAtMe:",
                        lookDot > 0.85 and "YES" or (lookDot > 0.5 and "near" or "no"), lookDot)
                end
            end
            local bp = p:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
            if bp then
                local items = bp:GetChildren()
                local names = {}
                for i, t in ipairs(items) do if i <= 4 then names[#names+1] = t.Name end end
                backpackLn.Text = string.format("%-14s %d items %s", "Backpack:",
                    #items, #names > 0 and ("("..table.concat(names, ", ")..")") or "")
            else
                backpackLn.Text = string.format("%-14s %s", "Backpack:", "(server-only)")
            end
        end)
        track(updConn)
        row.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
            if not parent and updConn then updConn:Disconnect() end
        end)
    end

    local expanded = false
    header.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        expanded = not expanded
        info.Visible = expanded
        arrow.Text = expanded and "v" or ">"
        if expanded then populate() end
    end)

    rowByPlayer[p] = marker
end

local function refreshUserList()
    for _, c in ipairs(userList:GetChildren()) do
        if c:IsA("Frame") or c:IsA("TextLabel") then c:Destroy() end
    end

    local players = Players:GetPlayers()
    for k in pairs(rowByPlayer) do rowByPlayer[k] = nil end
    for _, p in ipairs(players) do
        buildUserRow(p)
    end

    if SilenceAPI._ownsScriptPass then
        for _, p in ipairs(players) do
            task.spawn(function()
                local owns = SilenceAPI._ownsScriptPass(p)
                local marker = rowByPlayer[p]
                if owns and marker and marker.Parent and SettingsPopup.Visible then
                    marker.Visible = true
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end

SettingsPopup:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
    if SettingsPopup.Visible then refreshUserList() end
end)
track(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function() if SettingsPopup.Visible then refreshUserList() end end))
track(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function() if SettingsPopup.Visible then refreshUserList() end end))

local hudGear = makeHudBtn("", 262, function()
    SettingsPopup.Visible = not SettingsPopup.Visible
end, SilenceAPI._gearIcon)

-- Drawn Discord mark, used when no PNG loaded. The shell is a wide rounded
-- rectangle and the two flares are squares turned 45 degrees, placed at the
-- OUTER bottom corners so a point drops below each side - the old pair sat
-- near the middle, which is why it did not read as the logo.
local function drawDiscordGlyph(b)
    local z = b.ZIndex + 1

    local shell = Instance.new("Frame", b)
    shell.Name = "DiscordVec"
    shell.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 15)
    shell.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -11, 0.5, -9)
    shell.BorderSizePixel = 0
    shell.ZIndex = z
    corner(shell, 7)
    SilenceAPI._theme(shell, "BackgroundColor3", "text")

    local function flare(x)
        local f = Instance.new("Frame", b)
        f.Name = "DiscordVec"
        f.Size = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8)
        f.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, x, 0.5, 0)
        f.Rotation = 45
        f.BorderSizePixel = 0
        f.ZIndex = z
        corner(f, 2)
        SilenceAPI._theme(f, "BackgroundColor3", "text")
    end
    flare(-12); flare(4)

    local function eye(x)
        local e = Instance.new("Frame", b)
        e.Name = "DiscordVec"
        e.Size = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 6)
        e.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, x, 0.5, -4)
        e.BorderSizePixel = 0
        e.ZIndex = z + 1
        local c = Instance.new("UICorner", e); c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        SilenceAPI._theme(e, "BackgroundColor3", "bg")
    end
    eye(-6); eye(2)
end
-- both published so the Logs tab can rebuild the mark after a re-check
SilenceAPI._drawDiscord = drawDiscordGlyph

local hudDiscord = makeHudBtn("", 296, function()
    notify("Discord", "Coming soon", 2)
end)
SilenceAPI._discordHost = hudDiscord

if DISCORD_IMG then
    local img = Instance.new("ImageLabel", hudDiscord)
    img.Name = "DiscordImg"
    img.Size = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 20)
    img.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -10, 0.5, -10)
    img.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    img.Image = DISCORD_IMG
    img.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
    img.ZIndex = hudDiscord.ZIndex + 1
    -- an executor can hand back a content string for an image that never
    -- decodes, leaving the button blank; verify, then fall back
    task.delay(2, function()
        if img and img.Parent and not img.IsLoaded then
            print("[Silence] discord image failed to load, using drawn glyph")
            img:Destroy()
            drawDiscordGlyph(hudDiscord)
        end
    end)
else
    drawDiscordGlyph(hudDiscord)
end

local _fpsFrames, _fpsLast = 0, tick()
track(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function() _fpsFrames = _fpsFrames + 1 end))
task.spawn(function()
    while Hud.Parent do
        task.wait(0.5)
        if not Hud or not Hud.Parent then return end
        local now = tick()
        local fps = math.floor(_fpsFrames / math.max(0.001, now - _fpsLast) + 0.5)
        _fpsFrames, _fpsLast = 0, now
        pcall(function()
            local players = #Players:GetPlayers()
            local ping = 0
            local ok, p = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer:GetNetworkPing() end)
            if ok and p then ping = math.floor(p * 1000 + 0.5) end
            hudFps.Text = tostring(fps)
            hudPlayers.Text = tostring(players)
            hudPing.Text = tostring(ping)
            -- one sampler drives both readouts; the shell header is built
            -- after the HUD, so it is looked up rather than captured
            if SilenceAPI._pushStats then
                SilenceAPI._pushStats(fps, players, ping)
            end
        end)
    end
end)

end)
if not _hudOk then
    warn("[Silence] HUD error: " .. tostring(_hudErr))
else
    print("[Silence] HUD built")
end

-- Shell geometry lives in one place: the minimize animation and the final
-- "INJECT" size lock both read these instead of repeating the numbers.
SilenceAPI._panelW, SilenceAPI._panelH = 860, 620

local Container = Instance.new("Frame", Gui)
Container.Name = "Container"
-- published so Commands.unload (defined above this line) can reach it
SilenceAPI._container = Container
Container.Size = UDim2.new(0, SilenceAPI._panelW, 0, SilenceAPI._panelH)
Container.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -SilenceAPI._panelW/2, 0.5, -SilenceAPI._panelH/2)
Container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
Container.Active = true
-- panel zoom: 860x620 is comfortable on a desktop and enormous on a laptop
SilenceAPI._uiScale = Instance.new("UIScale", Container)
SilenceAPI._uiScale.Scale = 1
function SilenceAPI._setUiScale(v)
    local n = tonumber(v) or 1
    n = math.clamp(n, 0.5, 1.8)
    State.uiScale = n
    SilenceAPI._uiScale.Scale = n
end

local Panel=Instance.new("Frame", Container)
Panel.Name="Panel"
-- centred anchor, so the open animation scales from the middle of the shell
Panel.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5)
Panel.Position=UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.5,0)
Panel.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
Panel.BorderSizePixel=0
SilenceAPI._theme(Panel,"BackgroundColor3","bg")
-- A Frame only swallows mouse input when it is Active. Without this every
-- click on an empty patch of the panel went straight through to the game
-- underneath - so dragging a slider could also swing a tool.
Panel.Active=true
Panel.ClipsDescendants=true
-- less see-through than the little bar: a full page of rows needs the
-- contrast, and the blur behind is doing half the work already
SilenceAPI._glass(Panel, { radius = 18, transparency = 0.25, edge = 0.35,
                           glowThickness = 2 })

-- The open pop rides its OWN UIScale (on Panel) so it can never fight the
-- user zoom setting, which lives on the UIScale attached to Container.
SilenceAPI._panelScale = Instance.new("UIScale", Panel)
SilenceAPI._panelScale.Scale = 1
track(Container:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
    if SilenceAPI._setMenuBlur then SilenceAPI._setMenuBlur() end
    if not Container.Visible then return end
    SilenceAPI._panelScale.Scale = 0.9
    TweenService:Create(SilenceAPI._panelScale,
        TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
        { Scale = 1 }):Play()
end))

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- BACKGROUND BLUR
-- A BlurEffect lives in Lighting, so it has to be torn down explicitly on
-- unload - destroying the ScreenGui would leave the world blurred forever.
----------------------------------------------------------------
SilenceAPI._blur = Instance.new("BlurEffect")
SilenceAPI._blur.Name    = "__SIL_Blur"
SilenceAPI._blur.Size    = 0
SilenceAPI._blur.Enabled = false
SilenceAPI._blur.Parent  = Lighting

function SilenceAPI._setMenuBlur()
    local b = SilenceAPI._blur
    if not b or not b.Parent then return end
    -- On while the full panel is open, or while any registered popup is.
    -- Minimized you are playing rather than reading, so the world stays
    -- sharp and the little bar frosts itself instead (see _setMinimized).
    -- Freecam always wins over the blur.
    local want = State.menuBlur and not State.freecam
    if want then
        local shown = Container.Visible and not SilenceAPI._minimized
        if not shown then
            for _, f in ipairs(SilenceAPI._blurSurfaces) do
                if f and f.Parent and f.Visible then shown = true break end
            end
        end
        want = shown
    end
    local goal = want and 12 or 0
    b.Enabled = true
    TweenService:Create(b,
        TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
        { Size = goal }):Play()
    if goal == 0 then
        task.delay(0.34, function()
            if b.Parent and b.Size <= 0.05 then b.Enabled = false end
        end)
    end
end

-- Header pieces that only fit on the wide layout; the compact bar hides them.
SilenceAPI._hdrWide = {}

-- Minimize collapses the shell to a small rounded bar instead of hiding it
-- outright, so the way back is always on screen.
SilenceAPI._minimized = false
SilenceAPI._miniW     = 380
SilenceAPI._miniH     = 48
function SilenceAPI._setMinimized(v)
    v = v and true or false
    if SilenceAPI._minimized == v then return end
    SilenceAPI._minimized = v

    -- The two layouts are separate bars rather than one header that reflows:
    -- the wide header keeps its absolute positions and the compact bar has
    -- its own, so nothing is ever seen squeezed at an in-between width.
    if v then
        for _, o in ipairs(SilenceAPI._hdrWide) do
            if o and o.Parent then o.Visible = false end
        end
        if SilenceAPI._header  then SilenceAPI._header.Visible  = false end
        if SilenceAPI._miniBar then SilenceAPI._miniBar.Visible = true  end
    else
        if SilenceAPI._miniBar then SilenceAPI._miniBar.Visible = false end
        -- the wide header comes back straight away; the pieces that need the
        -- full width follow once the tween has made room for them
        if SilenceAPI._header  then SilenceAPI._header.Visible  = true  end
    end

    -- Frosted, not blurred: Roblox cannot blur only what sits behind one GUI
    -- element - a BlurEffect is whole-screen. So the shell itself goes
    -- translucent while minimized and the world reads through the black.
    TweenService:Create(Panel,
        TweenInfo.new(0.34, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
        { BackgroundTransparency = v and 0.38 or 0.25 }):Play()

    local tw = TweenService:Create(Container,
        TweenInfo.new(0.34, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
        { Size = UDim2.new(0, v and SilenceAPI._miniW or SilenceAPI._panelW,
                           0, v and SilenceAPI._miniH or SilenceAPI._panelH) })
    if not v then
        tw.Completed:Connect(function()
            if SilenceAPI._minimized then return end
            for _, o in ipairs(SilenceAPI._hdrWide) do
                if o and o.Parent then o.Visible = true end
            end
            if SilenceAPI._syncTabInd then
                SilenceAPI._syncTabInd(SilenceAPI._activeTabBtn, false)
            end
        end)
    end
    tw:Play()
    SilenceAPI._setMenuBlur()
end

local AcBanner = Instance.new("Frame", Container)
AcBanner.Name = "AcBanner"
AcBanner.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26)
AcBanner.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, -28)
AcBanner.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bad
AcBanner.BorderSizePixel = 0
AcBanner.Visible = false
AcBanner.ZIndex = 90
corner(AcBanner, 6)
local acBannerLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", AcBanner)
acBannerLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, 0)
acBannerLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0)
acBannerLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
acBannerLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
acBannerLbl.TextSize = 12
acBannerLbl.TextColor3 = Theme.text
acBannerLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
acBannerLbl.Text = "ANTI-CHEAT DETECTED - features disabled. Enable RISKY in AntiCheat tab to override."
acBannerLbl.ZIndex = 91

local function refreshAcBanner()
    local blocked = State.acBlocked
    if not blocked then
        AcBanner.Visible = false
        return
    end
    AcBanner.Visible = true
    if State.acRiskyOverride then
        AcBanner.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 120, 30)
        acBannerLbl.Text = string.format(
            "STEALTH MODE ACTIVE (%d AC hit(s)) - features adapted: smoothed aimbot, jittered silent-aim, "..
            "physics-fly, local invisibility, hitbox disabled.", State.acHits or 0)
    else
        AcBanner.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bad
        acBannerLbl.Text = string.format(
            "ANTI-CHEAT DETECTED (%d hits) - features disabled. Enable RISKY in AntiCheat tab.",
            State.acHits or 0)
    end
end
SilenceAPI._refreshAcBanner = refreshAcBanner

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- HEADER
----------------------------------------------------------------
local Header=Instance.new("Frame", Panel)
Header.Name="Header"
Header.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,64)
SilenceAPI._theme(Header,"BackgroundColor3","bg2")
-- a denser band on top of the glass rather than a solid lid. The cap below
-- shares the same alpha so the two never show as different materials.
Header.BackgroundTransparency=0.25
Header.BorderSizePixel=0
Header.Active = true
Header.ZIndex = 5
corner(Header,18)
gradient(Header, Theme.bg4, Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0), 90)
makeHeaderDrag(Container, Header)
-- _setMinimized is defined before this line, so it reaches the header here
SilenceAPI._header = Header

do -- squares off the header bottom corners, plus the sweeping hairline
local cap=Instance.new("Frame", Header)
cap.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,18); cap.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,1,-18)
cap.BorderSizePixel=0; cap.ZIndex=5
-- squares off the header's rounded bottom, carrying the same alpha as the
-- header itself; the 18px where they overlap comes out slightly denser and
-- sits under the hairline, which reads as a soft shadow rather than a seam
cap.BackgroundTransparency=0.25
SilenceAPI._theme(cap,"BackgroundColor3","bg2")

local line=Instance.new("Frame", Header)
line.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,1); line.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,1,-1)
line.BorderSizePixel=0; line.ZIndex=6
SilenceAPI._theme(line,"BackgroundColor3","accent")
-- the compact bar is a plain rounded pill: no squared-off cap, no hairline
table.insert(SilenceAPI._hdrWide, cap)
table.insert(SilenceAPI._hdrWide, line)
local lg=Instance.new("UIGradient", line)
lg.Transparency=NumberSequence.new({
    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,    1),
    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.34, 0.4),
    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,  0),
    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.66, 0.4),
    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,    1),
})

    lg.Offset = Vector2.new(math.sin(tick() * 0.55) * 0.85, 0)

    k.BackgroundTransparency=1; k.Text=caption
    k.Font=Enum.Font.GothamMedium; k.TextSize=9

    v.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; v.TextSize=13
    v.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; v.ZIndex=8

end
chip(300, "fps",     "FPS")
chip(392, "ping",    "PING")

function SilenceAPI._pushStats(fps, players, ping)
    if vals.fps     and vals.fps.Parent     then vals.fps.Text     = tostring(fps) end
    if vals.ping    and vals.ping.Parent    then vals.ping.Text    = tostring(ping) .. " ms" end
    if vals.players and vals.players.Parent then vals.players.Text = tostring(players) end
    -- the compact bar carries the same three numbers
    if SilenceAPI._miniStats then SilenceAPI._miniStats(fps, players, ping) end
end
end

-- the same setting also has a switch in the Settings popup, so both read
-- State and both sit in _toggleRefreshers - flipping either syncs the other
local function refresh()
    local shown = not State.tagsHidden
    v.Text = shown and "ON" or "OFF"
    v.TextColor3 = shown and Theme.text or Theme.textDim
    st.Color = Theme.accent
    st.Transparency = shown and 0.82 or 0.3
end
f.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
    TweenService:Create(f, SilenceAPI._twFade, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg4 }):Play()
end)
f.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
    TweenService:Create(f, SilenceAPI._twFade, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg3 }):Play()
end)
f.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if SilenceAPI._setTagsHidden then
        SilenceAPI._setTagsHidden(not State.tagsHidden)
    else
        State.tagsHidden = not State.tagsHidden
    end
    for _, r in ipairs(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers) do pcall(r) end
end)
refresh()
table.insert(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers, refresh)
table.insert(SilenceAPI._hdrWide, f)

        TweenService:Create(b,  SilenceAPI._twFade, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg3 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(st, SilenceAPI._twFade, { Transparency = 0.7 }):Play()

    return b
end

-- Navigation is two levels now: five icons down the left pick a GROUP, and
-- the tabs of that group appear as a strip across the top of the page. The
-- old accordion showed every tab at once and never fit on screen.
local Sidebar=Instance.new("Frame", Panel)
Sidebar.Name="IconRail"
Sidebar.Size=UDim2.new(0,76,1,-64); Sidebar.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,64)
-- transparent on purpose: Panel paints the background, and Panel is the only
-- thing with the rounded corners. An opaque rail would square off the
-- bottom-left corner by painting over the arc.
Sidebar.BackgroundTransparency=1
Sidebar.BorderSizePixel=0
local SbLayout=Instance.new("UIListLayout", Sidebar)
SbLayout.Padding=UDim.new(0,8); SbLayout.SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
SbLayout.HorizontalAlignment=Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
padding(Sidebar,16,16,10,10)

do -- hairline that separates the icon rail from the pages

d.BorderSizePixel=0; d.ZIndex=4
SilenceAPI._theme(d,"BackgroundColor3","bg4")
end

local Content=Instance.new("Frame", Panel)
Content.Name="Content"
Content.Size=UDim2.new(1,-77,1,-64); Content.Position=UDim2.new(0,77,0,64)
-- transparent for the same reason as the rail: the bottom-right corner arc
-- belongs to Panel and nothing may paint over it
Content.BackgroundTransparency=1
Content.BorderSizePixel=0
Content.ClipsDescendants=true

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- TAB STRIP  -  the tabs of the selected group, plus the search box.
-- The active tab is marked by one shared underline that slides between
-- pills rather than a bar per pill.
----------------------------------------------------------------
do
local bar=Instance.new("Frame", Content)
bar.Name="TabBar"
bar.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,54); bar.BorderSizePixel=0; bar.ZIndex=6
bar.BackgroundTransparency=1

local strip=Instance.new("Frame", bar)
strip.Name="Strip"
strip.Size=UDim2.new(1,-276,1,0); strip.Position=UDim2.new(0,16,0,0)
strip.BackgroundTransparency=1; strip.BorderSizePixel=0
strip.ClipsDescendants=true
strip.ZIndex=7
local sl=Instance.new("UIListLayout", strip)
sl.FillDirection=Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
sl.VerticalAlignment=Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
sl.SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
sl.Padding=UDim.new(0,6)
SilenceAPI._tabStrip = strip

local line=Instance.new("Frame", bar)
line.Size=UDim2.new(1,-32,0,1); line.Position=UDim2.new(0,16,1,-1)
line.BorderSizePixel=0; line.ZIndex=7
SilenceAPI._theme(line,"BackgroundColor3","bg4")

-- lives on the bar, not inside the strip: a child of the strip would be
-- swept up by its UIListLayout and pushed in between the pills
local ind=Instance.new("Frame", bar)
ind.Size=UDim2.new(0,0,0,3); ind.Position=UDim2.new(0,16,1,-3)
ind.BorderSizePixel=0; ind.ZIndex=8
corner(ind,2)
SilenceAPI._theme(ind,"BackgroundColor3","accent")

-- AbsolutePosition is in real screen pixels, which already include both the
-- user's zoom and the open-animation pop; the offsets we write back are not,
-- so divide the difference out or the underline drifts off the pill.
SilenceAPI._syncTabInd = function(btn, animate)
    if not btn or not btn.Parent or not btn.Visible then
        ind.Size = UDim2.new(0,0,0,3)
        return
    end
    local sc = 1
    if SilenceAPI._uiScale    then sc = sc * SilenceAPI._uiScale.Scale end
    if SilenceAPI._panelScale then sc = sc * SilenceAPI._panelScale.Scale end
    if sc <= 0 then return end
    local w = btn.AbsoluteSize.X / sc
    if w <= 1 then return end
    local x = (btn.AbsolutePosition.X - bar.AbsolutePosition.X) / sc
    local goalP = UDim2.new(0, math.floor(x + 0.5), 1, -3)
    local goalS = UDim2.new(0, math.floor(w + 0.5), 0, 3)
    if animate then
        TweenService:Create(ind,
            TweenInfo.new(0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
            { Position = goalP, Size = goalS }):Play()
    else
        ind.Position = goalP
        ind.Size = goalS
    end
end

-- pills resize when the search box appends a hit count, and the whole strip
-- is re-laid out on a group switch; both land here
sl:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
    SilenceAPI._syncTabInd(SilenceAPI._activeTabBtn, false)
end)

SilenceAPI._searchHost = bar
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- TABS
----------------------------------------------------------------
local Tabs={}
-- A tab is a pill in the top strip. AutomaticSize.X means the pill is
-- exactly as wide as its label, which is what lets the shared underline
-- line up with it without any hard-coded widths.
local function makeTabButton(name)
    local b=Instance.new("TextButton", SilenceAPI._tabStrip)
    b.Name="Tab_"..name
    b.AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.X
    b.Size=UDim2.new(0,0,0,32)
    b.BorderSizePixel=0
    b.BackgroundColor3=Theme.bg2
    b.BackgroundTransparency=1
    b.Text=name
    b.Font=Enum.Font.GothamMedium; b.TextSize=13
    b.TextColor3=Theme.textDim
    b.AutoButtonColor=false
    b.Visible=false
    b.ZIndex=7
    corner(b,9); padding(b,0,0,15,15)
    b:SetAttribute("Active", false)
    b:SetAttribute("SilName", name)

    local glow = addAnimatedGlow(b, { thickness=1.5, transparency=0.3, window=0.22 })
    glow.Enabled = false

    local function paint()
        local on = b:GetAttribute("Active")
        b.BackgroundColor3       = Theme.bg2
        b.BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or 1
        b.TextColor3             = on and Theme.text or Theme.textDim
        glow.Enabled             = on
    end
    b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        if b:GetAttribute("Active") then return end
        TweenService:Create(b, SilenceAPI._twFade,
            { BackgroundTransparency = 0.45, TextColor3 = Theme.text }):Play()
    end)
    b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        if b:GetAttribute("Active") then return end
        TweenService:Create(b, SilenceAPI._twFade,
            { BackgroundTransparency = 1, TextColor3 = Theme.textDim }):Play()
    end)
    if SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers then
        table.insert(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers, paint)
    end
    return b, paint
end
local function makeTabPage()
    local page=Instance.new("ScrollingFrame", Content)
    -- 54px reserved at the top for the tab strip. The 10px pulled off the
    -- width and height keeps the scrollbar clear of the panel's rounded
    -- bottom-right corner.
    page.Size=UDim2.new(1,-10,1,-64); page.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,54)
    page.BackgroundTransparency=1; page.BorderSizePixel=0
    page.Visible=false
    page.ScrollBarThickness=4
    page.ScrollBarImageTransparency=0.4
    SilenceAPI._theme(page,"ScrollBarImageColor3","accent")
    page.CanvasSize=UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
    page.AutomaticCanvasSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    local layout=Instance.new("UIListLayout", page)
    layout.Padding=UDim.new(0,9); layout.SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    padding(page,18,26,20,20)
    return page
end
local function selectTab(name)
    if not Tabs[name] then return end
    -- A tab can be reached from outside its group (the search box jumps to
    -- the first hit), so make sure that group's strip is the one on screen
    -- before anything else. _showCat only swaps pill visibility, so this
    -- cannot recurse back into here.
    local cat = (SilenceAPI._catOf or {})[name]
    if cat and SilenceAPI._activeCat ~= cat and SilenceAPI._showCat then
        SilenceAPI._showCat(cat)
    end
    if cat and SilenceAPI._lastTabOf then SilenceAPI._lastTabOf[cat] = name end

    for n,t in pairs(Tabs) do
        local active = (n == name)
        t.btn:SetAttribute("Active", active)
        if t.paint then t.paint() end
        if active then
            -- the incoming page always starts from the same offset and the
            -- tween always lands on the same one, so an interrupted switch
            -- can never strand a page part-way. The previous tween is
            -- cancelled outright so two of them cannot fight over Position
            -- when tabs are clicked quickly.
            if t.slide then pcall(function() t.slide:Cancel() end) end
            t.page.Visible = true
            t.page.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,66)
            t.slide = TweenService:Create(t.page,
                TweenInfo.new(0.24, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                { Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,54) })
            t.slide:Play()
        else
            t.page.Visible = false
        end
    end
    SilenceAPI._activeTab    = name
    SilenceAPI._activeTabBtn = Tabs[name].btn
    if SilenceAPI._pageDesc then
        SilenceAPI._pageDesc.Text = (SilenceAPI._tabDesc or {})[name] or ""
    end
    -- the pill has to be laid out before the underline can measure it
    if SilenceAPI._syncTabInd then
        local btn = Tabs[name].btn
        task.defer(function()
            if SilenceAPI._activeTab == name then SilenceAPI._syncTabInd(btn, true) end
        end)
    end
    if SilenceAPI._refreshAcLocks then SilenceAPI._refreshAcLocks() end
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- SEARCH BAR - filters every registered row across all tabs
-- (do-block: five widgets that would otherwise blow the 200-local cap)
----------------------------------------------------------------
do
local SearchWrap = Instance.new("Frame", SilenceAPI._searchHost)
SearchWrap.Size = UDim2.new(0, 240, 0, 32)

SearchWrap.BorderSizePixel = 0
SearchWrap.ZIndex = 8

corner(SearchWrap, 9)
local SearchStroke = stroke(SearchWrap, Theme.accent, 1, 0.78)

local SearchBox = Instance.new("TextBox", SearchWrap)
SearchBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -46, 1, 0); SearchBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
SearchBox.BackgroundTransparency = 1
SearchBox.Text = ""
SearchBox.PlaceholderText = "search every toggle..."
SearchBox.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium; SearchBox.TextSize = 12
SearchBox.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
SearchBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
SearchBox.ZIndex = 9
SilenceAPI._theme(SearchBox, "PlaceholderColor3", "textDim")
SilenceAPI._theme(SearchBox, "TextColor3", "text")
SearchBox.Focused:Connect(function()
    TweenService:Create(SearchStroke, SilenceAPI._twFade, { Transparency = 0.1 }):Play()
end)
SearchBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
    TweenService:Create(SearchStroke, SilenceAPI._twFade, { Transparency = 0.78 }):Play()
end)

local SearchClear = Instance.new("TextButton", SearchWrap)
SearchClear.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 22); SearchClear.Position = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0.5, -11)
SearchClear.BackgroundTransparency = 1
SearchClear.Text = "X"
SearchClear.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; SearchClear.TextSize = 11
SearchClear.Visible = false
SearchClear.AutoButtonColor = false
SearchClear.ZIndex = 9
SilenceAPI._theme(SearchClear, "TextColor3", "textDim")

local function applySearch(q)
    q = string.lower(q or "")
    SearchClear.Visible = q ~= ""

    local function setSections(vis)
        for _, t in pairs(Tabs) do
            for _, c in ipairs(t.page:GetChildren()) do
                if c:GetAttribute("SilSection") then c.Visible = vis end
            end
        end
    end

    -- Some rows live inside a collapsible group that is zero-height while its
    -- parent toggle is off, so a search hit inside it used to be invisible.
    -- Each group registers a function here that forces itself open on search.
    for _, fn in ipairs(SilenceAPI._searchExpanders or {}) do pcall(fn, q ~= "") end

    -- a pill shows its own hit count, a rail tile shows its group's total,
    -- so hits outside the group you are looking at are still findable
    local function paintCounts(counts)
        for n, t in pairs(Tabs) do
            local c = counts and counts[n]
            t.btn.Text = n .. (c and ("   " .. c) or "")
        end
        local catTotals = {}
        if counts then
            for n, c in pairs(counts) do
                local cat = (SilenceAPI._catOf or {})[n]
                if cat then catTotals[cat] = (catTotals[cat] or 0) + c end
            end
        end
        for key, badge in pairs(SilenceAPI._catBadge or {}) do
            local c = catTotals[key]
            badge.Visible = c ~= nil
            badge.Text = c and tostring(c) or ""
        end
    end

    if q == "" then
        for _, e in ipairs(SilenceAPI._searchRows) do
            if e.inst and e.inst.Parent then e.inst.Visible = true end
        end
        setSections(true)
        paintCounts(nil)
        return
    end

    setSections(false)
    local counts, firstHit = {}, nil
    for _, e in ipairs(SilenceAPI._searchRows) do
        if e.inst and e.inst.Parent then
            local hit = string.find(e.label, q, 1, true) ~= nil
            e.inst.Visible = hit
            if hit and e.tab then
                counts[e.tab] = (counts[e.tab] or 0) + 1
                if not firstHit then firstHit = e.tab end
            end
        end
    end
    paintCounts(counts)
    -- jump to a tab that actually has results; selectTab switches the group
    -- along with it, so a hit in a collapsed group is still reachable
    if firstHit and not counts[SilenceAPI._activeTab] then selectTab(firstHit) end
end

SearchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
    applySearch(SearchBox.Text)
end)
SearchClear.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    SearchBox.Text = ""
end)
SilenceAPI._clearSearch = function()
    if SearchBox then SearchBox.Text = "" end
end
end -- SEARCH BAR
