-- One icon tile per group. The glyphs are drawn from plain Frames on
-- purpose: Gotham has no symbol characters (they come out as empty boxes,
-- which is exactly what got stripped out of this panel once already), and an
-- rbxasset texture path that moves would leave the rail blank.
local function makeRailButton(cat)
    local b = Instance.new("TextButton", Sidebar)
    b.Name = "Rail_" .. cat.key
    b.Size = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, 58)
    b.BorderSizePixel = 0
    b.Text = ""
    b.AutoButtonColor = false
    b.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg2
    b.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    b.LayoutOrder = cat.order
    corner(b, 12)
    local st = stroke(b, Theme.accent, 1, 1)
    local glow = addAnimatedGlow(b, { thickness = 1.5, transparency = 0.3, window = 0.2 })
    glow.Enabled = false

    local ico = Instance.new("Frame", b)
    ico.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 26); ico.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -13, 0, 8)
    ico.BackgroundTransparency = 1; ico.BorderSizePixel = 0

    local parts = {}
    local function px(w, h, x, y, r)
        local f = Instance.new("Frame", ico)
        f.Size = UDim2.new(0, w, 0, h); f.Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, y)
        f.BorderSizePixel = 0
        if r then corner(f, r) end
        table.insert(parts, { f, "BackgroundColor3" })
        return f
    end
    local function dot(d, x, y)
        local f = px(d, d, x, y)
        local c = Instance.new("UICorner", f); c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        return f
    end
    local function ring(d, x, y, th)
        local f = Instance.new("Frame", ico)
        f.Size = UDim2.new(0, d, 0, d); f.Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, y)
        f.BackgroundTransparency = 1; f.BorderSizePixel = 0
        local c = Instance.new("UICorner", f); c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        local o = Instance.new("UIStroke", f); o.Thickness = th or 2
        table.insert(parts, { o, "Color" })
        return f
    end

    if cat.icon == "home" then          -- four tiles: an overview
        px(10,10,  2, 2, 3); px(10,10, 14, 2, 3)
        px(10,10,  2,14, 3); px(10,10, 14,14, 3)
    elseif cat.icon == "self" then      -- head and shoulders
        dot(10, 8, 1)
        px(20,12, 3,14, 6)
    elseif cat.icon == "larp" then      -- a hat: costume, roleplay
        px(12,15, 7, 3, 3)
        px(24, 4, 1,18, 2)
    elseif cat.icon == "combat" then    -- crosshair
        ring(22, 2, 2, 2)
        dot(4, 11, 11)
        px(2,5, 12, 0, 1); px(2,5, 12,21, 1)
        px(5,2,  0,12, 1); px(5,2, 21,12, 1)
    elseif cat.icon == "games" then     -- a gamepad: body, dpad, two buttons
        px(26, 12, 0, 7, 5)
        px(2, 6, 5, 10, 1); px(6, 2, 3, 12, 1)
        dot(4, 16, 10); dot(4, 20, 14)
    elseif cat.icon == "voice" then     -- a microphone: capsule, arc, stand
        px(8, 12, 9, 2, 4)                          -- body, centred on x=13
        px(2, 6, 6, 9, 1); px(2, 6, 18, 9, 1)       -- arc arms, 6..8 / 18..20
        px(14, 2, 6, 15, 1)                         -- floor spans 6..20 exactly
        px(2, 4, 12, 17, 1); px(10, 2, 8, 21, 1)    -- stand and base
    else                                -- three bars: settings
        px(22,3, 2, 4, 2); px(15,3, 2,11, 2); px(19,3, 2,18, 2)
    end

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", b)
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 12); lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 39)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = cat.label
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; lbl.TextSize = 9
    lbl.TextColor3 = Theme.textDim

    -- search-hit count, so hits in a group you are not looking at are visible
    local badge = Instance.new("TextLabel", b)
    badge.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 14); badge.Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 2)
    badge.BorderSizePixel = 0
    badge.Text = ""
    badge.Visible = false
    badge.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; badge.TextSize = 9
    badge.ZIndex = 4
    corner(badge, 7)
    SilenceAPI._theme(badge, "BackgroundColor3", "accent")
    SilenceAPI._theme(badge, "TextColor3", "bg")
    SilenceAPI._catBadge[cat.key] = badge

    local function paint()
        local on = (SilenceAPI._activeCat == cat.key)
        b.BackgroundColor3       = Theme.bg2
        b.BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or 1
        st.Color                 = Theme.accent
        st.Transparency          = on and 0.35 or 1
        glow.Enabled             = on
        lbl.TextColor3           = on and Theme.text or Theme.textDim
        local c = on and Theme.accent or Theme.textDim
        for _, p in ipairs(parts) do pcall(function() p[1][p[2]] = c end) end
    end
    b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        if SilenceAPI._activeCat == cat.key then return end
        TweenService:Create(b, SilenceAPI._twFade, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.5 }):Play()
        lbl.TextColor3 = Theme.text
        for _, p in ipairs(parts) do pcall(function() p[1][p[2]] = Theme.text end) end
    end)
    b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        if SilenceAPI._activeCat == cat.key then return end
        TweenService:Create(b, SilenceAPI._twFade, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
        paint()
    end)
    b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local want = SilenceAPI._lastTabOf[cat.key] or SilenceAPI._firstTabOf(cat.key)
        if want then selectTab(want) end
    end)
    table.insert(SilenceAPI._railPaint, paint)
    return b
end

-- lowest LayoutOrder tab that actually exists in a group
SilenceAPI._firstTabOf = function(cat)
    local best, bestOrder = nil, math.huge
    for n, c in pairs(TAB_CATEGORY) do
        if c == cat and Tabs[n] then
            local o = TAB_LO_INSIDE[n] or 100
            if o < bestOrder then best, bestOrder = n, o end
        end
    end
    return best
end

-- Swaps which group's pills are on screen. Deliberately does NOT pick a tab,
-- so selectTab can call it without recursing back into itself.
SilenceAPI._showCat = function(cat)
    SilenceAPI._activeCat = cat
    for n, t in pairs(Tabs) do
        t.btn.Visible = (TAB_CATEGORY[n] == cat)
    end
    for _, fn in ipairs(SilenceAPI._railPaint) do pcall(fn) end
end

local catButtons = {}
for i, cat in ipairs(CATEGORIES) do
    cat.order = i * 10
    catButtons[cat.key] = makeRailButton(cat)
end

local function addTab(name)
    local btn, paint = makeTabButton(name)
    btn.LayoutOrder = TAB_LO_INSIDE[name] or 100
    local page = makeTabPage()
    Tabs[name] = { btn = btn, page = page, paint = paint }
    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() selectTab(name) end)
    -- rows created from here on belong to this tab (used by the search box)
    SilenceAPI._currentTabName = name
    return page
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- RISKY TAB LOCK OVERLAY
----------------------------------------------------------------
-- One overlay, parented to Content, shown when the page you are looking at
-- is a risky one. The old build parented a lock Frame into each page, but a
-- page is a UIListLayout, so the "overlay" was laid out as an ordinary row
-- between the widgets instead of covering anything.
local riskyLocks = {}

local function refreshAcLocks()
    local lock = SilenceAPI._acLock
    if not lock or not lock.Parent then return end
    local t = Tabs[SilenceAPI._activeTab or ""]
    lock.Visible = (State.acBlocked and not State.acRiskyOverride)
                   and t ~= nil and riskyLocks[t.page] == true
end
SilenceAPI._refreshAcLocks = refreshAcLocks

local function lockTabIfRisky(page)
    riskyLocks[page] = true
    refreshAcLocks()
end

do
    local lock = Instance.new("Frame", Content)
    lock.Name = "__AcLock"
    -- matches a page exactly, including the 10px kept clear of the panel's
    -- rounded bottom-right corner
    lock.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, -64)
    lock.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 54)
    lock.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    lock.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12
    lock.BorderSizePixel = 0
    lock.Visible = false
    lock.ZIndex = 200
    lock.Active = true

    local badge = Instance.new("Frame", lock)
    badge.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    badge.Size = UDim2.new(0, 320, 0, 176)
    badge.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    badge.BorderSizePixel = 0
    badge.ZIndex = 201
    SilenceAPI._theme(badge, "BackgroundColor3", "bg2")
    corner(badge, 14)
    stroke(badge, Theme.bad, 1, 0.3)
    addAnimatedGlow(badge, { thickness = 2, transparency = 0.35, window = 0.18,
        color = Theme.bad })

    local title = Instance.new("TextLabel", badge)
    title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 24)
    title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 22)
    title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    title.Text = "FEATURES LOCKED"
    title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    title.TextSize = 17
    title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    title.ZIndex = 202
    SilenceAPI._theme(title, "TextColor3", "text")

    local body = Instance.new("TextLabel", badge)
    body.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 48)
    body.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 54)
    body.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    body.Text = "Anti-Cheat detected in this game. Enable RISKY on the AntiCheat tab to use these features anyway."
    body.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    body.TextSize = 12
    body.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    body.TextWrapped = true
    body.ZIndex = 202
    SilenceAPI._theme(body, "TextColor3", "textDim")

    local jumpBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", badge)
    jumpBtn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    jumpBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 190, 0, 34)
    jumpBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 116)
    jumpBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bad
    jumpBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    jumpBtn.Text = "OPEN ANTICHEAT TAB"
    jumpBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    jumpBtn.TextSize = 12
    jumpBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
    jumpBtn.ZIndex = 203
    corner(jumpBtn, 8)
    SilenceAPI._theme(jumpBtn, "TextColor3", "text")
    jumpBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(jumpBtn, SilenceAPI._twFade,
            { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 80, 80) }):Play()
    end)
    jumpBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(jumpBtn, SilenceAPI._twFade,
            { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bad }):Play()
    end)
    jumpBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() selectTab("AntiCheat") end)

    SilenceAPI._acLock = lock
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- WIDGETS
----------------------------------------------------------------
-- The registries themselves live near the top of the file; see there.
-- The five rail tiles repaint from Theme too, and they are built before the
-- widget factories, so they are pulled in through one shared entry.
table.insert(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers, function()
    for _, fn in ipairs(SilenceAPI._railPaint or {}) do pcall(fn) end
end)

-- every widget registers itself so the header search box can filter globally
function SilenceAPI._registerRow(inst, label)
    inst:SetAttribute("SilLabel", label)
    table.insert(SilenceAPI._searchRows,
        { inst = inst, label = string.lower(tostring(label)), tab = SilenceAPI._currentTabName })
    return inst
end

local function sectionLabel(parent,text)
    local row=Instance.new("Frame",parent)
    row.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,28); row.BackgroundTransparency=1
    row.BorderSizePixel=0
    -- the search box hides section headings by this attribute, so it has to
    -- sit on the object that is actually a direct child of the page
    row:SetAttribute("SilSection", true)

    local mark=Instance.new("Frame",row)
    mark.Size=UDim2.new(0,3,0,12); mark.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0.5,-6)
    mark.BorderSizePixel=0
    corner(mark,2)
    SilenceAPI._theme(mark,"BackgroundColor3","accent")

    local lbl=Instance.new("TextLabel",row)
    lbl.Size=UDim2.new(1,-14,1,0); lbl.Position=UDim2.new(0,13,0,0)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency=1
    lbl.Text=text; lbl.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; lbl.TextSize=11
    lbl.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    SilenceAPI._theme(lbl,"TextColor3","accent2")
    return row
end

SilenceAPI._twKnob = TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
SilenceAPI._twFade = TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)

local function toggleRow(parent,label,getter,setter)
    local row=Instance.new("Frame",parent)
    row.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,40)
    row.BorderSizePixel=0
    corner(row,9)
    SilenceAPI._registerRow(row, label)
    SilenceAPI._theme(row,"BackgroundColor3","bg2")
    local rs=stroke(row, Theme.bg4, 1, 0.5, "bg4")

    local l=Instance.new("TextLabel",row)
    l.Size=UDim2.new(1,-80,1,0); l.Position=UDim2.new(0,14,0,0)
    l.BackgroundTransparency=1
    l.Text=label; l.Font=Enum.Font.GothamMedium; l.TextSize=13
    l.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    SilenceAPI._theme(l,"TextColor3","text")

    -- the whole row is clickable, not just the little switch; the hit layer
    -- stops short of the switch so it never swallows its click
    local hit=Instance.new("TextButton",row)
    hit.Size=UDim2.new(1,-66,1,0); hit.BackgroundTransparency=1
    hit.Text=""; hit.AutoButtonColor=false

    local sw=Instance.new("TextButton",row)
    sw.Size=UDim2.new(0,46,0,24); sw.Position=UDim2.new(1,-60,0.5,-12)
    sw.BackgroundColor3=Theme.bg3; sw.BorderSizePixel=0; sw.Text=""
    sw.AutoButtonColor=false
    corner(sw,12)
    local knob=Instance.new("Frame",sw)
    knob.Size=UDim2.new(0,18,0,18); knob.Position=UDim2.new(0,3,0.5,-9)
    knob.BackgroundColor3=Theme.textDim
    knob.BorderSizePixel=0
    corner(knob,9)
    local glow = addAnimatedGlow(sw, { thickness=2, transparency=0.15, window=0.22 })
    glow.Enabled = false

    -- Both switch colours are written here rather than registered against a
    -- theme token, because they depend on the on/off state; _applyUiColors
    -- runs every refresher, so a recolour still reaches them.
    local function refresh()
        local on = getter() and true or false
        glow.Enabled = on
        TweenService:Create(sw, SilenceAPI._twKnob,
            { BackgroundColor3 = on and Theme.accent or Theme.bg3 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(knob, SilenceAPI._twKnob, {
            Position        = on and UDim2.new(0,25,0.5,-9) or UDim2.new(0,3,0.5,-9),
            BackgroundColor3 = on and Theme.bg or Theme.textDim,
        }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(rs, SilenceAPI._twFade, {
            Color        = on and Theme.accent or Theme.bg4,
            Transparency = on and 0.6 or 0.5,
        }):Play()
    end
    local function flip()
        setter(not getter()); refresh()
    end
    row.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(row, SilenceAPI._twFade, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg3 }):Play()
    end)
    row.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(row, SilenceAPI._twFade, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg2 }):Play()
    end)
    sw.MouseButton1Click:Connect(flip)
    hit.MouseButton1Click:Connect(flip)
    refresh()
    table.insert(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers, refresh)
    return row,refresh
end
local function buttonRow(parent,label,cb)
    local b=Instance.new("TextButton",parent)
    b.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,36)
    b.BorderSizePixel=0; b.Text=label
    b.Font=Enum.Font.GothamMedium; b.TextSize=13
    b.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    b.AutoButtonColor=false
    corner(b,9); padding(b,0,0,14,36)
    SilenceAPI._theme(b,"BackgroundColor3","bg2"); SilenceAPI._theme(b,"TextColor3","text")
    SilenceAPI._registerRow(b, label)
    local bs=stroke(b, Theme.bg4, 1, 0.5, "bg4")

    local arrow=Instance.new("TextLabel", b)
    arrow.Size=UDim2.new(0,18,1,0); arrow.Position=UDim2.new(1,-4,0,0)
    arrow.BackgroundTransparency=1
    arrow.Text=">"; arrow.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; arrow.TextSize=12
    SilenceAPI._theme(arrow,"TextColor3","textDim")

    b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(b,     SilenceAPI._twFade, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg3 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(bs,    SilenceAPI._twFade, { Color = Theme.accent, Transparency = 0.5 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(arrow, SilenceAPI._twFade,
            { Position = UDim2.new(1,2,0,0), TextColor3 = Theme.text }):Play()
    end)
    b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(b,     SilenceAPI._twFade, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg2 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(bs,    SilenceAPI._twFade, { Color = Theme.bg4, Transparency = 0.5 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(arrow, SilenceAPI._twFade,
            { Position = UDim2.new(1,-4,0,0), TextColor3 = Theme.textDim }):Play()
    end)
    b.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
        b.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg4
    end)
    b.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(b, SilenceAPI._twFade, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg3 }):Play()
    end)
    b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(cb)
    return b
end
local function inputRow(parent, label, default, cb, opts)
    opts = opts or {}
    local hasSlider = opts.min ~= nil and opts.max ~= nil

    local row = Instance.new("Frame", parent)
    row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, hasSlider and 60 or 40)
    row.BorderSizePixel = 0
    corner(row, 9)
    SilenceAPI._registerRow(row, label)
    SilenceAPI._theme(row,"BackgroundColor3","bg2")
    stroke(row, Theme.bg4, 1, 0.5, "bg4")

    local l = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
    l.Size = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 0, hasSlider and 22 or 40)
    l.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, hasSlider and 6 or 0)
    l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    l.Text = label
    l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    l.TextSize = 13
    l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    SilenceAPI._theme(l,"TextColor3","text")

    local box = Instance.new("TextBox", row)
    box.BorderSizePixel = 0
    box.Text = tostring(default or "")
    box.Font = Enum.Font.Code
    box.TextSize = 12
    box.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    corner(box, 7); padding(box, 0, 0, 9, 9)
    SilenceAPI._theme(box,"BackgroundColor3","bg3"); SilenceAPI._theme(box,"TextColor3","text")
    local boxStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", box)
    boxStroke.Color = Theme.border; boxStroke.Thickness = 1; boxStroke.Transparency = 0.5
    -- focus ring: the only way to tell which field is live on a dark sheet
    box.Focused:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(boxStroke, SilenceAPI._twFade,
            { Color = Theme.accent, Transparency = 0 }):Play()
    end)
    box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(boxStroke, SilenceAPI._twFade,
            { Color = Theme.border, Transparency = 0.5 }):Play()
    end)
    if hasSlider then
        box.Size = UDim2.new(0, 62, 0, 24)
        box.Position = UDim2.new(1, -76, 0, 5)
        box.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    else
        box.Size = UDim2.new(0.45, -14, 0, 26)
        box.Position = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 0.5, -13)
    end

    if not hasSlider then
        box.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter) if enter then cb(box.Text) end end)
        local function setText(v, fireCb)
            box.Text = tostring(v)
            if fireCb then cb(box.Text) end
        end
        return box, setText
    end

    -- named rail, not "track": a local called track here shadowed the
    -- connection tracker of the same name for the rest of the function
    local rail = Instance.new("Frame", row)
    rail.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 6)
    rail.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 1, -18)
    rail.BorderSizePixel = 0
    corner(rail, 3)
    SilenceAPI._theme(rail, "BackgroundColor3", "bg3")

    local fill = Instance.new("Frame", rail)
    fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    fill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
    corner(fill, 3)
    SilenceAPI._theme(fill, "BackgroundColor3", "accent")

    local thumb = Instance.new("Frame", rail)
    thumb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
    thumb.Position = UDim2.new(0, -7, 0.5, -7)
    thumb.BorderSizePixel = 0
    thumb.ZIndex = 2
    corner(thumb, 7)
    SilenceAPI._theme(thumb, "BackgroundColor3", "accent")
    stroke(thumb, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0), 2, 0.25)

    local function fmtNum(n)
        if opts.integer then return tostring(math.floor(n + 0.5)) end
        if math.abs(n - math.floor(n + 0.5)) < 0.005 then
            return tostring(math.floor(n + 0.5))
        end
        return string.format("%.2f", n)
    end

    local function setValue(n, fireCb)
        n = math.clamp(n, opts.min, opts.max)
        if opts.integer then n = math.floor(n + 0.5) end
        local frac = (opts.max == opts.min) and 0 or (n - opts.min) / (opts.max - opts.min)
        fill.Size = UDim2.new(frac, 0, 1, 0)
        thumb.Position = UDim2.new(frac, -7, 0.5, -7)
        if not box:IsFocused() then box.Text = fmtNum(n) end
        if fireCb then cb(tostring(n)) end
    end

    setValue(tonumber(default) or opts.min, false)

    box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        local n = tonumber(box.Text)
        if n then setValue(n, true) else setValue(tonumber(default) or opts.min, false) end
    end)

    local dragging = false
    local function pickFromX(absX)
        local w = rail.AbsoluteSize.X
        if w <= 0 then return end
        local rel = math.clamp((absX - rail.AbsolutePosition.X) / w, 0, 1)
        setValue(opts.min + rel * (opts.max - opts.min), true)
    end
    rail.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true; pickFromX(input.Position.X)
            TweenService:Create(thumb, SilenceAPI._twKnob,
                { Size = UDim2.new(0,18,0,18) }):Play()
        end
    end)
    rail.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = false
            TweenService:Create(thumb, SilenceAPI._twKnob,
                { Size = UDim2.new(0,14,0,14) }):Play()
        end
    end)
    SilenceAPI._onDrag(function(input)
        if dragging then pickFromX(input.Position.X) end
    end)

    return box, setValue
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- COLOR PICKER POPUP
----------------------------------------------------------------
local ColorPicker = { h = 0, s = 1, v = 1, callback = nil, built = false }

local function buildColorPicker()
    if ColorPicker.built then return end
    ColorPicker.built = true

    local cp = Instance.new("Frame", Gui)
    cp.Name = "ColorPicker"
    cp.Size = UDim2.new(0, 290, 0, 360)
    cp.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -145, 0.5, -180)
    cp.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg
    cp.BorderSizePixel = 0
    cp.Visible = false
    cp.Active = true
    cp.ZIndex = 100
    SilenceAPI._glass(cp, { radius = 16, transparency = 0.2, edge = 0.3,
                            glowThickness = 2 })
    SilenceAPI._blurWatch(cp)

    local hdr = Instance.new("Frame", cp)
    hdr.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34)
    hdr.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg
    hdr.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
    hdr.BorderSizePixel = 0
    hdr.ZIndex = 101
    hdr.Active = true
    corner(hdr, 16)
    local hdrSep = Instance.new("Frame", hdr)
    hdrSep.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 1)
    hdrSep.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -1)
    hdrSep.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg4
    hdrSep.BorderSizePixel = 0
    hdrSep.ZIndex = 102

    local title = Instance.new("TextLabel", hdr)
    title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0)
    title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 0)
    title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    title.Text = "COLOR PICKER"
    title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    title.TextSize = 13
    title.TextColor3 = Theme.text
    title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    title.ZIndex = 102

    local close = Instance.new("TextButton", hdr)
    close.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 26)
    close.Position = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 4)
    close.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    close.BorderSizePixel = 0
    close.Text = "X"
    close.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    close.TextSize = 13
    close.TextColor3 = Theme.text
    close.AutoButtonColor = false
    close.ZIndex = 102
    corner(close, 6); stroke(close, Theme.accent, 1, 0.5)
    close.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() cp.Visible = false end)

    makeHeaderDrag(cp, hdr)

    local body = Instance.new("Frame", cp)
    body.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -42)
    body.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 38)
    body.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    body.ZIndex = 101
    local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout", body)
    lay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
    lay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

    local previewRow = Instance.new("Frame", body)
    previewRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 38)
    previewRow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    previewRow.LayoutOrder = 1
    previewRow.ZIndex = 101

    local previewSwatch = Instance.new("Frame", previewRow)
    previewSwatch.Size = UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 38)
    previewSwatch.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    previewSwatch.BorderSizePixel = 0
    previewSwatch.ZIndex = 102
    corner(previewSwatch, 6); stroke(previewSwatch, Theme.text, 1, 0.4)

    local hexBox = Instance.new("TextBox", previewRow)
    hexBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -46, 0, 38)
    hexBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 0)
    hexBox.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg2
    hexBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
    hexBox.Font = Enum.Font.Code
    hexBox.TextSize = 16
    hexBox.TextColor3 = Theme.text
    hexBox.PlaceholderText = "#RRGGBB"
    hexBox.PlaceholderColor3 = Theme.textDim
    hexBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    hexBox.Text = "#FFFFFF"
    hexBox.ZIndex = 102
    corner(hexBox, 6); padding(hexBox, 0, 0, 12, 12)

    local function makeStrip(name, order)
        local container = Instance.new("Frame", body)
        container.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36)
        container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        container.LayoutOrder = order
        container.ZIndex = 101

        local lblL = Instance.new("TextLabel", container)
        lblL.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 14)
        lblL.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lblL.Text = name
        lblL.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        lblL.TextSize = 11
        lblL.TextColor3 = Theme.textDim
        lblL.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        lblL.ZIndex = 102

        local strip = Instance.new("Frame", container)
        strip.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18)
        strip.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 16)
        strip.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        strip.BorderSizePixel = 0
        strip.ZIndex = 102
        corner(strip, 4)
        local grad = Instance.new("UIGradient", strip)

        local thumb = Instance.new("Frame", strip)
        thumb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 4, 1, 8)
        thumb.Position = UDim2.new(0, -2, 0, -4)
        thumb.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        thumb.BorderSizePixel = 0
        thumb.ZIndex = 103
        stroke(thumb, Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 1, 0.3)
        corner(thumb, 2)

        return strip, thumb, grad
    end

    local hueStrip, hueThumb, hueGrad = makeStrip("Hue", 2)
    local satStrip, satThumb, satGrad = makeStrip("Saturation", 3)
    local valStrip, valThumb, valGrad = makeStrip("Brightness", 4)

    local hueKps = {}
    for i = 0, 12 do
        table.insert(hueKps, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(i/12, Color3.fromHSV(i/12, 1, 1)))
    end
    hueGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new(hueKps)

    local presetRow = Instance.new("Frame", body)
    presetRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44)
    presetRow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    presetRow.LayoutOrder = 5
    presetRow.ZIndex = 101
    local pLay = Instance.new("UIGridLayout", presetRow)
    pLay.CellSize = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 20)
    pLay.CellPadding = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 4)
    pLay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

    local presets = {
        Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200),
        Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 120), Theme.bg4,
        Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),       Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60),
        Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 30),  Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60),
        Color3.fromRGB(60, 230, 90),   Color3.fromRGB(60, 200, 255),
        Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 255),   Color3.fromRGB(180, 80, 255),
        Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 200),  Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 60),
    }
    local presetButtons = {}
    for i, c in ipairs(presets) do
        local b = Instance.new("TextButton", presetRow)
        b.BackgroundColor3 = c
        b.Text = ""
        b.AutoButtonColor = false
        b.BorderSizePixel = 0
        b.LayoutOrder = i
        b.ZIndex = 102
        corner(b, 4); stroke(b, Theme.text, 1, 0.6)
        presetButtons[i] = { btn = b, color = c }
    end

    function ColorPicker.update(silent)
        local c = Color3.fromHSV(ColorPicker.h, ColorPicker.s, ColorPicker.v)
        previewSwatch.BackgroundColor3 = c
        if not hexBox:IsFocused() then
            hexBox.Text = string.format("#%02X%02X%02X",
                math.floor(c.R*255+0.5),
                math.floor(c.G*255+0.5),
                math.floor(c.B*255+0.5))
        end
        hueThumb.Position = UDim2.new(ColorPicker.h, -2, 0, -4)
        satThumb.Position = UDim2.new(ColorPicker.s, -2, 0, -4)
        valThumb.Position = UDim2.new(ColorPicker.v, -2, 0, -4)
        satGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new(
            Color3.fromHSV(ColorPicker.h, 0, 1),
            Color3.fromHSV(ColorPicker.h, 1, 1))
        valGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new(
            Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
            Color3.fromHSV(ColorPicker.h, math.max(ColorPicker.s, 0.001), 1))
        if not silent and ColorPicker.callback then ColorPicker.callback(c) end
    end

    local function bindStrip(strip, setVal)
        local dragging = false
        local function pick(absX)
            local rel = math.clamp((absX - strip.AbsolutePosition.X) / strip.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1)
            setVal(rel); ColorPicker.update()
        end
        strip.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true; pick(input.Position.X)
            end
        end)
        strip.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = false
            end
        end)
        SilenceAPI._onDrag(function(input)
            if dragging then pick(input.Position.X) end
        end)
    end
    bindStrip(hueStrip, function(v)
        ColorPicker.h = v
        -- White has saturation 0 and black has brightness 0. In both cases the
        -- hue strip changes a value nothing can see, which reads as a broken
        -- slider. Lift them so dragging hue always does something visible.
        if ColorPicker.s < 0.05 then ColorPicker.s = 1 end
        if ColorPicker.v < 0.05 then ColorPicker.v = 1 end
    end)
    bindStrip(satStrip, function(v)
        ColorPicker.s = v
        if ColorPicker.v < 0.05 then ColorPicker.v = 1 end
    end)
    bindStrip(valStrip, function(v) ColorPicker.v = v end)

    for _, p in ipairs(presetButtons) do
        p.btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            local h, s, v = Color3.toHSV(p.color)
            ColorPicker.h, ColorPicker.s, ColorPicker.v = h, s, v
            ColorPicker.update()
        end)
    end

    hexBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        local s = (hexBox.Text or ""):gsub("#",""):gsub("%s","")
        if #s == 3 then
            s = s:sub(1,1)..s:sub(1,1)..s:sub(2,2)..s:sub(2,2)..s:sub(3,3)..s:sub(3,3)
        end
        if #s == 6 then
            local r = tonumber(s:sub(1,2), 16)
            local g = tonumber(s:sub(3,4), 16)
            local b = tonumber(s:sub(5,6), 16)
            if r and g and b then
                local h, ss, v = Color3.toHSV(Color3.fromRGB(r, g, b))
                ColorPicker.h, ColorPicker.s, ColorPicker.v = h, ss, v
            end
        end
        ColorPicker.update()
    end)

    ColorPicker.frame = cp
    ColorPicker.titleLbl = title
end

local function openColorPicker(initial, onChange, what)
    buildColorPicker()
    ColorPicker.callback = onChange
    if ColorPicker.titleLbl then
        ColorPicker.titleLbl.Text = what and ("COLOR: " .. string.upper(what)) or "COLOR PICKER"
    end
    local h, s, v = Color3.toHSV(initial or Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
    ColorPicker.h, ColorPicker.s, ColorPicker.v = h, s, v
    ColorPicker.frame.Visible = true
    ColorPicker.update(true)
end

local function hexColorPickerRow(parent, label, initial, onChange)
    -- Colour rows are used both on pages (ZIndex 1) and inside the tag popup
    -- (ZIndex 50+), so they take their layer from whatever holds them rather
    -- than hard-coding one that punches through the page header on a page.
    local z = (parent.ZIndex or 1) + 1

    local row = Instance.new("Frame", parent)
    row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
    row.BorderSizePixel = 0
    row.ZIndex = z
    corner(row, 9)
    SilenceAPI._theme(row, "BackgroundColor3", "bg2")
    local rs = stroke(row, Theme.bg4, 1, 0.5, "bg4")
    SilenceAPI._registerRow(row, label)

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -90, 1, 0)
    lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 0)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = label
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    lbl.TextSize = 12
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    lbl.ZIndex = z + 1
    SilenceAPI._theme(lbl, "TextColor3", "text")

    local swatch = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
    swatch.Size = UDim2.new(0, 62, 0, 24)
    swatch.Position = UDim2.new(1, -76, 0.5, -12)
    swatch.BackgroundColor3 = initial
    swatch.BorderSizePixel = 0
    swatch.AutoButtonColor = false
    swatch.Text = ""
    swatch.ZIndex = z + 1
    corner(swatch, 7)
    local ss = stroke(swatch, Theme.text, 1, 0.5, "text")

    row.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(row, SilenceAPI._twFade, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg3 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(rs,  SilenceAPI._twFade, { Color = Theme.accent, Transparency = 0.5 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(ss,  SilenceAPI._twFade, { Transparency = 0 }):Play()
    end)
    row.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(row, SilenceAPI._twFade, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg2 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(rs,  SilenceAPI._twFade, { Color = Theme.bg4, Transparency = 0.5 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(ss,  SilenceAPI._twFade, { Transparency = 0.5 }):Play()
    end)

    swatch.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        openColorPicker(swatch.BackgroundColor3, function(c)
            swatch.BackgroundColor3 = c
            onChange(c)
        end, label)
    end)

    return row
end

-- TAB: IMPORTANT

;(function()
local p = addTab("IMPORTANT")
sectionLabel(p, "SILENCE ACCESS PASS")

local card = Instance.new("Frame", p)
card.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 190)
card.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg2
card.BorderSizePixel = 0
corner(card, 10)
addAnimatedGlow(card, { thickness=2, transparency=0.3, window=0.18 })
padding(card, 14, 14, 14, 14)
local cardLay = Instance.new("UIListLayout", card)
cardLay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
cardLay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

local title = Instance.new("TextLabel", card)
title.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22)
title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
title.Text = "Silence Access Pass"
title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
title.TextSize = 16
title.TextColor3 = Theme.text
title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
title.LayoutOrder = 1

local statusLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", card)
statusLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18)
statusLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
statusLbl.Text = "Status: checking..."
statusLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
statusLbl.TextSize = 12
statusLbl.TextColor3 = Theme.textDim
statusLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
statusLbl.LayoutOrder = 2

local desc = Instance.new("TextLabel", card)
desc.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32)
desc.BackgroundTransparency = 1
desc.Text = "Required to be recognized as a Silence user."
desc.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
desc.TextSize = 11
desc.TextColor3 = Theme.textDim
desc.TextWrapped = true
desc.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
desc.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
desc.LayoutOrder = 3

local buyBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", card)
buyBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
buyBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg2
buyBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
buyBtn.Text = "BUY ACCESS PASS"
buyBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
buyBtn.TextSize = 13
buyBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.text
buyBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
buyBtn.LayoutOrder = 4
corner(buyBtn, 6); stroke(buyBtn, Theme.accent, 1, 0.5)
addAnimatedGlow(buyBtn, { thickness=2, transparency=0.2, window=0.2 })

local function refreshStatus()
    if SCRIPT_PASS_ID == 0 then
        statusLbl.Text = "Status: NOT CONFIGURED"
        statusLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 120, 120)
        buyBtn.Text = "PASS NOT CONFIGURED"
        return
    end
    local ok, owns = pcall(function()
        return MarketplaceService:UserOwnsGamePassAsync(LocalPlayer.UserId, SCRIPT_PASS_ID)
    end)
    if ok and owns then
        statusLbl.Text = "Status: OWNED"
        statusLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 220, 140)
        buyBtn.Text = "ALREADY OWNED"
    else
        statusLbl.Text = "Status: NOT OWNED"
        statusLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 130, 130)
        buyBtn.Text = "BUY ACCESS PASS"
    end
end

buyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if SCRIPT_PASS_ID == 0 then
        notify("Pass", "Pass ID not configured", 3)
        return
    end
    local function tryPrompt(svc, method, ...)
        local fn = svc and svc[method]
        if type(fn) ~= "function" then return false, "no method" end
        local args = {...}
        return pcall(function() return fn(svc, table.unpack(args)) end)
    end
    local passUrl = "https://www.roblox.com/game-pass/" .. SCRIPT_PASS_ID

    local ok = tryPrompt(MarketplaceService, "PromptGamePassPurchase", LocalPlayer, SCRIPT_PASS_ID)
    if ok then return end

    ok = tryPrompt(MarketplaceService, "PromptPurchase", LocalPlayer, SCRIPT_PASS_ID)
    if ok then return end

    local copied = false
    pcall(function() setclipboard(passUrl); copied = true end)
    if not copied then
        pcall(function() (toclipboard or writeclipboard)(passUrl); copied = true end)
    end
    if copied then
        notify("Pass", "Executor blocked prompt - URL copied to clipboard. Paste in browser.", 6)
    else
        notify("Pass", "Open this URL in browser:  " .. passUrl, 8)
    end
end)

task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(0.5); refreshStatus()
end)

track(MarketplaceService.PromptGamePassPurchaseFinished:Connect(function(player, passId, purchased)
    if player == LocalPlayer and passId == SCRIPT_PASS_ID then
        task.spawn(function() task.wait(1); refreshStatus() end)
        if purchased then notify("Silence", "Access Pass purchased", 3) end
    end
end))
end)()

-- TAB: PLAYER

;(function()
local p = addTab("Player")
sectionLabel(p,"MOVEMENT")
toggleRow(p,"Fly",            function() return State.flying end, function(v) if v then startFly() else stopFly() end end)
toggleRow(p,"Infinite Jump",  function() return State.infJump end, function(v) State.infJump=v end)
toggleRow(p,"Noclip",         function() return State.noclip end, function(v) SilenceAPI._setNoclip(v) end)
inputRow(p,"Walk Speed", 16, function(v) setWS(tonumber(v) or 16) end,
    {min=0, max=200, integer=true})
inputRow(p,"Jump Power", 50, function(v) setJP(tonumber(v) or 50) end,
    {min=0, max=500, integer=true})

    {min=0, max=300, integer=true})
sectionLabel(p,"BODY")
toggleRow(p,"God Mode",          function() return State.godmode end, setGod)

    function() return State.antiVoid end, function(v) SilenceAPI._setAntiVoid(v) end)

    {min=5, max=200, integer=true})

    {min=10, max=120, integer=true})

    local goal = State.espOn and (espInner.AbsoluteSize.Y > 0 and espInner.AbsoluteSize.Y or 460) or 0
    TweenService:Create(espExpand, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad),
        { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, goal) }):Play()
end
-- Rows in here are searchable, but the group is zero-height while Player ESP

-- as long as a search is running.

    if searching then
        espExpand.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0,
            espInner.AbsoluteSize.Y > 0 and espInner.AbsoluteSize.Y or 460)
    else
        updateEspExpand()
    end
end)

    for _,set in pairs(State.espObjects) do
        if set.box then set.box.Enabled = State.espOn and v end
    end
end)

    State.espHealthBar = v
    for _,set in pairs(State.espObjects) do
        if set.hbBg then set.hbBg.Visible = State.espOn and v end
    end
end)

    State.espTeamCheck = v; applyColors()

    State.colors.espFriendly = c; applyColors()
end)

    State.colors.espEnemy = c; applyColors()
end)

    State.colors.espNeutral = c; applyColors()

lockTabIfRisky(p)

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- TAB: FPS BOOST
----------------------------------------------------------------
local restoreFpsBoost
;(function()
local pFPS = addTab("FPS Boost")

local fpsOrig = {
    shadows   = Lighting.GlobalShadows,
    fogEnd    = Lighting.FogEnd,
    fogStart  = Lighting.FogStart,
    waterWave = Workspace.Terrain.WaterWaveSize,
    waterRefl = Workspace.Terrain.WaterReflectance,
    waterTrns = Workspace.Terrain.WaterTransparency,
}

local function setFx(on, classNames)
    for _, item in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if classNames[item.ClassName] then
            pcall(function() item.Enabled = not on end)
        end
    end
end

local FX_CLASSES = {
    ParticleEmitter=true, Smoke=true, Fire=true, Sparkles=true, Trail=true, Beam=true,
}

local fpsBoostActive = false
local fpsSnapshot    = nil

local function applyFpsBoost()
    if fpsBoostActive then return end
    fpsBoostActive = true
    fpsSnapshot = {
        lighting = {
            GlobalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows,
            FogEnd        = Lighting.FogEnd,
            FogStart      = Lighting.FogStart,
        },
        terrain = {
            WaterWaveSize     = Workspace.Terrain.WaterWaveSize,
            WaterWaveSpeed    = Workspace.Terrain.WaterWaveSpeed,
            WaterReflectance  = Workspace.Terrain.WaterReflectance,
            WaterTransparency = Workspace.Terrain.WaterTransparency,
        },
        parts={}, effects={}, postEffects={}, atmospheres={}, decals={},
        quality=nil,
    }
    pcall(function() Lighting.GlobalShadows = false end)
    pcall(function() Lighting.FogEnd = 9e9; Lighting.FogStart = 9e9 end)
    pcall(function()
        Workspace.Terrain.WaterWaveSize=0; Workspace.Terrain.WaterWaveSpeed=0
        Workspace.Terrain.WaterReflectance=0; Workspace.Terrain.WaterTransparency=1
    end)
    for _,e in ipairs(Lighting:GetDescendants()) do
        if e:IsA("PostEffect") then
            fpsSnapshot.postEffects[e] = e.Enabled
            pcall(function() e.Enabled = false end)
        elseif e:IsA("Atmosphere") then
            fpsSnapshot.atmospheres[e] = e.Enabled
            pcall(function() e.Enabled = false end)
        end
    end
    for _,item in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if FX_CLASSES[item.ClassName] then
            fpsSnapshot.effects[item] = item.Enabled
            pcall(function() item.Enabled = false end)
        elseif item:IsA("BasePart") then
            local s = { Material = item.Material, Reflectance = item.Reflectance }
            if item:IsA("MeshPart") then s.RenderFidelity = item.RenderFidelity end
            fpsSnapshot.parts[item] = s
            pcall(function()
                item.Material = Enum.Material.Plastic
                item.Reflectance = 0
                if item:IsA("MeshPart") then item.RenderFidelity = Enum.RenderFidelity.Performance end
            end)
        elseif item:IsA("Decal") or item:IsA("Texture") then
            fpsSnapshot.decals[item] = item.Transparency
            pcall(function() item.Transparency = 1 end)
        end
    end
    pcall(function()
        local us = UserSettings():GetService("UserGameSettings")
        fpsSnapshot.quality = us.SavedQualityLevel
        us.SavedQualityLevel = Enum.SavedQualitySetting.QualityLevel1
    end)
    print("[Silence] MAX FPS BOOST applied - snapshot taken for restore")
end

function restoreFpsBoost()
    if not fpsBoostActive or not fpsSnapshot then return end
    fpsBoostActive = false
    pcall(function()
        Lighting.GlobalShadows = fpsSnapshot.lighting.GlobalShadows
        Lighting.FogEnd        = fpsSnapshot.lighting.FogEnd
        Lighting.FogStart      = fpsSnapshot.lighting.FogStart
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local t = Workspace.Terrain
        t.WaterWaveSize     = fpsSnapshot.terrain.WaterWaveSize
        t.WaterWaveSpeed    = fpsSnapshot.terrain.WaterWaveSpeed
        t.WaterReflectance  = fpsSnapshot.terrain.WaterReflectance
        t.WaterTransparency = fpsSnapshot.terrain.WaterTransparency
    end)
    for e, en in pairs(fpsSnapshot.postEffects) do
        if e and e.Parent then pcall(function() e.Enabled = en end) end
    end
    for e, en in pairs(fpsSnapshot.atmospheres) do
        if e and e.Parent then pcall(function() e.Enabled = en end) end
    end
    for item, en in pairs(fpsSnapshot.effects) do
        if item and item.Parent then pcall(function() item.Enabled = en end) end
    end
    for item, s in pairs(fpsSnapshot.parts) do
        if item and item.Parent then
            pcall(function()
                item.Material    = s.Material
                item.Reflectance = s.Reflectance
                if s.RenderFidelity then item.RenderFidelity = s.RenderFidelity end
            end)
        end
    end
    for d, tr in pairs(fpsSnapshot.decals) do
        if d and d.Parent then pcall(function() d.Transparency = tr end) end
    end
    pcall(function()
        if fpsSnapshot.quality then
            UserSettings():GetService("UserGameSettings").SavedQualityLevel = fpsSnapshot.quality
        end
    end)
    fpsSnapshot = nil
    print("[Silence] MAX FPS BOOST restored - original graphics back")
end

sectionLabel(pFPS, "PROFILES  (one-click)")
-- Forward declarations. The profile buttons below close over these, but the
-- bodies are defined further down - without this they compile as globals and
-- every profile button dies with "attempt to call a nil value".
local fpsState = { shadows=false, fog=false, particles=false, postFx=false, atmosphere=false, flatWater=false }
local fpsRefreshers = {}
local setShadowsOff, setFogOff, setParticlesOff, setPostFxOff, setAtmosphereOff, setFlatWaterOff

local function profileBtn(label, applyFn)
    local b = Instance.new("TextButton", pFPS)
    b.Size = UDim2.new(0.333, -6, 0, 36)
    b.BorderSizePixel = 0
    b.Text = label
    b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    b.TextSize = 12
    b.AutoButtonColor = false
    b.LayoutOrder = 1
    corner(b, 9); stroke(b, Theme.accent, 1, 0.5)
    SilenceAPI._theme(b, "BackgroundColor3", "bg2")
    SilenceAPI._theme(b, "TextColor3", "text")
    b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(b, SilenceAPI._twFade, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg3 }):Play()
    end)
    b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(b, SilenceAPI._twFade, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg2 }):Play()
    end)
    b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        applyFn()
        for _,r in ipairs(fpsRefreshers) do pcall(r) end
        notify("FPS Boost", label .. " applied", 2)
    end)
    return b
end

local profileRow = Instance.new("Frame", pFPS)
profileRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36)
profileRow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
profileRow.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
profileRow.LayoutOrder = 2
local prLay = Instance.new("UIListLayout", profileRow)
prLay.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
prLay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)

profileBtn("Performance", function()
    setShadowsOff(true); setFogOff(true); setParticlesOff(true)
    setPostFxOff(true); setAtmosphereOff(true); setFlatWaterOff(true)
end).Parent = profileRow
profileBtn("Balanced", function()
    setShadowsOff(true); setFogOff(false); setParticlesOff(true)
    setPostFxOff(true); setAtmosphereOff(true); setFlatWaterOff(false)
end).Parent = profileRow
profileBtn("Quality", function()
    setShadowsOff(false); setFogOff(false); setParticlesOff(false)
    setPostFxOff(false); setAtmosphereOff(false); setFlatWaterOff(false)
end).Parent = profileRow

sectionLabel(pFPS, "INDIVIDUAL TOGGLES")

function setShadowsOff(v)
    fpsState.shadows = v
    pcall(function() Lighting.GlobalShadows = not v end)
end
function setFogOff(v)
    fpsState.fog = v
    pcall(function()
        if v then Lighting.FogEnd = 9e9; Lighting.FogStart = 9e9
        else Lighting.FogEnd = fpsOrig.fogEnd; Lighting.FogStart = fpsOrig.fogStart end
    end)
end
function setParticlesOff(v)
    fpsState.particles = v
    setFx(v, FX_CLASSES)
end
function setPostFxOff(v)
    fpsState.postFx = v
    for _,e in ipairs(Lighting:GetDescendants()) do
        if e:IsA("PostEffect") then pcall(function() e.Enabled = not v end) end
    end
end
function setAtmosphereOff(v)
    fpsState.atmosphere = v
    for _,a in ipairs(Lighting:GetDescendants()) do
        if a:IsA("Atmosphere") then pcall(function() a.Enabled = not v end) end
    end
end
function setFlatWaterOff(v)
    fpsState.flatWater = v
    pcall(function()
        if v then
            Workspace.Terrain.WaterWaveSize = 0
            Workspace.Terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
            Workspace.Terrain.WaterReflectance = 0
        else
            Workspace.Terrain.WaterWaveSize = fpsOrig.waterWave
            Workspace.Terrain.WaterReflectance = fpsOrig.waterRefl
        end
    end)
end

local function addFpsToggle(label, getter, setter)
    local _, refresh = toggleRow(pFPS, label, getter, setter)
    table.insert(fpsRefreshers, refresh)
end

addFpsToggle("Disable Shadows",                  function() return fpsState.shadows    end, setShadowsOff)
addFpsToggle("Disable Fog",                      function() return fpsState.fog        end, setFogOff)
addFpsToggle("Disable Particles & FX",           function() return fpsState.particles  end, setParticlesOff)
addFpsToggle("Disable Post-Processing",          function() return fpsState.postFx     end, setPostFxOff)
addFpsToggle("Disable Atmosphere",               function() return fpsState.atmosphere end, setAtmosphereOff)
addFpsToggle("Flat Water (no waves/reflect)",    function() return fpsState.flatWater  end, setFlatWaterOff)

sectionLabel(pFPS, "ONE-SHOT ACTIONS")
buttonRow(pFPS, "Plastic Material All Parts", function()
    local n = 0
    for _,item in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if item:IsA("BasePart") then
            pcall(function() item.Material = Enum.Material.Plastic end); n = n + 1
        end
    end
    notify("FPS Boost", n.." parts -> Plastic", 3)
end)
buttonRow(pFPS, "Hide All Decals & Textures", function()
    local n = 0
    for _,item in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if item:IsA("Decal") or item:IsA("Texture") then
            pcall(function() item.Transparency = 1 end); n = n + 1
        end
    end
    notify("FPS Boost", n.." decals hidden", 3)
end)
buttonRow(pFPS, "Set Render Quality LOW (Level 1)", function()
    pcall(function()
        UserSettings():GetService("UserGameSettings").SavedQualityLevel = Enum.SavedQualitySetting.QualityLevel1
    end)
end)
buttonRow(pFPS, "Restore Render Quality (Auto)", function()
    pcall(function()
        UserSettings():GetService("UserGameSettings").SavedQualityLevel = Enum.SavedQualitySetting.Automatic
    end)
end)

end)()

    if on then startTrolling("siton", tgt()) else stopTrolling() end
end)

    if on then startTrolling("orbit", tgt()) else stopTrolling() end
end)
toggleRow(p, "Follow Target", function() return trollMode=="follow" end, function(on)
    if on then startTrolling("follow", tgt()) else stopTrolling() end
end)

    if on then startTrolling("stick", tgt()) else stopTrolling() end
end)
toggleRow(p, "Random Wobble", function() return trollMode=="spam" end, function(on)
    if on then startTrolling("spam", tgt()) else stopTrolling() end
end)

local stopAllBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", p)
stopAllBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,42); stopAllBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180,50,90)

    local t = tgt(); if t then dashAt(t) else notify("Troll","No target") end
end)
buttonRow(p, "Teleport To Target", function()
    local t = tgt()
    if t and t.Character then
        local hrp = getHRP()
        local x = t.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hrp and x then hrp.CFrame = x.CFrame + Vector3.new(0,3,0) end
    else notify("Troll","No target") end
end)
buttonRow(p, "Spectate / Unspectate Target", function()
    local t = tgt()
    if State.spectateTgt and (not t or State.spectateTgt == t) then
        setSpectate(nil); notify("Troll","Unspectated", 2)
    elseif t then setSpectate(t); notify("Troll","Spectating "..t.Name, 2)
    else notify("Troll","No target", 2) end
end)
lockTabIfRisky(p)
end)()

        if p ~= LocalPlayer then table.insert(list, p) end
    end

    copyPlayerSkin(list[math.random(1,#list)])
end)

buttonRow(pSkins, "SAVE TYPED NAME GLOBALLY", function()
    local p = findPlayer(skinSearch.Text)
    if p then saveSkinGlobally(p) else notify("Skin","Player not found",3) end
end)
buttonRow(pSkins, "SAVE CURRENTLY-COPIED GLOBALLY", function()
    if currentSkinPlayer then saveSkinGlobally(currentSkinPlayer)
    else notify("Skin","Copy a skin first",3) end
end)

sectionLabel(pSkins, "ONLINE PLAYERS")
local skinList = Instance.new("Frame", pSkins)
skinList.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,0); skinList.BackgroundTransparency = 1
skinList.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
local skinLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", skinList)
skinLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0,4); skinLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

local function refreshSkinList()
    for _,c in ipairs(skinList:GetChildren()) do
        if c:IsA("TextButton") then c:Destroy() end
    end
    for _,p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= LocalPlayer then
            local b = Instance.new("TextButton", skinList)
            b.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,32); b.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg2
            b.BorderSizePixel = 0
            b.Text = "  "..p.DisplayName.."  (@"..p.Name..")"
            b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium; b.TextSize = 12
            b.TextColor3 = Theme.text
            b.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            b.AutoButtonColor = false
            corner(b,6)
            b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(40,40,40)}):Play()
            end)
            b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundColor3=Theme.bg2}):Play()
            end)
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() copyPlayerSkin(p) end)
        end
    end
end
refreshSkinList()
track(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(refreshSkinList))
track(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(refreshSkinList))

local lastSkinUpdate = 0
track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if not wearingLbl or not wearingLbl.Parent then return end
    if tick() - lastSkinUpdate < 0.5 then return end
    lastSkinUpdate = tick()
    pcall(function()
        if currentSkinPlayer then
            wearingLbl.Text = "  Wearing: "..currentSkinPlayer.Name
            wearingLbl.TextColor3 = Theme.accent2
        else
            wearingLbl.Text = "  Original (your avatar)"
            wearingLbl.TextColor3 = Theme.text
        end
    end)
end))

lockTabIfRisky(pSkins)
end)()

SilenceAPI._slotUI = {}

-- One compact line per slot: name, asset id, which body part it welds to, and
-- its own switch. Hand-built instead of stacking three generic widgets so a
-- slot stays a single row.
local PART_SHORT = { Head = "HEAD", Torso = "TORSO", RightLeg = "R.LEG", LeftLeg = "L.LEG" }
local function nextSlotPart(cur)
    local order = SilenceAPI._slotPartOrder
    for i, p in ipairs(order) do
        if p == cur then return order[(i % #order) + 1] end
    end
    return order[1]
end

local function slotRow(i)
    local sl = State.accSlots[i]
    local row = Instance.new("Frame", pAv)
    row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
    row.BorderSizePixel = 0
    corner(row, 9)
    SilenceAPI._theme(row, "BackgroundColor3", "bg2")
    stroke(row, Theme.bg4, 1, 0.5, "bg4")
    SilenceAPI._registerRow(row, sl.name .. " accessory slot")

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 74, 1, 0); lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 0)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = sl.name
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium; lbl.TextSize = 12
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    SilenceAPI._theme(lbl, "TextColor3", "text")

    local idBox = Instance.new("TextBox", row)
    idBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 104, 0, 24); idBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 92, 0.5, -12)
    idBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
    idBox.Text = tostring(sl.id or "")
    idBox.PlaceholderText = "asset id"
    idBox.PlaceholderColor3 = Theme.textDim
    idBox.Font = Enum.Font.Code; idBox.TextSize = 11
    idBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    corner(idBox, 4); padding(idBox, 0, 0, 6, 6)
    SilenceAPI._theme(idBox, "BackgroundColor3", "bg3")
    SilenceAPI._theme(idBox, "TextColor3", "text")
    idBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        SilenceAPI._setSlotId(i, idBox.Text)
        idBox.Text = tostring(State.accSlots[i].id or "")
    end)

    local partBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
    partBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 58, 0, 24); partBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 204, 0.5, -12)
    partBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    partBtn.Text = PART_SHORT[sl.part] or "HEAD"
    partBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; partBtn.TextSize = 10
    partBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
    corner(partBtn, 4); stroke(partBtn, Theme.accent, 1, 0.6)
    SilenceAPI._theme(partBtn, "BackgroundColor3", "bg3")
    SilenceAPI._theme(partBtn, "TextColor3", "text")
    partBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        SilenceAPI._setSlotPart(i, nextSlotPart(State.accSlots[i].part))
        partBtn.Text = PART_SHORT[State.accSlots[i].part] or "HEAD"
    end)

    -- HIDE blanks the body part underneath, which is what makes a Korblox
    -- leg look like a replacement rather than a second leg on top
    local hideBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
    hideBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 24); hideBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 268, 0.5, -12)
    hideBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    hideBtn.Text = "HIDE"
    hideBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; hideBtn.TextSize = 9
    hideBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
    corner(hideBtn, 4)
    local hideStroke = stroke(hideBtn, Theme.accent, 1, 0.6)
    SilenceAPI._theme(hideBtn, "BackgroundColor3", "bg3")
    local function refreshHide()
        local on = State.accSlots[i].hide
        hideBtn.TextColor3 = on and Theme.text or Theme.textDim
        hideStroke.Transparency = on and 0 or 0.75
    end
    hideBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        SilenceAPI._setSlotHide(i, not State.accSlots[i].hide)
        refreshHide()
    end)
    refreshHide()

    local sw = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
    sw.Size = UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 24); sw.Position = UDim2.new(1, -60, 0.5, -12)
    sw.BorderSizePixel = 0; sw.Text = ""
    sw.AutoButtonColor = false
    corner(sw, 12)
    local knob = Instance.new("Frame", sw)
    knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18); knob.Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, -9)
    knob.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.textDim
    knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
    corner(knob, 9)
    local glow = addAnimatedGlow(sw, { thickness = 2, transparency = 0.15, window = 0.22 })
    glow.Enabled = false

    local function refresh()
        local on = State.accSlots[i].on
        glow.Enabled = on
        TweenService:Create(sw, SilenceAPI._twKnob,
            { BackgroundColor3 = on and Theme.accent or Theme.bg3 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(knob, SilenceAPI._twKnob, {
            Position         = on and UDim2.new(0,25,0.5,-9) or UDim2.new(0,3,0.5,-9),
            BackgroundColor3 = on and Theme.bg or Theme.textDim,
        }):Play()
        if not idBox:IsFocused() then idBox.Text = tostring(State.accSlots[i].id or "") end
        partBtn.Text = PART_SHORT[State.accSlots[i].part] or "HEAD"
        refreshHide()
    end
    sw.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        SilenceAPI._setSlot(i, not State.accSlots[i].on)
        refresh()
    end)
    refresh()
    table.insert(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers, refresh)
    SilenceAPI._slotUI[i] = refresh
    return row
end

for i = 1, #State.accSlots do slotRow(i) end

buttonRow(pAv, "All Slots ON", function()
    local n = 0
    for i = 1, #State.accSlots do
        if tostring(State.accSlots[i].id or "") ~= "" then

end)

    for _, r in ipairs(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers) do pcall(r) end
end)

local slotHint = Instance.new("TextLabel", pAv)
