----------------------------------------------------------------
-- TAB: ANTICHEAT
----------------------------------------------------------------
;(function()
local pAC = addTab("AntiCheat")

sectionLabel(pAC, "SCANNER")

local resultBox = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame", pAC)
resultBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 240)
resultBox.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg2
resultBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
resultBox.ScrollBarThickness = 4
resultBox.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.accent
resultBox.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
resultBox.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
resultBox.LayoutOrder = 100
corner(resultBox, 6)
local rLay = Instance.new("UIListLayout", resultBox)
rLay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 2)
local rPad = Instance.new("UIPadding", resultBox)
rPad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6); rPad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6)
rPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8); rPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8)

local function addResult(text, color)
    local l = Instance.new("TextLabel", resultBox)
    l.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    l.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    l.Text = text
    l.Font = Enum.Font.Code
    l.TextSize = 11
    l.TextColor3 = color or Theme.text
    l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    l.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
    l.TextWrapped = true
    return l
end

local function clearResults()
    for _, c in ipairs(resultBox:GetChildren()) do
        if c:IsA("TextLabel") then c:Destroy() end
    end
end

local AC_PATTERNS = {
    "anticheat", "anti%-cheat", "antiexploit", "anti%-exploit",
    "antihack", "anti%-hack", "antiabuse",
    "sanction", "shieldsdk", "shield", "guardian", "watchdog",
    "vesteria", "wally", "kohls",
    "report", "moderation", "moderator", "admin%w*panel",
    "logevent", "abuselog", "playerlog",
    "kick", "ban[^k]", "warn[^t]", "punish", "flag",
    "exploit", "hack", "cheater", "cheat",
    "speedcheck", "speedlimit", "noflyzone", "noclipcheck",
    "positioncheck", "teleportcheck",
}

local CHECK_LOCATIONS = {
    "ServerScriptService", "ReplicatedStorage", "ReplicatedFirst",
    "StarterPlayer", "Workspace", "Lighting", "Chat", "Players",
}

local function matchesAC(name)
    if not name or name == "" then return nil end
    local low = string.lower(name)
    for _, pat in ipairs(AC_PATTERNS) do
        if string.find(low, pat) then return pat end
    end
    return nil
end

local function scanContainer(root, hits, maxResults)
    local ok, list = pcall(function() return root:GetDescendants() end)
    if not ok or not list then return end
    local count = 0
    for _, inst in ipairs(list) do
        count = count + 1
        if count % 100 == 0 then task.wait() end
        if #hits >= maxResults then return end
        local cls = inst.ClassName
        if cls == "Script" or cls == "LocalScript" or cls == "ModuleScript"
           or cls == "RemoteEvent" or cls == "RemoteFunction"
           or cls == "BindableEvent" or cls == "BindableFunction"
           or cls == "ScreenGui" or cls == "Folder" then
            -- loop-body scope: the source-match branch below needs this too
            local path
            local function fullName()
                if not path then
                    local okP, p = pcall(function() return inst:GetFullName() end)
                    path = okP and p or inst.Name
                end
                return path
            end

            local pat = matchesAC(inst.Name)
            if pat then
                table.insert(hits, { name = inst.Name, class = cls, path = fullName(), pat = pat })
            end
            -- Auch Skript-Inhalt pruefen (nur bei Script/ModuleScript)
            if cls == "Script" or cls == "LocalScript" or cls == "ModuleScript" then
                local ok3, src = pcall(function() return inst.Source end)
                if ok3 and src and type(src) == "string" then
                    local lowSrc = string.lower(src)   -- hoisted out of the loop
                    for _, srcPat in ipairs(AC_PATTERNS) do
                        if string.find(lowSrc, srcPat) then
                            table.insert(hits, { name = inst.Name, class = cls, path = fullName(), pat = "script:"..srcPat })
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

local scanning = false
local function doScan()
    if scanning then return end
    scanning = true
    clearResults()
    addResult("Scanning game...", Theme.textDim)
    task.spawn(function()
        local hits = {}
        local MAX_HITS = 80
        for _, locName in ipairs(CHECK_LOCATIONS) do
            local svc = game:FindFirstChild(locName)
            if svc then scanContainer(svc, hits, MAX_HITS) end
            task.wait()
        end
        clearResults()
        local criticalCount = 0
        if #hits == 0 then
            addResult("No anti-cheat patterns found.", Color3.fromRGB(120, 220, 140))
            addResult("Either the game has none, or it's well-hidden.", Theme.textDim)
        else
            addResult(string.format("Found %d match(es):", #hits), Theme.warn)
            addResult("", Theme.text)
            for _, h in ipairs(hits) do
                local color = Theme.text
                local critical = false
                if h.pat == "anticheat" or h.pat == "anti%-cheat"
                   or h.pat == "antiexploit" or h.pat == "anti%-exploit"
                   or h.pat == "sanction" or h.pat == "watchdog" then
                    color = Theme.bad
                    critical = true
                elseif h.pat == "kick" or h.pat == "ban[^k]" or h.pat == "report"
                       or h.pat == "punish" or h.pat == "flag" then
                    color = Theme.warn
                end
                if critical then criticalCount = criticalCount + 1 end
                addResult(string.format("[%s] %s", h.class, h.path), color)
            end
        end
        State.acHits = criticalCount
        if criticalCount > 0 then
            State.acBlocked = true
            notify("AntiCheat", criticalCount .. " critical hit(s) - features disabled. RISKY in AC tab to override.", 6)
            if SilenceAPI._acForceOffAll then SilenceAPI._acForceOffAll() end
        else
            State.acBlocked = false
        end
        if SilenceAPI._refreshAcBanner then SilenceAPI._refreshAcBanner() end
        if SilenceAPI._refreshAcLocks  then SilenceAPI._refreshAcLocks()  end
        scanning = false
    end)
end

buttonRow(pAC, "Scan Game for Anti-Cheat", doScan)

sectionLabel(pAC, "OVERRIDE")
toggleRow(pAC, "RISKY  (ignore AC block)",
    function() return State.acRiskyOverride end,
    function(v)
        State.acRiskyOverride = v
        if SilenceAPI._refreshAcBanner then SilenceAPI._refreshAcBanner() end
        if SilenceAPI._refreshAcLocks  then SilenceAPI._refreshAcLocks()  end
        if v then
            notify("RISKY", "Stealth mode ON - features adapt to bypass AC.", 4)
        else
            notify("RISKY", "Stealth OFF - block re-applied if AC detected.", 3)
            if State.acBlocked and SilenceAPI._acForceOffAll then
                SilenceAPI._acForceOffAll()
            end
        end
    end)

sectionLabel(pAC, "LIVE WATCH")
local watchState = { enabled = false, resets = 0, wsReverts = 0, lastSetWS = nil, conns = {} }
local watchLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", pAC)
watchLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 50)
watchLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
watchLbl.Text = "Resets: 0   |   WalkSpeed reverts: 0\nEnable below to monitor."
watchLbl.Font = Enum.Font.Code
watchLbl.TextSize = 11
watchLbl.TextColor3 = Theme.textDim
watchLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
watchLbl.TextWrapped = true
watchLbl.LayoutOrder = 200

local function updateWatchLabel()
    -- may outlive the GUI if the script is unloaded mid-watch
    if not watchLbl or not watchLbl.Parent then return end
    watchLbl.Text = string.format(
        "Resets: %d   |   WalkSpeed reverts: %d\nResets within ~3s of action = likely server kill.\nWS reverts mean server overrides WalkSpeed.",
        watchState.resets, watchState.wsReverts)
end

local function disconnectWatch()
    for _, c in ipairs(watchState.conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    watchState.conns = {}
    if watchState.humConn then
        pcall(function() watchState.humConn:Disconnect() end)
        watchState.humConn = nil
    end
end

local function startWatch()
    disconnectWatch()
    watchState.resets = 0
    watchState.wsReverts = 0
    updateWatchLabel()
    table.insert(watchState.conns, LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        if watchState.enabled then
            watchState.resets = watchState.resets + 1
            updateWatchLabel()
        end
        local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
        if not hum then return end
        watchState.lastSetWS = hum.WalkSpeed
        -- one connection per character, not one appended per respawn forever
        if watchState.humConn then
            pcall(function() watchState.humConn:Disconnect() end)
        end
        watchState.humConn = hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("WalkSpeed"):Connect(function()
            if watchState.lastSetWS and math.abs(hum.WalkSpeed - watchState.lastSetWS) > 1
               and (hum.WalkSpeed >= 14 and hum.WalkSpeed <= 18) then
                watchState.wsReverts = watchState.wsReverts + 1
                updateWatchLabel()
            end
            watchState.lastSetWS = hum.WalkSpeed
        end)
    end))
end
SilenceAPI._disconnectWatch = disconnectWatch

toggleRow(pAC, "Monitor (resets + WS reverts)",
    function() return watchState.enabled end,
    function(v)
        watchState.enabled = v
        if v then startWatch() else disconnectWatch(); updateWatchLabel() end
    end)

buttonRow(pAC, "Reset Counters", function()
    watchState.resets = 0; watchState.wsReverts = 0; updateWatchLabel()
end)

end)()

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- TAB: MISC
----------------------------------------------------------------
;(function()
local pMisc=addTab("Misc")
sectionLabel(pMisc,"TAG")
buttonRow(pMisc,"Open Tag Editor", function()
    if SilenceAPI._toggleTagEditor then SilenceAPI._toggleTagEditor() end
end)
sectionLabel(pMisc,"GUI VISIBILITY")
toggleRow(pMisc, "Top-Right HUD", function() return Hud and Hud.Visible or false end, function(v)
    if Hud then Hud.Visible = v end
end)
toggleRow(pMisc, "Left Float Icon", function() return State.floatVisible end, function(v)
    State.floatVisible = v
    if FloatWrap then FloatWrap.Visible = v end
end)
toggleRow(pMisc, "Background Blur  (open panel only)",
    function() return State.menuBlur end,
    function(v)
        State.menuBlur = v
        if SilenceAPI._setMenuBlur then SilenceAPI._setMenuBlur() end
    end)
sectionLabel(pMisc,"UTILITY")
toggleRow(pMisc,"Anti-AFK", function() return State.antiAfk end, setAntiAfk)
inputRow(pMisc,"Command Prefix",";",function(v) if v and #v>0 then State.prefix=v:sub(1,1) end end)

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- VOICE
-- Its own group on the rail, four tabs across the top. Wrapped in do..end
-- because the main chunk is at 198 of Luau's 200 locals and four page
-- handles would not fit.
--
-- What this can and cannot do, stated once: it rebuilds ROBLOX'S voice
-- session. There is no second channel and there cannot be one - Lua is given
-- no microphone samples and cannot play raw audio, so no script carries voice
-- itself. This fixes a session that dropped. An account Roblox has voice
-- banned is checked server-side on join, and no client talks it out of that.
--
-- An IIFE rather than do..end: a do..end block shares registers with the
-- chunk around it, so everything declared here would be live alongside its
-- locals and count towards Luau's limit of 200. A function body has its own.
----------------------------------------------------------------
;(function()
    local pVcBan  = addTab("Anti Ban")
    local pVcMute = addTab("Mute")
    local pVcMic  = addTab("Mic")
    local pVcLog  = addTab("Voice Log")

    ------------------------------------------------------------ API LAYER
    -- Names are probed, never assumed. A live probe on this build reported
    -- VoiceChatInternal present with PublishPause and SubscribePause, but
    -- NO JoinVoice and no PausedVoiceConnectionEvent - so the join goes
    -- through JoinByGroupIdToken and the reconnect cannot be event driven.
    local function svcOf(name)
        local v
        pcall(function() v = game:GetService(name) end)
        return v
    end
    local function hasM(svc, m)
        if not svc then return false end
        local ok, v = pcall(function() return svc[m] end)
        return ok and v ~= nil
    end
    local function callM(svc, m, ...)
        if not hasM(svc, m) then return false end
        local a = table.pack(...)
        local ok = pcall(function() svc[m](svc, table.unpack(a, 1, a.n)) end)
        return ok
    end

    local function groupId()
        local vci = svcOf("VoiceChatInternal")
        local gid
        pcall(function() if hasM(vci, "GetGroupId") then gid = vci:GetGroupId() end end)
        return gid
    end

    local function vcJoin()
        local vci = svcOf("VoiceChatInternal")
        local gid = groupId()
        if gid and callM(vci, "JoinByGroupIdToken", gid, false) then
            return "JoinByGroupIdToken"
        end
        if gid and callM(vci, "JoinByGroupId", gid, false) then return "JoinByGroupId" end
        if callM(vci, "Join") then return "Join" end
        local vcv = svcOf("VoiceChatService")
        if callM(vcv, "JoinVoice") then return "VoiceChatService:JoinVoice" end
        if callM(vcv, "joinVoice") then return "VoiceChatService:joinVoice" end
        return nil
    end

    local function vcLeave()
        local vci = svcOf("VoiceChatInternal")
        if callM(vci, "Leave")      then return "Leave" end
        if callM(vci, "LeaveVoice") then return "LeaveVoice" end
        return nil
    end

    -- Two eras of API. The current one puts an AudioDeviceInput under each
    -- Player; the older one goes through VoiceChatInternal. The probe found
    -- only the older one here, so that path is what actually runs.
    local function muteSelf(on)
        local ok = false
        pcall(function()
            local d = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("AudioDeviceInput")
            if d then d.Muted = on and true or false; ok = true end
        end)
        if not ok then ok = callM(svcOf("VoiceChatInternal"), "PublishPause", on and true or false) end
        return ok
    end
    local function mutePlayer(p, on)
        if not p or p == LocalPlayer then return false end
        local ok = false
        pcall(function()
            local d = p:FindFirstChildOfClass("AudioDeviceInput")
            if d then d.Muted = on and true or false; ok = true end
        end)
        if not ok then
            ok = callM(svcOf("VoiceChatInternal"), "SubscribePause", p.UserId, on and true or false)
        end
        return ok
    end

    -- A channel id only exists while a session is actually up, so this is a
    -- real answer rather than the "is a group configured" guess it replaces.
    local function voiceState()
        local vci = svcOf("VoiceChatInternal")
        local cid
        pcall(function()
            if hasM(vci, "GetChannelId") then cid = vci:GetChannelId() end
        end)
        if cid and tostring(cid) ~= "" and tostring(cid) ~= "nil" then
            return true, "connected  -  channel " .. tostring(cid):sub(1, 20)
        end
        local dev = false
        pcall(function()
            dev = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("AudioDeviceInput") ~= nil
        end)
        if dev then return true, "connected  -  microphone device present" end
        return false, "no live voice session"
    end

    -- THE question, and it turns out Roblox will answer it directly.
    -- IsVoiceEnabledForUserIdAsync is the same check the server does when you
    -- try to join voice. false means Roblox has voice switched off for this
    -- account - that IS the ban, and no amount of rejoining changes it.
    -- true with no session means the session merely dropped, which is the
    -- case a reset does fix. Returns: enabled, known.
    local function voiceEligible()
        local vcv = svcOf("VoiceChatService")
        if not hasM(vcv, "IsVoiceEnabledForUserIdAsync") then return false, false end
        local ok, res = pcall(function()
            return vcv:IsVoiceEnabledForUserIdAsync(LocalPlayer.UserId)
        end)
        if not ok then return false, false end
        return res and true or false, true
    end

    -- The API keeps the mute state, so read it instead of tracking a copy
    -- that can drift out of step with what is really happening.
    local function isSelfMuted()
        local v
        pcall(function()
            local vci = svcOf("VoiceChatInternal")
            if hasM(vci, "IsPublishPaused") then v = vci:IsPublishPaused() end
        end)
        return v and true or false
    end
    local function isMutedFor(p)
        local v
        pcall(function()
            local vci = svcOf("VoiceChatInternal")
            if hasM(vci, "IsSubscribePaused") then v = vci:IsSubscribePaused(p.UserId) end
        end)
        return v and true or false
    end

    ------------------------------------------------------------ TAB: ANTI BAN
    sectionLabel(pVcBan, "SESSION")

    local stRow = Instance.new("Frame", pVcBan)
    stRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36)
    stRow.BorderSizePixel = 0
    corner(stRow, 8)
    SilenceAPI._theme(stRow, "BackgroundColor3", "bg2")
    stroke(stRow, Theme.bg4, 1, 0.6, "bg4")
    SilenceAPI._registerRow(stRow, "voice status connected session")

    local stDot = Instance.new("Frame", stRow)
    stDot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8); stDot.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0.5, -4)
    stDot.BorderSizePixel = 0
    local sdc = Instance.new("UICorner", stDot); sdc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

    local stLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", stRow)
    stLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0); stLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 0)
    stLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    stLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium; stLbl.TextSize = 11
    stLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    stLbl.Text = "voice: checking..."
    SilenceAPI._theme(stLbl, "TextColor3", "text")

    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            local live, why = voiceState()
            stLbl.Text = "voice: " .. why
            stDot.BackgroundColor3 = live and Color3.fromRGB(90, 230, 120)
                                          or Color3.fromRGB(240, 80, 80)
            task.wait(1.5)
        end
    end)

    -- The old pulse only ever pushed JOIN, and a client that still believes
    -- its session is alive ignores a join outright - which is why a half dead
    -- session survived it. Leaving first is the part that was missing.
    local function hardReset(loud)
        local steps = {}
        local left = vcLeave()
        steps[#steps+1] = left and ("left via " .. left) or "no leave method"

        local micOk = false
        pcall(function()
            local us = UserSettings():GetService("UserGameSettings")
            local cur = us.MicrophonePermission
            us.MicrophonePermission = false
            task.wait(0.25)
            us.MicrophonePermission = (cur ~= nil) and cur or true
            micOk = true
        end)
        steps[#steps+1] = micOk and "mic permission cycled" or "mic permission unavailable"

        task.wait(0.6)
        local joined = vcJoin()
        steps[#steps+1] = joined and ("rejoined via " .. joined) or "NO JOIN METHOD FOUND"

        pcall(function()
            local vci = svcOf("VoiceChatInternal")
            if hasM(vci, "GetMicDevices") and hasM(vci, "SetMicDevice") then
                local devs = vci:GetMicDevices()
                if type(devs) == "table" and devs[1] then
                    vci:SetMicDevice(devs[1].Guid or devs[1].guid or devs[1])
                    steps[#steps+1] = "mic device reselected"
                end
            end
        end)

        task.wait(1.2)
        local live, why = voiceState()
        steps[#steps+1] = live and ("OK - " .. why) or ("still down - " .. why)

        print("[Silence] voice hard reset:")
        for _, st in ipairs(steps) do print("  - " .. st) end
        if loud then notify("Voice", table.concat(steps, "  /  "), 10) end
        return live
    end
    SilenceAPI._vcHardReset = hardReset
    SilenceAPI._vcState = function() local ok = voiceState() return ok end

    -- StateChanged exists on this build, so the reconnect listens instead of
    -- polling. The timer that remains is only a backstop for a state change
    -- that never fires, and it is deliberately slow.
    local autoConns, autoFails = {}, 0
    local function autoOff()
        for _, c in ipairs(autoConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
        autoConns = {}
    end
    local function tryRebuild(why)
        if autoFails >= 5 then
            State.vcBypass = false
            autoOff()
            notify("Voice", "Gave up after 5 rebuilds - is voice on in this game?", 8)
            for _, fn in ipairs(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers) do pcall(fn) end
            return
        end
        autoFails = autoFails + 1
        print("[Silence] voice rebuild triggered by " .. tostring(why))
        task.spawn(function()
            if hardReset(false) then autoFails = 0 end
        end)
    end
    local function autoOn()
        autoOff()
        autoFails = 0
        pcall(function()
            local vci = svcOf("VoiceChatInternal")
            if hasM(vci, "StateChanged") then
                autoConns[#autoConns+1] = vci.StateChanged:Connect(function()
                    if not State.vcBypass then return end
                    task.wait(0.3)
                    local live = voiceState()
                    if not live then tryRebuild("StateChanged") end
                end)
            end
        end)
        -- backstop, 30s apart, for a build where the signal stays quiet
        task.spawn(function()
            while State.vcBypass do
                task.wait(30)
                if State.vcBypass and not voiceState() then tryRebuild("backstop") end
            end
        end)
    end
    SilenceAPI._vcAutoOn  = autoOn
    SilenceAPI._vcAutoOff = autoOff

    sectionLabel(pVcBan, "IS THIS ACCOUNT ALLOWED TO TALK")
    local elRow = Instance.new("Frame", pVcBan)
    elRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 52)
    elRow.BorderSizePixel = 0
    corner(elRow, 8)
    SilenceAPI._theme(elRow, "BackgroundColor3", "bg2")
    stroke(elRow, Theme.bg4, 1, 0.6, "bg4")
    SilenceAPI._registerRow(elRow, "voice ban eligibility allowed check")

    local elLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", elRow)
    elLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 1, 0); elLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 0)
    elLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    elLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium; elLbl.TextSize = 11
    elLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    elLbl.TextWrapped = true
    elLbl.Text = "not checked yet"
    SilenceAPI._theme(elLbl, "TextColor3", "text")

    buttonRow(pVcBan, "Check If Roblox Allows Me Voice", function()
        elLbl.Text = "asking Roblox..."
        elLbl.TextColor3 = Theme.textDim
        task.spawn(function()
            local enabled, known = voiceEligible()
            if not known then
                elLbl.Text = "Roblox would not answer. Try again inside a game"
                          .. " that has voice chat switched on."
                elLbl.TextColor3 = Theme.textDim
            elseif enabled then
                elLbl.Text = "ALLOWED. Roblox has voice enabled for this account,"
                          .. " so if you cannot talk the session dropped - the"
                          .. " reset below is the right button."
                elLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 230, 120)
            else
                elLbl.Text = "NOT ALLOWED. Roblox has voice switched off for this"
                          .. " account. This is decided on their servers and no"
                          .. " script can change it. Rejoining will not help."
                elLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 90, 90)
            end
        end)
    end)

    ------------------------------------------------------------ FORCE UNMUTE
    -- There are two ways Roblox could be enforcing a short voice mute, and
    -- they need completely different answers:
    --
    --   CLIENT SIDE - it tells your client to pause publishing and trusts it
    --   to obey. Then setting PublishPause(false) back defeats it outright,
    --   and this is very likely what the scripts that "work" are doing.
    --
    --   SERVER SIDE - the voice backend refuses to relay your stream. Then
    --   nothing here helps, because your client is not the one saying no.
    --
    -- IsPublishPaused() tells the two apart. If something keeps flipping it
    -- back to true after this sets it false, the mute is being pushed to your
    -- client and the counter below will climb. If the counter stays at zero
    -- and people still cannot hear you, it is server side and no script wins.
    --
    -- So this is the fix for one case and the measurement that identifies
    -- which case you are in. Leave it on while muted and read the number.
    sectionLabel(pVcBan, "FORCE UNMUTE")

    -- Two counters, not one. The single counter this replaces could not tell
    -- "something keeps re-muting me" apart from "my unmute call does nothing
    -- at all", and those look identical from outside: in both cases
    -- IsPublishPaused stays true and the number climbs once per loop.
    --   STUCK   - PublishPause(false) was called and the flag did not move,
    --             so there is no live session to unmute and this is pointless
    --   RE-MUTE - the flag DID go false, and something set it back after,
    --             which is a real fight this loop can win
    local forceOn, forceStuck, forceRemute, forceStart = false, 0, 0, 0
    local forceLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", pVcBan)
    forceLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 64)
    forceLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    forceLbl.Text = "  off"
    forceLbl.Font = Enum.Font.Code
    forceLbl.TextSize = 11
    forceLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    forceLbl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
    SilenceAPI._theme(forceLbl, "TextColor3", "textDim")

    local function forcePaint()
        if not forceOn then forceLbl.Text = "  off" return end
        local secs = math.max(1, math.floor(tick() - forceStart))
        local head = "  running " .. secs .. "s   stuck " .. forceStuck
                     .. "   re-muted " .. forceRemute .. "\n"
        if forceRemute > 0 then
            forceLbl.Text = head
                .. "  SOMETHING KEEPS MUTING YOU and this undoes it.\n"
                .. "  Ask someone whether they hear you now."
        elseif forceStuck > 0 then
            forceLbl.Text = head
                .. "  Unmuting has NO EFFECT - the flag will not move.\n"
                .. "  There is no live voice session to unmute. Nothing to win here."
        else
            forceLbl.Text = head
                .. "  You are not muted from the client side."
        end
    end

    local function forceStop()
        forceOn = false
        forcePaint()
    end
    local function forceStartFn()
        forceOn, forceStuck, forceRemute, forceStart = true, 0, 0, tick()
        forcePaint()
        task.spawn(function()
            while forceOn do
                -- never fight a mute the user asked for
                if not State.vcSelfMuted and isSelfMuted() then
                    muteSelf(false)
                    -- Read straight back. This is the whole measurement: a
                    -- call that changes nothing leaves the flag set, a call
                    -- that worked clears it and only something ELSE can put
                    -- it back before the next pass.
                    if isSelfMuted() then
                        forceStuck = forceStuck + 1
                    else
                        forceRemute = forceRemute + 1
                        print("[Silence] unmute took effect, was re-muted #" .. forceRemute)
                    end
                end
                forcePaint()
                task.wait(0.5)
            end
        end)
    end

    toggleRow(pVcBan, "Force Unmute  (undo a client-side mute)",
        function() return forceOn end,
        function(v) if v then forceStartFn() else forceStop() end end)

    local forceHint = Instance.new("TextLabel", pVcBan)
    forceHint.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 60)
    forceHint.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    forceHint.Text = "  stuck  = the unmute call does nothing, there is no session\n"
                  .. "  re-muted = it worked and something put it back - winnable\n"
                  .. "  Ground truth is still a person telling you they hear you.\n"
                  .. "  Numbers alone prove the flag moved, not that audio arrives."
    forceHint.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    forceHint.TextSize = 10
    forceHint.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    forceHint.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
    forceHint.TextWrapped = true
    SilenceAPI._theme(forceHint, "TextColor3", "textDim")

    ------------------------------------------------------------ BAN WATCHER
    -- A short voice ban expires on its own. The scripts going around get
    -- credit for that: you press their buttons for two minutes, the timer
    -- runs out, voice returns, and it looks like the script did it.
    --
    -- This watches the one authoritative signal - the same eligibility check
    -- Roblox's own servers use - and writes down when it changes. If voice
    -- comes back at the five minute mark no matter what you pressed, the
    -- log says so. If a reset genuinely brings it back early, the log says
    -- that too. Either way you stop guessing.
    --
    -- It also rejoins the moment eligibility returns, so you get to talk
    -- again immediately instead of testing every 30 seconds.
    sectionLabel(pVcBan, "BAN WATCHER")

    local watchLog = {}
    local watchOn, watchConn = false, nil
    local watchLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", pVcBan)
    watchLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 116)
    watchLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    watchLbl.Text = "  off"
    watchLbl.Font = Enum.Font.Code
    watchLbl.TextSize = 11
    watchLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    watchLbl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
    SilenceAPI._theme(watchLbl, "TextColor3", "textDim")

    local function stamp()
        local ok, t = pcall(function() return os.date("%H:%M:%S") end)
        return ok and t or tostring(math.floor(tick()))
    end
    local function watchNote(line)
        table.insert(watchLog, 1, "  " .. stamp() .. "  " .. line)
        while #watchLog > 7 do table.remove(watchLog) end
        watchLbl.Text = table.concat(watchLog, "\n")
        print("[Silence] voice watch: " .. line)
    end

    local function startWatch()
        watchOn = true
        watchLog = {}
        watchNote("watching - checking every 15s")
        task.spawn(function()
            local was = nil
            local since = tick()
            while watchOn do
                local enabled, known = voiceEligible()
                if not known then
                    watchNote("Roblox did not answer")
                elseif was == nil then
                    was = enabled
                    since = tick()
                    watchNote(enabled and "voice ALLOWED" or "voice NOT allowed")
                elseif enabled ~= was then
                    local mins = math.floor((tick() - since) / 60)
                    local secs = math.floor((tick() - since) % 60)
                    watchNote(string.format("changed to %s after %dm %02ds",
                        enabled and "ALLOWED" or "NOT allowed", mins, secs))
                    was = enabled
                    since = tick()
                    if enabled then
                        watchNote("rejoining voice now")
                        notify("Voice", "Voice is back - rejoining.", 6)
                        task.spawn(function() hardReset(false) end)
                    else
                        notify("Voice", "Voice was just taken away.", 6)
                    end
                end
                for _ = 1, 15 do
                    if not watchOn then break end
                    task.wait(1)
                end
            end
        end)
    end
    local function stopWatch()
        watchOn = false
        watchNote("stopped")
    end

    toggleRow(pVcBan, "Watch For The Ban Lifting  (auto rejoin)",
        function() return watchOn end,
        function(v)
            if v then startWatch() else stopWatch() end
        end)

    buttonRow(pVcBan, "Copy Watch Log", function()
        local ok = false
        pcall(function() setclipboard(table.concat(watchLog, "\n")); ok = true end)
        notify("Voice", ok and "Watch log copied." or "No clipboard access.", 3)
    end)

    local watchHint = Instance.new("TextLabel", pVcBan)
    watchHint.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 46)
    watchHint.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    watchHint.Text = "  Turn this on while you are banned. It records exactly when\n"
                  .. "  voice comes back and rejoins for you - and it shows whether\n"
                  .. "  the reset did anything or the ban simply ran out."
    watchHint.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    watchHint.TextSize = 10
    watchHint.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    watchHint.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
    watchHint.TextWrapped = true
    SilenceAPI._theme(watchHint, "TextColor3", "textDim")

    sectionLabel(pVcBan, "REBUILD")
    buttonRow(pVcBan, "Voice Hard Reset  (leave, cycle mic, rejoin)", function()
        notify("Voice", "Rebuilding voice session...", 3)
        task.spawn(function() hardReset(true) end)
    end)

    sectionLabel(pVcBan, "AUTOMATIC")
    toggleRow(pVcBan, "Auto Reconnect  (watch and rebuild)",
        function() return State.vcBypass end,
        function(v)
            setVcBypass(v)
            notify("Voice", v and "ON - rebuilds the session when it drops."
                              or "OFF", 3)
        end)

    local acWarn = Instance.new("TextLabel", pVcBan)
    acWarn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 60)
    acWarn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    acWarn.Text = "  This rebuilds Roblox's own voice session. It fixes a session that\n"
               .. "  dropped, which is what most 'anti VC ban' scripts actually do.\n"
               .. "  An account-level voice ban is checked on Roblox's servers and\n"
               .. "  cannot be lifted from here by any script."
    acWarn.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    acWarn.TextSize = 10
    acWarn.TextColor3 = Theme.warn
    acWarn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    acWarn.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
    acWarn.TextWrapped = true

    sectionLabel(pVcBan, "LAST RESORT")
    buttonRow(pVcBan, "Rejoin Server  (full voice refresh)", function()
        notify("Voice", "Rejoining server...", 2)
        task.wait(0.3)
        TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, LocalPlayer)
    end)

    ------------------------------------------------------------ TAB: MUTE
    sectionLabel(pVcMute, "YOURSELF")
    -- reads IsPublishPaused rather than a local copy, so the switch shows
    -- what is actually true even if something else changed it
    toggleRow(pVcMute, "Mute My Microphone",
        function() return isSelfMuted() end,
        function(v)
            State.vcSelfMuted = v
            local ok = muteSelf(v)
            notify("Voice", ok and (v and "Microphone muted." or "Microphone live.")
                              or "No voice API answered.", 4)
        end)

    -- The one thing in this whole tab that genuinely reduces your chance of
    -- being banned: audio that is never sent cannot be reported. Bindable to
    -- a key from the Hotkeys tab, because reaching for a menu is exactly the
    -- three seconds in which the thing you did not want recorded gets sent.
    function SilenceAPI._vcPanicMute()
        local ok = muteSelf(true)
        State.vcSelfMuted = true
        for _, fn in ipairs(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers) do pcall(fn) end
        notify("Voice", ok and "MIC CUT." or "No voice API answered.", 4)
    end
    function SilenceAPI._vcToggleMic()
        local want = not isSelfMuted()
        local ok = muteSelf(want)
        State.vcSelfMuted = want
        for _, fn in ipairs(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers) do pcall(fn) end
        notify("Voice", ok and (want and "MIC CUT." or "Mic live.")
                          or "No voice API answered.", 3)
    end

    buttonRow(pVcMute, "PANIC MUTE  (cut my microphone now)", SilenceAPI._vcPanicMute)

    toggleRow(pVcMute, "Cut Microphone On Every Server Join",
        function() return State.vcAutoMute end,
        function(v)
            State.vcAutoMute = v
            notify("Voice", v and "Your mic will start muted from now on."
                              or "Mic will start live.", 4)
        end)

    -- runs once per injection, which is once per server
    task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(4)
        if State.vcAutoMute then
            muteSelf(true)
            State.vcSelfMuted = true
            for _, fn in ipairs(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers) do pcall(fn) end
            notify("Voice", "Microphone muted on join.", 4)
        end
    end)

    sectionLabel(pVcMute, "OTHER PLAYERS")
    local muteHint = Instance.new("TextLabel", pVcMute)
    muteHint.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
    muteHint.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    muteHint.Text = "  Local only: this changes what YOU hear. Nobody else is affected."
    muteHint.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    muteHint.TextSize = 10
    muteHint.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    SilenceAPI._theme(muteHint, "TextColor3", "textDim")

    local muted = {}
    local vcList = Instance.new("Frame", pVcMute)
    vcList.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    vcList.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    vcList.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    local vcLay = Instance.new("UIListLayout", vcList)
    vcLay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
    vcLay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    SilenceAPI._registerRow(vcList, "voice mute players list")

    local function rebuildVoice()
        for _, c in ipairs(vcList:GetChildren()) do
            if not c:IsA("UIListLayout") then c:Destroy() end
        end
        local n = 0
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= LocalPlayer then
                n = n + 1
                local row = Instance.new("Frame", vcList)
                row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32)
                row.BorderSizePixel = 0
                row.LayoutOrder = n
                corner(row, 8)
                SilenceAPI._theme(row, "BackgroundColor3", "bg2")
                stroke(row, Theme.bg4, 1, 0.6, "bg4")

                local nm = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
                nm.Size = UDim2.new(1, -110, 1, 0)
                nm.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
                nm.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                nm.Text = p.DisplayName .. "  (@" .. p.Name .. ")"
                nm.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
                nm.TextSize = 12
                nm.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                nm.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
                SilenceAPI._theme(nm, "TextColor3", "text")

                local b = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
                b.Size = UDim2.new(0, 90, 0, 22)
                b.Position = UDim2.new(1, -100, 0.5, -11)
                b.BorderSizePixel = 0
                b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                b.TextSize = 10
                b.AutoButtonColor = false
                corner(b, 6)
                local bs = stroke(b, Theme.bg4, 1, 0.5, "bg4")

                local function paint()
                    local on = isMutedFor(p)
                    b.Text = on and "MUTED" or "MUTE"
                    b.BackgroundColor3 = on and Theme.accent or Theme.bg3
                    b.TextColor3       = on and Theme.bg     or Theme.text
                    bs.Transparency    = on and 0 or 0.5
                end
                paint()
                b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    local want = not isMutedFor(p)
                    if mutePlayer(p, want) then
                        paint()
                    else
                        notify("Voice", "No voice API answered for " .. p.Name .. ".", 4)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
        if n == 0 then
            local empty = Instance.new("TextLabel", vcList)
            empty.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
            empty.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            empty.Text = "  Nobody else in this server."
            empty.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
            empty.TextSize = 11
            empty.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            SilenceAPI._theme(empty, "TextColor3", "textDim")
        end
    end
    rebuildVoice()
    SilenceAPI._rebuildVoiceList = rebuildVoice
    track(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function() task.wait(0.5) rebuildVoice() end))
    track(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function() task.wait(0.2) rebuildVoice() end))
    -- fires when somebody joins or leaves the voice channel, which is not the
    -- same moment as joining the server
    pcall(function()
        local vci = svcOf("VoiceChatInternal")
        if hasM(vci, "ParticipantsStateChanged") then
            track(vci.ParticipantsStateChanged:Connect(function()
                task.wait(0.2); rebuildVoice()
            end))
        end
    end)

    -- SubscribePauseAll does the whole server in one call; the per player
    -- loop stays as the fallback for builds without it
    local function muteAll(on)
        if callM(svcOf("VoiceChatInternal"), "SubscribePauseAll", on) then
            rebuildVoice()
            return -1
        end
        local hit = 0
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= LocalPlayer and mutePlayer(p, on) then hit = hit + 1 end
        end
        rebuildVoice()
        return hit
    end
    buttonRow(pVcMute, "Mute Everyone", function()
        local n = muteAll(true)
        notify("Voice", n < 0 and "Everyone muted locally."
                              or (n .. " player(s) muted locally."), 3)
    end)
    buttonRow(pVcMute, "Unmute Everyone", function()
        muteAll(false)
        notify("Voice", "Everyone unmuted locally.", 3)
    end)

    -- Hearing is a separate subscription from speaking. A mute that arrives
    -- from outside can pause the incoming side too, and then "I hear nobody"
    -- looks like a ban when it is only a stuck subscription.
    buttonRow(pVcMute, "Force Hearing Back On", function()
        local vci = svcOf("VoiceChatInternal")
        local fixed = 0
        if callM(vci, "SubscribePauseAll", false) then fixed = -1 end
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= LocalPlayer and isMutedFor(p) then
                if mutePlayer(p, false) and fixed >= 0 then fixed = fixed + 1 end
            end
        end
        rebuildVoice()
        notify("Voice", fixed < 0 and "Incoming audio unpaused."
                                  or (fixed .. " player(s) unpaused."), 4)
    end)

    ------------------------------------------------------------ TAB: MIC
    sectionLabel(pVcMic, "INPUT DEVICE")
    local micBox = Instance.new("Frame", pVcMic)
    micBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    micBox.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    micBox.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    local micLay = Instance.new("UIListLayout", micBox)
    micLay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
    micLay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    SilenceAPI._registerRow(micBox, "microphone input device list")

    local function rebuildMics()
        for _, c in ipairs(micBox:GetChildren()) do
            if not c:IsA("UIListLayout") then c:Destroy() end
        end
        local vci = svcOf("VoiceChatInternal")
        local devs
        pcall(function()
            if hasM(vci, "GetMicDevices") then devs = vci:GetMicDevices() end
        end)
        if type(devs) ~= "table" or #devs == 0 then
            local none = Instance.new("TextLabel", micBox)
            none.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44)
            none.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            none.Text = "  This build exposes no microphone list."
            none.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
            none.TextSize = 11
            none.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            SilenceAPI._theme(none, "TextColor3", "textDim")
            return
        end
        for i, d in ipairs(devs) do
            local label = tostring(d.Name or d.name or d)
            local guid  = d.Guid or d.guid or d
            local b = Instance.new("TextButton", micBox)
            b.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32)
            b.BorderSizePixel = 0
            b.Text = "  " .. label
            b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
            b.TextSize = 12
            b.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            b.AutoButtonColor = false
            b.LayoutOrder = i
            corner(b, 8)
            SilenceAPI._theme(b, "BackgroundColor3", "bg2")
            SilenceAPI._theme(b, "TextColor3", "text")
            stroke(b, Theme.bg4, 1, 0.6, "bg4")
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                local ok = callM(vci, "SetMicDevice", guid)
                notify("Voice", ok and ("Microphone: " .. label)
                                  or "SetMicDevice is not available here.", 4)
            end)
        end
    end
    rebuildMics()
    buttonRow(pVcMic, "Refresh Device List", rebuildMics)

    ------------------------------------------------------------ TAB: VOICE LOG
    sectionLabel(pVcLog, "WHAT THIS BUILD OFFERS")
    local logLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", pVcLog)
    logLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 340)
    logLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    logLbl.Text = "  press Probe below"
    logLbl.Font = Enum.Font.Code
    logLbl.TextSize = 11
    logLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    logLbl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
    SilenceAPI._theme(logLbl, "TextColor3", "textDim")

    -- Names are not guessable, so the whole plausible surface is tested and
    -- what answers is reported. This is how the join was found to be
    -- JoinByGroupIdToken and not JoinVoice.
    local VCI_NAMES = {
        "JoinByGroupIdToken", "JoinByGroupId", "Join", "JoinVoice",
        "Leave", "LeaveVoice",
        "PublishPause", "SubscribePause", "SubscribePauseAll",
        "IsPublishPaused", "IsSubscribePaused",
        "GetGroupId", "GetChannelId",
        "GetMicDevices", "SetMicDevice", "GetMicDevice",
        "GetSpeakerDevices", "SetSpeakerDevice",
        "SubscribeRenderStepped", "SubscribePauseVoiceConnectionEvents",
        "ParticipantsStateChanged", "StateChanged",
        "PlayerMicPermissionsChanged", "LocalPlayerMicEnabledChanged",
        "PausedVoiceConnectionEvent",
    }
    local VCS_NAMES = {
        "JoinVoice", "joinVoice", "IsVoiceEnabledForUserIdAsync",
        "EnableDefaultVoice", "VoiceChatEnabledForPlaceOnRcc",
    }

    local function probe()
        local out = {}
        local vci = svcOf("VoiceChatInternal")
        local vcv = svcOf("VoiceChatService")
        out[#out+1] = "VoiceChatInternal service : " .. (vci and "yes" or "NO")
        out[#out+1] = "VoiceChatService service  : " .. (vcv and "yes" or "NO")
        local dev = false
        pcall(function()
            dev = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("AudioDeviceInput") ~= nil
        end)
        out[#out+1] = "your AudioDeviceInput     : " .. (dev and "yes" or "no")
        local others = 0
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= LocalPlayer then
                pcall(function()
                    if p:FindFirstChildOfClass("AudioDeviceInput") then
                        others = others + 1
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
        out[#out+1] = "others with a mic device  : " .. others
        out[#out+1] = "group id                  : " .. tostring(groupId())
        out[#out+1] = ""
        out[#out+1] = "VoiceChatInternal:"
        for _, m in ipairs(VCI_NAMES) do
            out[#out+1] = "  " .. (hasM(vci, m) and "yes  " or "  .  ") .. m
        end
        out[#out+1] = ""
        out[#out+1] = "VoiceChatService:"
        for _, m in ipairs(VCS_NAMES) do
            out[#out+1] = "  " .. (hasM(vcv, m) and "yes  " or "  .  ") .. m
        end
        local txt = table.concat(out, "\n")
        logLbl.Text = txt
        print("[Silence] VOICE PROBE")
        for _, l in ipairs(out) do print("  " .. l) end
        return txt
    end

    buttonRow(pVcLog, "Probe Voice API", function()
        probe()
        notify("Voice", "Probe done - see the list above.", 3)
    end)
    buttonRow(pVcLog, "Copy Probe To Clipboard", function()
        local txt = probe()
        local ok = false
        pcall(function() setclipboard(txt); ok = true end)
        notify("Voice", ok and "Copied." or "No clipboard access in this executor.", 4)
    end)

    -- rows created after this block belong to Misc again, or the search index
    -- would file them under the last tab opened here
    SilenceAPI._currentTabName = "Misc"
end)()

    notify("Jumpscare", "Fetching frames, this can take a moment...", 4)

        -- pulls the frames in if they are not here yet, which is the point:

        local txt = table.concat(out, "   ")
        print("[Silence] jumpscare assets: " .. txt)
        notify("Jumpscare", txt, 10)
    end)
end)

    State.jumpSound = v

sectionLabel(pMisc,"UI COLORS")

-- Repaint is driven by the registry that every widget joins at creation time:
-- each entry remembers WHICH token it was painted from. The previous version
-- matched live colours by equality, but white is accent AND text AND on by
-- default, so dragging a picker through a shared value fused those roles and
-- they recoloured together afterwards. Looking the role up removes that.
local function applyUiColorsNow()
    local c = State.colors
    if c.uiAccent  then Theme.accent = c.uiAccent; Theme.on = c.uiAccent end
    if c.uiAccent2 then Theme.accent2 = c.uiAccent2 end
    if c.uiText    then Theme.text    = c.uiText    end
    if c.uiTextDim then Theme.textDim = c.uiTextDim end
    if c.uiBg      then Theme.bg      = c.uiBg      end
    if c.uiBg2     then Theme.bg2     = c.uiBg2     end
    if c.uiBg3     then Theme.bg3     = c.uiBg3     end

    -- walk backwards so destroyed widgets can be dropped in the same pass
    for i = #SilenceAPI._themed, 1, -1 do
        local e = SilenceAPI._themed[i]
        if e.inst and e.inst.Parent then
            pcall(function() e.inst[e.prop] = Theme[e.token] end)
        else
            table.remove(SilenceAPI._themed, i)
        end
    end
    for i = #SilenceAPI._themedGrads, 1, -1 do
        local g = SilenceAPI._themedGrads[i]
        if g and g.Parent then
            pcall(function() g.Color = ColorSequence.new(Theme.accent) end)
        else
            table.remove(SilenceAPI._themedGrads, i)
        end
    end
    -- the toggle switches repaint themselves from Theme when refreshed
    for _, r in ipairs(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers) do pcall(r) end
end

-- Dragging a picker fires this on every mouse move. Coalesce to one repaint
-- per frame, otherwise the panel stutters while you drag.
local uiColorQueued = false
function SilenceAPI._applyUiColors()
    if uiColorQueued then return end
    uiColorQueued = true
    task.defer(function()
        uiColorQueued = false
        applyUiColorsNow()
    end)
end

hexColorPickerRow(pMisc, "Glow / Accent", State.colors.uiAccent, function(col)
    State.colors.uiAccent = col
    SilenceAPI._applyUiColors()
end)
hexColorPickerRow(pMisc, "Accent (soft)", State.colors.uiAccent2, function(col)
    State.colors.uiAccent2 = col
    SilenceAPI._applyUiColors()
end)
hexColorPickerRow(pMisc, "Text", State.colors.uiText, function(col)
    State.colors.uiText = col
    SilenceAPI._applyUiColors()
end)
hexColorPickerRow(pMisc, "Text (dim)", State.colors.uiTextDim, function(col)
    State.colors.uiTextDim = col
    SilenceAPI._applyUiColors()
end)
hexColorPickerRow(pMisc, "Panel Background", State.colors.uiBg, function(col)
    State.colors.uiBg = col
    SilenceAPI._applyUiColors()
end)
hexColorPickerRow(pMisc, "Row Background", State.colors.uiBg2, function(col)
    State.colors.uiBg2 = col
    SilenceAPI._applyUiColors()
end)
hexColorPickerRow(pMisc, "Input Background", State.colors.uiBg3, function(col)
    State.colors.uiBg3 = col
    SilenceAPI._applyUiColors()
end)

buttonRow(pMisc, "Neon Purple Preset", function()
    State.colors.uiAccent   = Color3.fromRGB(170, 90, 255)
    State.colors.uiAccent2  = Color3.fromRGB(205, 160, 255)
    State.colors.uiText     = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    State.colors.uiTextDim  = Color3.fromRGB(160, 140, 190)

    State.colors.uiBg2      = Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 12)
    State.colors.uiBg3      = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 22)

end)

local uiColHint = Instance.new("TextLabel", pMisc)

sectionLabel(pMisc,"WAYPOINTS")
local wpNameBox = inputRow(pMisc, "Waypoint Name", "", function() end)
local wpList = Instance.new("Frame", pMisc)
wpList.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,0); wpList.BackgroundTransparency = 1
wpList.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
local wpLay = Instance.new("UIListLayout", wpList)
wpLay.Padding = UDim.new(0,4); wpLay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

local refreshWaypoints
refreshWaypoints = function()
    for _, c in ipairs(wpList:GetChildren()) do
        if not c:IsA("UIListLayout") then c:Destroy() end
    end
    if #State.waypoints == 0 then
        local empty = Instance.new("TextLabel", wpList)
        empty.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,26); empty.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        empty.Text = "  no waypoints saved yet"
        empty.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham; empty.TextSize = 11
        empty.TextColor3 = Theme.textDim
        empty.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        return
    end
    for i, w in ipairs(State.waypoints) do
        local row = Instance.new("Frame", wpList)
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,32); row.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg2
        row.BorderSizePixel = 0; row.LayoutOrder = i
        corner(row, 6)

        local go = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        go.Size = UDim2.new(1,-46,1,0); go.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
        go.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        go.Text = "  " .. w.name .. string.format("   (%d, %d, %d)",
            math.floor(w.x), math.floor(w.y), math.floor(w.z))
        go.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium; go.TextSize = 12
        go.TextColor3 = Theme.text; go.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        go.AutoButtonColor = false
        go.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(row, SilenceAPI._twFade, {BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(30,30,30)}):Play()
        end)
        go.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(row, SilenceAPI._twFade, {BackgroundColor3=Theme.bg2}):Play()
        end)
        go.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() SilenceAPI._gotoWaypoint(i) end)

        local del = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        del.Size = UDim2.new(0,34,0,22); del.Position = UDim2.new(1,-40,0.5,-11)
        del.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120,30,45); del.BorderSizePixel = 0
        del.Text = "X"; del.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; del.TextSize = 12
        del.TextColor3 = Theme.text; del.AutoButtonColor = true
        corner(del, 4)
        del.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            SilenceAPI._deleteWaypoint(i); refreshWaypoints()
        end)
    end
end
buttonRow(pMisc, "Save Current Position", function()
    if SilenceAPI._saveWaypoint(wpNameBox.Text) then
        wpNameBox.Text = ""
        refreshWaypoints()
    end
end)
refreshWaypoints()
SilenceAPI._refreshWaypoints = refreshWaypoints

sectionLabel(pMisc,"SERVER")
buttonRow(pMisc, "Server Hop  (find another server)", function() SilenceAPI._serverHop() end)

sectionLabel(pMisc,"WORLD PHYSICS")
local _, setGravityInput = inputRow(pMisc,"Gravity (default 196.2)", 196.2, function(v)
    local n = tonumber(v); if n then Workspace.Gravity = n end
end, {min=0, max=500})
buttonRow(pMisc,"Reset Gravity", function()
    Workspace.Gravity = origGravity
    if setGravityInput then setGravityInput(origGravity, false) end
end)
local _, setSlowmoInput = inputRow(pMisc,"Slowmo Factor", 1, function(v) setSlowmo(tonumber(v) or 1) end,
    {min=0.1, max=5})
buttonRow(pMisc,"Reset Slowmo", function()
    setSlowmo(1)
    if setSlowmoInput then setSlowmoInput(1, false) end
end)

;(function()
local pHotkeys = addTab("Hotkeys")
sectionLabel(pHotkeys, "CUSTOMIZE KEY BINDINGS")
local function hotkeyRow(label, keyStateGetter, onChange)
    local row = Instance.new("Frame", pHotkeys)
    row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
    row.BorderSizePixel = 0
    corner(row, 9)
    SilenceAPI._theme(row, "BackgroundColor3", "bg2")
    stroke(row, Theme.bg4, 1, 0.5, "bg4")
    -- these were the only rows the header search could not find
    SilenceAPI._registerRow(row, label .. " hotkey")

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0)
    lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 0)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = label
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    lbl.TextSize = 13
    lbl.TextColor3 = Theme.text
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 130, 0, 26)
    btn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -144, 0.5, -13)
    btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    btn.Text = tostring(State.hotkeys[keyStateGetter])
    btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    btn.TextSize = 12
    btn.AutoButtonColor = false
    corner(btn, 7); stroke(btn, Theme.accent, 1, 0.5)
    SilenceAPI._theme(btn, "BackgroundColor3", "bg3")
    SilenceAPI._theme(btn, "TextColor3", "text")

    local listening = false
    local function finish(newKey)
        listening = false
        if newKey then
            State.hotkeys[keyStateGetter] = newKey
            btn.Text = tostring(newKey)
            pcall(onChange, newKey)
        else
            btn.Text = tostring(State.hotkeys[keyStateGetter])
        end
    end

    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        listening = true
        btn.Text = "press a key..."
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg4
        local conn
        conn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
            if listening and not gameProcessed then
                finish(input.KeyCode)
                conn:Disconnect()
                btn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg3
            end
        end)
        task.delay(5, function()
            if listening then
                listening = false
                conn:Disconnect()
                btn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg3
                btn.Text = tostring(State.hotkeys[keyStateGetter])
            end
        end)
    end)
    return row
end

hotkeyRow("Toggle GUI", "toggleGUI", function(k)
    -- keine zusaetzliche Aktion noetig
end)
hotkeyRow("Minimize GUI", "minimize", function(k)
    -- keine zusaetzliche Aktion noetig
end)
hotkeyRow("Stop Emote", "stopEmote", function(k)
    -- keine zusaetzliche Aktion noetig
end)

-- FEATURE KEYBINDS - bind any toggle below to a key of your choice

sectionLabel(pHotkeys, "FEATURE KEYBINDS")

-- id -> { label, get, set }.  Kept in one table so the binder rows, the
-- dispatcher and the config saver all read the same definition.
local BINDABLE = {
    { id="fly",        label="Fly",              get=function() return State.flying end,
      set=function(v) if v then startFly() else stopFly() end end },
    { id="noclip",     label="Noclip",           get=function() return State.noclip end,
      set=function(v) SilenceAPI._setNoclip(v) end },
    { id="infJump",    label="Infinite Jump",    get=function() return State.infJump end,
      set=function(v) State.infJump=v end },
    { id="esp",        label="Player ESP",       get=function() return State.espOn end,  set=setEsp },
    { id="chams",      label="Chams",            get=function() return State.chamsOn end, set=setChams },
    { id="tracers",    label="Tracers",          get=function() return State.tracersOn end, set=setTracers },
    { id="aimbot",     label="Aimbot",           get=function() return State.aimbot end, set=setAimbot },
    { id="freecam",    label="Freecam",          get=function() return State.freecam end, set=setFreecam },
    { id="god",        label="God Mode",         get=function() return State.godmode end, set=setGod },
    { id="antiFling",  label="Anti-Fling",       get=function() return State.antiFling end,
      set=function(v) SilenceAPI._setAntiFling(v) end },
    { id="antiVoid",   label="Anti-Void",        get=function() return State.antiVoid end,
      set=function(v) SilenceAPI._setAntiVoid(v) end },
    { id="ghost",      label="Ghost",            get=function() return State.ghost end,
      set=function(v) SilenceAPI._setGhost(v) end },
    { id="invisParts", label="Invisible Limbs",  get=function() return State.invisParts end,
      set=function(v) SilenceAPI._setInvisParts(v) end },
    { id="headless",   label="Headless",         get=function() return State.headless end,
      set=function(v) SilenceAPI._setHeadless(v) end },
    { id="accessories",label="Custom Accessories", get=function() return State.accessoriesOn end,
      set=function(v) SilenceAPI._setAccessories(v) end },
    { id="itemEsp",    label="Item ESP",         get=function() return State.itemEsp end,
      set=function(v) SilenceAPI._setItemEsp(v) end },
    { id="autoInteract", label="Auto-Interact",  get=function() return State.autoInteract end,
      set=function(v) SilenceAPI._setAutoInteract(v) end },
    { id="vcAutoMute", label="Mute Mic On Join",  get=function() return State.vcAutoMute end,
      set=function(v) State.vcAutoMute = v end },
    -- one-shot actions: no state to flip, so they carry .action instead
    { id="vcPanic", label="PANIC MUTE (cut mic)",
      action=function() if SilenceAPI._vcPanicMute then SilenceAPI._vcPanicMute() end end },
    { id="vcMicToggle", label="Toggle My Microphone",
      action=function() if SilenceAPI._vcToggleMic then SilenceAPI._vcToggleMic() end end },
    { id="wpSave",  label="Save Waypoint Here",
      action=function() SilenceAPI._saveWaypoint("")
                        if SilenceAPI._refreshWaypoints then SilenceAPI._refreshWaypoints() end end },
    { id="wpGo1",   label="Go To Waypoint 1", action=function() SilenceAPI._gotoWaypoint(1) end },
    { id="wpGo2",   label="Go To Waypoint 2", action=function() SilenceAPI._gotoWaypoint(2) end },
    { id="wpGo3",   label="Go To Waypoint 3", action=function() SilenceAPI._gotoWaypoint(3) end },
}
SilenceAPI._bindable = BINDABLE

local function featureKeyRow(entry)
    local row = Instance.new("Frame", pHotkeys)
    row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 38)
    row.BorderSizePixel = 0
    corner(row, 9)
    SilenceAPI._theme(row, "BackgroundColor3", "bg2")
    stroke(row, Theme.bg4, 1, 0.5, "bg4")
    SilenceAPI._registerRow(row, entry.label .. " keybind")

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0); lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 0)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = entry.label
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium; lbl.TextSize = 13
    lbl.TextColor3 = Theme.text
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

    local function keyText()
        local k = State.featureKeys[entry.id]
        return k and tostring(k):gsub("Enum.KeyCode.", "") or "unbound"
    end

    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 120, 0, 26); btn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -168, 0.5, -13)
    btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    btn.Text = keyText()
    btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium; btn.TextSize = 12
    btn.AutoButtonColor = false
    corner(btn, 7); stroke(btn, Theme.accent, 1, 0.5)
    SilenceAPI._theme(btn, "BackgroundColor3", "bg3")
    SilenceAPI._theme(btn, "TextColor3", "text")

    local clr = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
    clr.Size = UDim2.new(0, 34, 0, 26); clr.Position = UDim2.new(1, -42, 0.5, -13)
    clr.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 25, 40); clr.BorderSizePixel = 0
    clr.Text = "X"; clr.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; clr.TextSize = 11
    clr.AutoButtonColor = true
    corner(clr, 7)
    SilenceAPI._theme(clr, "TextColor3", "text")
    clr.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        State.featureKeys[entry.id] = nil
        btn.Text = keyText()
    end)

    local listening = false
    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if listening then return end
        listening = true
        btn.Text = "press a key..."
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg4
        local conn
        local function stop()
            listening = false
            if conn then conn:Disconnect(); conn = nil end
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg3
            btn.Text = keyText()
        end
        conn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
            if not listening or gp then return end
            if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
            if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then stop(); return end
            -- one key, one action
            for otherId, k in pairs(State.featureKeys) do
                if k == input.KeyCode then State.featureKeys[otherId] = nil end
            end
            State.featureKeys[entry.id] = input.KeyCode
            stop()
        end)
        task.delay(5, function() if listening then stop() end end)
    end)
    return row
end

for _, entry in ipairs(BINDABLE) do featureKeyRow(entry) end

-- single dispatcher for every feature keybind
track(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
    if gameProcessed then return end
    if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
    for _, entry in ipairs(BINDABLE) do
        if State.featureKeys[entry.id] == input.KeyCode then
            if entry.action then
                local ok, err = pcall(entry.action)
                if not ok then
                    warn("[Silence] keybind " .. entry.id .. " failed: " .. tostring(err))
                end
                return
            end
            local ok, err = pcall(function() entry.set(not entry.get()) end)
            if ok then
                notify(entry.label, entry.get() and "ON" or "OFF", 1.5)
                for _, r in ipairs(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers) do pcall(r) end
            else
                warn("[Silence] keybind " .. entry.id .. " failed: " .. tostring(err))
            end
            return
        end
    end
end))

sectionLabel(pHotkeys, "NOTES")
local note = Instance.new("TextLabel", pHotkeys)
note.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 54)
note.BackgroundTransparency = 1
note.Text = "Click a button, then press any key to assign. Esc cancels, X unbinds.\nA key can only drive one action - rebinding steals it from the other.\nKeybinds are saved with your config."
note.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
note.TextSize = 11
note.TextColor3 = Theme.textDim
note.TextWrapped = true
note.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

end)()

FloatBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg3

    color = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255) })
do -- same glass sheen the HUD strip and the compact bar carry
    local sh = Instance.new("Frame", FloatBtn)
    sh.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    sh.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    sh.BorderSizePixel = 0

    local g = Instance.new("UIGradient", sh)

;(function()
    local dragging = false
    local dragMoved = false
    local dragStart, startPos

    local function startDrag(input)
        dragging  = true
        dragMoved = false
        dragStart = input.Position
        startPos  = FloatWrap.Position
    end

    FloatBtn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            startDrag(input)
        end
    end)
    FloatWrap.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            startDrag(input)
        end
    end)

    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
                       or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local d = input.Position - dragStart
            if math.abs(d.X) > 4 or math.abs(d.Y) > 4 then dragMoved = true end
            FloatWrap.Position = UDim2.new(
                startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X,
                startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
        end
    end)

    UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            if dragging and not dragMoved then
                Container.Visible = not Container.Visible
            end
            dragging = false
        end
    end)
end)()

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- CUSTOM NOTIFICATIONS (Queue)
----------------------------------------------------------------
-- Up to four cards stack above one another in the bottom-right corner and
-- slide in from off screen. The previous build parked a single card at
-- Position (1,-16),(1,-16) with the default AnchorPoint, which put its
-- top-left corner ON the bottom-right corner of the screen - the whole card
-- was outside the viewport, so notifications were never actually visible.
local notifCards = {}
local notifSlotH = 78
-- forward declared: buildNotif's dismiss() re-stacks the survivors
local layoutNotifs

local function buildNotif(title, text, dur)
    dur = dur or 3

    local card = Instance.new("Frame", Gui)
    card.Name = "__SIL_Notif"
    card.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1)
    card.Size = UDim2.new(0, 330, 0, 70)
    card.Position = UDim2.new(1, 360, 1, -16)
    card.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg2
    card.BorderSizePixel = 0
    card.ZIndex = 300
    -- no travelling glow on these: four of them can be on screen at once and
    -- four rotating gradients in the corner of the eye is a lot of movement
    local _, border = SilenceAPI._glass(card, { radius = 14, transparency = 0.18,
                                                edge = 0.55, glow = false })

    local stripe = Instance.new("Frame", card)
    stripe.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, -22)
    stripe.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 9)
    stripe.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.accent
    stripe.BorderSizePixel = 0
    stripe.ZIndex = 301
    corner(stripe, 2)

    local titleLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", card)
    titleLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 18)
    titleLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 9)
    titleLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    titleLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    titleLbl.TextSize = 13
    titleLbl.TextColor3 = Theme.text
    titleLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    titleLbl.ZIndex = 301
    titleLbl.Text = title or "Silence"

    local bodyLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", card)
    bodyLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 34)
    bodyLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 27)
    bodyLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    bodyLbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    bodyLbl.TextSize = 12
    bodyLbl.TextColor3 = Theme.textDim
    bodyLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    bodyLbl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
    bodyLbl.TextWrapped = true
    bodyLbl.ZIndex = 301
    bodyLbl.Text = text or ""

    -- countdown rail, so a card never disappears without warning
    local prog = Instance.new("Frame", card)
    prog.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 2)
    prog.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -6)
    prog.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.accent
    prog.BorderSizePixel = 0
    prog.ZIndex = 301
    corner(prog, 1)
    TweenService:Create(prog, TweenInfo.new(dur, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear),
        { Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 2) }):Play()

    local clickBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", card)
    clickBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    clickBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    clickBtn.Text = ""
    clickBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
    clickBtn.ZIndex = 302
    clickBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(card, SilenceAPI._twFade, { BackgroundTransparency = 0 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(border, SilenceAPI._twFade, { Transparency = 0.1 }):Play()
    end)
    clickBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(card, SilenceAPI._twFade, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.18 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(border, SilenceAPI._twFade, { Transparency = 0.55 }):Play()
    end)

    table.insert(notifCards, card)
    while #notifCards > 4 do
        local oldest = table.remove(notifCards, 1)
        pcall(function() oldest:Destroy() end)
    end
    layoutNotifs()

    local dismissed = false
    local function dismiss()
        if dismissed then return end
        dismissed = true
        for i = #notifCards, 1, -1 do
            if notifCards[i] == card then table.remove(notifCards, i) end
        end
        if card.Parent then
            local slide = TweenService:Create(card,
                TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In),
                { Position = UDim2.new(1, 360, 1, card.Position.Y.Offset) })
            slide.Completed:Connect(function() pcall(function() card:Destroy() end) end)
            slide:Play()
        end
        layoutNotifs()
    end
    clickBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(dismiss)
    task.delay(dur, dismiss)
end

function layoutNotifs()
    local y = -16
    for i = #notifCards, 1, -1 do
        local c = notifCards[i]
        if c and c.Parent then
            TweenService:Create(c,
                TweenInfo.new(0.24, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                { Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, y) }):Play()
            y = y - notifSlotH
        else
            table.remove(notifCards, i)
        end
    end
end

NotifyStack = {
    push = function(_, title, text, dur)
        pcall(buildNotif, title, text, dur or 3)
    end
}

Container.Size     = UDim2.new(0, SilenceAPI._panelW, 0, SilenceAPI._panelH)
Container.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -SilenceAPI._panelW/2, 0.5, -SilenceAPI._panelH/2)

    TweenInfo.new(0.36, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
    { Scale = 1 }):Play()
SilenceAPI._setMenuBlur()
print("[Silence] GUI geometry locked, Container.Visible=true")

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- COMMAND PARSER
----------------------------------------------------------------
local function execute(raw)
    if not raw or raw=="" then return end
    if raw:sub(1,1)==State.prefix then raw=raw:sub(2) end
    local parts={}
    for w in raw:gmatch("%S+") do table.insert(parts,w) end
    local cmd=(parts[1] or ""):lower()
    table.remove(parts,1)
    local fn=Commands[cmd]
    if fn then
        local ok,err=pcall(fn,parts)
        if not ok then notify("Error",err) warn("[Silence]",err) end
    else
        notify("Unknown","Command: "..cmd)
    end
end
Commands.cmds=function()
    local list={}
    for k,_ in pairs(Commands) do table.insert(list,k) end
    table.sort(list)
    notify("Silence",#list.." commands. See console.",5)
    print("[Silence] Commands: "..table.concat(list,", "))
end
Commands.commands=Commands.cmds
Commands.panel=function() Container.Visible = not Container.Visible end

-- Shift-lock bypass: frees the cursor so the panel can be clicked.
-- It has to stand down in three cases, or it fights the game for the mouse:
--   * freecam drives MouseBehavior itself
--   * minimized, the panel is a small bar and you are playing, not clicking
--   * right/middle button held - that is Roblox locking the cursor in place
--     so the camera can be turned, and forcing Default every frame here is
--     exactly why holding RMB did nothing while the panel was up
track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if not Container.Visible or State.freecam then return end
    if SilenceAPI._minimized then return end
    if UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2)
    or UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton3) then
        return
    end
    if UserInputService.MouseBehavior ~= Enum.MouseBehavior.Default then
        UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
    end
    if not UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled then
        UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = true
    end
end))

SilenceAPI._mainVisible    = function() return Container.Visible end
SilenceAPI._setMainVisible = function(v) Container.Visible = v and true or false end

track(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(i,gp)
    if gp then return end
    if i.KeyCode == State.hotkeys.toggleGUI then
        -- With a window up, the toggle key closes THAT rather than the panel
        -- behind it. Otherwise the only way out of an editor is its X, and
        -- the key would hide a panel you cannot even see.
        if SilenceAPI._closeTopModal and SilenceAPI._closeTopModal() then return end
        Container.Visible = not Container.Visible
    elseif i.KeyCode == State.hotkeys.minimize then
        -- collapses to the header bar rather than vanishing, so the panel
        -- can always be brought back from the same place
        SilenceAPI._setMinimized(not SilenceAPI._minimized)
    end
end))
track(LocalPlayer.Chatted:Connect(function(msg)
    if msg:sub(1,1)==State.prefix then execute(msg) end
end))
track(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    task.wait(0.5)
    origHeadSize=nil
    if State.flying    then startFly(State.flySpeed) end
    if State.trail     then setTrail(true) end
    if State.bighead   then setBigHead(4) end
    if State.crashFx   then setCrashFx(true) end
end))

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- LARP STUDIO
-- One window for the whole costume: the accessory slots, the body switches,
-- a live view of what you actually look like, and a list of the other
-- Silence users whose costume you can switch off for yourself.
--
-- The preview is a ViewportFrame with a CLONE of your character in it. It
-- has to be a clone: a ViewportFrame only renders what is parented inside
-- it, and moving the real character in there would take it out of the world.
-- The clone is rebuilt on demand rather than every frame, because copying a
-- rigged character is not cheap.
--
-- An IIFE: a do..end shares the chunk's registers and this file runs at 198
-- of Luau's 200.
----------------------------------------------------------------
