end

local refreshPreview
local function flatBtn(parent, label, x, y, w, cb)
    local b = Instance.new("TextButton", parent)
    b.Size = UDim2.new(0, w, 0, 22)
    b.Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, y)
    b.BorderSizePixel = 0
    b.Text = label
    b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; b.TextSize = 10
    b.AutoButtonColor = false
    b.ZIndex = 193
    corner(b, 6)
    b.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg3
    SilenceAPI._theme(b, "TextColor3", "textDim")
    b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(b, SilenceAPI._twFade,
            { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.accent }):Play()
        b.TextColor3 = Theme.bg
    end)
    b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(b, SilenceAPI._twFade,
            { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg3 }):Play()
        b.TextColor3 = Theme.textDim
    end)
    b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(cb)
    return b
end

------------------------------------------------------------ slots
cap("COSTUME SLOTS")
local slotPaints = {}
for i, sl in ipairs(State.accSlots) do
    order = order + 1
    local r = Instance.new("Frame", left)
    r.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 62)
    r.BorderSizePixel = 0
    r.LayoutOrder = order
    r.ZIndex = 192
    corner(r, 9)
    SilenceAPI._theme(r, "BackgroundColor3", "bg2")

    local nm = Instance.new("TextBox", r)
    nm.Size = UDim2.new(0, 130, 0, 20); nm.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 8)
    nm.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    nm.Text = sl.name
    nm.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; nm.TextSize = 12
    nm.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    nm.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    nm.ZIndex = 193
    SilenceAPI._theme(nm, "TextColor3", "text")
    nm.FocusLost:Connect(function() sl.name = nm.Text:sub(1, 18) end)

    local idb = Instance.new("TextBox", r)
    idb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 150, 0, 20); idb.Position = UDim2.new(0, 150, 0, 8)
    idb.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    idb.Text = tostring(sl.id or "")
    idb.PlaceholderText = "asset id"
    idb.Font = Enum.Font.Code; idb.TextSize = 11
    idb.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    idb.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    idb.ZIndex = 193
    SilenceAPI._theme(idb, "TextColor3", "textDim")
    idb.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        SilenceAPI._setSlotId(i, idb.Text)
        idb.Text = tostring(State.accSlots[i].id or "")
        refreshPreview()
    end)

    local onBtn = flatBtn(r, "OFF", 12, 32, 62, function()
        SilenceAPI._setSlot(i, not State.accSlots[i].on)
        for _, fn in ipairs(slotPaints) do fn() end
        refreshPreview()
    end)
    -- setSlotPart(i, part) wants the NAME of the target. Calling it with
    -- just the index handed it nil, which fails its validity check and falls
    -- back to "Head" - so the button silently reset every slot instead of
    -- cycling it, and a leg slot could never be set at all.
    local partBtn = flatBtn(r, sl.part, 82, 32, 74, function()
        local ordr = SilenceAPI._slotPartOrder or { "Head" }
        local cur, nxt = State.accSlots[i].part, ordr[1]
        for k, nm in ipairs(ordr) do
            if nm == cur then nxt = ordr[(k % #ordr) + 1] break end
        end
        SilenceAPI._setSlotPart(i, nxt)
        for _, fn in ipairs(slotPaints) do fn() end
        refreshPreview()
    end)
    local hideBtn = flatBtn(r, "HIDE", 164, 32, 62, function()
        SilenceAPI._setSlotHide(i, not State.accSlots[i].hide)
        for _, fn in ipairs(slotPaints) do fn() end
        refreshPreview()
    end)

    local function paint()
        local d = State.accSlots[i]
        onBtn.Text = d.on and "ON" or "OFF"
        onBtn.BackgroundColor3 = d.on and Theme.accent or Theme.bg3
        onBtn.TextColor3 = d.on and Theme.bg or Theme.textDim
        partBtn.Text = d.part
        hideBtn.BackgroundColor3 = d.hide and Theme.accent or Theme.bg3
        hideBtn.TextColor3 = d.hide and Theme.bg or Theme.textDim
    end
    slotPaints[#slotPaints + 1] = paint
    paint()
    table.insert(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers, paint)
end

cap("BODY")
do
    order = order + 1
    local r = Instance.new("Frame", left)
    r.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 76)

    local defs = {
        { "HEADLESS", function() return State.headless end,
          function(v) SilenceAPI._setHeadless(v) end },
        { "INVIS LIMBS", function() return State.invisParts end,
          function(v) SilenceAPI._setInvisParts(v) end },
        { "GHOST", function() return State.ghost end,
          function(v) SilenceAPI._setGhost(v) end },
        { "ACCESSORIES", function() return State.accessoriesOn end,
          function(v) SilenceAPI._setAccessories(v) end },
    }
    for k, d in ipairs(defs) do
        local b
        b = flatBtn(r, d[1], 12 + (k - 1) * 122, 10, 114, function()
            d[3](not d[2]())
            refreshPreview()
        end)
        local function paint()
            local on = d[2]()
            b.BackgroundColor3 = on and Theme.accent or Theme.bg3
            b.TextColor3 = on and Theme.bg or Theme.textDim
        end
        paint()
        table.insert(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers, paint)

    local scale = Instance.new("TextBox", r)

    face.ZIndex = 193
    SilenceAPI._theme(face, "TextColor3", "text")
    face.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        SilenceAPI._setFace(face.Text)
        refreshPreview()
    end)
end

local vp = Instance.new("ViewportFrame", vpBox)
vp.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -34)
vp.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 24)
vp.BackgroundTransparency = 1
vp.BorderSizePixel = 0
vp.ZIndex = 192
-- Without these a ViewportFrame lights nothing and the model is a black
-- silhouette on a black panel, which looks exactly like "nothing is there".
vp.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(190, 190, 195)
vp.LightColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
vp.LightDirection = Vector3.new(-0.4, -1, -0.6)
local vpCam = Instance.new("Camera")
vpCam.Parent = vp
vp.CurrentCamera = vpCam

local clone, spin = nil, 0
refreshPreview = function()
    task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(0.35)   -- let the setter finish mounting before copying
        if clone then pcall(function() clone:Destroy() end); clone = nil end
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not char then return end
        local ok, c = pcall(function() return char:Clone() end)
        if not ok or not c then return end
        for _, d in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("BaseScript") or d:IsA("Sound") or d:IsA("BillboardGui")
            or d:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or d:IsA("Fire") or d:IsA("Smoke") then
                pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
            elseif d:IsA("BasePart") then
                -- nothing simulates inside a ViewportFrame, but an unanchored
                -- part still reports oddly and the humanoid keeps ticking
                d.Anchored = true
                d.CanCollide = false
            end
        end
        local hum = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then pcall(function() hum.DisplayDistanceType =
            Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None end) end
        c.Parent = vp
        clone = c
    end)
end

track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
    if not LS.Visible or not clone or not clone.Parent then return end
    spin = (spin + dt * 0.6) % (math.pi * 2)
    -- GetBoundingBox centres on the whole rig rather than on the root part,
    -- which sits at hip height and left the head cropped off the top.
    local okB, cf, size = pcall(function() return clone:GetBoundingBox() end)
    if not okB or not cf then return end
    local p = cf.Position
    local dist = math.max(size and size.Magnitude or 8, 6) * 1.1
    vpCam.CFrame = CFrame.new(
        p + Vector3.new(math.sin(spin) * dist, dist * 0.15, math.cos(spin) * dist), p)
end))

local function rebuildOthers()
    for _, c in ipairs(othList:GetChildren()) do
        if not c:IsA("UIListLayout") then c:Destroy() end
    end
    local n = 0
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= LocalPlayer then
            n = n + 1
            local r = Instance.new("Frame", othList)
            r.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 30)
            r.BorderSizePixel = 0
            r.LayoutOrder = n
            r.ZIndex = 192
            corner(r, 7)
            SilenceAPI._theme(r, "BackgroundColor3", "bg2")

            local l = Instance.new("TextLabel", r)
            l.Size = UDim2.new(1, -90, 1, 0); l.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
            l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            l.Text = p.Name
            l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium; l.TextSize = 11
            l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            l.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
            l.ZIndex = 193
            SilenceAPI._theme(l, "TextColor3", "text")

            local b
            b = flatBtn(r, "SHOWN", 0, 4, 76, function()
                local hid = SilenceAPI._isLarpHidden and SilenceAPI._isLarpHidden(p)
                if SilenceAPI._setLarpHidden then
                    SilenceAPI._setLarpHidden(p, not hid)
                end
                local now = SilenceAPI._isLarpHidden and SilenceAPI._isLarpHidden(p)
                b.Text = now and "HIDDEN" or "SHOWN"
                b.BackgroundColor3 = now and Theme.accent or Theme.bg3
                b.TextColor3 = now and Theme.bg or Theme.textDim
            end)
            b.Position = UDim2.new(1, -84, 0, 4)
            local hid = SilenceAPI._isLarpHidden and SilenceAPI._isLarpHidden(p)
            b.Text = hid and "HIDDEN" or "SHOWN"
            b.BackgroundColor3 = hid and Theme.accent or Theme.bg3
            b.TextColor3 = hid and Theme.bg or Theme.textDim
        end
    end
    if n == 0 then
        local e = Instance.new("TextLabel", othList)
        e.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26)
        e.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        e.Text = "  nobody else here"
        e.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham; e.TextSize = 11
        e.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        e.ZIndex = 192
        SilenceAPI._theme(e, "TextColor3", "textDim")
    end
end
rebuildOthers()
track(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function() task.wait(0.5) rebuildOthers() end))
track(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function() task.wait(0.2) rebuildOthers() end))

LS:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
    if LS.Visible then rebuildOthers(); refreshPreview() end
end)
track(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    task.wait(1.5); if LS.Visible then refreshPreview() end
end))

SilenceAPI._openLarpStudio = function() LS.Visible = not LS.Visible end
-- A BillboardGui lives in the world, not in this ScreenGui, so no amount of
-- Active on a Frame stops a click reaching the tag over your head. The tag
-- has to stand down by itself while a window is open.
SilenceAPI._modalWatch(LS)
end)()

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- SKY
-- Purely local: Lighting, Atmosphere and post-processing belong to your
-- client, so none of this is visible to anyone else and none of it touches
-- the game's own state on the server.
--
-- Everything it changes is written down first and put back by RESET, because
-- a game that dims the lights for a cutscene and finds them already dimmed
-- has no way to recover on its own.
--
-- An IIFE, not do..end: a do..end shares the chunk's registers, and this
-- file runs at 198 of Luau's 200.
----------------------------------------------------------------
;(function()
local pSky = addTab("Sky")

local LIGHT_KEYS = { "ClockTime", "Brightness", "Ambient", "OutdoorAmbient",
                     "FogColor", "FogStart", "FogEnd", "ColorShift_Top",
                     "ColorShift_Bottom", "GlobalShadows", "ExposureCompensation" }
local SKY_KEYS   = { "SkyboxUp", "SkyboxDn", "SkyboxLf", "SkyboxRt",
                     "SkyboxFt", "SkyboxBk", "SunTextureId", "MoonTextureId",
                     "StarCount", "CelestialBodiesShown", "MoonAngularSize",
                     "SunAngularSize" }

local orig, origSky, made, weatherOn = nil, nil, {}, false

local function ownSky()
    local sk = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Sky")
    if not sk then
        sk = Instance.new("Sky")
        sk.Name = "__SIL_Sky"
        sk.Parent = Lighting
        made[#made+1] = sk
    end
    return sk
end

local function remember()
    if orig then return end
    orig = {}
    for _, k in ipairs(LIGHT_KEYS) do
        local ok, v = pcall(function() return Lighting[k] end)
        if ok then orig[k] = v end
    end
    local sk = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Sky")
    if sk then
        origSky = {}
        for _, k in ipairs(SKY_KEYS) do
            local ok, v = pcall(function() return sk[k] end)
            if ok then origSky[k] = v end
        end
    end
end

local function clearMade()
    weatherOn = false
    for _, o in ipairs(made) do pcall(function() o:Destroy() end) end
    made = {}
end

local function resetSky()
    clearMade()
    if orig then
        for k, v in pairs(orig) do pcall(function() Lighting[k] = v end) end
    end
    local sk = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Sky")
    if sk and origSky then
        for k, v in pairs(origSky) do pcall(function() sk[k] = v end) end
    end
    notify("Sky", "Put back the way the game had it.", 3)
end

-- One part high above the camera, moved with it, so the weather follows you
-- instead of being a puddle of particles where you switched it on.
local function weather(kind, col, rate, speed, size, rise)
    local p = Instance.new("Part")
    p.Name = "__SIL_Weather"
    p.Size = Vector3.new(140, 1, 140)
    p.Transparency = 1
    p.Anchored = true
    p.CanCollide = false
    p.CanQuery = false
    p.CanTouch = false
    p.Locked = true
    p.Parent = Workspace
    made[#made+1] = p

    local e = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter", p)
    e.Texture = kind
    e.Rate = rate
    e.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(1.6, 2.6)
    e.Speed = NumberRange.new(speed * 0.8, speed)
    e.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(8, 8)
    e.Size = NumberSequence.new(size)
    e.Color = ColorSequence.new(col)
    e.LightEmission = 0.4
    e.LightInfluence = 0
    e.EmissionDirection = rise and Enum.NormalId.Top or Enum.NormalId.Bottom
    e.Acceleration = Vector3.new(0, rise and 8 or -70, 0)
    e.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,    1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.12, 0.1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.9,  0.2),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,    1),
    })

    weatherOn = true
    task.spawn(function()
        while weatherOn and p.Parent do
            local c = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if c then
                p.CFrame = CFrame.new(c.CFrame.Position
                    + Vector3.new(0, rise and -12 or 60, 0))
            end
            task.wait()
        end
    end)
end

local function tint(col, contrast, sat, bloom)
    local cc = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
    cc.Name = "__SIL_CC"
    cc.TintColor = col
    cc.Contrast = contrast or 0
    cc.Saturation = sat or 0
    cc.Parent = Lighting
    made[#made+1] = cc
    if bloom and bloom > 0 then
        local b = Instance.new("BloomEffect")
        b.Name = "__SIL_Bloom"
        b.Intensity = bloom
        b.Size = 10          -- 24 smeared light across whole surfaces
        b.Threshold = 1.4    -- 0.8 caught mid-tones, not just highlights
        b.Parent = Lighting
        made[#made+1] = b
    end
end

-- a slow pulse on the tint, for the presets that should breathe
local function pulse(cc, lo, hi, period)
    task.spawn(function()
        while weatherOn == false and cc.Parent do task.wait(0.2) end
        while cc.Parent do
            local t = (tick() % period) / period
            local k = lo + (hi - lo) * (0.5 - 0.5 * math.cos(t * math.pi * 2))
            cc.Brightness = k
            task.wait()
        end
    end)
end

local RAIN   = "rbxasset://textures/particles/sparkles_main.dds"
local SMOKE  = "rbxasset://textures/particles/smoke_main.dds"
local FIRE   = "rbxasset://textures/particles/fire_main.dds"

                task.wait(0.06)
                Lighting.Brightness = b
                task.wait(0.07)

            end

        Lighting.FogColor = Color3.fromRGB(55, 48, 62)
        Lighting.FogEnd = 1600
        local sk = ownSky()
        sk.SunAngularSize = 40

    end },
}

local function applyPreset(ps)
    remember()
    clearMade()
    -- back to a known state first, or two presets stack their fog and tints
    if orig then
        for k, v in pairs(orig) do pcall(function() Lighting[k] = v end) end
    end
    local ok, err = pcall(ps.apply)
    if not ok then
        notify("Sky", "Preset failed: " .. tostring(err):sub(1, 70), 6)
        return
    end
    notify("Sky", ps.label, 3)
end

    remember()
    local n = math.clamp(tonumber(v) or 14, 0, 24)
    pcall(function() Lighting.ClockTime = n end)
end, { min = 0, max = 24 })
buttonRow(pSky, "Force Day", function()
    remember(); pcall(function() Lighting.ClockTime = 14 end)
end)

    remember(); pcall(function() Lighting.ClockTime = 0 end)
end)

    function() return State.fullbright end,
    function(v) remember(); SilenceAPI._setFullbright(v) end)

    function() return State.disco end,
    function(v) remember(); SilenceAPI._setDisco(v) end)

            TweenService:Create(bs, SilenceAPI._twFade,

    end
end

            .. " down before the first change, so RESET puts the game's own"
            .. " lighting back exactly as it was."
