----------------------------------------------------------------
-- GAMES  /  GERMAN VOICE
-- An aura is drawn by whoever is looking at it, never by the server. A
-- client cannot make another client render anything - instances it creates
-- do not replicate - so "everyone can see it" is not something a local
-- script can do on its own.
--
-- What it CAN do is the trick the tags already use: publish the settings to
-- the endpoint and let every other Silence client draw you with them. The
-- result looks identical from inside the group. Players without Silence see
-- nothing, and there is no way around that which does not involve making the
-- game's own server hand out items it was supposed to charge for.
--
-- An IIFE rather than do..end. A do..end block shares registers with the
-- chunk around it, so the locals below would be live alongside its 198 and
-- push the total to 210 - past Luau's limit of 200. That is a COMPILE error,
-- not a runtime one: the script fails to load and nothing appears at all,
-- which is exactly what happened. A function body has its own registers,
-- which is why every other tab block in this file is written this way.
----------------------------------------------------------------
;(function()
local pGV = addTab("German Voice")

-- Engine textures only. An uploaded asset id would fail on the first client
-- whose game has it moderated or unavailable; these ship with Roblox.
local AURA_STYLES = {
    { key = "sparkle", label = "Sparkle",
      tex = "rbxasset://textures/particles/sparkles_main.dds",
      rate = 26, speed = 2.0, size = 1.2, life = 1.3, spread = 45 },
    { key = "flame",   label = "Flame",
      tex = "rbxasset://textures/particles/fire_main.dds",
      rate = 44, speed = 4.0, size = 2.2, life = 0.9, spread = 14 },
    { key = "smoke",   label = "Smoke",
      tex = "rbxasset://textures/particles/smoke_main.dds",
      rate = 16, speed = 1.4, size = 3.2, life = 2.1, spread = 32 },
    { key = "storm",   label = "Storm",
      tex = "rbxasset://textures/particles/sparkles_main.dds",
      rate = 70, speed = 8.0, size = 0.8, life = 0.6, spread = 110 },
}
local AURAMAP = {}
for _, a in ipairs(AURA_STYLES) do AURAMAP[a.key] = a end

local auraFor = {}   -- [player] = { instances }

local function killAura(player)
    local set = auraFor[player]
    if set then
        for _, o in ipairs(set) do
            pcall(function()
                -- stop emitting, wipe what is already in the air, then go
                if o:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then o.Enabled = false; o:Clear() end
                o:Destroy()
            end)
        end
    end
    auraFor[player] = nil

    -- Sweep by name as well. The registry only knows what the last build put
    -- in it; an orphan from any other path would otherwise sit there forever
    -- with nothing holding a reference to it.
    local char = player and player.Character
    if char then
        for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if d.Name == "__SIL_Aura" or d.Name == "__SIL_AuraLight" then
                pcall(function()
                    if d:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then d.Enabled = false; d:Clear() end
                    d:Destroy()
                end)
            end
        end
    end
end

-- cfg is the same shape whether it came from State.aura or off the endpoint
local function buildAura(player, cfg)
    killAura(player)
    if not player or not cfg or not cfg.on then return end
    local char = player.Character
    if not char then return end
    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                 or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                 or char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
    if not root then return end

    local st = AURAMAP[cfg.style] or AURA_STYLES[1]
    local c1 = cfg.color  or Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60)
    local c2 = cfg.color2 or c1
    local sz = tonumber(cfg.size) or 1
    local rt = tonumber(cfg.rate) or 1
    local set = {}

    local pe = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
    pe.Name = "__SIL_Aura"
    pe.Texture = st.tex
    pe.Rate = st.rate * rt
    pe.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(st.life * 0.7, st.life)
    pe.Speed = NumberRange.new(st.speed * 0.4, st.speed)
    pe.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(st.spread, st.spread)
    pe.Size = NumberSequence.new(st.size * sz)
    pe.LightEmission = 1
    pe.LightInfluence = 0
    pe.Color = cfg.gradient and ColorSequence.new(c1, c2) or ColorSequence.new(c1)
    -- fade in and out rather than popping, which is what makes it read as an
    -- aura instead of a cloud of dots
    pe.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,    1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.18, 0.15),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,    1),
    })
    pe.Parent = root
    set[#set+1] = pe

    local lt = Instance.new("PointLight")
    lt.Name = "__SIL_AuraLight"
    lt.Color = c1
    lt.Range = 10 * sz
    lt.Brightness = 2
    lt.Parent = root
    set[#set+1] = lt

    auraFor[player] = set
end

-- Reachable from the tag block, which is what draws other people's auras
-- once their settings arrive from the endpoint.
SilenceAPI._applyAuraFor = function(player, cfg) pcall(buildAura, player, cfg) end
SilenceAPI._killAuraFor  = function(player) pcall(killAura, player) end

local function refreshOwnAura()
    buildAura(LocalPlayer, State.aura)
end
SilenceAPI._refreshAura = refreshOwnAura

-- One shared cycle for every rainbow aura in the server, so twenty of them
-- cost one loop rather than twenty.
track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    local hue = (tick() * 0.25) % 1
    local col = Color3.fromHSV(hue, 0.9, 1)
    for player, set in pairs(auraFor) do
        local want = (player == LocalPlayer) and State.aura
                     or (SilenceAPI._auraOf and SilenceAPI._auraOf(player))
        if want and want.rainbow then
            for _, o in ipairs(set) do
                if o:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
                    o.Color = ColorSequence.new(col)
                elseif o:IsA("PointLight") then
                    o.Color = col
                end
            end
        end
    end
end))

track(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    task.wait(1)
    refreshOwnAura()
end))
track(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p) killAura(p) end))

------------------------------------------------------------ UI
sectionLabel(pGV, "AURA")
toggleRow(pGV, "Aura", function() return State.aura.on end, function(v)
    State.aura.on = v
    refreshOwnAura()
    if SilenceAPI._pushStyle then SilenceAPI._pushStyle() end
end)

local styleBox = Instance.new("Frame", pGV)
styleBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
styleBox.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
styleBox.BackgroundTransparency = 1
local styleLay = Instance.new("UIGridLayout", styleBox)
styleLay.CellSize = UDim2.new(0, 120, 0, 30)
styleLay.CellPadding = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 6)
styleLay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
SilenceAPI._registerRow(styleBox, "aura style sparkle flame smoke storm")

for i, a in ipairs(AURA_STYLES) do
    local b = Instance.new("TextButton", styleBox)
    b.BorderSizePixel = 0
    b.Text = a.label
    b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    b.TextSize = 12
    b.AutoButtonColor = false
    b.LayoutOrder = i
    corner(b, 8)
    local bs = stroke(b, Theme.bg4, 1, 0.6, "bg4")
    local function paint()
        local on = (State.aura.style == a.key)
        b.BackgroundColor3 = on and Theme.accent or Theme.bg2
        b.TextColor3       = on and Theme.bg     or Theme.text
        bs.Transparency    = on and 0 or 0.6
    end
    paint()
    table.insert(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers, paint)
    b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        State.aura.style = a.key
        refreshOwnAura()
        for _, fn in ipairs(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers) do pcall(fn) end
        if SilenceAPI._pushStyle then SilenceAPI._pushStyle() end
    end)
end

sectionLabel(pGV, "COLOURS")
buttonRow(pGV, "Aura Colour", function()
    openColorPicker(State.aura.color, function(c)
        State.aura.color = c
        refreshOwnAura()
        if SilenceAPI._pushStyle then SilenceAPI._pushStyle() end
    end, "Aura")
end)
buttonRow(pGV, "Gradient Second Colour", function()
    openColorPicker(State.aura.color2, function(c)
        State.aura.color2 = c
        refreshOwnAura()
        if SilenceAPI._pushStyle then SilenceAPI._pushStyle() end
    end, "Aura gradient")
end)
toggleRow(pGV, "Gradient", function() return State.aura.gradient end, function(v)
    State.aura.gradient = v
    refreshOwnAura()
    if SilenceAPI._pushStyle then SilenceAPI._pushStyle() end
end)
toggleRow(pGV, "Rainbow  (overrides the colours)",
    function() return State.aura.rainbow end, function(v)
    State.aura.rainbow = v
    refreshOwnAura()
    if SilenceAPI._pushStyle then SilenceAPI._pushStyle() end
end)

sectionLabel(pGV, "SHAPE")
inputRow(pGV, "Size", State.aura.size, function(v)
    State.aura.size = math.clamp(tonumber(v) or 1, 0.2, 5)
    refreshOwnAura()
    if SilenceAPI._pushStyle then SilenceAPI._pushStyle() end
end, { min = 0.2, max = 5 })
inputRow(pGV, "Density", State.aura.rate, function(v)
    State.aura.rate = math.clamp(tonumber(v) or 1, 0.1, 4)
    refreshOwnAura()
    if SilenceAPI._pushStyle then SilenceAPI._pushStyle() end
end, { min = 0.1, max = 4 })

sectionLabel(pGV, "WHO SEES THIS")
local gvHint = Instance.new("TextLabel", pGV)
gvHint.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 92)
gvHint.BackgroundTransparency = 1
gvHint.Text = "  Your aura travels to every other Silence user through the same\n"
           .. "  endpoint the tag designs use, so they see it on you within a\n"
           .. "  few seconds. Players without Silence see nothing - a client\n"
           .. "  cannot make another client draw something, only the server can,\n"
           .. "  and reaching that far means making the game hand out items it\n"
           .. "  was meant to charge for. That part is not in here."
gvHint.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
gvHint.TextSize = 10
gvHint.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
gvHint.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
gvHint.TextWrapped = true
gvHint.TextColor3 = Theme.textDim

task.spawn(function() task.wait(2) refreshOwnAura() end)
end)()

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- SILENCE USER TAG
-- Wrapped in a pcall on purpose. This block is long, it talks to the
-- Marketplace and to the network, and everything that comes AFTER it in the
-- file (Logs tab, Config tab, the /silenceadmin listener) is built later.
-- Letting it throw uncaught meant one bad line in here silently deleted
-- three unrelated features.
----------------------------------------------------------------
local _tagOk, _tagErr = pcall(function()
    local TAG_MARKER = "\194\160\226\136\158SILENCE\226\136\158\194\160"
    local silenceUsers = { [LocalPlayer] = true }
    local activeTags   = {}
    local customTitles = {}

    local TITLES_FILE = "silence_custom_titles.json"
    local function saveCustomTitles()
        local HS = game:GetService("HttpService")
        local data = {}
        for uid, t in pairs(customTitles) do data[tostring(uid)] = t end
        pcall(function() writefile(TITLES_FILE, HS:JSONEncode(data)) end)
    end
    local function loadCustomTitles()
        local HS = game:GetService("HttpService")
        local ok, content = pcall(function() return readfile(TITLES_FILE) end)
        if not ok or not content or content == "" then return end
        local ok2, data = pcall(function() return HS:JSONDecode(content) end)
        if not ok2 or type(data) ~= "table" then return end
        for uid, t in pairs(data) do
            local n = tonumber(uid)
            if n and type(t) == "string" then customTitles[n] = t end
        end
        print("[Silence] loaded custom titles")
    end
    loadCustomTitles()

    local TagSettingsPopup
    local syncEditorTitle = function() end

    local TagSettings = {
        bg        = Theme.bg,
        border    = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
        accent    = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
        text      = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
        sub       = Color3.fromRGB(170, 170, 170),
        particle  = "None",
        title     = "SILENCE USER",
        font      = Enum.Font.GothamBold,  -- title face
        titleSize = 11,
        showUser  = true,                  -- draw the @username line
        radius    = 10,                    -- corner rounding, in px
        bgAlpha   = 0,                     -- 0 solid .. 1 invisible
        glow      = 1.5,                   -- travelling glow thickness
        width     = 170,                   -- px, before the distance scaler
        bg2       = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 28),  -- second gradient stop
        gradient  = false,
        gradRot   = 90,
        outline   = false,                 -- text stroke behind the title
        outlineCol= Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
        rainbow   = false,                 -- cycle the edge through the hues
        rainbowSpeed = 0.2,
        showBadge = true,                  -- the round avatar on the left
        centered  = false,                 -- centre the text instead of left
        offsetY   = 2.8,                   -- studs above the head
        maxDist   = 1000,                  -- studs before it stops drawing
        -- How much the card fights perspective. 1 pins it to a constant
        -- number of pixels no matter how far away the player is, which is
        -- what it used to do unconditionally - and next to a character that
        -- has shrunk to nothing, a full size card looks enormous. 0 lets it
        -- shrink like any object in the world, which is unreadable at range.
        -- 0.5 keeps it legible without taking over the screen.
        distGrow  = 0.5,
        -- Unicode codepoint, 0 for none. Stored as a NUMBER and turned into
        -- text with utf8.char at runtime, so the source file stays pure
        -- ASCII - a raw emoji in here would be a high byte, and this file
        -- has a hard rule against those because most of its fonts render
        -- them as empty boxes.
        emoji     = 0,
        partRate  = 1,                     -- particle spawn multiplier
        pulse     = false,                 -- slow breathing scale
    }
    -- the card loses its second line when the username is hidden, and the
    -- distance scaler further down has to agree with that
    -- utf8.char is missing on nothing that runs Luau, but a bad codepoint
    -- would throw and take a tag down with it, so it stays guarded.
    function SilenceAPI._emojiPrefix(cp)
        cp = tonumber(cp) or 0
        if cp <= 0 then return "" end
        local ok, ch = pcall(function() return utf8.char(cp) end)
        if ok and type(ch) == "string" and ch ~= "" then return ch .. " " end
        return ""
    end

    local function tagH() return TagSettings.showUser and 42 or 34 end
    local function lookH(lk) return lk.showUser and 42 or 34 end

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- RANKS
    -- A rank is a title plus a PARTIAL look. Anything it does not name falls
    -- through to TagSettings via the metatable, so every tag in the server
    -- keeps the same shape and only the colours say who is who. Layout
    -- (width, badge, username line, height) is deliberately not overridable:
    -- the distance scaler assumes one size for every tag.
    --
    -- The first entry carries no look at all, which is what lets your own tag
    -- keep whatever you built in the editor while everyone else defaults.
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    local playerRank = {}   -- [userId] = rank key
    local RANKS = {
        { key = "user",    title = "SILENCE USER" },
        { key = "vip",     title = "VIP", look = {
            bg = Color3.fromRGB(16,12,2), bg2 = Color3.fromRGB(58,44,6),
            accent = Color3.fromRGB(255,205,70), border = Color3.fromRGB(255,205,70),
            text = Color3.fromRGB(255,246,214), sub = Color3.fromRGB(205,180,120),
            gradient = true, gradRot = 60, glow = 3, particle = "Money" } },
        { key = "premium", title = "PREMIUM", look = {
            bg = Color3.fromRGB(8,4,16), bg2 = Color3.fromRGB(38,10,64),
            accent = Color3.fromRGB(170,90,255), border = Color3.fromRGB(170,90,255),
            text = Color3.fromRGB(240,225,255), sub = Color3.fromRGB(160,130,200),
            gradient = true, gradRot = 45, glow = 3, particle = "Sparkles" } },
        { key = "og",      title = "OG", look = {
            bg = Color3.fromRGB(2,12,12), bg2 = Color3.fromRGB(4,44,44),
            accent = Color3.fromRGB(70,230,210), border = Color3.fromRGB(70,230,210),
            text = Color3.fromRGB(225,255,252), sub = Color3.fromRGB(130,195,190),
            gradient = true, gradRot = 90, glow = 2.5, particle = "Stars" } },
        { key = "staff",   title = "STAFF", look = {
            bg = Color3.fromRGB(2,8,18), bg2 = Color3.fromRGB(6,30,66),
            accent = Color3.fromRGB(80,160,255), border = Color3.fromRGB(80,160,255),
            text = Color3.fromRGB(228,240,255), sub = Color3.fromRGB(140,175,215),
            gradient = true, gradRot = 90, glow = 2.5, particle = "None" } },
        { key = "mod",     title = "MODERATOR", look = {
            bg = Color3.fromRGB(4,14,6), bg2 = Color3.fromRGB(10,44,18),
            accent = Color3.fromRGB(120,255,140), border = Color3.fromRGB(120,255,140),
            text = Color3.fromRGB(230,255,235), sub = Color3.fromRGB(130,190,150),
            gradient = true, gradRot = 120, glow = 3, particle = "None" } },
        { key = "dev",     title = "DEVELOPER", look = {
            bg = Color3.fromRGB(2,10,14), bg2 = Color3.fromRGB(4,40,52),
            accent = Color3.fromRGB(60,240,255), border = Color3.fromRGB(60,240,255),
            text = Color3.fromRGB(220,252,255), sub = Color3.fromRGB(120,190,205),
            gradient = true, gradRot = 0, glow = 3.5, particle = "Bolts" } },
        { key = "admin",   title = "ADMIN", look = {
            bg = Color3.fromRGB(18,2,3), bg2 = Color3.fromRGB(62,4,8),
            accent = Color3.fromRGB(255,70,70), border = Color3.fromRGB(255,70,70),
            text = Color3.fromRGB(255,228,228), sub = Color3.fromRGB(200,130,130),
            gradient = true, gradRot = 90, glow = 3.5,
            outline = true, outlineCol = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0), particle = "Bolts" } },
        { key = "coowner", title = "CO OWNER", look = {
            bg = Color3.fromRGB(16,6,2), bg2 = Color3.fromRGB(58,18,4),
            accent = Color3.fromRGB(255,140,40), border = Color3.fromRGB(255,140,40),
            text = Color3.fromRGB(255,235,215), sub = Color3.fromRGB(200,150,110),
            gradient = true, gradRot = 90, glow = 4,
            outline = true, outlineCol = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0), particle = "Fire" } },
        { key = "owner",   title = "OWNER", look = {
            bg = Color3.fromRGB(20,0,0), bg2 = Color3.fromRGB(70,6,10),
            accent = Color3.fromRGB(255,60,80), border = Color3.fromRGB(255,60,80),
            text = Color3.fromRGB(255,235,240), sub = Color3.fromRGB(220,160,175),
            gradient = true, gradRot = 90, glow = 4.5,
            outline = true, outlineCol = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0), particle = "Blood" } },
        { key = "founder", title = "FOUNDER", look = {
            bg = Color3.fromRGB(6,6,6), bg2 = Color3.fromRGB(26,26,26),
            accent = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255), border = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255),
            text = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255), sub = Color3.fromRGB(200,200,200),
            gradient = false, glow = 5, rainbow = true, rainbowSpeed = 0.35,
            outline = true, outlineCol = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0), particle = "Sparkles" } },
    }
    local RANKMAP = {}
    for _, r in ipairs(RANKS) do
        if r.look then setmetatable(r.look, { __index = TagSettings }) end
        RANKMAP[r.key] = r
    end

    -- Everyone is drawn with THEIR OWN design. A player publishes the same
    -- SILTAG1 string the EXPORT page produces, everybody else pulls it and
    -- draws them with it. Before this, another player's tag borrowed your
    -- fonts, sizes and text colours and only took its background from their
    -- rank - which is why somebody else's tag looked like a mix of the two.
    local styleLook, styleTitle, styleRaw = {}, {}, {}

    -- Your own tag always follows the editor, so the live preview and the
    -- card over your head can never disagree. Handing your own tag the rank's
    -- look instead was why applying a preset looked right in the preview but
    -- the real one kept doing something else - a rank like FOUNDER carries
    -- rainbow = true, so it cycled colours forever.
    local function lookFor(player)
        if player == LocalPlayer then return TagSettings end
        local s = styleLook[player.UserId]
        if s then return s end
        local r = RANKMAP[playerRank[player.UserId] or ""]
        return (r and r.look) or TagSettings
    end

    -- A title set from the admin panel outranks everything, then the rank's
    -- own name, then whatever the player called themselves.
    -- The aura loop in the Games tab asks this to find out whose settings
    -- apply to whom; only this scope can see styleLook.
    SilenceAPI._larpOf = function(p)
        if not p or p == LocalPlayer then return nil end
        local lk = styleLook[p.UserId]
        return lk and lk.larp
    end

    SilenceAPI._auraOf = function(p)
        if not p then return nil end
        if p == LocalPlayer then return State.aura end
        local lk = styleLook[p.UserId]
        return lk and lk.aura
    end

    local function titleFor(player)
        local t = customTitles[player.UserId]
        if t and t ~= "" then return t end
        local r = RANKMAP[playerRank[player.UserId] or ""]
        if r and r.title then return r.title end
        local st = styleTitle[player.UserId]
        if st and st ~= "" then return st end
        return "SILENCE USER"
    end

    local RANKS_FILE = "silence_ranks.json"
    local function saveRanks()
        local HS = game:GetService("HttpService")
        local data = {}
        for uid, k in pairs(playerRank) do data[tostring(uid)] = k end
        pcall(function() writefile(RANKS_FILE, HS:JSONEncode(data)) end)
    end
    local function loadRanks()
        local HS = game:GetService("HttpService")
        local ok, content = pcall(function() return readfile(RANKS_FILE) end)
        if not ok or not content or content == "" then return end
        local ok2, data = pcall(function() return HS:JSONDecode(content) end)
        if not ok2 or type(data) ~= "table" then return end
        for uid, k in pairs(data) do
            local n = tonumber(uid)
            if n and RANKMAP[k] then playerRank[n] = k end
        end
    end
    loadRanks()

    -- ASCII only on purpose: Gotham has no symbol characters, and anything
    -- it cannot draw comes out as an empty box.
    local PARTICLE_KINDS = { "None", "Sparkles", "Stars", "Hearts", "Fire",
                             "Snow", "Diamonds", "Blood", "Money", "Skulls",
                             "Bolts", "Rain", "Bubbles", "Runes" }
    local PARTICLE_GLYPHS = {
        Sparkles = "*",
        Stars    = "+",
        Hearts   = "<3",
        Fire     = "^",
        Snow     = ".",
        Diamonds = "o",
        Blood    = "'",
        Money    = "$",
        Skulls   = "X",
        Bolts    = "/",
        Rain     = "|",
        Bubbles  = "O",
        Runes    = "#",
    }
    -- kinds that fall from the top instead of rising from the bottom
    local PARTICLE_FALL = { Snow = true, Rain = true, Blood = true, Money = true }
    local PARTICLE_TINT = {
        Fire    = Color3.fromRGB(255, 140,  30),
        Hearts  = Color3.fromRGB(255,  80, 130),
        Snow    = Color3.fromRGB(220, 240, 255),
        Blood   = Color3.fromRGB(190,  25,  35),
        Money   = Color3.fromRGB( 90, 220, 120),
        Skulls  = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220),
        Bolts   = Color3.fromRGB(255, 225,  70),
        Rain    = Color3.fromRGB(140, 200, 255),
        Bubbles = Color3.fromRGB(170, 225, 255),
        Runes   = Color3.fromRGB(180, 120, 255),
    }

    local function destroyTag(player)
        local g = activeTags[player]
        if g then pcall(function() g:Destroy() end); activeTags[player] = nil end
        if player and player.Character then
            local head = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
            if head then
                for _, child in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
                    if child.Name == "__SilenceTag" then
                        pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- Turn a published SILTAG1 string into a look table. Deliberately NOT
    -- into TagSettings: that is your own design and must not move because
    -- somebody else changed theirs. Missing keys fall through the metatable
    -- to the defaults, so an old or truncated code degrades instead of
    -- erroring the moment a tag is drawn.
    local function decodeTagCode(str)
        local body = tostring(str or ""):match("SILTAG1|(.+)$")
        if not body then return nil end
        local HS = game:GetService("HttpService")
        local okD, t = pcall(function() return HS:JSONDecode(body) end)
        if not okD or type(t) ~= "table" then return nil end

        local function col(v)
            if type(v) == "table" and #v >= 3 then
                return Color3.fromRGB(v[1], v[2], v[3])
            end
            return nil
        end
        local lk = {}
        lk.bg     = col(t.bg);  lk.bg2  = col(t.bg2)
        lk.accent = col(t.ac);  lk.text = col(t.tx)
        lk.sub    = col(t.sb);  lk.outlineCol = col(t.oc)
        if lk.accent then lk.border = lk.accent end
        lk.titleSize    = tonumber(t.ts); lk.width   = tonumber(t.wd)
        lk.radius       = tonumber(t.rd); lk.bgAlpha = tonumber(t.al)
        lk.glow         = tonumber(t.gl); lk.gradRot = tonumber(t.gr)
        lk.rainbowSpeed = tonumber(t.rs); lk.offsetY = tonumber(t.oy)
        lk.maxDist      = tonumber(t.md); lk.partRate = tonumber(t.pr)
        lk.distGrow     = tonumber(t.dg)
        lk.emoji        = tonumber(t.em)
        if type(t.lp) == "table" then
            lk.larp = {
                headless = t.lp.headless and true or false,
                head  = type(t.lp.head)  == "table" and t.lp.head  or {},
                torso = type(t.lp.torso) == "table" and t.lp.torso or {},
                slots = type(t.lp.slots) == "table" and t.lp.slots or {},
                scale = tonumber(t.lp.scale) or 1,
            }
        end
        if type(t.au) == "table" then
            local au = t.au
            lk.aura = {
                on       = au.on and true or false,
                style    = tostring(au.st or "sparkle"),
                gradient = au.gd and true or false,
                rainbow  = au.rb and true or false,
                size     = tonumber(au.sz) or 1,
                rate     = tonumber(au.rt) or 1,
                color    = col(au.c1) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60),
                color2   = col(au.c2) or Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 255),
            }
        end
        if type(t.su) == "boolean" then lk.showUser  = t.su end
        if type(t.bd) == "boolean" then lk.showBadge = t.bd end
        if type(t.ce) == "boolean" then lk.centered  = t.ce end
        if type(t.gd) == "boolean" then lk.gradient  = t.gd end
        if type(t.ol) == "boolean" then lk.outline   = t.ol end
        if type(t.rb) == "boolean" then lk.rainbow   = t.rb end
        if type(t.pu) == "boolean" then lk.pulse     = t.pu end
        if type(t.pa) == "string" and (t.pa == "None" or PARTICLE_GLYPHS[t.pa]) then
            lk.particle = t.pa
        end
        if type(t.fn) == "string" then
            -- a client without that font would throw on the lookup
            local okF, f = pcall(function() return Enum.Font[t.fn] end)
            if okF and f then lk.font = f end
        end
        setmetatable(lk, { __index = TagSettings })
        return lk, (type(t.ti) == "string" and t.ti or nil)
    end

    local function createTag(player)
        if not player or not player.Character then return end
        if State.tagsHidden then return end
        local head = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if not head then
            -- The head lags behind the character on a join or a respawn.
            -- Returning here was final: nothing retried, so the tag simply
            -- never appeared for anyone whose head arrived a moment late.
            local char = player.Character
            task.spawn(function()
                local h = char:WaitForChild("Head", 10)
                if h and player.Character == char and not State.tagsHidden then
                    createTag(player)
                end
            end)
            return
        end
        destroyTag(player)

        -- Everything below reads from this one table: the player's published
        -- style, or their rank, or the defaults. Nothing reads TagSettings
        -- directly any more, because TagSettings is YOURS.
        local look = lookFor(player)

        local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        bb.Name = "__SilenceTag"
        bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, look.width, 0, lookH(look))
        bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, look.offsetY, 0)
        bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
        bb.Active = true
        bb.LightInfluence = 0
        bb.MaxDistance = look.maxDist
        bb.Parent = head

        -- Every part is named, and every optional part exists from the
        -- start. applyLook() below then rewrites this card in place instead
        -- of destroying it and building a new one, which is what made tags
        -- flicker: the endpoint is polled every few seconds and is eventually
        -- consistent, so a rebuild-on-change loop rebuilt constantly.
        -- A gradient that is not wanted is disabled rather than absent, so
        -- switching it on later needs no new instance.
        local frame = Instance.new("Frame", bb)
        frame.Name = "Card"
        frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        frame.BackgroundColor3 = look.bg
        frame.BackgroundTransparency = look.bgAlpha
        frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
        local fc = Instance.new("UICorner", frame)
        fc.Name = "Round"
        fc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, look.radius)

        local border = Instance.new("UIStroke", frame)
        border.Name = "Edge"
        border.Color = look.border
        border.Thickness = 1
        border.Transparency = 0.45

        local gr = Instance.new("UIGradient", frame)
        gr.Name = "Grad"
        gr.Color = ColorSequence.new(look.bg, look.bg2)
        gr.Rotation = look.gradRot
        gr.Enabled = look.gradient and true or false

        local glowStroke = addAnimatedGlow(frame, {
            thickness = math.max(0.01, tonumber(look.glow) or 0),
            transparency = 0.35, window = 0.18, color = look.accent })
        glowStroke.Name = "Glow"
        glowStroke.Enabled = (tonumber(look.glow) or 0) > 0
        -- the pulse drives this rather than the billboard size, which the
        -- distance scaler further down already owns
        local pulseScale = Instance.new("UIScale", frame)
        pulseScale.Scale = 1

        local badgeSize = 30
        local badge = Instance.new("Frame", frame)
        badge.Name = "Badge"
        badge.Visible = look.showBadge
        badge.Size = UDim2.new(0, badgeSize, 0, badgeSize)
        badge.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0.5, -badgeSize/2)
        badge.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        badge.BorderSizePixel = 0
        local bc = Instance.new("UICorner", badge)
        bc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

        local badgeGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient", badge)
        badgeGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,    Theme.bg4),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,  Theme.bg2),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,    Color3.fromRGB(0,  0,  0)),
        })
        badgeGrad.Rotation = 90

        local bs = Instance.new("UIStroke", badge)
        bs.Name = "BadgeEdge"
        bs.Color = look.accent
        bs.Thickness = 1.5
        bs.Transparency = 0.2
        bs.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border

        local innerRing = Instance.new("Frame", badge)
        innerRing.Name = "Ring"
        innerRing.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 1, -6)
        innerRing.Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 3)
        innerRing.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        innerRing.BorderSizePixel = 0
        local irc = Instance.new("UICorner", innerRing); irc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        local irs = Instance.new("UIStroke", innerRing)
        irs.Name = "RingEdge"
        irs.Color = look.accent
        irs.Thickness = 0.6
        irs.Transparency = 0.65

        local badgeImg = Instance.new("ImageLabel", badge)
        badgeImg.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 1, -4)
        badgeImg.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2)
        badgeImg.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        badgeImg.Image = "rbxthumb://type=AvatarHeadShot&id=" .. player.UserId .. "&w=150&h=150"
        badgeImg.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
        badgeImg.ZIndex = 3
        local bic = Instance.new("UICorner", badgeImg); bic.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

        -- text starts after the badge, or near the edge when it is hidden
        local padL  = look.showBadge and 38 or 12
        local align = look.centered and Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
                                     or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

        local title = Instance.new("TextLabel", frame)
        title.Name = "Title"
        title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(padL + 8), 0, 16)
        -- with the username hidden there is only one line, so it centres
        title.Position = UDim2.new(0, padL, 0, look.showUser and 5 or 9)
        title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        -- titleFor() existed but nothing called it, so a rank could only ever
        -- change the colours and never the name written on the card
        -- Prefixed onto the title rather than given its own label: the card
        -- has a fixed geometry that the distance scaler depends on, and an
        -- extra element would have meant moving the text every time the
        -- emoji changed.
        title.Text = SilenceAPI._emojiPrefix(look.emoji) .. titleFor(player)
        title.Font = look.font
        title.TextSize = look.titleSize
        title.TextColor3 = look.text
        title.TextXAlignment = align
        title.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        title.TextStrokeColor3 = look.outlineCol
        title.TextStrokeTransparency = look.outline and 0 or 1

        local sub = Instance.new("TextLabel", frame)
        sub.Name = "Sub"
        sub.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(padL + 8), 0, 13)
        sub.Position = UDim2.new(0, padL, 0, 22)
        sub.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        sub.Text = "@" .. player.Name
        sub.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        sub.TextSize = 9
        sub.TextColor3 = look.sub
        sub.TextXAlignment = align
        sub.Visible = look.showUser

        -- ranked players carry their own particles too, not just you; the
        -- loop idles cheaply when the look asks for none
        do
            local pLayer = Instance.new("Frame", frame)
            pLayer.Name = "__UIParticles"
            pLayer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            pLayer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            pLayer.ClipsDescendants = true
            pLayer.ZIndex = 2

            task.spawn(function()
                while pLayer and pLayer.Parent do
                    -- re-resolved each round so a style change takes effect
                    -- without the card having to be rebuilt
                    local look = lookFor(player)
                    local kind = look.particle
                    if kind == "None" or not PARTICLE_GLYPHS[kind] then
                        task.wait(0.25)
                    else
                        local glyph = PARTICLE_GLYPHS[kind]
                        local color = PARTICLE_TINT[kind] or look.accent

                        local pTxt = Instance.new("TextLabel", pLayer)
                        pTxt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        pTxt.Text = glyph
                        pTxt.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                        pTxt.TextSize = math.random(6, 9)
                        pTxt.TextColor3 = color
                        pTxt.Size = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10)
                        pTxt.TextScaled = false
                        pTxt.ZIndex = 2
                        local sx = math.random(8, 92) / 100
                        local fallDown = PARTICLE_FALL[kind] and true or false
                        local startY = fallDown and 0 or 1
                        pTxt.Position = UDim2.new(sx, -5, startY, -5)
                        pTxt.TextTransparency = 1

                        task.spawn(function()
                            local life = 1.4 + math.random() * 0.6
                            local t = 0
                            while t < life and pTxt.Parent do
                                local dt = task.wait()
                                t = t + dt
                                local p = math.min(1, t / life)
                                local y = fallDown and (p * 1.05) or (1 - p * 1.05)
                                pTxt.Position = UDim2.new(sx, -5, y, -5)
                                local trans
                                if p < 0.25 then trans = 0.4 - (p / 0.25) * 0.4
                                elseif p > 0.7 then trans = ((p - 0.7) / 0.3) * 1
                                else trans = 0 end
                                pTxt.TextTransparency = math.clamp(trans, 0, 1)
                            end
                            if pTxt and pTxt.Parent then pTxt:Destroy() end
                        end)

                        local rate = math.max(0.15, tonumber(look.partRate) or 1)
                        task.wait((0.4 + math.random() * 0.25) / rate)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        if player == LocalPlayer then
            local clickArea = Instance.new("TextButton", frame)
            clickArea.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            clickArea.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            clickArea.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
            clickArea.Text = ""
            clickArea.AutoButtonColor = false
            clickArea.Modal = false
            clickArea.Active = true
            clickArea.Selectable = true
            clickArea.ZIndex = 50
            local function onClick()
                -- a window is over the screen; this click was meant for it
                if SilenceAPI._modalOpen and SilenceAPI._modalOpen() then return end
                if TagSettingsPopup then
                    TagSettingsPopup.Visible = not TagSettingsPopup.Visible
                end
            end
            clickArea.MouseButton1Click:Connect(onClick)
            clickArea.Activated:Connect(onClick)
        end

        activeTags[player] = bb
        return bb
    end

    -- Rewrite an existing card from the player's current look. This is the
    -- ONLY thing that should run when a rank, a title or a published style
    -- changes; createTag is for a card that does not exist yet or whose head
    -- was replaced by a respawn. Everything createTag sets, this sets too, so
    -- the two cannot drift apart.
    -- Returns false when there is nothing to update, so callers can fall back.
    local function applyLook(player)
        local bb = activeTags[player]
        if not bb or not bb.Parent then return false end
        local frame = bb:FindFirstChild("Card")
        if not frame then return false end
        local look = lookFor(player)

        bb.Size        = UDim2.new(0, look.width, 0, lookH(look))
        bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, look.offsetY, 0)
        bb.MaxDistance = look.maxDist

        frame.BackgroundColor3       = look.bg
        frame.BackgroundTransparency = look.bgAlpha

        local fc = frame:FindFirstChild("Round")
        if fc then fc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, look.radius) end
        local edge = frame:FindFirstChild("Edge")
        if edge then edge.Color = look.border end
        local gr = frame:FindFirstChild("Grad")
        if gr then
            gr.Color    = ColorSequence.new(look.bg, look.bg2)
            gr.Rotation = look.gradRot
            gr.Enabled  = look.gradient and true or false
        end
        local glow = frame:FindFirstChild("Glow")
        if glow then
            local g = tonumber(look.glow) or 0
            glow.Enabled   = g > 0
            glow.Thickness = math.max(0.01, g)
            glow.Color     = look.accent
        end

        local padL  = look.showBadge and 38 or 12
        local align = look.centered and Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
                                     or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

        local badge = frame:FindFirstChild("Badge")
        if badge then
            badge.Visible = look.showBadge and true or false
            local be = badge:FindFirstChild("BadgeEdge")
            if be then be.Color = look.accent end
            local ring = badge:FindFirstChild("Ring")
            if ring then
                local rs = ring:FindFirstChild("RingEdge")
                if rs then rs.Color = look.accent end
            end
        end

        local title = frame:FindFirstChild("Title")
        if title then
            title.Size     = UDim2.new(1, -(padL + 8), 0, 16)
            title.Position = UDim2.new(0, padL, 0, look.showUser and 5 or 9)
            title.Text     = SilenceAPI._emojiPrefix(look.emoji) .. titleFor(player)
            title.Font     = look.font
            title.TextSize = look.titleSize
            title.TextColor3 = look.text
            title.TextXAlignment = align
            title.TextStrokeColor3 = look.outlineCol
            title.TextStrokeTransparency = look.outline and 0 or 1
        end

        local sub = frame:FindFirstChild("Sub")
        if sub then
            sub.Size      = UDim2.new(1, -(padL + 8), 0, 13)
            sub.Position  = UDim2.new(0, padL, 0, 22)
            sub.TextColor3 = look.sub
            sub.TextXAlignment = align
            sub.Visible   = look.showUser and true or false
        end
        return true
    end
    SilenceAPI._applyTagLook = applyLook

    -- update in place if the card is there, build it only if it is not
    local function paintTag(player)
        if not player or not player.Character then return end
        if State.tagsHidden then return end
        if not applyLook(player) then createTag(player) end
    end

    local function refreshSelfTag()
        if LocalPlayer.Character then paintTag(LocalPlayer) end
    end

    local tagHooked = {}
    local function tagUser(player)
        silenceUsers[player] = true
        if player.Character then paintTag(player) end
        -- Connecting on every call meant a player who fell off the presence
        -- list and came back collected another respawn handler each round,
        -- and then rebuilt their card once per handler on the next death.
        if tagHooked[player] then return end
        tagHooked[player] = true
        track(player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
            task.wait(0.4)
            if silenceUsers[player] then createTag(player) end
            -- the emitter hung off the old HumanoidRootPart and the horns
            -- off the old head; both are gone with the previous character
            if player ~= LocalPlayer then
                local lk = styleLook[player.UserId]
                if SilenceAPI._applyAuraFor then
                    SilenceAPI._applyAuraFor(player, lk and lk.aura)
                end
                if SilenceAPI._applyLarpFor then
                    SilenceAPI._applyLarpFor(player, lk and lk.larp)
                end
            end
        end))
        track(player.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function(char)
            if char then
                local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
                if head then
                    for _, child in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
                        if child.Name == "__SilenceTag" then
                            pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            activeTags[player] = nil
        end))
    end

    tagUser(LocalPlayer)

    track(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p)
        tagHooked[p] = nil
        if SilenceAPI._clearLarpFor then SilenceAPI._clearLarpFor(p) end
        silenceUsers[p] = nil; destroyTag(p)
    end))

    local viewSize = Camera.ViewportSize
    track(Camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(function()
        viewSize = Camera.ViewportSize
    end))
    track(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        local fovTan = math.tan(math.rad(Camera.FieldOfView/2))
        -- computed once per frame, not once per tag
        local now = tick()
        for player, bb in pairs(activeTags) do
            if bb and bb.Parent and player.Character then
                local head = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                if head then
                    -- Size belongs to the player being drawn too. Rescaling
                    -- every tag in the server with YOUR width and YOUR line
                    -- count undid half of what createTag had just built.
                    local pLook = lookFor(player)
                    local d = (Camera.CFrame.Position - head.Position).Magnitude
                    -- Sizing off the real distance gives a constant pixel
                    -- size at any range. Sizing off a compressed distance
                    -- gives partial compensation instead: close up it is
                    -- still pinned, and past LOCK studs it falls off as
                    -- (LOCK/d)^(1-distGrow) rather than not at all.
                    local LOCK = 24
                    local g = tonumber(pLook.distGrow)
                    if g == nil then g = 0.5 end
                    g = math.clamp(g, 0, 1)
                    local dEff = d
                    if d > LOCK and g < 1 then
                        dEff = LOCK * (d / LOCK) ^ g
                    end
                    local px2stud = dEff * 2 * fovTan / viewSize.Y
                    bb.SizeOffset = Vector2.new(0, 0)
                    bb.Size = UDim2.new((tonumber(pLook.width) or 170) * px2stud, 0,
                                        lookH(pLook) * px2stud, 0)
                    local pulseAt
                    if pLook.pulse then
                        pulseAt = 1 + math.sin(now * 3) * 0.05
                    end
                    local rainbowCol
                    if pLook.rainbow then
                        local hue = (now * (tonumber(pLook.rainbowSpeed) or 0.2)) % 1
                        rainbowCol = Color3.fromHSV(hue, 0.85, 1)
                    end
                    if rainbowCol or pulseAt then
                        local fr = bb:FindFirstChildOfClass("Frame")
                        if fr then
                            if rainbowCol then
                                for _, o in ipairs(fr:GetChildren()) do
                                    if o:IsA("UIStroke") then o.Color = rainbowCol end
                                end
                            end
                            if pulseAt then
                                local sc = fr:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale")
                                if sc then sc.Scale = pulseAt end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end))

    track(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
           and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local head = char and char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if not head then return end
        if not activeTags[LocalPlayer] then return end
        local sp, onScreen = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(
            head.Position + Vector3.new(0, TagSettings.offsetY, 0))
        if not onScreen then return end
        local m = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
        if m.X >= sp.X - 85 and m.X <= sp.X + 85
           and m.Y >= sp.Y - 21 and m.Y <= sp.Y + 21
           and TagSettingsPopup then
            TagSettingsPopup.Visible = not TagSettingsPopup.Visible
        end
    end))

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- TAG EDITOR
    -- A window rather than a list of colour rows: an icon rail down the
    -- left, a live face that redraws on every keystroke, and four pages of
    -- controls. Everything writes into TagSettings and then calls touched(),
    -- which re-renders both the overhead tag and the preview.
    --
    -- The controls are built here instead of reusing the panel's toggleRow /
    -- inputRow: those register themselves with the panel's search index, and
    -- rows belonging to a different window would then be hidden by a search
    -- typed on the main panel.
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    ;(function()
    local EZ = 51                  -- base layer for everything in the window
    local pages, rails, pageFit = {}, {}, {}
    local activePage = "TEXT"
    local face, statusLbl = {}, nil

    -- Fonts this client actually ships; a build without one simply skips it
    -- rather than erroring while the GUI is being assembled.
    local FONTS, FONTMAP = {}, {}
    for _, n in ipairs({ "GothamBlack", "GothamBold", "Gotham", "Bangers",
                         "Creepster", "PermanentMarker", "Michroma",
                         "SciFi", "Arcade", "Fantasy", "Code", "Oswald" }) do
        local okF, f = pcall(function() return Enum.Font[n] end)
        if okF and f then
            FONTS[#FONTS+1] = { label = n, font = f }
            FONTMAP[n] = f
        end
    end
    -- presets name their face as a string and come through here, so a client
    -- missing one simply falls back rather than erroring mid-build
    local function fontOr(n) return FONTMAP[n] or Enum.Font.GothamBold end

    -- What the card over your head actually says: an admin-given title, else
    -- your rank's name, else the default. There is deliberately no setter any
    -- more - the name is handed out from the admin panel and travels to
    -- everyone through the endpoint, so letting anyone type their own here
    -- would have made that meaningless.
    local function myTitle() return titleFor(LocalPlayer) end

    local function drawFace() end   -- forward declared, defined with the preview
    -- assigned further down, once tagCode() exists; touched() is up here and
    -- has to be able to reach it
    local saveStyleSoon = function() end
    local function touched(note)
        refreshSelfTag()
        drawFace()
        -- changing your tag is pointless if nobody else ever finds out
        if SilenceAPI._pushStyle then SilenceAPI._pushStyle() end
        saveStyleSoon()
        if statusLbl then statusLbl.Text = note or "applied" end
    end

    ------------------------------------------------------------ WINDOW
    TagSettingsPopup = Instance.new("Frame", Gui)
    TagSettingsPopup.Name = "TagEditor"
    TagSettingsPopup.Size = UDim2.new(0, 720, 0, 496)
    TagSettingsPopup.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -360, 0.5, -248)
    TagSettingsPopup.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg
    TagSettingsPopup.BorderSizePixel = 0
    TagSettingsPopup.Visible = false
    TagSettingsPopup.Active = true
    TagSettingsPopup.ClipsDescendants = true
    TagSettingsPopup.ZIndex = EZ - 1
    SilenceAPI._glass(TagSettingsPopup, { radius = 18, transparency = 0.2,
                                          edge = 0.3, glowThickness = 2 })
    SilenceAPI._blurWatch(TagSettingsPopup)

    ------------------------------------------------------------ HEADER
    local pHdr = Instance.new("Frame", TagSettingsPopup)
    pHdr.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 52)
    pHdr.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg2
    pHdr.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
    pHdr.BorderSizePixel = 0
    pHdr.ZIndex = EZ
    pHdr.Active = true
    corner(pHdr, 18)
    do -- squares the header's rounded bottom, same trick as the main shell
        local cap = Instance.new("Frame", pHdr)
        cap.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18); cap.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -18)
        cap.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg2
        cap.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
        cap.BorderSizePixel = 0; cap.ZIndex = EZ
        local line = Instance.new("Frame", pHdr)
        line.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1); line.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -1)
        line.BorderSizePixel = 0; line.ZIndex = EZ + 1
        SilenceAPI._theme(line, "BackgroundColor3", "accent")
        local lg = Instance.new("UIGradient", line)
        lg.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,    1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.34, 0.45),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,  0.05),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.66, 0.45),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,    1),
        })
        track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            lg.Offset = Vector2.new(math.sin(tick() * 0.5) * 0.85, 0)
        end))
    end
    makeHeaderDrag(TagSettingsPopup, pHdr)

    do -- mark + wordmark
        local mark = Instance.new("Frame", pHdr)
        mark.Size = UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 32); mark.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0.5, -16)
        mark.BorderSizePixel = 0; mark.ZIndex = EZ + 2
        SilenceAPI._theme(mark, "BackgroundColor3", "bg3")
        corner(mark, 10); stroke(mark, Theme.accent, 1, 0.5)
        local ms = Instance.new("TextLabel", mark)
        ms.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0); ms.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        ms.Text = "S"; ms.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack; ms.TextSize = 18
        ms.ZIndex = EZ + 3
        SilenceAPI._theme(ms, "TextColor3", "text")

        local wm = Instance.new("TextLabel", pHdr)
        wm.Size = UDim2.new(0, 260, 0, 18); wm.Position = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0.5, -15)
        wm.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        wm.Text = "TAG EDITOR"
        wm.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack; wm.TextSize = 16
        wm.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; wm.ZIndex = EZ + 2
        SilenceAPI._theme(wm, "TextColor3", "text")

        local sb = Instance.new("TextLabel", pHdr)
        sb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 300, 0, 12); sb.Position = UDim2.new(0, 57, 0.5, 4)
        sb.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        sb.Text = "OVERHEAD NAMETAG  /  ONLY YOU SEE IT"
        sb.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium; sb.TextSize = 9
        sb.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; sb.ZIndex = EZ + 2
        SilenceAPI._theme(sb, "TextColor3", "textDim")
    end

    local pClose = Instance.new("TextButton", pHdr)
    pClose.Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 30); pClose.Position = UDim2.new(1, -42, 0.5, -15)
    pClose.BorderSizePixel = 0
    pClose.Text = "X"; pClose.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; pClose.TextSize = 13
    pClose.AutoButtonColor = false; pClose.ZIndex = EZ + 2
    pClose.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
    SilenceAPI._theme(pClose, "BackgroundColor3", "bg3")
    SilenceAPI._theme(pClose, "TextColor3", "text")
    corner(pClose, 9); stroke(pClose, Theme.accent, 1, 0.6)
    pClose.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        TagSettingsPopup.Visible = false
    end)

    ------------------------------------------------------------ ICON RAIL
    local rail = Instance.new("Frame", TagSettingsPopup)
    rail.Size = UDim2.new(0, 72, 1, -52); rail.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 52)
    rail.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    rail.BorderSizePixel = 0
    rail.ZIndex = EZ
    do
        local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout", rail)
        lay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
        lay.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
        lay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding", rail)
        pad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 14)
        local div = Instance.new("Frame", TagSettingsPopup)
        div.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 1, -72); div.Position = UDim2.new(0, 72, 0, 62)
        div.BorderSizePixel = 0; div.ZIndex = EZ
        SilenceAPI._theme(div, "BackgroundColor3", "bg4")
    end

    local function showPage(key)
        activePage = key
        for k, p in pairs(pages) do p.Visible = (k == key) end
        for _, fn in ipairs(rails) do fn() end
        -- a page only gets laid out once it is on screen, so its scroll
        -- height has to be recomputed the moment it becomes the visible one
        if pageFit[key] then
            pageFit[key]()
            task.defer(pageFit[key])
        end
    end

    -- Glyphs are drawn from Frames for the same reason the main rail's are:
    -- Gotham has no symbol characters and would render empty boxes.
    local function railTile(key, label, kind, order)
        local b = Instance.new("TextButton", rail)
        b.Size = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, 56)
        b.BorderSizePixel = 0
        b.Text = ""
        b.AutoButtonColor = false
        b.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg2
        b.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        b.LayoutOrder = order
        b.ZIndex = EZ + 1
        corner(b, 12)
        local st = stroke(b, Theme.accent, 1, 1)

        local ico = Instance.new("Frame", b)
        ico.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 22); ico.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -11, 0, 9)
        ico.BackgroundTransparency = 1; ico.BorderSizePixel = 0; ico.ZIndex = EZ + 2

        local parts = {}
        local function px(w, h, x, y, r, rot)
            local f = Instance.new("Frame", ico)
            f.Size = UDim2.new(0, w, 0, h); f.Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, y)
            f.BorderSizePixel = 0; f.ZIndex = EZ + 2
            if rot then f.Rotation = rot end
            if r then corner(f, r) end
            parts[#parts+1] = { f, "BackgroundColor3" }
            return f
        end
        local function ring(d, x, y, th)
            local f = Instance.new("Frame", ico)
            f.Size = UDim2.new(0, d, 0, d); f.Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, y)
            f.BackgroundTransparency = 1; f.BorderSizePixel = 0; f.ZIndex = EZ + 2
            local c = Instance.new("UICorner", f); c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
            local o = Instance.new("UIStroke", f); o.Thickness = th or 2
            parts[#parts+1] = { o, "Color" }
            return f
        end

        if kind == "text" then          -- three lines of copy
            px(20, 3, 1,  3, 1); px(14, 3, 1, 10, 1); px(17, 3, 1, 17, 1)
        elseif kind == "style" then     -- a palette: ring plus a filled dot
            ring(20, 1, 1, 2)
            local d = px(7, 7, 7.5, 7.5); local c = Instance.new("UICorner", d)
            c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        elseif kind == "fx" then        -- a four point spark
            px(3, 22, 9.5, 0, 1); px(22, 3, 0, 9.5, 1)
            px(3, 14, 9.5, 4, 1, 45); px(14, 3, 4, 9.5, 1, 45)
        elseif kind == "save" then      -- a floppy: four edges, shutter, label
            px(22, 3, 0,  0, 1); px(22, 3, 0, 19, 1)
            px(3, 22, 0,  0, 1); px(3, 22, 19, 0, 1)
            px(9,  6, 6.5, 3, 1)
            px(12, 5, 5,  13, 1)
        elseif kind == "export" then    -- a box with an arrow leaving it
            px(16, 3, 0, 19, 1); px(3, 8, 0, 12, 1); px(3, 8, 13, 12, 1)
            px(3, 13, 9.5, 1, 1)
            px(3, 7, 7, 2, 1, 45); px(3, 7, 12, 2, 1, -45)
        else                            -- four preset tiles
            px(9, 9, 1, 1, 3); px(9, 9, 12, 1, 3)
            px(9, 9, 1, 12, 3); px(9, 9, 12, 12, 3)
        end

        local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", b)
        lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 12); lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 37)
        lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lbl.Text = label
        lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; lbl.TextSize = 9
        lbl.TextColor3 = Theme.textDim
        lbl.ZIndex = EZ + 2

        local function paint()
            local on = (activePage == key)
            b.BackgroundColor3       = Theme.bg2
            b.BackgroundTransparency = on and 0.15 or 1
            st.Color                 = Theme.accent
            st.Transparency          = on and 0.35 or 1
            lbl.TextColor3           = on and Theme.text or Theme.textDim
            local col = on and Theme.accent or Theme.textDim
            for _, p in ipairs(parts) do pcall(function() p[1][p[2]] = col end) end
        end
        b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            if activePage == key then return end
            TweenService:Create(b, SilenceAPI._twFade, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.55 }):Play()
        end)
        b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            if activePage == key then return end
            TweenService:Create(b, SilenceAPI._twFade, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
            paint()
        end)
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() showPage(key) end)
        rails[#rails+1] = paint
        table.insert(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers, paint)
    end

    ------------------------------------------------------------ LIVE PREVIEW
    local stage = Instance.new("Frame", TagSettingsPopup)
    stage.Size = UDim2.new(1, -96, 0, 132); stage.Position = UDim2.new(0, 80, 0, 62)
    stage.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg
    stage.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
    stage.BorderSizePixel = 0
    stage.ClipsDescendants = true
    stage.ZIndex = EZ
    corner(stage, 12); stroke(stage, Theme.accent, 1, 0.75)

    do -- a faint grid, so a transparent tag still has something behind it
        for i = 1, 11 do
            local v = Instance.new("Frame", stage)
            v.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 1, 0); v.Position = UDim2.new(i / 12, 0, 0, 0)
            v.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.accent
            v.BackgroundTransparency = 0.93
            v.BorderSizePixel = 0; v.ZIndex = EZ
        end
        for i = 1, 3 do
            local h = Instance.new("Frame", stage)
            h.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1); h.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, i / 4, 0)
            h.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.accent
            h.BackgroundTransparency = 0.93
            h.BorderSizePixel = 0; h.ZIndex = EZ
        end
    end

    local stageCap = Instance.new("TextLabel", stage)
    stageCap.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 12); stageCap.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 8)
    stageCap.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    stageCap.Text = "LIVE PREVIEW"
    stageCap.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; stageCap.TextSize = 9
    stageCap.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    stageCap.ZIndex = EZ + 3
    SilenceAPI._theme(stageCap, "TextColor3", "textDim")

    -- The preview is a plain GUI copy of the tag rather than a second
    -- BillboardGui: it has to sit inside this window, and it is redrawn from
    -- scratch on every change so no stale property can survive.
    drawFace = function()
        for _, o in ipairs(face) do pcall(function() o:Destroy() end) end
        face = {}

        local card = Instance.new("Frame", stage)
        card.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        card.Size = UDim2.new(0, TagSettings.width, 0, tagH())
        card.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 6)
        card.BackgroundColor3 = TagSettings.bg
        card.BackgroundTransparency = TagSettings.bgAlpha
        card.BorderSizePixel = 0
        card.ZIndex = EZ + 4
        local c = Instance.new("UICorner", card)
        c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, TagSettings.radius)
        local bd = Instance.new("UIStroke", card)
        bd.Color = TagSettings.border; bd.Thickness = 1; bd.Transparency = 0.45
        if TagSettings.glow > 0 then
            addAnimatedGlow(card, { thickness = TagSettings.glow,
                transparency = 0.35, window = 0.18, color = TagSettings.accent })
        end
        face[#face+1] = card

        local badge = Instance.new("Frame", card)
        badge.Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 30); badge.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0.5, -15)
        badge.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        badge.BorderSizePixel = 0; badge.ZIndex = EZ + 5
        local bc = Instance.new("UICorner", badge); bc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        local bs = Instance.new("UIStroke", badge)
        bs.Color = TagSettings.accent; bs.Thickness = 1.5; bs.Transparency = 0.2
        local bimg = Instance.new("ImageLabel", badge)
        bimg.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 1, -4); bimg.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2)
        bimg.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        bimg.Image = "rbxthumb://type=AvatarHeadShot&id=" .. LocalPlayer.UserId .. "&w=150&h=150"
        bimg.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
        bimg.ZIndex = EZ + 6
        local bic = Instance.new("UICorner", bimg); bic.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

        local t = Instance.new("TextLabel", card)
        t.Size = UDim2.new(1, -42, 0, 16)
        t.Position = UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, TagSettings.showUser and 5 or 9)
        t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        t.Text = myTitle()
        t.Font = TagSettings.font
        t.TextSize = TagSettings.titleSize
        t.TextColor3 = TagSettings.text
        t.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        t.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        t.ZIndex = EZ + 5

        if TagSettings.showUser then
            local u = Instance.new("TextLabel", card)
            u.Size = UDim2.new(1, -42, 0, 13); u.Position = UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 22)
            u.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            u.Text = "@" .. LocalPlayer.Name
            u.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham; u.TextSize = 9
            u.TextColor3 = TagSettings.sub
            u.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            u.ZIndex = EZ + 5
        end
    end

    ------------------------------------------------------------ CONTROLS
    local function makePage(key)
        local p = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame", TagSettingsPopup)
        p.Size = UDim2.new(1, -96, 1, -238); p.Position = UDim2.new(0, 80, 0, 206)
        p.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        p.BorderSizePixel = 0
        p.Visible = false
        p.ScrollBarThickness = 4
        p.ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.4
        p.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        p.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
        p.ZIndex = EZ + 1
        SilenceAPI._theme(p, "ScrollBarImageColor3", "accent")
        local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout", p)
        lay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8); lay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

        -- The canvas is driven by hand rather than with AutomaticCanvasSize.
        -- This whole window is built while it is invisible, and a
        -- ScrollingFrame inside a hidden ancestor is never laid out - so the
        -- automatic size stayed at zero and never recovered once the window
        -- was opened. A page that cannot scroll just hides its lower half,
        -- which is exactly what happened to the presets.
        local function fit()
            p.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, lay.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 14)
        end
        lay:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(fit)
        pageFit[key] = fit
        pages[key] = p
        return p
    end

    local function eCaption(parent, text)
        local l = Instance.new("TextLabel", parent)
        l.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20); l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        l.Text = text
        l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; l.TextSize = 10
        l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        l.ZIndex = EZ + 2
        SilenceAPI._theme(l, "TextColor3", "accent2")
        return l
    end

    local function eRow(parent, h)
        local r = Instance.new("Frame", parent)
        r.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, h or 38)
        r.BorderSizePixel = 0
        r.ZIndex = EZ + 2
        corner(r, 9)
        SilenceAPI._theme(r, "BackgroundColor3", "bg2")
        stroke(r, Theme.bg4, 1, 0.5, "bg4")
        local l = Instance.new("TextLabel", r)
        l.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 38); l.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 0)
        l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium; l.TextSize = 12
        l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        l.ZIndex = EZ + 3
        SilenceAPI._theme(l, "TextColor3", "text")
        return r, l
    end

    local function eToggle(parent, label, get, set)
        local r, l = eRow(parent, 38)
        l.Text = label
        local sw = Instance.new("TextButton", r)
        sw.Size = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 22); sw.Position = UDim2.new(1, -58, 0.5, -11)
        sw.BorderSizePixel = 0; sw.Text = ""; sw.AutoButtonColor = false
        sw.ZIndex = EZ + 3
        corner(sw, 11)
        local knob = Instance.new("Frame", sw)
        knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16); knob.Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, -8)
        knob.BorderSizePixel = 0; knob.ZIndex = EZ + 4
        corner(knob, 8)
        local function paint()
            local on = get() and true or false
            TweenService:Create(sw, SilenceAPI._twKnob,
                { BackgroundColor3 = on and Theme.accent or Theme.bg3 }):Play()
            TweenService:Create(knob, SilenceAPI._twKnob, {
                Position         = on and UDim2.new(0,25,0.5,-8) or UDim2.new(0,3,0.5,-8),
                BackgroundColor3 = on and Theme.bg or Theme.textDim,
            }):Play()
        end
        sw.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() set(not get()); paint() end)
        paint()
        table.insert(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers, paint)
        return paint
    end

    local function eSlider(parent, label, mn, mx, get, set, whole)
        local r, l = eRow(parent, 56)
        l.Text = label
        local val = Instance.new("TextLabel", r)
        val.Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 38); val.Position = UDim2.new(1, -74, 0, 0)
        val.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        val.Font = Enum.Font.Code; val.TextSize = 12
        val.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
        val.ZIndex = EZ + 3
        SilenceAPI._theme(val, "TextColor3", "text")

        local bar = Instance.new("Frame", r)
        bar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 6); bar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 1, -16)
        bar.BorderSizePixel = 0; bar.ZIndex = EZ + 3
        corner(bar, 3)
        SilenceAPI._theme(bar, "BackgroundColor3", "bg3")
        local fill = Instance.new("Frame", bar)
        fill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0); fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
        fill.ZIndex = EZ + 3
        corner(fill, 3)
        SilenceAPI._theme(fill, "BackgroundColor3", "accent")
        local knob = Instance.new("Frame", bar)
        knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 13, 0, 13); knob.Position = UDim2.new(0, -6, 0.5, -6)
        knob.BorderSizePixel = 0; knob.ZIndex = EZ + 4
        corner(knob, 7)
        SilenceAPI._theme(knob, "BackgroundColor3", "accent")
        stroke(knob, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0), 2, 0.3)

        local function paint()
            local v = tonumber(get()) or mn
            local frac = (mx == mn) and 0 or math.clamp((v - mn) / (mx - mn), 0, 1)
            fill.Size = UDim2.new(frac, 0, 1, 0)
            knob.Position = UDim2.new(frac, -6, 0.5, -6)
            val.Text = whole and tostring(math.floor(v + 0.5)) or string.format("%.2f", v)
        end
        local dragging = false
        local function pick(absX)
            local w = bar.AbsoluteSize.X
            if w <= 0 then return end
            local rel = math.clamp((absX - bar.AbsolutePosition.X) / w, 0, 1)
            local v = mn + rel * (mx - mn)
            if whole then v = math.floor(v + 0.5) end
            set(v); paint()
        end
        bar.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true; pick(i.Position.X)
            end
        end)
        bar.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then dragging = false end
        end)
        SilenceAPI._onDrag(function(i) if dragging then pick(i.Position.X) end end)
        paint()
        table.insert(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers, paint)
        return paint
    end

    local function eColor(parent, label, get, set)
        local r, l = eRow(parent, 38)
        l.Text = label
        local sw = Instance.new("TextButton", r)
        sw.Size = UDim2.new(0, 58, 0, 22); sw.Position = UDim2.new(1, -72, 0.5, -11)
        sw.BackgroundColor3 = get()
        sw.BorderSizePixel = 0; sw.Text = ""; sw.AutoButtonColor = false
        sw.ZIndex = EZ + 3
        corner(sw, 7); stroke(sw, Theme.text, 1, 0.5, "text")
        sw.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            openColorPicker(sw.BackgroundColor3, function(c)
                sw.BackgroundColor3 = c
                set(c)
                touched(label .. " changed")
            end, label)
        end)
        return sw
    end

    -- a wrapping grid of choice buttons; used for fonts and for particles
    local function eGrid(parent, items, get, set, cell)
        local box = Instance.new("Frame", parent)
        box.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 0)
        box.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        box.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        box.ZIndex = EZ + 2
        local lay = Instance.new("UIGridLayout", box)
        lay.CellSize = UDim2.new(0, cell or 96, 0, 28)
        lay.CellPadding = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 6)
        lay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

        local paints = {}
        for i, it in ipairs(items) do
            local b = Instance.new("TextButton", box)
            b.BorderSizePixel = 0
            b.Text = it.label
            b.Font = it.font or Enum.Font.GothamMedium
            b.TextSize = 12
            b.AutoButtonColor = false
            b.LayoutOrder = i
            b.ZIndex = EZ + 3
            corner(b, 8)
            local st = stroke(b, Theme.accent, 1, 0.7)
            SilenceAPI._theme(b, "BackgroundColor3", "bg2")
            local function paint()
                local on = (get() == it.value)
                b.TextColor3    = on and Theme.bg or Theme.text
                b.BackgroundColor3 = on and Theme.accent or Theme.bg2
                st.Transparency = on and 0 or 0.7
            end
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                set(it.value)
                for _, fn in ipairs(paints) do fn() end
            end)
            paints[#paints+1] = paint
            paint()
            table.insert(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers, paint)
        end
        return box
    end

    local function eButton(parent, label, cb)
        local b = Instance.new("TextButton", parent)
        b.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 34)
        b.BorderSizePixel = 0
        b.Text = label
        b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium; b.TextSize = 12
        b.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        b.AutoButtonColor = false
        b.ZIndex = EZ + 2
        corner(b, 9); padding(b, 0, 0, 14, 14)
        SilenceAPI._theme(b, "BackgroundColor3", "bg2")
        SilenceAPI._theme(b, "TextColor3", "text")
        local st = stroke(b, Theme.bg4, 1, 0.5, "bg4")
        b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(b,  SilenceAPI._twFade, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg3 }):Play()
            TweenService:Create(st, SilenceAPI._twFade, { Color = Theme.accent }):Play()
        end)
        b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(b,  SilenceAPI._twFade, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg2 }):Play()
            TweenService:Create(st, SilenceAPI._twFade, { Color = Theme.bg4 }):Play()
        end)
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(cb)
        return b
    end

    ------------------------------------------------------------ PAGE: TEXT
    do
        local p = makePage("TEXT")
        eCaption(p, "TITLE")

        local r, rl = eRow(p, 62)
        rl.Text = "Displayed name"
        local count = Instance.new("TextLabel", r)
        count.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 38); count.Position = UDim2.new(1, -114, 0, 0)
        count.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        count.Text = "ADMIN ONLY"
        count.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; count.TextSize = 9
        count.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right

        -- Read only. Kept as a TextBox rather than a label so it still looks
        -- like the field it used to be, but nothing here writes a title.
        local box = Instance.new("TextBox", r)
        box.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 24); box.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 1, -32)
        box.BorderSizePixel = 0

        box.Font = Enum.Font.Code; box.TextSize = 12
