        box.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        box.ZIndex = EZ + 3
        corner(box, 6); padding(box, 0, 0, 9, 9)
        SilenceAPI._theme(box, "BackgroundColor3", "bg3")
        SilenceAPI._theme(box, "TextColor3", "textDim")
        stroke(box, Theme.border, 1, 0.6)

        local hintT = Instance.new("TextLabel", p)
        hintT.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 30)
        hintT.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        hintT.Text = "  Your name is given out by an admin and is the same for"
                  .. " everyone who sees you."
        hintT.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham; hintT.TextSize = 10
        hintT.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        hintT.TextWrapped = true
        hintT.ZIndex = EZ + 2
        SilenceAPI._theme(hintT, "TextColor3", "textDim")

        -- the admin panel rewrites this title, so let it push in here
        syncEditorTitle = function()
            if box and box.Parent then box.Text = myTitle(); drawFace() end
        end

        eCaption(p, "EMOJI")
        do
            -- Codepoints, not literal characters: see the note on
            -- TagSettings.emoji. Chosen to cover the same ground as the
            -- shop's own emoji list.
            local CPS = {
                0, 0x1F480, 0x1F525, 0x2764, 0x2B50, 0x1F451, 0x1F47D,
                0x1F47B, 0x1F608, 0x2744, 0x26A1, 0x1F308, 0x1F984,
                0x1F987, 0x1F383, 0x1F48E, 0x1F4B0, 0x1F52A, 0x1F4A3,
                0x1F441, 0x1F9E0, 0x1F409, 0x1F40D, 0x1F43A, 0x1F981,
                0x1F980, 0x1F352, 0x1F319, 0x2600, 0x2601, 0x1F30A,
                0x1F4A5, 0x2728, 0x1F339, 0x1F340, 0x1F3AF, 0x1F3B2,
                0x1F947, 0x1F6E1, 0x1F5E1, 0x1F3B8, 0x1F3A9,
                -- blood and the rest of that corner, since the shop sells it
                0x1FA78, 0x1F489, 0x1F9DB, 0x1F9DF, 0x1F571, 0x2620,
                0x1F5A4, 0x1F494, 0x1F9E1, 0x1F49C, 0x1F49A, 0x1F499,
            }
            local box = Instance.new("Frame", p)
            box.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 0)
            box.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
            box.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            box.ZIndex = EZ + 2
            local lay = Instance.new("UIGridLayout", box)
            lay.CellSize = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 40)
            lay.CellPadding = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 6)
            lay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

            local paints = {}
            for i, cp in ipairs(CPS) do
                local b = Instance.new("TextButton", box)
                b.BorderSizePixel = 0
                b.AutoButtonColor = false
                b.LayoutOrder = i
                b.ZIndex = EZ + 3
                b.TextSize = cp == 0 and 11 or 20
                b.Font = cp == 0 and Enum.Font.GothamBold or Enum.Font.Gotham
                b.Text = cp == 0 and "OFF" or SilenceAPI._emojiPrefix(cp)
                corner(b, 8)
                local st = stroke(b, Theme.bg4, 1, 0.6, "bg4")
                SilenceAPI._theme(b, "TextColor3", "text")
                local function paint()
                    local on = ((tonumber(TagSettings.emoji) or 0) == cp)
                    b.BackgroundColor3 = on and Theme.accent or Theme.bg2
                    st.Transparency = on and 0 or 0.6
                end
                paint()
                paints[#paints+1] = paint
                b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    TagSettings.emoji = cp
                    for _, fn in ipairs(paints) do fn() end
                    touched(cp == 0 and "emoji off" or "emoji set")
                end)
            end
        end

        eCaption(p, "FONT")
        local fontItems = {}
        for _, f in ipairs(FONTS) do
            fontItems[#fontItems+1] = { label = f.label, value = f.font, font = f.font }
        end
        eGrid(p, fontItems,
            function() return TagSettings.font end,
            function(v) TagSettings.font = v; touched("font changed") end, 104)

        eCaption(p, "LAYOUT")
        eSlider(p, "Title size", 8, 20,
            function() return TagSettings.titleSize end,
            function(v) TagSettings.titleSize = v; touched("size " .. v) end, true)
        eSlider(p, "Card width", 120, 260,
            function() return TagSettings.width end,
            function(v) TagSettings.width = v; touched("width " .. v) end, true)
        eToggle(p, "Show @username line",
            function() return TagSettings.showUser end,
            function(v) TagSettings.showUser = v; touched(v and "username shown" or "username hidden") end)
        eToggle(p, "Show avatar badge",
            function() return TagSettings.showBadge end,
            function(v) TagSettings.showBadge = v; touched(v and "badge on" or "badge off") end)
        eToggle(p, "Centre the text",
            function() return TagSettings.centered end,
            function(v) TagSettings.centered = v; touched(v and "centred" or "left aligned") end)

        eCaption(p, "OUTLINE")
        eToggle(p, "Outline behind the title",
            function() return TagSettings.outline end,
            function(v) TagSettings.outline = v; touched(v and "outline on" or "outline off") end)
        eColor(p, "Outline colour", function() return TagSettings.outlineCol end,
               function(c) TagSettings.outlineCol = c end)
    end

    ------------------------------------------------------------ PAGE: STYLE
    do
        local p = makePage("STYLE")
        eCaption(p, "COLOURS")
        eColor(p, "Background",   function() return TagSettings.bg end,
               function(c) TagSettings.bg = c end)

               function(c) TagSettings.accent = c; TagSettings.border = c end)
        eColor(p, "Title text",   function() return TagSettings.text end,

               function(c) TagSettings.sub = c end)

            function() return TagSettings.gradient end,
            function(v) TagSettings.gradient = v; touched(v and "gradient on" or "gradient off") end)

               function(c) TagSettings.bg2 = c end)

            function() return TagSettings.gradRot end,

            function() return TagSettings.rainbow end,
            function(v) TagSettings.rainbow = v; touched(v and "rainbow on" or "rainbow off") end)
        eSlider(p, "Cycle speed", 0.05, 1.5,
            function() return TagSettings.rainbowSpeed end,
            function(v) TagSettings.rainbowSpeed = v; touched("speed") end)

        rbHint.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 26); rbHint.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        rbHint.Text = "  Rainbow drives the live tag every frame. The preview above keeps the fixed colour."
        rbHint.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham; rbHint.TextSize = 10
        rbHint.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        rbHint.TextWrapped = true
        rbHint.ZIndex = EZ + 2
        SilenceAPI._theme(rbHint, "TextColor3", "textDim")
    end

            TagSettings.distGrow  = 0.5

    ------------------------------------------------------------ PAGE: EXPORT
    -- The whole look as one line of text: copy it, send it to someone, paste
    -- theirs back in. Colours go out as 0-255 triples and the font as its
    -- name, so a build without that font falls back instead of erroring.
    local function tagCode()
        local HS = game:GetService("HttpService")
        local function c3(col)
            return { math.floor(col.R * 255 + 0.5),
                     math.floor(col.G * 255 + 0.5),
                     math.floor(col.B * 255 + 0.5) }
        end
        local t = {
            bg = c3(TagSettings.bg),   bg2 = c3(TagSettings.bg2),
            ac = c3(TagSettings.accent), tx = c3(TagSettings.text),
            sb = c3(TagSettings.sub),  oc = c3(TagSettings.outlineCol),
            fn = tostring(TagSettings.font):gsub("Enum%.Font%.", ""),
            ts = TagSettings.titleSize, wd = TagSettings.width,
            rd = TagSettings.radius,    al = TagSettings.bgAlpha,
            gl = TagSettings.glow,      pa = TagSettings.particle,
            su = TagSettings.showUser,  bd = TagSettings.showBadge,
            ce = TagSettings.centered,  gd = TagSettings.gradient,
            gr = TagSettings.gradRot,   ol = TagSettings.outline,
            rb = TagSettings.rainbow,   rs = TagSettings.rainbowSpeed,
            oy = TagSettings.offsetY,   md = TagSettings.maxDist,
            dg = TagSettings.distGrow,
            em = TagSettings.emoji,
            -- horns, headless and body scale go out with the rest, so the
            -- worker needs no new field for them either
            lp = SilenceAPI._myLarpPayload and SilenceAPI._myLarpPayload() or nil,
            pr = TagSettings.partRate,  pu = TagSettings.pulse,
            ti = myTitle(),
            -- The aura rides along in the same string the tag design uses,
            -- so it reaches everyone through the endpoint that is already
            -- there and the worker needs no new field.
            au = {
                on = State.aura.on and true or false,
                st = tostring(State.aura.style or "sparkle"),
                gd = State.aura.gradient and true or false,
                rb = State.aura.rainbow and true or false,
                sz = tonumber(State.aura.size) or 1,
                rt = tonumber(State.aura.rate) or 1,
                c1 = c3(State.aura.color),
                c2 = c3(State.aura.color2),
            },
        }
        local ok, j = pcall(function() return HS:JSONEncode(t) end)
        if not ok then return "" end
        return "SILTAG1|" .. j
    end
    -- the sync block lives outside this window's scope and needs to be able
    -- to ask what your tag currently looks like
    SilenceAPI._tagCode = tagCode

    local function applyTagCode(str)
        local body = tostring(str or ""):match("SILTAG1|(.+)$")
        if not body then return false, "not a Silence tag code" end
        local HS = game:GetService("HttpService")
        local ok, t = pcall(function() return HS:JSONDecode(body) end)
        if not ok or type(t) ~= "table" then return false, "code is damaged" end

        local function col(v, fallback)
            if type(v) == "table" and #v >= 3 then
                return Color3.fromRGB(v[1], v[2], v[3])
            end
            return fallback
        end
        local function num(v, fallback)
            local n = tonumber(v); return n or fallback
        end
        local function boo(v, fallback)
            if type(v) == "boolean" then return v end
            return fallback
        end

        TagSettings.bg         = col(t.bg,  TagSettings.bg)
        TagSettings.bg2        = col(t.bg2, TagSettings.bg2)
        TagSettings.accent     = col(t.ac,  TagSettings.accent)
        TagSettings.border     = TagSettings.accent
        TagSettings.text       = col(t.tx,  TagSettings.text)
        TagSettings.sub        = col(t.sb,  TagSettings.sub)
        TagSettings.outlineCol = col(t.oc,  TagSettings.outlineCol)
        TagSettings.titleSize  = num(t.ts,  TagSettings.titleSize)
        TagSettings.width      = num(t.wd,  TagSettings.width)
        TagSettings.radius     = num(t.rd,  TagSettings.radius)
        TagSettings.bgAlpha    = num(t.al,  TagSettings.bgAlpha)
        TagSettings.glow       = num(t.gl,  TagSettings.glow)
        TagSettings.gradRot    = num(t.gr,  TagSettings.gradRot)
        TagSettings.rainbowSpeed = num(t.rs, TagSettings.rainbowSpeed)
        TagSettings.offsetY    = num(t.oy,  TagSettings.offsetY)
        TagSettings.maxDist    = num(t.md,  TagSettings.maxDist)
        TagSettings.distGrow   = num(t.dg,  TagSettings.distGrow)
        TagSettings.emoji      = num(t.em,  TagSettings.emoji)
        -- a saved slot restores the aura with the rest of the look
        if type(t.au) == "table" then
            local au = t.au
            State.aura.on       = au.on and true or false
            State.aura.style    = tostring(au.st or State.aura.style)
            State.aura.gradient = au.gd and true or false
            State.aura.rainbow  = au.rb and true or false
            State.aura.size     = num(au.sz, State.aura.size)
            State.aura.rate     = num(au.rt, State.aura.rate)
            State.aura.color    = col(au.c1, State.aura.color)
            State.aura.color2   = col(au.c2, State.aura.color2)
            if SilenceAPI._refreshAura then pcall(SilenceAPI._refreshAura) end
        end
        TagSettings.partRate   = num(t.pr,  TagSettings.partRate)
        TagSettings.showUser   = boo(t.su,  TagSettings.showUser)
        TagSettings.showBadge  = boo(t.bd,  TagSettings.showBadge)
        TagSettings.centered   = boo(t.ce,  TagSettings.centered)
        TagSettings.gradient   = boo(t.gd,  TagSettings.gradient)
        TagSettings.outline    = boo(t.ol,  TagSettings.outline)
        TagSettings.rainbow    = boo(t.rb,  TagSettings.rainbow)
        TagSettings.pulse      = boo(t.pu,  TagSettings.pulse)
        if type(t.pa) == "string" and PARTICLE_GLYPHS[t.pa] then
            TagSettings.particle = t.pa
        elseif t.pa == "None" then
            TagSettings.particle = "None"
        end
        if type(t.fn) == "string" then
            local okF, f = pcall(function() return Enum.Font[t.fn] end)
            if okF and f then TagSettings.font = f end
        end
        -- The title deliberately does not come back from a code. It is handed
        -- out from the admin panel, and honouring t.ti here would have been a
        -- one-line way around that: paste any SILTAG1 string, get any name.
        -- Saved slots and the on-disk restore go through this same function.
        return true
    end
    -- exported here rather than next to _tagCode, which sits above this
    -- definition and would have captured a nil
    SilenceAPI._applyTagCode = applyTagCode

    ------------------------------------------------------------ PAGE: SAVES
    -- Two different things live here. The editor writes the current look to
    -- disk after every change, so a reload comes back exactly as you left it
    -- without anyone pressing save. On top of that sit five named slots you
    -- fill by hand - and a slot is just the SILTAG1 string the EXPORT page
    -- already produces, so storing one is "keep this string" and restoring
    -- one is applyTagCode.
    local STYLE_FILE = "silence_tagstyle.txt"
    local SLOTS_FILE = "silence_tagslots.json"
    local styleSlots = {}
    for i = 1, 5 do styleSlots[i] = { name = "Slot " .. i, code = "" } end

    local function canTagFile()
        return type(writefile) == "function" and type(readfile) == "function"
    end

    local function saveStyleSlots()
        if not canTagFile() then return end
        local HS = game:GetService("HttpService")
        pcall(function() writefile(SLOTS_FILE, HS:JSONEncode(styleSlots)) end)
    end

    if canTagFile() then
        local HS = game:GetService("HttpService")
        local okR, content = pcall(function() return readfile(SLOTS_FILE) end)
        if okR and type(content) == "string" and content ~= "" then
            local okD, data = pcall(function() return HS:JSONDecode(content) end)
            if okD and type(data) == "table" then
                for i = 1, 5 do
                    local e = data[i]
                    if type(e) == "table" then
                        styleSlots[i].name = tostring(e.name or styleSlots[i].name):sub(1, 22)
                        styleSlots[i].code = tostring(e.code or "")
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- Debounced: dragging a slider calls touched() every frame, and each one
    -- would otherwise be its own disk write.
    do
        local dirty = false
        saveStyleSoon = function()
            if not canTagFile() or dirty then return end
            dirty = true
            task.delay(0.8, function()
                dirty = false
                pcall(function() writefile(STYLE_FILE, tagCode()) end)
            end)
        end
    end

    -- Restore before anything is drawn with it. The repaint is deferred
    -- because the widgets showing these values are still being built.
    if canTagFile() then
        local okR, content = pcall(function() return readfile(STYLE_FILE) end)
        if okR and type(content) == "string" and content ~= "" then
            local okA = pcall(applyTagCode, content)
            if okA then
                task.defer(function()
                    for _, fn in ipairs(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers) do pcall(fn) end
                    pcall(refreshSelfTag)
                    pcall(drawFace)
                end)
            end
        end
    end

    do
        local p = makePage("SAVES")
        eCaption(p, "SAVED STYLES")

        local function slotRow(i)
            local r = Instance.new("Frame", p)
            r.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 68)
            r.BorderSizePixel = 0
            r.ZIndex = EZ + 2
            corner(r, 9)
            SilenceAPI._theme(r, "BackgroundColor3", "bg2")
            stroke(r, Theme.bg4, 1, 0.5, "bg4")

            local nameBox = Instance.new("TextBox", r)
            nameBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 240, 0, 22)
            nameBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 10)
            nameBox.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            nameBox.Text = styleSlots[i].name
            nameBox.PlaceholderText = "Slot " .. i
            nameBox.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; nameBox.TextSize = 12
            nameBox.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            nameBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
            nameBox.ZIndex = EZ + 3
            SilenceAPI._theme(nameBox, "TextColor3", "text")

            local state = Instance.new("TextLabel", r)
            state.Size = UDim2.new(0, 120, 0, 22)
            state.Position = UDim2.new(1, -134, 0, 10)
            state.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            state.Font = Enum.Font.Code; state.TextSize = 10
            state.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
            state.ZIndex = EZ + 3
            SilenceAPI._theme(state, "TextColor3", "textDim")

            local function paint()
                state.Text = (styleSlots[i].code ~= "" and "saved" or "empty")
            end
            paint()

            nameBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
                local t = tostring(nameBox.Text):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
                if t == "" then t = "Slot " .. i end
                styleSlots[i].name = t:sub(1, 22)
                nameBox.Text = styleSlots[i].name
                saveStyleSlots()
                if statusLbl then statusLbl.Text = "renamed" end
            end)

            local function mini(label, x, w, cb)
                local b = Instance.new("TextButton", r)
                b.Size = UDim2.new(0, w, 0, 22)
                b.Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, 38)
                b.BorderSizePixel = 0
                b.Text = label
                b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; b.TextSize = 10
                b.AutoButtonColor = false
                b.ZIndex = EZ + 3
                corner(b, 7)
                SilenceAPI._theme(b, "BackgroundColor3", "bg3")
                SilenceAPI._theme(b, "TextColor3", "text")
                local st = stroke(b, Theme.bg4, 1, 0.6, "bg4")
                b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                    TweenService:Create(st, SilenceAPI._twFade,
                        { Color = Theme.accent, Transparency = 0.2 }):Play()
                end)
                b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                    TweenService:Create(st, SilenceAPI._twFade,
                        { Color = Theme.bg4, Transparency = 0.6 }):Play()
                end)
                b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(cb)
                return b
            end

            mini("SAVE", 14, 84, function()
                styleSlots[i].code = tagCode()
                saveStyleSlots(); paint()
                if statusLbl then statusLbl.Text = "stored in " .. styleSlots[i].name end
            end)
            mini("LOAD", 104, 84, function()
                if styleSlots[i].code == "" then
                    if statusLbl then statusLbl.Text = "that slot is empty" end
                    return
                end
                local okA, why = applyTagCode(styleSlots[i].code)
                if not okA then
                    if statusLbl then statusLbl.Text = tostring(why) end
                    return
                end
                for _, fn in ipairs(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers) do pcall(fn) end
                touched(styleSlots[i].name .. " loaded")
            end)
            mini("CLEAR", 194, 84, function()
                styleSlots[i].code = ""
                saveStyleSlots(); paint()
                if statusLbl then statusLbl.Text = "slot cleared" end
            end)
        end

        for i = 1, 5 do slotRow(i) end

        eCaption(p, "HOW THIS WORKS")
        local hint = Instance.new("TextLabel", p)
        hint.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 72)
        hint.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        hint.Text = "  Everything you change is written to disk straight away, so a\n"
                 .. "  reload brings your tag back on its own - the slots are not\n"
                 .. "  needed for that. They are for keeping several looks side by\n"
                 .. "  side. Click a slot name to rename it."
        hint.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham; hint.TextSize = 10
        hint.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        hint.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
        hint.ZIndex = EZ + 2
        SilenceAPI._theme(hint, "TextColor3", "textDim")
    end

    do
        local p = makePage("EXPORT")
        eCaption(p, "YOUR TAG AS TEXT")

        local outRow = Instance.new("Frame", p)
        outRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 92)
        outRow.BorderSizePixel = 0
        outRow.ZIndex = EZ + 2
        corner(outRow, 9)
        SilenceAPI._theme(outRow, "BackgroundColor3", "bg2")
        stroke(outRow, Theme.bg4, 1, 0.5, "bg4")

        local outBox = Instance.new("TextBox", outRow)
        outBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -20); outBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10)
        outBox.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        outBox.Font = Enum.Font.Code; outBox.TextSize = 10
        outBox.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        outBox.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
        outBox.TextWrapped = true
        outBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        outBox.MultiLine = true
        outBox.Text = ""
        outBox.ZIndex = EZ + 3
        SilenceAPI._theme(outBox, "TextColor3", "textDim")

        local function refreshCode() outBox.Text = tagCode() end
        refreshCode()

        eButton(p, "Refresh code", function()
            refreshCode(); touched("code refreshed")
        end)
        eButton(p, "Copy code to clipboard", function()
            refreshCode()
            local done = false
            pcall(function() setclipboard(outBox.Text); done = true end)
            if not done then pcall(function() toclipboard(outBox.Text); done = true end) end
            notify("Tag Editor", done and "Tag code copied."
                                     or "No clipboard access in this executor.", 4)
        end)

        eCaption(p, "LOAD SOMEONE ELSE'S")
        local inRow = Instance.new("Frame", p)
        inRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 70)
        inRow.BorderSizePixel = 0
        inRow.ZIndex = EZ + 2
        corner(inRow, 9)
        SilenceAPI._theme(inRow, "BackgroundColor3", "bg2")
        stroke(inRow, Theme.bg4, 1, 0.5, "bg4")

        local inBox = Instance.new("TextBox", inRow)
        inBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -20); inBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10)
        inBox.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        inBox.Font = Enum.Font.Code; inBox.TextSize = 10
        inBox.PlaceholderText = "paste a SILTAG1|... code here"
        inBox.Text = ""
        inBox.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        inBox.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
        inBox.TextWrapped = true
        inBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        inBox.MultiLine = true
        inBox.ZIndex = EZ + 3
        SilenceAPI._theme(inBox, "PlaceholderColor3", "textDim")
        SilenceAPI._theme(inBox, "TextColor3", "text")

        eButton(p, "Apply pasted code", function()
            local okA, why = applyTagCode(inBox.Text)
            if okA then
                for _, fn in ipairs(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers) do pcall(fn) end
                if syncEditorTitle then pcall(syncEditorTitle) end
                touched("code applied")
                refreshCode()
                notify("Tag Editor", "Tag loaded from code.", 3)
            else
                notify("Tag Editor", why or "That code could not be read.", 4)
            end
        end)

        local exHint = Instance.new("TextLabel", p)
        exHint.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 40); exHint.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        exHint.Text = "  The code carries every setting on the other four pages, including the title. Fonts that a client does not have fall back instead of failing."
        exHint.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham; exHint.TextSize = 10
        exHint.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        exHint.TextWrapped = true
        exHint.ZIndex = EZ + 2
        SilenceAPI._theme(exHint, "TextColor3", "textDim")
    end

    ------------------------------------------------------------ FOOTER
    do
        local bar = Instance.new("Frame", TagSettingsPopup)
        bar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -96, 0, 22); bar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 80, 1, -30)
        bar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
        bar.ZIndex = EZ + 2

        local dot = Instance.new("Frame", bar)
        dot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 6); dot.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -3)
        dot.BorderSizePixel = 0; dot.ZIndex = EZ + 3
        local dc = Instance.new("UICorner", dot); dc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        SilenceAPI._theme(dot, "BackgroundColor3", "accent")

        statusLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", bar)
        statusLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -140, 1, 0); statusLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 0)
        statusLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        statusLbl.Text = "ready"
        statusLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium; statusLbl.TextSize = 10
        statusLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        statusLbl.ZIndex = EZ + 3
        SilenceAPI._theme(statusLbl, "TextColor3", "textDim")

        local note = Instance.new("TextLabel", bar)
        note.Size = UDim2.new(0, 130, 1, 0); note.Position = UDim2.new(1, -130, 0, 0)
        note.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        note.Text = "client-side only"
        note.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham; note.TextSize = 10
        note.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
        note.ZIndex = EZ + 3
        SilenceAPI._theme(note, "TextColor3", "textDim")
    end

    railTile("TEXT",   "TEXT",   "text",   1)
    railTile("STYLE",  "STYLE",  "style",  2)
    railTile("FX",     "FX",     "fx",     3)
    railTile("PRESET", "PRESETS","preset", 4)
    railTile("SAVES",  "SAVES",  "save",   5)
    railTile("EXPORT", "EXPORT", "export", 6)
    showPage("TEXT")
    drawFace()

    -- The card in the preview is rebuilt on every change, so the cycling is
    -- driven from outside it: look up whatever card is current and tint it.
    -- Without this the preview sat still while the real tag cycled.
    track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not TagSettingsPopup.Visible then return end
        if not TagSettings.rainbow then return end
        local card = face[1]
        if not card or not card.Parent then return end
        local hue = (tick() * (tonumber(TagSettings.rainbowSpeed) or 0.2)) % 1
        local col = Color3.fromHSV(hue, 0.85, 1)
        for _, o in ipairs(card:GetChildren()) do
            if o:IsA("UIStroke") then o.Color = col end
        end
    end))

    -- opening the window is the first time anything in it gets measured
    track(TagSettingsPopup:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
        if not TagSettingsPopup.Visible then return end
        task.defer(function()
            for _, fit in pairs(pageFit) do pcall(fit) end
        end)
    end))

    SilenceAPI._modalWatch(TagSettingsPopup)

    SilenceAPI._toggleTagEditor = function()
        TagSettingsPopup.Visible = not TagSettingsPopup.Visible
        if TagSettingsPopup.Visible then drawFace() end
    end

    end)()  -- TagSettingsPopup IIFE

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- ADMIN PANEL
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    print("[Silence] building admin panel...")
    -- The long one always keeps working as a way back in: rebind it to
    -- something you then forget and you would have locked yourself out of
    -- the only panel that can set ranks.
    local ADMIN_COMMAND  = "/silenceadmin"
    local ADMIN_PASSWORD = "sln.admin.tylerdaddy"
    -- Load password from file if possible
    pcall(function()
        local content = readfile("silence_admin_pass.txt")
        if content and content ~= "" then
            ADMIN_PASSWORD = content:gsub("\n",""):gsub("\r","")
        end
    end)
    local adminAuthed = false

    local function refreshPlayerTag(p)
        if p and p.Character and silenceUsers[p] then paintTag(p) end
    end

    local passOwnership, passError = {}, {}

    local function ownsScriptPass(p)
        if not p then return false end
        if SCRIPT_PASS_ID == 0 then return true end
        if passOwnership[p.UserId] ~= nil then return passOwnership[p.UserId] end
        local ok, owns = pcall(function()
            return MarketplaceService:UserOwnsGamePassAsync(p.UserId, SCRIPT_PASS_ID)
        end)
        if ok then
            passOwnership[p.UserId] = owns
            passError[p.UserId] = nil
            return owns
        end
        -- a thrown call is not an answer, so it is not cached: the next scan
        -- gets to try again instead of writing the player off forever
        passError[p.UserId] = tostring(owns):sub(1, 60)
        return false
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        if SCRIPT_PASS_ID == 0 then return end
        task.wait(3)
        local ok, owns = pcall(function()
            return MarketplaceService:UserOwnsGamePassAsync(LocalPlayer.UserId, SCRIPT_PASS_ID)
        end)
        if ok and owns then
            passOwnership[LocalPlayer.UserId] = true
            return
        end
        passOwnership[LocalPlayer.UserId] = false
        notify("Silence", "Access Pass required - opening purchase...", 4)
        local promptOk, promptErr = pcall(function()
            MarketplaceService:PromptGamePassPurchase(LocalPlayer, SCRIPT_PASS_ID)
        end)
        if not promptOk then
            notify("Silence", "Open IMPORTANT tab to buy pass manually", 6)
        end
    end)

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- FINDING OTHER SILENCE USERS
    -- Roblox refuses UserOwnsGamePassAsync for anyone but the local player:
    --     "can only query local player"
    -- Asking about other people is a server-only operation, so a client
    -- script simply cannot detect pass owners on its own. No amount of
    -- retrying changes that.
    --
    -- So it works the other way round. Each client checks ITS OWN pass,
    -- which is allowed, and if it owns it, announces itself to the endpoint.
    -- Everyone then reads that one list and tags whoever is on it. Detection
    -- becomes a lookup instead of a query.
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- knownUsers is who is running Silence RIGHT NOW and is the only thing
    -- that may put a tag over somebody's head. allUsers is everyone the
    -- endpoint has ever seen, name included, and only feeds the admin panel.
    -- Keeping them apart is what stops a player who used Silence last week
    -- from being tagged while standing there without it.
    local knownUsers = {}   -- [userId] = true, online now
    local allUsers   = {}   -- [userId] = name, ever seen
    local missCount  = {}   -- [userId] = consecutive polls without them
    local selfOwns  = false

    local function tagKnownPlayers()
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if knownUsers[p.UserId] and not silenceUsers[p] then
                tagUser(p)
                print("[Silence] Silence user in server: " .. p.Name)
                if p ~= LocalPlayer then
                    local lk = styleLook[p.UserId]
                    if SilenceAPI._applyAuraFor then
                        SilenceAPI._applyAuraFor(p, lk and lk.aura)
                    end
                    if SilenceAPI._applyLarpFor then
                        SilenceAPI._applyLarpFor(p, lk and lk.larp)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- kept so the rest of the file has something to call; a newly joined
    -- player only needs checking against the list we already hold
    local function queueScan(p)
        if p and knownUsers[p.UserId] and not silenceUsers[p] then tagUser(p) end
    end
    SilenceAPI._queueTagScan = queueScan

    -- asking about yourself is the one query the client is allowed
    task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(2)
        if SCRIPT_PASS_ID == 0 then
            selfOwns = true
        else
            local ok, owns = pcall(function()
                return MarketplaceService:UserOwnsGamePassAsync(
                    LocalPlayer.UserId, SCRIPT_PASS_ID)
            end)
            selfOwns = (ok and owns) and true or false
            passOwnership[LocalPlayer.UserId] = selfOwns
            if not ok then passError[LocalPlayer.UserId] = tostring(owns):sub(1, 60) end
        end
        if selfOwns and SilenceAPI._announceSelf then
            SilenceAPI._announceSelf()
        end
    end)

    track(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(p)
        task.wait(1)
        queueScan(p)
    end))

    track(MarketplaceService.PromptGamePassPurchaseFinished:Connect(function(player, passId, purchased)
        if passId ~= SCRIPT_PASS_ID or not purchased then return end
        passOwnership[player.UserId] = true
        -- somebody buying it mid-session should light up straight away
        if player == LocalPlayer then
            refreshSelfTag()
        elseif not silenceUsers[player] then
            tagUser(player)
        end
    end))

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- RANK SYNC CLIENT
    -- Pull on join and every 45s, push whenever a rank or title changes.
    -- Everything is a plain GET so it works on executors that only expose
    -- HttpGet; the worker treats /set as a write and /all as a read.
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    local function urlEnc(v)
        return tostring(v):gsub("[^%w%-%._~]", function(ch)
            return string.format("%%%02X", string.byte(ch))
        end)
    end

    local pullNote, joinNote = "not run yet", "not run yet"
    local joinOk, joinTries, announcing = false, 0, false
    SilenceAPI._syncNotes = function() return pullNote, joinNote end

    -- One fetch brings back both halves: who counts as a Silence user, and
    -- what rank each of them carries.
    local function pullRanks()
        if SYNC_BASE == "" then pullNote = "SYNC_BASE empty" return end
        local okG, body = pcall(function()
            return game:HttpGet(SYNC_BASE .. "all?t=" .. tostring(math.random(1, 1000000)), true)
        end)
        if not okG then
            pullNote = "HttpGet threw: " .. tostring(body):sub(1, 70)
            return
        end
        if type(body) ~= "string" then pullNote = "no body returned" return end
        local HS = game:GetService("HttpService")
        local okD, data = pcall(function() return HS:JSONDecode(body) end)
        if not okD or type(data) ~= "table" then
            pullNote = "not JSON: " .. tostring(body):sub(1, 70)
            return
        end
        if data.error then pullNote = "worker said: " .. tostring(data.error) end

        local ranks  = (type(data.ranks) == "table") and data.ranks or data
        local online = (type(data.online) == "table") and data.online
                       or ((type(data.users) == "table") and data.users or nil)
        local names  = (type(data.known)  == "table") and data.known or nil

        -- Presence is rebuilt from scratch every pull rather than added to.
        -- The endpoint lets an online key expire a couple of minutes after
        -- the last heartbeat, so somebody who closed the script drops out of
        -- this set and loses their tag here. Adding to the old set would have
        -- meant a tag once earned never went away.
        if online then
            local fresh = {}
            for _, uid in ipairs(online) do
                local n = tonumber(uid)
                if n then
                    fresh[n] = true
                    missCount[n] = 0
                    if allUsers[n] == nil then allUsers[n] = "?" end
                end
            end
            -- Hysteresis. The endpoint is eventually consistent, so one
            -- answer that leaves somebody out is not evidence that they
            -- left - and acting on a single one was enough to drop a tag and
            -- put it back seconds later, over and over. Three misses in a row
            -- is evidence; one is noise.
            for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                local uid = p.UserId
                if p ~= LocalPlayer and silenceUsers[p] and not fresh[uid] then
                    missCount[uid] = (missCount[uid] or 0) + 1
                    if missCount[uid] < 3 then
                        fresh[uid] = true
                    else
                        silenceUsers[p] = nil
                        destroyTag(p)
                    end
                end
            end
            -- your own tag is yours to decide, never the endpoint's
            if knownUsers[LocalPlayer.UserId] then fresh[LocalPlayer.UserId] = true end
            knownUsers = fresh
        end

        if names then
            for uid, nm in pairs(names) do
                local n = tonumber(uid)
                if n and type(nm) == "string" and nm ~= "" then allUsers[n] = nm end
            end
        end
        -- anyone standing here is worth a real name in the registry
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if allUsers[p.UserId] ~= nil or knownUsers[p.UserId] then
                allUsers[p.UserId] = p.Name
            end
        end
        allUsers[LocalPlayer.UserId] = LocalPlayer.Name

        -- Commands addressed to this account. The counter only ever goes up,
        -- so there is nothing to acknowledge and nothing to clear. The first
        -- pull only records where the counter stands - otherwise every load
        -- would replay whatever was last sent.
        if type(data.cmds) == "table" then
            local mine = data.cmds[tostring(LocalPlayer.UserId)]
            if type(mine) == "table" and tonumber(mine.n) then
                local cn = tonumber(mine.n)
                if SilenceAPI._lastCmdN == nil then
                    SilenceAPI._lastCmdN = cn
                elseif cn > SilenceAPI._lastCmdN then
                    SilenceAPI._lastCmdN = cn
                    if mine.what == "jump" and SilenceAPI._jumpscare then
                        pcall(SilenceAPI._jumpscare, tostring(mine.from or "Someone"))
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local changed = {}

        -- Somebody else's design, decoded once and kept until the string
        -- itself changes: re-parsing 20 players' styles every 45 seconds
        -- would be pure waste.
        local styles = (type(data.styles) == "table") and data.styles or nil
        if styles then
            for uid, entry in pairs(styles) do
                local n = tonumber(uid)
                local code = (type(entry) == "table") and entry.code or entry
                if n and n ~= LocalPlayer.UserId
                   and type(code) == "string" and code ~= "" then
                    -- having a saved design says nothing about being online
                    if allUsers[n] == nil then allUsers[n] = "?" end
                    if styleRaw[n] ~= code then
                        styleRaw[n] = code
                        local lk, ti = decodeTagCode(code)
                        styleLook[n]  = lk
                        styleTitle[n] = (type(entry) == "table" and entry.title) or ti
                        changed[n] = true
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        for uid, entry in pairs(ranks) do
            local n = tonumber(uid)
            if n and type(entry) == "table" then
                local rk = entry.rank
                local ti = entry.title
                if rk == nil or rk == "user" or not RANKMAP[rk] then rk = nil end
                if playerRank[n] ~= rk then playerRank[n] = rk; changed[n] = true end
                if type(ti) == "string" and ti ~= "" then
                    if customTitles[n] ~= ti then customTitles[n] = ti; changed[n] = true end
                elseif customTitles[n] ~= nil and n ~= LocalPlayer.UserId then
                    -- "rank only" from the endpoint, so a stale local title
                    -- has to go: titleFor prefers a custom title and an old
                    -- one would keep winning over the rank name forever.
                    --
                    -- Never for your own account. Your title is something you
                    -- type in the editor, the endpoint has no opinion on it,
                    -- and clearing it here wiped whatever you had just typed
                    -- on the very next poll - within five seconds.
                    customTitles[n] = nil; changed[n] = true
                end
                -- a rank is permanent, presence is not; only the registry
                if allUsers[n] == nil then allUsers[n] = "?" end
            end
        end

        local n = 0
        for _ in pairs(knownUsers) do n = n + 1 end
        if not data.error then
            pullNote = "ok, " .. n .. " on the list"
            if data.online == nil and data.users == nil then
                pullNote = pullNote .. "  (old worker - it sends no presence list)"
            end
        end

        -- The list came back and we are not on it, so the announce never
        -- landed. Ask again instead of sitting invisible until the next
        -- reload: the pull recovers on its own every 45s, the announce used
        -- to get exactly one shot at startup and no second chance.
        if online and selfOwns and not knownUsers[LocalPlayer.UserId]
           and SilenceAPI._announceSelf then
            SilenceAPI._announceSelf()
        end

        tagKnownPlayers()
        -- only redraw the tags that actually moved
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if changed[p.UserId] then
                if silenceUsers[p] then paintTag(p) end
                -- Your own name changing is almost always somebody ELSE
                -- having changed it, so the editor has to be told. It only
                -- refreshed on a local admin-panel click before, which is
                -- the one case where it was already right.
                if p == LocalPlayer then
                    if syncEditorTitle then pcall(syncEditorTitle) end
                    if SilenceAPI._refreshAdminTitleFor then
                        pcall(SilenceAPI._refreshAdminTitleFor, p.UserId)
                    end
                end
                -- your own aura is driven by the editor, never by the pull
                if p ~= LocalPlayer then
                    local lk = styleLook[p.UserId]
                    if SilenceAPI._applyAuraFor then
                        SilenceAPI._applyAuraFor(p, lk and lk.aura)
                    end
                    if SilenceAPI._applyLarpFor then
                        SilenceAPI._applyLarpFor(p, lk and lk.larp)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- One write on the way out, so your tag disappears for everybody else
    -- immediately instead of lingering until the presence entry expires.
    function SilenceAPI._leave()
        if SYNC_BASE == "" then return end
        pcall(function()
            game:HttpGet(SYNC_BASE .. "leave?id=" .. tostring(LocalPlayer.UserId)
                .. "&t=" .. tostring(math.random(1, 1000000)), true)
        end)
    end

    -- Keeps this account's online key from expiring. It has to repeat: the
    -- endpoint drops the key a couple of minutes after the last one, and
    -- that expiry is exactly what makes your tag vanish for everybody else
    -- when you close the script.
    function SilenceAPI._heartbeat()
        if SYNC_BASE == "" then return end
        pcall(function()
            game:HttpGet(SYNC_BASE .. "join?id=" .. tostring(LocalPlayer.UserId)
                .. "&name=" .. urlEnc(LocalPlayer.Name)
                .. "&t=" .. tostring(math.random(1, 1000000)), true)
        end)
    end

    -- Tell the endpoint this account is running Silence. Owning the pass is
    -- checked locally first, which is the one thing the client may ask.
    function SilenceAPI._announceSelf()
        if SYNC_BASE == "" then joinNote = "SYNC_BASE empty" return end
        if joinOk or announcing then return end
        if joinTries >= 20 then
            joinNote = "gave up after " .. joinTries .. " tries - press RESCAN"
            return
        end
        announcing = true
        task.spawn(function()
            -- Five tries with a widening gap. One failed request at startup
            -- must not decide whether this account is ever visible to anyone.
            for attempt = 1, 5 do
                joinTries = joinTries + 1
                -- The join URL used to be byte for byte identical on every
                -- attempt, so the first answer stuck: one failed announce
                -- against a worker that was still being deployed got cached
                -- and replayed forever, and no number of retries could ever
                -- get past it. /all never had this problem because it always
                -- carried a random t=. Now this one does too.
                local okJ, res = pcall(function()
                    return game:HttpGet(SYNC_BASE .. "join?id=" .. tostring(LocalPlayer.UserId)
                        .. "&name=" .. urlEnc(LocalPlayer.Name)
                        .. "&t=" .. tostring(math.random(1, 1000000)) .. "_" .. tostring(joinTries), true)
                end)
                if not okJ then
                    joinNote = "try " .. joinTries .. ": threw " .. tostring(res):sub(1, 55)
                elseif tostring(res):find('"ok"', 1, true) then
                    joinOk = true
                    joinNote = "ok (try " .. joinTries .. ")"
                    -- the endpoint has us now, so the report can say so
                    -- without waiting a whole poll cycle to hear it back
                    knownUsers[LocalPlayer.UserId] = true
                    -- a fresh install has never touched the editor, so this
                    -- is the only chance to publish what it already looks like
                    if SilenceAPI._pushStyle then SilenceAPI._pushStyle() end
                    break
                else
                    joinNote = "try " .. joinTries .. ": " .. tostring(res):sub(1, 55)
                end
                task.wait(attempt * 2)
            end
            announcing = false
            task.wait(1)
            pcall(pullRanks)
        end)
    end

    -- Takes a Player or a bare userId, because the admin panel can now set a
    -- rank on somebody who is not in this server and has no Player object.
    function SilenceAPI._pushRank(p)
        if not p then return end
        local uid = (type(p) == "number") and p or p.UserId
        if not uid then return end
        if SYNC_BASE == "" then notify("Rank", "SYNC_BASE is empty - local only.", 5) return end
        if SYNC_KEY == "" then
            notify("Rank", "No write key. Set getgenv().SilenceSyncKey before running.", 7)
            return
        end
        local rk = playerRank[uid] or "user"
        local ti = customTitles[uid] or ""
        task.spawn(function()
            local okP, res = pcall(function()
                return game:HttpGet(SYNC_BASE .. "set?key=" .. urlEnc(SYNC_KEY)
                    .. "&id=" .. tostring(uid)
                    .. "&rank=" .. urlEnc(rk)
                    .. "&title=" .. urlEnc(ti)
                    .. "&t=" .. tostring(math.random(1, 1000000)), true)
            end)
            -- the worker answers {"ok":true} or {"error":"nope"} for a bad
            -- key; either way the user should see which one it was
            if not okP then
                notify("Rank", "push failed: " .. tostring(res):sub(1, 90), 8)
            elseif tostring(res):find("error") then
                notify("Rank", "worker refused: " .. tostring(res):sub(1, 90), 8)
            end
        end)
    end

    -- Key protected: this is the one thing in here that reaches into
    -- somebody else's screen, so it stays with whoever holds the write key.
    function SilenceAPI._sendJumpscare(uid, name)
        if SYNC_BASE == "" then notify("Admin", "No sync endpoint.", 4) return end
        if SYNC_KEY == "" then notify("Admin", "Needs the write key.", 5) return end
        task.spawn(function()
            local okC, res = pcall(function()
                return game:HttpGet(SYNC_BASE .. "cmd?key=" .. urlEnc(SYNC_KEY)
                    .. "&id=" .. tostring(uid)
                    .. "&what=jump"
                    .. "&from=" .. urlEnc(LocalPlayer.Name)
                    .. "&t=" .. tostring(math.random(1, 1000000)), true)
            end)
            if not okC then
                notify("Admin", "send failed: " .. tostring(res):sub(1, 80), 6)
            elseif tostring(res):find("error") then
                notify("Admin", "worker refused: " .. tostring(res):sub(1, 80), 6)
            else
                notify("Admin", "Jumpscare sent to " .. tostring(name or uid)
                       .. " - lands within ~5s.", 5)
            end
        end)
    end

    -- Publishing your own design needs no admin key, because it only ever
    -- writes your own id. Someone could forge another player's style with a
    -- hand-made request; that is a fair trade against handing the write key
    -- to everyone who runs the script. Ranks stay key-protected, since those
    -- are the ones actually worth protecting.
    --
    -- Debounced: dragging a slider calls touched() dozens of times a second
    -- and each one would otherwise be its own request.
    local stylePending, lastStyleCode = false, ""
    function SilenceAPI._pushStyle()
        if SYNC_BASE == "" or not selfOwns then return end
        if stylePending then return end
        stylePending = true
        task.delay(6, function()
            stylePending = false
            local code = ""
            if SilenceAPI._tagCode then
                local okC, c = pcall(SilenceAPI._tagCode)
                if okC and type(c) == "string" then code = c end
            end
            -- dragging a slider ends on the value it started from often
            -- enough that this alone removes most of the writes
            if code == "" or code == lastStyleCode then return end
            lastStyleCode = code
            pcall(function()
                game:HttpGet(SYNC_BASE .. "style?id=" .. tostring(LocalPlayer.UserId)
                    .. "&name=" .. urlEnc(LocalPlayer.Name)
                    .. "&code=" .. urlEnc(code)
                    .. "&title=" .. urlEnc(customTitles[LocalPlayer.UserId] or "")
                    .. "&t=" .. tostring(math.random(1, 1000000)), true)
            end)
        end)
    end

    if SYNC_BASE ~= "" then
        -- Reading from the endpoint is cheap, writing to it is not: its free
        -- tier allows a hundred thousand reads a day but only a thousand
        -- writes. So the pull runs every few seconds and the heartbeat only
        -- every few minutes, and quitting sends one explicit goodbye rather
        -- than waiting for the presence entry to time out.
        --   getgenv().SilenceSyncInterval = 5   seconds between pulls
        --   getgenv().SilenceBeatMinutes  = 5   minutes between heartbeats
        local pullEvery, beatEvery = 5, 300
        pcall(function()
            local g = getgenv()
            if g then
                local a = tonumber(g.SilenceSyncInterval)
                local b = tonumber(g.SilenceBeatMinutes)
                if a and a >= 2 then pullEvery = a end
                if b and b >= 1 then beatEvery = b * 60 end
            end
        end)
        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(1.5)
            -- the announce already wrote one, so the next is a full gap away
            local lastBeat = tick()
            while true do
                if selfOwns and (tick() - lastBeat) >= beatEvery then
                    lastBeat = tick()
                    pcall(SilenceAPI._heartbeat)
                end
                pcall(pullRanks)
                task.wait(pullEvery)
            end
        end)
    end

    -- Everything the tag system knows, in one block. The Logs tab renders
    -- this; it is the fastest way to tell "nobody owns the pass" apart from
    -- "the ownership call is failing" apart from "the tag never got drawn".
    SilenceAPI._tagReport = function()
        local out = {}
        out[#out+1] = "pass id : " .. tostring(SCRIPT_PASS_ID)
            .. (SCRIPT_PASS_ID == 0 and "   (0 = detection disabled)" or "")
        out[#out+1] = "sync    : " .. (SYNC_BASE ~= "" and SYNC_BASE or "off, ranks stay local")
        out[#out+1] = "write   : " .. (SYNC_KEY ~= "" and "key present" or "no key, read only")
        out[#out+1] = ""
        local pn, jn = "-", "-"
        if SilenceAPI._syncNotes then pn, jn = SilenceAPI._syncNotes() end
        out[#out+1] = "my pass : " .. (selfOwns and "owned, announced" or "not owned")
        out[#out+1] = "join    : " .. tostring(jn)
        out[#out+1] = "pull    : " .. tostring(pn)
        local known = 0
        for _ in pairs(knownUsers) do known = known + 1 end
        out[#out+1] = "list    : " .. known .. " known Silence account(s)"
        out[#out+1] = ""
        local styled = 0
        for _ in pairs(styleLook) do styled = styled + 1 end
        out[#out+1] = "styles  : " .. styled .. " design(s) pulled"
        out[#out+1] = ""
        out[#out+1] = string.format("%-14s %-6s %-4s %-5s %-9s %s",
            "PLAYER", "LIST", "TAG", "STYLE", "RANK", "SHOWS AS")
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            out[#out+1] = string.format("%-14s %-6s %-4s %-5s %-9s %s",
                tostring(p.Name):sub(1, 14),
                knownUsers[p.UserId] and "yes" or "no",
                silenceUsers[p] and "yes" or "no",
                (p == LocalPlayer and "own") or (styleLook[p.UserId] and "yes" or "no"),
                playerRank[p.UserId] or "-",
                titleFor(p))
        end
        out[#out+1] = ""
        out[#out+1] = "LARP MOUNTS  (part:assetid=result, FAILED = asset never loaded)"
        local anyL = false
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            local lg = SilenceAPI._larpLog and SilenceAPI._larpLog[p]
            if lg and #lg > 0 then
                anyL = true
                out[#out+1] = "  " .. tostring(p.Name):sub(1, 14) .. "  "
                              .. table.concat(lg, "  ")
            end
        end
        if not anyL then
            out[#out+1] = "  nothing mounted - no larp data arrived for anyone"
        end
        out[#out+1] = ""
        out[#out+1] = "SHOWS AS is what THIS client draws over them right now."
        out[#out+1] = "If yours differs from theirs, the title did not travel;"
        out[#out+1] = "check /all in a browser for a title under \"ranks\"."
        if passError[LocalPlayer.UserId] then
            out[#out+1] = ""
            out[#out+1] = "own pass check failed: " .. tostring(passError[LocalPlayer.UserId])
        end
        if SYNC_BASE == "" then
            out[#out+1] = ""
            out[#out+1] = "SYNC_BASE is empty, so nobody can be discovered."
            out[#out+1] = "Roblox forbids a client from asking about another"
            out[#out+1] = "player's passes, so the endpoint is the only route."
        end
        return table.concat(out, "\n")
    end

    -- One list the admin panel can render straight: everybody the endpoint
    -- knows, whether or not they are in this server or even online, sorted so
    -- the people in front of you come first.
    SilenceAPI._silenceRoster = function()
        local byId = {}
        local function slot(uid)
            if not byId[uid] then
                byId[uid] = { id = uid, name = allUsers[uid] or "?",
                              online = knownUsers[uid] and true or false,
                              here = false }
            end
            return byId[uid]
        end
        for uid in pairs(allUsers)   do slot(uid) end
        for uid in pairs(knownUsers) do slot(uid).online = true end
        for uid in pairs(playerRank) do slot(uid) end
        for uid in pairs(customTitles) do slot(uid) end
        local me = slot(LocalPlayer.UserId)
        me.name, me.online, me.here = LocalPlayer.Name, true, true
        me.player = LocalPlayer

        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            local e = byId[p.UserId]
            if e then e.name, e.player, e.here = p.Name, p, true end
        end

        local out = {}
        for _, e in pairs(byId) do out[#out+1] = e end
        table.sort(out, function(a, b)
            if a.here   ~= b.here   then return a.here   end
            if a.online ~= b.online then return a.online end
            return tostring(a.name):lower() < tostring(b.name):lower()
        end)
        return out
    end

    SilenceAPI._rescanTags = function()
        for uid in pairs(passError) do passError[uid] = nil end
        joinOk, joinTries, announcing = false, 0, false
        if selfOwns then SilenceAPI._announceSelf() end
        pcall(pullRanks)
        tagKnownPlayers()
    end

    -- force a tag on somebody regardless of the pass, for testing
    SilenceAPI._forceTag = function(name)
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p.Name:lower():sub(1, #name) == name:lower() and not silenceUsers[p] then
                tagUser(p)
                return p.Name
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    SilenceAPI._ownsScriptPass = ownsScriptPass
    SilenceAPI._setTagsHidden = function(hidden)
        State.tagsHidden = hidden
        if hidden then
            for p, _ in pairs(activeTags) do destroyTag(p) end
        else
            for p, _ in pairs(silenceUsers) do
                if p.Character then createTag(p) end
            end
        end
    end

    local function tryAuth()
        if pwBox.Text == ADMIN_PASSWORD then
            adminAuthed = true
            PwPopup.Visible = false
            pwBox.Text = ""
            if AdminPanel then AdminPanel.Visible = true end
            notify("Silence Admin", "Authenticated", 3)
        else
            pwBox.Text = ""
            notify("Silence Admin", "Wrong password", 3)
        end
    end
    pwSubmit.MouseButton1Click:Connect(tryAuth)
    pwBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter) if enter then tryAuth() end end)
