    local adminRowSync = {}
    SilenceAPI._refreshAdminTitleFor = function(plr)
        local uid = (type(plr) == "number") and plr or (plr and plr.UserId)
        local fn = uid and adminRowSync[uid]; if fn then fn() end
    end

    -- Takes a roster entry rather than a Player: most accounts in this list
    -- are not in the server, so there is no Player object to hand it. `plr`
    -- is nil for those, and every use of it below is guarded.
    local function buildRow(e)
        local uid = e.id
        local plr = e.player
        local nm  = e.name or "?"

        local row = Instance.new("Frame", apList)
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 132)
        row.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg2
        row.BorderSizePixel = 0
        row.ZIndex = 181
        corner(row, 6)

        local nameLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
        nameLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -180, 0, 18)
        nameLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 6)
        nameLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        nameLbl.Text = (plr and (plr.DisplayName .. "  (@" .. plr.Name .. ")"))
                       or (nm .. "   #" .. tostring(uid))
        nameLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        nameLbl.TextSize = 12
        nameLbl.TextColor3 = Theme.text
        nameLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        nameLbl.ZIndex = 182

        -- three states worth telling apart: standing here, running Silence
        -- somewhere else, or not running it at all
        local where = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
        where.Size = UDim2.new(0, 160, 0, 18)
        where.Position = UDim2.new(1, -170, 0, 6)
        where.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        where.Text = e.here and "IN THIS SERVER"
                     or (e.online and "ONLINE ELSEWHERE" or "OFFLINE")
        where.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        where.TextSize = 9
        where.TextColor3 = e.here and Theme.accent
                           or (e.online and Theme.text or Theme.textDim)
        where.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
        where.ZIndex = 182

        local titleBox = Instance.new("TextBox", row)
        titleBox.Size = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 0, 26)
        titleBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 30)
        titleBox.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg3
        titleBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
        titleBox.Font = Enum.Font.Code
        titleBox.TextSize = 12
        titleBox.TextColor3 = Theme.text
        titleBox.Text = customTitles[uid] or ""
        titleBox.PlaceholderText = "custom title"
        titleBox.PlaceholderColor3 = Theme.textDim
        titleBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        titleBox.ZIndex = 182
        corner(titleBox, 4); padding(titleBox, 0, 0, 8, 8)

        local applyBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        applyBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 26)
        applyBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0.55, 16, 0, 30)
        applyBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg2
        applyBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        applyBtn.Text = "APPLY"
        applyBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        applyBtn.TextSize = 11
        applyBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.text
        applyBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        applyBtn.ZIndex = 182
        corner(applyBtn, 4); stroke(applyBtn, Theme.accent, 1, 0.5)

        local tagBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        tagBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 80, 0, 26)
        tagBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -90, 0, 30)
        tagBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg2
        tagBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        tagBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        tagBtn.TextSize = 11
        tagBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.text
        tagBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        tagBtn.ZIndex = 182
        corner(tagBtn, 4); stroke(tagBtn, Theme.accent, 1, 0.5)

        local jumpBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        jumpBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 84, 0, 26)
        jumpBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -180, 0, 30)
        jumpBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg2
        jumpBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        jumpBtn.Text = "JUMPSCARE"
        jumpBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        jumpBtn.TextSize = 10
        jumpBtn.TextColor3 = Theme.text
        jumpBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        jumpBtn.ZIndex = 182
        corner(jumpBtn, 4); stroke(jumpBtn, Theme.accent, 1, 0.5)
        jumpBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if SilenceAPI._sendJumpscare then
                SilenceAPI._sendJumpscare(uid, nm)
            end
        end)

        local function refreshTagBtn()
            if not plr then tagBtn.Text = "NOT HERE"; return end
            tagBtn.Text = silenceUsers[plr] and "TAG: ON" or "TAG: OFF"
        end
        refreshTagBtn()

        ------------------------------------------------------ RANK PICKER
        -- One chip per rank, painted in that rank's own accent so the grid
        -- reads as a legend. Picking one writes the rank, saves it, rebuilds
        -- that player's tag, and pushes it out to the other clients.
        local rankLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
        rankLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 14)
        rankLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 62)
        rankLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        rankLbl.Text = "RANK"
        rankLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        rankLbl.TextSize = 9
        rankLbl.TextColor3 = Theme.textDim
        rankLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        rankLbl.ZIndex = 182

        local rankBox = Instance.new("Frame", row)
        rankBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 52)
        rankBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 76)
        rankBox.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        rankBox.ZIndex = 182
        local rlay = Instance.new("UIGridLayout", rankBox)
        rlay.CellSize = UDim2.new(0, 96, 0, 22)
        rlay.CellPadding = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 5)
        rlay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

        local chipPaint = {}
        for i, r in ipairs(RANKS) do
            local accent = (r.look and r.look.accent) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            local c = Instance.new("TextButton", rankBox)
            c.BorderSizePixel = 0
            c.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg
            c.Text = r.title
            c.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            c.TextSize = 9
            c.TextColor3 = accent
            c.AutoButtonColor = false
            c.LayoutOrder = i
            c.ZIndex = 183
            corner(c, 5)
            local cst = Instance.new("UIStroke", c)
            cst.Color = accent; cst.Thickness = 1; cst.Transparency = 0.75

            local function paint()
                local on = ((playerRank[uid] or "user") == r.key)
                c.BackgroundColor3 = on and accent or Theme.bg
                c.TextColor3       = on and Theme.bg or accent
                cst.Transparency   = on and 0 or 0.75
            end
            c.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                playerRank[uid] = (r.key ~= "user") and r.key or nil
                saveRanks()
                -- an explicit custom title would hide the rank's own title,
                -- so picking a rank clears it and the rank name takes over
                customTitles[uid] = nil
                saveCustomTitles()
                titleBox.Text = ""
                if plr then
                    if not silenceUsers[plr] then tagUser(plr) else refreshPlayerTag(plr) end
                end
                if plr == LocalPlayer then refreshSelfTag(); syncEditorTitle() end
                for _, fn in ipairs(chipPaint) do fn() end
                if SilenceAPI._pushRank then SilenceAPI._pushRank(uid) end
                notify("Admin", nm .. " -> " .. r.title, 3)
            end)
            chipPaint[#chipPaint+1] = paint
            paint()
        end

        applyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            local txt = titleBox.Text
            customTitles[uid] = (txt ~= "" and txt) or nil
            saveCustomTitles()
            if plr and silenceUsers[plr] then refreshPlayerTag(plr) end
            if plr == LocalPlayer then
                refreshSelfTag()
                syncEditorTitle()
            end
            if SilenceAPI._pushRank then SilenceAPI._pushRank(uid) end
            notify("Admin", "Title set for " .. nm, 2)
        end)

        adminRowSync[uid] = function()
            titleBox.Text = customTitles[uid] or ""
        end

        tagBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if not plr then
                notify("Admin", nm .. " is not in this server.", 3); return
            end
            if silenceUsers[plr] then
                silenceUsers[plr] = nil
                destroyTag(plr)
            else
                tagUser(plr)
            end
            refreshTagBtn()
        end)

        return row
    end

    -- Everybody the endpoint knows, grouped by rank, filtered by the search
    -- box. The old version asked Roblox which players owned the pass - which
    -- a client may only ask about itself - so in practice this list never
    -- contained anyone but you.
    local function rebuildList()
        for _, c in ipairs(apList:GetChildren()) do
            if c:IsA("Frame") or c:IsA("TextLabel") then c:Destroy() end
        end

        local roster = {}
        if SilenceAPI._silenceRoster then
            local okR, r = pcall(SilenceAPI._silenceRoster)
            if okR and type(r) == "table" then roster = r end
        end

        local q = tostring(apSearch.Text):lower():match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
        local shown, order = 0, 0

        local function header(text, n)
            order = order + 1
            local h = Instance.new("TextLabel", apList)
            h.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24)
            h.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            h.Text = "  " .. text .. "   (" .. n .. ")"
            h.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            h.TextSize = 10
            h.TextColor3 = Theme.accent2 or Theme.textDim
            h.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            h.LayoutOrder = order
            h.ZIndex = 182
        end

        -- highest rank first, plain users last
        local groups, groupOf = {}, {}
        for i = #RANKS, 1, -1 do
            groups[#groups+1] = RANKS[i]
            groupOf[RANKS[i].key] = {}
        end

        for _, e in ipairs(roster) do
            local hit = (q == "")
                or (tostring(e.name):lower():find(q, 1, true) ~= nil)
                or (tostring(e.id):find(q, 1, true) ~= nil)
            if hit then
                local key = playerRank[e.id] or "user"
                if not groupOf[key] then key = "user" end
                table.insert(groupOf[key], e)
                shown = shown + 1
            end
        end

        for _, r in ipairs(groups) do
            local list = groupOf[r.key]
            if #list > 0 then
                header(r.title, #list)
                for _, e in ipairs(list) do
                    order = order + 1
                    local row = buildRow(e)
                    if row then row.LayoutOrder = order end
                end
            end
        end

        apCount.Text = (q ~= "") and (shown .. " of " .. #roster)
                       or (#roster .. " account(s)")

        if shown == 0 then
            local status = Instance.new("TextLabel", apList)
            status.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 48)
            status.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            status.Text = (q ~= "") and "  Nothing matches that search."
                or ("  No Silence accounts known yet." .. "\n"
                    .. "  They show up here once they have run the script.")
            status.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
            status.TextSize = 11
            status.TextColor3 = Theme.textDim
            status.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            status.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
            status.LayoutOrder = 0
            status.ZIndex = 182
        end
    end

    -- typing rebuilds the list, but not once per keystroke
    do
        local pending = false
        apSearch:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            if pending then return end
            pending = true
            task.delay(0.15, function()
                pending = false
                if AdminPanel.Visible then rebuildList() end
            end)
        end)
    end

    track(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function() if AdminPanel.Visible then rebuildList() end end))
    track(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function() if AdminPanel.Visible then rebuildList() end end))
    AdminPanel:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
        if AdminPanel.Visible then rebuildList() end
    end)

    SilenceAPI._modalWatch(AdminPanel)

    local function openAdminFlow()
        -- The write key only exists on the machine that sets it via
        -- getgenv(), never in the file everyone downloads. It is therefore
        -- the only thing here that actually separates you from a user - the
        -- password below is in the public source and protects nothing.
        -- Without the key a rank change would be refused by the endpoint
        -- anyway, so this refuses it earlier and more honestly.
        if SYNC_KEY == "" then
            notify("Admin", "Admin panel is for the key holder only.", 4)
            return
        end
        if adminAuthed then
            AdminPanel.Visible = true
        else
            pwBox.Text = ""
            PwPopup.Visible = true
            pwBox:CaptureFocus()
        end
    end
    SilenceAPI._openAdminFlow = openAdminFlow
    track(LocalPlayer.Chatted:Connect(function(msg)
        local custom = tostring(State.adminCmd or "")
        if msg == ADMIN_COMMAND or (custom ~= "" and msg == custom) then
            openAdminFlow()
        end
    end))

    SilenceAPI._destroyTags = function()
        for p,_ in pairs(activeTags) do destroyTag(p) end
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p.Character then
                local head = p.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                if head then
                    for _, child in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
                        if child.Name == "__SilenceTag" then
                            pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

end)
if not _tagOk then
    SilenceAPI._tagFault = tostring(_tagErr)
    warn("[Silence] TAG BLOCK FAILED: " .. tostring(_tagErr))
    print("[Silence] the overhead tags and the admin panel are unavailable,")
    print("[Silence] everything else still loaded. Error above.")
    task.delay(4, function()
        notify("Tag system failed", tostring(_tagErr):sub(1, 180), 12)
    end)
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- TAB: LOGS  -  chat, remote traffic and notification history
----------------------------------------------------------------
;(function()
local pLog = addTab("Logs")

-- One scrollable monospace pane per log. Rendering is pull-based (a single
-- Text assignment) rather than one Frame per line: these lists cap at 150-200
-- entries and rebuilding hundreds of instances every second would stutter.
local function makeLogView(parent, height)
    local box = Instance.new("Frame", parent)
    box.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height)
    box.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg2
    box.BorderSizePixel = 0
    corner(box, 8)
    local bs = Instance.new("UIStroke", box)
    bs.Color = Theme.border; bs.Thickness = 1; bs.Transparency = 0.55

    local scroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame", box)
    scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, -8)
    scroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 4)
    scroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    scroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
    scroll.ScrollBarThickness = 3
    scroll.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.accent
    scroll.ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.35
    scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    scroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    scroll.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", scroll)
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 0)
    lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 0)
    lbl.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.Code
    lbl.TextSize = 11
    lbl.TextColor3 = Theme.textDim
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    lbl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
    lbl.TextWrapped = true
    lbl.Text = "  (empty)"
    return lbl, scroll
end

local function joinLines(lines, empty)
    if #lines == 0 then return "  " .. empty end
    return table.concat(lines, "\n")
end

--------------------------------------------------------------- CHAT
sectionLabel(pLog, "CHAT")
toggleRow(pLog, "Record chat", function() return State.chatLog end,
    function(v) SilenceAPI._setChatLog(v) end)
local chatView = makeLogView(pLog, 130)

--------------------------------------------------------------- REMOTES
sectionLabel(pLog, "REMOTE SPY")
toggleRow(pLog, "Record RemoteEvent / Function calls",
    function() return State.remoteSpy end,
    function(v)
        State.remoteSpy = v
        if v then
            -- the recorder lives inside the __namecall hook, which is only
            -- installed once Silent Aim has been switched on at least once
            if not _silentAimHookInstalled then installSilentAimHook() end
            if not _silentAimHookInstalled then
                notify("Remote Spy", "Your executor blocks __namecall hooks.", 5)
                State.remoteSpy = false
            end
        end
    end)
local remoteView = makeLogView(pLog, 150)

--------------------------------------------------------------- NOTIFS
sectionLabel(pLog, "NOTIFICATION HISTORY")
local notifView = makeLogView(pLog, 130)

--------------------------------------------------------------- ICONS
-- The icon search runs while the script is still loading, long before this
-- tab exists, so its result is reported here rather than only as print
-- output nobody sees unless the console happened to be open.
sectionLabel(pLog, "ICON FILES")
local iconView = makeLogView(pLog, 150)

local function refreshIconReport()
    local out = {}
    local function statusLine(label, img)
        if not img then return label .. " : not found" end
        return label .. " : LOADED  <- " .. tostring(ICON_DIRS.src[label] or "?")
    end
    out[#out+1] = statusLine("discord-icon.png",  DISCORD_IMG)
    out[#out+1] = statusLine("settings-icon.png", SETTINGS_IMG)
    out[#out+1] = ""
    if ICON_DIRS.note then
        out[#out+1] = "probe: " .. tostring(ICON_DIRS.note)
        out[#out+1] = ""
    end
    if #ICON_DIRS == 0 then
        out[#out+1] = "This executor keeps file access inside its own folder,"
        out[#out+1] = "so your Desktop cannot be reached. Use the two buttons"
        out[#out+1] = "below: 'Set Up Icon Folder' drops a marker you can find"
        out[#out+1] = "with Windows search, then 'Re-check Icons Now' after you"
        out[#out+1] = "have put the PNGs there. No re-inject needed."
    else
        out[#out+1] = "folders that were checked:"
        for _, d in ipairs(ICON_DIRS) do out[#out+1] = "  " .. d end
    end
    out[#out+1] = ""
    out[#out+1] = "Names must match exactly. Save the PNGs WHITE on transparent"
    out[#out+1] = "- the panel is black, a black icon looks like it never loaded."
    iconView.Text = table.concat(out, "\n")
end
refreshIconReport()

-- Re-runs the loader and rebuilds the glyphs on the buttons that already
-- exist, so dropping the files in does not need a fresh inject. The workspace
-- lookups run before the directory discovery, which is already done, so
-- anything placed since boot is picked up.
local function reloadIcons()
    DISCORD_IMG = loadLocalImage(
        { "discord-icon.png", "silence_discord.png", "discord.png" },
        "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Tyler-daddy/silence-admin-panel/main/discord-icon.png")
    SETTINGS_IMG = loadLocalImage(
        { "settings-icon.png", "silence_settings.png", "gear.png" },
        "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Tyler-daddy/silence-admin-panel/main/settings-icon.png")

    -- every gear re-registers itself, so take a snapshot and start a new list
    local hosts = SilenceAPI._gearHosts or {}
    SilenceAPI._gearHosts = {}
    for _, b in ipairs(hosts) do
        if b and b.Parent then
            for _, c in ipairs(b:GetChildren()) do
                if c.Name == "GearImg" or c.Name == "GearVec" then
                    pcall(function() c:Destroy() end)
                end
            end
            pcall(SilenceAPI._gearIcon, b)
        end
    end

    local dh = SilenceAPI._discordHost
    if dh and dh.Parent then
        for _, c in ipairs(dh:GetChildren()) do
            if c.Name == "DiscordImg" or c.Name == "DiscordVec" then
                pcall(function() c:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
        if DISCORD_IMG then
            local img = Instance.new("ImageLabel", dh)
            img.Name = "DiscordImg"
            img.Size = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 20)
            img.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -10, 0.5, -10)
            img.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            img.Image = DISCORD_IMG
            img.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
            img.ZIndex = dh.ZIndex + 1
        elseif SilenceAPI._drawDiscord then
            pcall(SilenceAPI._drawDiscord, dh)
        end
    end

    refreshIconReport()
end

--------------------------------------------------------------- TAGS
-- Who the tag system thinks is a Silence user, and why. Redrawn every time
-- the Logs tab is on screen, so it follows a scan as it happens.
sectionLabel(pLog, "SILENCE USERS")
local tagView = makeLogView(pLog, 150)

--------------------------------------------------------------- ACTIONS
sectionLabel(pLog, "ACTIONS")
local function copyOut(text, what)
    local done = false
    pcall(function() setclipboard(text); done = true end)
    if not done then pcall(function() toclipboard(text); done = true end) end
    notify("Logs", done and (what .. " copied to clipboard")
                        or "No clipboard access in this executor", 3)
end

buttonRow(pLog, "Rescan Silence Users", function()
    if SilenceAPI._rescanTags then
        SilenceAPI._rescanTags()
        notify("Tags", "Rescanning - watch the SILENCE USERS panel above.", 4)
    end
end)
buttonRow(pLog, "Test Tag On Someone Else", function()
    -- Splits the two halves of "I cannot see other people's tags" apart.
    -- This draws a tag over a random stranger without asking the endpoint
    -- anything. Works -> rendering is fine and the problem is discovery.
    -- Does not work -> the endpoint was never the issue.
    local target
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= LocalPlayer then target = p break end
    end
    if not target then
        notify("Tags", "Nobody else in this server to test on.", 3)
    elseif SilenceAPI._forceTag then
        SilenceAPI._forceTag(target.Name)
        notify("Tags", "Forced a tag on " .. target.Name .. " - go look at them.", 5)
    end
end)
buttonRow(pLog, "Copy Tag Report", function()
    copyOut(tagView.Text, "Tag report")
end)

buttonRow(pLog, "Copy Chat Log", function()
    local out = {}
    for _, e in ipairs(SilenceAPI._chatLog) do
        out[#out+1] = "[" .. e.t .. "] " .. e.who .. ": " .. e.msg
    end
    copyOut(table.concat(out, "\n"), "Chat log")
end)
buttonRow(pLog, "Copy Remote Log", function()
    local out = {}
    for _, e in ipairs(SilenceAPI._remoteLog) do
        out[#out+1] = "[" .. e.t .. "] " .. e.method .. "  " .. e.name .. "  (" .. e.args .. ")"
    end
    copyOut(table.concat(out, "\n"), "Remote log")
end)
buttonRow(pLog, "Set Up Icon Folder", function()
    if type(writefile) ~= "function" then
        notify("Icons", "This executor has no writefile - icons cannot come from disk.", 6)
        return
    end
    -- a marker with a name nothing else on the machine has: Windows search
    -- finds it instantly, and that is the folder the executor can read
    local okMark = pcall(function()
        writefile("SILENCE_PUT_ICONS_HERE.txt",
            "Put discord-icon.png and settings-icon.png next to this file, " ..
            "or into the Silence folder beside it. " ..
            "Save them WHITE on transparent - the panel is black.")
    end)
    local madeFolder = false
    pcall(function()
        if type(makefolder) == "function" then
            makefolder("Silence")
            madeFolder = true
            writefile("Silence/PUT_THE_TWO_PNGS_IN_HERE.txt",
                "discord-icon.png and settings-icon.png go in this folder.")
        end
    end)
    if okMark then
        notify("Icons",
            "Wrote SILENCE_PUT_ICONS_HERE.txt" ..
            (madeFolder and " and made a Silence folder beside it" or "") ..
            ". Search Windows for SILENCE_PUT_ICONS_HERE to open that folder, " ..
            "drop the two PNGs in, then press Re-check Icons Now.", 14)
    else
        notify("Icons", "The executor refused to write the marker file.", 6)
    end
end)
buttonRow(pLog, "Re-check Icons Now", function()
    reloadIcons()
    if DISCORD_IMG and SETTINGS_IMG then
        notify("Icons", "Both icons loaded and applied.", 4)
    else
        notify("Icons", "Still missing - see ICON FILES above for what was checked.", 6)
    end
end)
buttonRow(pLog, "Copy Icon Report", function()
    copyOut(iconView.Text, "Icon report")
end)
buttonRow(pLog, "Clear All Logs", function()
    for i = #SilenceAPI._chatLog, 1, -1      do SilenceAPI._chatLog[i] = nil end
    for i = #SilenceAPI._remoteLog, 1, -1    do SilenceAPI._remoteLog[i] = nil end
    for i = #SilenceAPI._notifHistory, 1, -1 do SilenceAPI._notifHistory[i] = nil end
    notify("Logs", "Cleared", 2)
end)

--------------------------------------------------------------- REFRESH
-- only redraws while the Logs tab is the visible one, so the panel costs
-- nothing on every other tab
local lastDraw = 0
track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if SilenceAPI._activeTab ~= "Logs" then return end
    if tick() - lastDraw < 0.5 then return end
    lastDraw = tick()

    local c = {}
    for _, e in ipairs(SilenceAPI._chatLog) do
        c[#c+1] = "[" .. e.t .. "] " .. e.who .. ": " .. e.msg
    end
    chatView.Text = joinLines(c, State.chatLog and "listening..." or "recording is off")

    local r = {}
    for _, e in ipairs(SilenceAPI._remoteLog) do
        r[#r+1] = "[" .. e.t .. "] " .. e.method .. " " .. e.name .. "\n        (" .. e.args .. ")"
    end
    remoteView.Text = joinLines(r, State.remoteSpy and "listening..." or "spy is off")

    local n = {}
    for _, e in ipairs(SilenceAPI._notifHistory) do
        n[#n+1] = "[" .. e.t .. "] " .. e.title .. " - " .. e.text
    end
    notifView.Text = joinLines(n, "nothing yet")

    if SilenceAPI._tagFault then
        tagView.Text = "THE TAG BLOCK CRASHED WHILE LOADING:\n\n"
            .. tostring(SilenceAPI._tagFault)
            .. "\n\nOverhead tags and the admin panel are off. Send me this line."
    elseif SilenceAPI._tagReport then
        local okR, txt = pcall(SilenceAPI._tagReport)
        tagView.Text = okR and txt or "report failed"
    else
        tagView.Text = "tag system not loaded"
    end
end))
end)()

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- TAB: CONFIG  -  save / restore everything to the executor workspace
-- Placed last on purpose: every setter and every toggle refresher has to
-- exist before this runs, otherwise auto-load would apply into a half-built
-- panel.
----------------------------------------------------------------
;(function()
local pCfg = addTab("Config")
local CFG_FILE = "silence_config.json"

local function canFile()
    return type(writefile) == "function" and type(readfile) == "function"
end

-- Toggle name -> the function that actually makes it happen. Restoring a
-- config means calling these, not just writing State, so connections and
-- visuals come back too.
local APPLY = {
    infJump      = function(v) State.infJump = v end,
    noclip       = function(v) SilenceAPI._setNoclip(v) end,
    espOn        = setEsp,
    chamsOn      = setChams,
    tracersOn    = setTracers,
    fullbright   = setFullbright,
    disco        = setDisco,
    antiAfk      = setAntiAfk,
    aimbot       = setAimbot,
    silentAim    = setSilentAim,
    magicBullets = setMagicBullets,
    godmode      = setGod,
    trail        = setTrail,
    bighead      = function(v) setBigHead(v and 4 or nil) end,
    crashFx      = setCrashFx,
    clickTpOn    = function(v) State.clickTpOn = v end,
    nametagsOn   = function(v) State.nametagsOn = v end,
    espTeamCheck = function(v) State.espTeamCheck = v end,
    espBox       = function(v) State.espBox = v end,
    espHealthBar = function(v) State.espHealthBar = v end,
    aimbotTeamcheck       = function(v) State.aimbotTeamcheck = v end,
    aimbotVisibilityCheck = function(v) State.aimbotVisibilityCheck = v end,
    antiFling    = function(v) SilenceAPI._setAntiFling(v) end,
    antiVoid     = function(v) SilenceAPI._setAntiVoid(v) end,
    ghost        = function(v) SilenceAPI._setGhost(v) end,
    invisParts   = function(v) SilenceAPI._setInvisParts(v) end,
    headless     = function(v) SilenceAPI._setHeadless(v) end,
    accessoriesOn= function(v) SilenceAPI._setAccessories(v) end,
    vcBypass     = setVcBypass,
    vcAutoMute   = function(v) State.vcAutoMute = v end,
    allowJumpscare = function(v) State.allowJumpscare = v end,
    playerLog    = function(v) if SilenceAPI._setPlayerLog then SilenceAPI._setPlayerLog(v) end end,
    autoInteract = function(v) SilenceAPI._setAutoInteract(v) end,
    autoClick    = function(v) SilenceAPI._setAutoClick(v) end,
    autoRejoin   = function(v) SilenceAPI._setAutoRejoin(v) end,
    itemEsp      = function(v) SilenceAPI._setItemEsp(v) end,
    streamerMode = function(v) State.streamerMode = v end,
    menuBlur     = function(v)
        State.menuBlur = v
        if SilenceAPI._setMenuBlur then SilenceAPI._setMenuBlur() end
    end,
    chatLog      = function(v) SilenceAPI._setChatLog(v) end,
    walkAnim     = function(v) SilenceAPI._setWalkAnim(v) end,
}
-- remoteSpy is intentionally not restored: it only works once the namecall
-- hook is installed, and silently logging every remote from boot is a trap.
-- fly/freecam deliberately absent: restoring them on inject drops you out of
-- the world before you can react.

local NUMBERS = { "flySpeed", "freecamSpeed", "aimbotFov", "bodyScale", "slowmo",
                  "autoInteractRange", "uiScale" }
local STRINGS = { "prefix", "faceId", "walkAnimId", "adminCmd", "jumpSound" }

local function c3ToTbl(c) return { math.floor(c.R*255+0.5), math.floor(c.G*255+0.5), math.floor(c.B*255+0.5) } end
local function tblToC3(t)
    if type(t) ~= "table" or #t < 3 then return nil end
    return Color3.fromRGB(t[1], t[2], t[3])
end
local function keyName(k) return k and (tostring(k):gsub("Enum%.KeyCode%.", "")) or nil end
local function keyFromName(n)
    if type(n) ~= "string" then return nil end
    local ok, k = pcall(function() return Enum.KeyCode[n] end)
    return ok and k or nil
end

local autoLoad = true

local function buildConfig()
    local cfg = { version = 1, autoLoad = autoLoad,
                  toggles = {}, numbers = {}, strings = {},
                  colors = {}, hotkeys = {}, featureKeys = {},
                  waypoints = {}, accHead = {}, accTorso = {} }
    for name in pairs(APPLY) do cfg.toggles[name] = State[name] and true or false end
    for _, n in ipairs(NUMBERS) do cfg.numbers[n] = tonumber(State[n]) end
    for _, n in ipairs(STRINGS) do cfg.strings[n] = tostring(State[n] or "") end
    for n, c in pairs(State.colors) do cfg.colors[n] = c3ToTbl(c) end
    for n, k in pairs(State.hotkeys) do cfg.hotkeys[n] = keyName(k) end
    for n, k in pairs(State.featureKeys) do cfg.featureKeys[n] = keyName(k) end
    for i, w in ipairs(State.waypoints) do
        cfg.waypoints[i] = { name = w.name, x = w.x, y = w.y, z = w.z }
    end
    cfg.aura = {
        on = State.aura.on and true or false,
        style = tostring(State.aura.style or "sparkle"),
        gradient = State.aura.gradient and true or false,
        rainbow = State.aura.rainbow and true or false,
        size = tonumber(State.aura.size) or 1,
        rate = tonumber(State.aura.rate) or 1,
        color  = c3ToTbl(State.aura.color),
        color2 = c3ToTbl(State.aura.color2),
    }
    for i, v in ipairs(State.accHead)  do cfg.accHead[i]  = v end
    for i, v in ipairs(State.accTorso) do cfg.accTorso[i] = v end
    cfg.accSlots = {}
    for i, sl in ipairs(State.accSlots) do
        cfg.accSlots[i] = { name = sl.name, id = tostring(sl.id or ""),
                            part = sl.part, hide = sl.hide and true or false,
                            on = sl.on and true or false }
    end
    return cfg
end

local function applyConfig(cfg)
    if type(cfg) ~= "table" then return false end
    if cfg.autoLoad ~= nil then autoLoad = cfg.autoLoad and true or false end

    for _, n in ipairs(NUMBERS) do
        local v = cfg.numbers and tonumber(cfg.numbers[n])
        if v then State[n] = v end
    end
    for _, n in ipairs(STRINGS) do
        local v = cfg.strings and cfg.strings[n]
        if type(v) == "string" then State[n] = v end
    end
    -- slowmo is a number but needs its setter to actually move gravity
    if State.slowmo and State.slowmo ~= 1 then pcall(setSlowmo, State.slowmo) end
    if State.uiScale then pcall(SilenceAPI._setUiScale, State.uiScale) end
    if type(cfg.colors) == "table" then
        for n, t in pairs(cfg.colors) do
            local c = tblToC3(t)
            if c and State.colors[n] ~= nil then State.colors[n] = c end
        end
        pcall(applyColors)
        if SilenceAPI._applyUiColors then pcall(SilenceAPI._applyUiColors) end
    end
    if type(cfg.hotkeys) == "table" then
        for n, s in pairs(cfg.hotkeys) do
            local k = keyFromName(s)
            if k and State.hotkeys[n] ~= nil then State.hotkeys[n] = k end
        end
    end
    if type(cfg.featureKeys) == "table" then
        State.featureKeys = {}
        for n, s in pairs(cfg.featureKeys) do
            local k = keyFromName(s)
            if k then State.featureKeys[n] = k end
        end
    end
    if type(cfg.waypoints) == "table" then
        State.waypoints = {}
        for _, w in ipairs(cfg.waypoints) do
            if type(w) == "table" and tonumber(w.x) and tonumber(w.y) and tonumber(w.z) then
                table.insert(State.waypoints,
                    { name = tostring(w.name or "Point"), x = w.x, y = w.y, z = w.z })
            end
        end
        if SilenceAPI._refreshWaypoints then pcall(SilenceAPI._refreshWaypoints) end
    end
    if type(cfg.aura) == "table" then
        local a = cfg.aura
        State.aura.on       = a.on and true or false
        State.aura.style    = tostring(a.style or State.aura.style)
        State.aura.gradient = a.gradient and true or false
        State.aura.rainbow  = a.rainbow and true or false
        State.aura.size     = tonumber(a.size) or State.aura.size
        State.aura.rate     = tonumber(a.rate) or State.aura.rate
        State.aura.color    = tblToC3(a.color)  or State.aura.color
        State.aura.color2   = tblToC3(a.color2) or State.aura.color2
        if SilenceAPI._refreshAura then pcall(SilenceAPI._refreshAura) end
    end
    if type(cfg.accHead)  == "table" then State.accHead  = SilenceAPI._parseIdList(cfg.accHead)  end
    if type(cfg.accTorso) == "table" then State.accTorso = SilenceAPI._parseIdList(cfg.accTorso) end
    if SilenceAPI._accBoxes then
        local b = SilenceAPI._accBoxes
        if b.head  then b.head.Text  = table.concat(State.accHead, ", ")  end
        if b.torso then b.torso.Text = table.concat(State.accTorso, ", ") end
    end

    if type(cfg.accSlots) == "table" then
        for i, sl in ipairs(State.accSlots) do
            local c = cfg.accSlots[i]
            if type(c) == "table" then
                sl.name = tostring(c.name or sl.name)
                sl.id   = tostring(c.id or ""):match("%d+") or ""
                sl.part = c.part or sl.part
                sl.hide = c.hide and true or false
                -- routed through the setter so it actually mounts
                pcall(SilenceAPI._setSlot, i, c.on and true or false)
            end
        end
    end

    -- body scale / face need their apply pass even when no toggle changed
    if SilenceAPI._setBodyScale then pcall(SilenceAPI._setBodyScale, State.bodyScale) end
    if SilenceAPI._setFace      then pcall(SilenceAPI._setFace,      State.faceId)    end
    if SilenceAPI._setWalkAnimId then pcall(SilenceAPI._setWalkAnimId, State.walkAnimId) end

    if type(cfg.toggles) == "table" then
        for name, fn in pairs(APPLY) do
            local want = cfg.toggles[name]
            if want ~= nil then pcall(fn, want and true or false) end
        end
    end

    for _, r in ipairs(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers) do pcall(r) end
    return true
end

local function saveConfig()
    if not canFile() then
        notify("Config", "This executor has no writefile - cannot save.", 4)
        return
    end
    local HS = game:GetService("HttpService")
    local ok, encoded = pcall(function() return HS:JSONEncode(buildConfig()) end)
    if not ok then notify("Config", "Encode failed: " .. tostring(encoded), 4); return end
    local okW, err = pcall(function() writefile(CFG_FILE, encoded) end)
    if okW then notify("Config", "Saved to " .. CFG_FILE, 3)
    else notify("Config", "Write failed: " .. tostring(err), 4) end
end

local function loadConfig(quiet)
    if not canFile() then
        if not quiet then notify("Config", "This executor has no readfile.", 4) end
        return false
    end
    if type(isfile) == "function" then
        local okI, exists = pcall(function() return isfile(CFG_FILE) end)
        if okI and not exists then
            if not quiet then notify("Config", "No saved config yet.", 3) end
            return false
        end
    end
    local ok, content = pcall(function() return readfile(CFG_FILE) end)
    if not ok or not content then
        if not quiet then notify("Config", "No saved config yet.", 3) end
        return false
    end
    local HS = game:GetService("HttpService")
    local ok2, data = pcall(function() return HS:JSONDecode(content) end)
    if not ok2 or type(data) ~= "table" then
        notify("Config", "Config file is corrupt.", 4)
        return false
    end
    local okA = pcall(applyConfig, data)
    if okA then
        if not quiet then notify("Config", "Config restored.", 3) end
        return true
    end
    notify("Config", "Restore failed.", 4)
    return false
end

sectionLabel(pCfg, "PROFILE")
buttonRow(pCfg, "SAVE CONFIG", saveConfig)
buttonRow(pCfg, "LOAD CONFIG", function() loadConfig(false) end)
buttonRow(pCfg, "DELETE SAVED CONFIG", function()
    if type(delfile) == "function" then
        local ok = pcall(function() delfile(CFG_FILE) end)
        notify("Config", ok and "Deleted." or "Delete failed.", 3)
    elseif canFile() then
        pcall(function() writefile(CFG_FILE, "{}") end)
        notify("Config", "Cleared.", 3)
    else
        notify("Config", "No file access in this executor.", 3)
    end
end)
toggleRow(pCfg, "Auto-load on inject", function() return autoLoad end, function(v)
    autoLoad = v
    notify("Config", v and "Will auto-load next inject" or "Auto-load off", 2)
end)

buttonRow(pCfg, "CONFIG EDITOR  (bundle a config with a tag)", function()
    if SilenceAPI._openConfigEditor then SilenceAPI._openConfigEditor() end
end)

sectionLabel(pCfg, "PROFILES")
do
    -- Five named profiles in one file. The quick SAVE/LOAD above still owns
    -- silence_config.json and is what auto-load reads on inject; these sit
    -- beside it so one setup per game can be kept without overwriting it.
    local PROF_FILE = "silence_config_slots.json"
    local profs = {}
    for i = 1, 5 do profs[i] = { name = "Profile " .. i, data = nil } end

    local function saveProfs()
        if not canFile() then return end
        local HS = game:GetService("HttpService")
        pcall(function() writefile(PROF_FILE, HS:JSONEncode(profs)) end)
    end

    if canFile() then
        local HS = game:GetService("HttpService")
        local okR, content = pcall(function() return readfile(PROF_FILE) end)
        if okR and type(content) == "string" and content ~= "" then
            local okD, data = pcall(function() return HS:JSONDecode(content) end)
            if okD and type(data) == "table" then
                for i = 1, 5 do
                    local e = data[i]
                    if type(e) == "table" then
                        profs[i].name = tostring(e.name or profs[i].name):sub(1, 22)
                        if type(e.data) == "table" then profs[i].data = e.data end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function profRow(i)
        local r = Instance.new("Frame", pCfg)
        r.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 68)
        r.BorderSizePixel = 0
        corner(r, 9)
        SilenceAPI._theme(r, "BackgroundColor3", "bg2")
        stroke(r, Theme.bg4, 1, 0.5, "bg4")
        SilenceAPI._registerRow(r, "profile " .. i)

        local nameBox = Instance.new("TextBox", r)
        nameBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 260, 0, 22)
        nameBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 10)
        nameBox.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        nameBox.Text = profs[i].name
        nameBox.PlaceholderText = "Profile " .. i
        nameBox.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; nameBox.TextSize = 13
        nameBox.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        nameBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        SilenceAPI._theme(nameBox, "TextColor3", "text")

        local state = Instance.new("TextLabel", r)
        state.Size = UDim2.new(0, 140, 0, 22)
        state.Position = UDim2.new(1, -154, 0, 10)
        state.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        state.Font = Enum.Font.Code; state.TextSize = 10
        state.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
        SilenceAPI._theme(state, "TextColor3", "textDim")

        local function paint()
            state.Text = profs[i].data and "saved" or "empty"
        end
        paint()

        nameBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            local t = tostring(nameBox.Text):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
            if t == "" then t = "Profile " .. i end
            profs[i].name = t:sub(1, 22)
            nameBox.Text = profs[i].name
            saveProfs()
        end)

        local function mini(label, x, w, cb)
            local b = Instance.new("TextButton", r)
            b.Size = UDim2.new(0, w, 0, 24)
            b.Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, 36)
            b.BorderSizePixel = 0
            b.Text = label
            b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; b.TextSize = 11
            b.AutoButtonColor = false
            corner(b, 7)
            SilenceAPI._theme(b, "BackgroundColor3", "bg3")
            SilenceAPI._theme(b, "TextColor3", "text")
            local st = stroke(b, Theme.bg4, 1, 0.6, "bg4")
            b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                TweenService:Create(st, SilenceAPI._twFade,
                    { Color = Theme.accent, Transparency = 0.2 }):Play()
            end)
            b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                TweenService:Create(st, SilenceAPI._twFade,
                    { Color = Theme.bg4, Transparency = 0.6 }):Play()
            end)
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(cb)
            return b
        end

        mini("SAVE", 14, 92, function()
            if not canFile() then
                notify("Config", "This executor has no writefile.", 4); return
            end
            profs[i].data = buildConfig()
            saveProfs(); paint()
            notify("Config", "Stored in " .. profs[i].name, 3)
        end)
        mini("LOAD", 112, 92, function()
            if not profs[i].data then
                notify("Config", profs[i].name .. " is empty.", 3); return
            end
            local okA = pcall(applyConfig, profs[i].data)
            notify("Config", okA and (profs[i].name .. " restored.")
                                  or "Restore failed.", 3)
        end)
        mini("CLEAR", 210, 92, function()
            profs[i].data = nil
            saveProfs(); paint()
            notify("Config", profs[i].name .. " cleared.", 2)
        end)
    end

    for i = 1, 5 do profRow(i) end
end

sectionLabel(pCfg, "ADMIN COMMAND")
inputRow(pCfg, "Chat command", State.adminCmd or "/sa", function(v)
    v = tostring(v or ""):gsub("%s+", "")
    if v == "" then v = "/sa" end
    if v:sub(1, 1) ~= "/" then v = "/" .. v end
    State.adminCmd = v
    notify("Config", "Type " .. v .. " in chat to open the admin panel.", 5)
end)
local cmdHint = Instance.new("TextLabel", pCfg)
cmdHint.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
cmdHint.BackgroundTransparency = 1
cmdHint.Text = "  /silenceadmin keeps working as well, so a typo here cannot lock you out."
cmdHint.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
cmdHint.TextSize = 10
cmdHint.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
cmdHint.TextColor3 = Theme.textDim

sectionLabel(pCfg, "APPEARANCE")
local _, setScaleBox = inputRow(pCfg, "Panel Scale", State.uiScale or 1, function(v)
    SilenceAPI._setUiScale(v)
end, {min = 0.6, max = 1.8})
buttonRow(pCfg, "Reset Panel Scale", function()
    SilenceAPI._setUiScale(1)
    if setScaleBox then setScaleBox(1, false) end
end)

sectionLabel(pCfg, "WHAT GETS SAVED")
local cfgHint = Instance.new("TextLabel", pCfg)
cfgHint.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 96)
cfgHint.BackgroundTransparency = 1
cfgHint.Text = "  Every toggle, all colours including the panel's own UI colours,\n"
            .. "  aimbot FOV, walk/fly speeds, every keybind, waypoints, the\n"
            .. "  Larp avatar setup (accessory IDs, body scale, face), your\n"
            .. "  aura, the admin chat command and the jumpscare sound.\n\n"
            .. "  Your TAG design is not in here - it saves itself on every\n"
            .. "  change to silence_tagstyle.txt and has its own five slots\n"
            .. "  under the tag editor's SAVES tab.\n\n"
            .. "  Fly and Freecam are never auto-restored - coming back mid-air\n"
            .. "  before the GUI is up is worse than re-clicking them."
cfgHint.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham; cfgHint.TextSize = 10
cfgHint.TextColor3 = Theme.textDim
cfgHint.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
cfgHint.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top

SilenceAPI._saveConfig = saveConfig
SilenceAPI._loadConfig = loadConfig
-- the config editor bundles one of these with a tag design, so it needs the
-- raw table rather than the file
SilenceAPI._buildConfig      = buildConfig
SilenceAPI._applyConfigTable = applyConfig

-- auto-load, deferred so the rest of the script finishes building first
task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(0.6)
    if not canFile() then return end
    local ok, content = pcall(function() return readfile(CFG_FILE) end)
    if not ok or not content then return end
    local HS = game:GetService("HttpService")
    local ok2, data = pcall(function() return HS:JSONDecode(content) end)
    if not ok2 or type(data) ~= "table" then return end
    if data.autoLoad == false then autoLoad = false; return end
    pcall(applyConfig, data)
    notify("Config", "Saved config restored.", 3)
end)
end)()

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- CONFIG EDITOR
-- Two columns and a thread between them. The left holds panel configs, the
-- right holds tag designs, and a config may be tied to one design. Loading
-- the left one then brings its partner with it.
--
-- They are kept as two lists rather than one combined blob on purpose: the
-- same tag design usually belongs to several configs, and copying it into
-- each of them would mean editing it in one place and silently having five
-- stale copies elsewhere. The link is a reference, not a duplicate.
--
-- Exactly one config may carry AUTO. It is applied a few seconds after
-- inject, once every setter and refresher exists - applyConfig walks all of
-- them and half are still being built while the tabs go up.
--
-- An IIFE: a do..end shares the chunk's registers and this file runs at 198
-- of Luau's 200.
----------------------------------------------------------------
;(function()
local BFILE, NS = "silence_bundles.json", 6
local cfgs, tags = {}, {}
for i = 1, NS do
    cfgs[i] = { name = "Config " .. i, data = nil, link = nil, auto = false }
    tags[i] = { name = "Design " .. i, code = nil }
end
local selC, selT = 1, 1

local function canB()
    return type(writefile) == "function" and type(readfile) == "function"
end
local function saveB()
    if not canB() then return end
    local HS = game:GetService("HttpService")
    pcall(function() writefile(BFILE, HS:JSONEncode({ cfgs = cfgs, tags = tags })) end)
end
local function loadB()
    if not canB() then return end
    local HS = game:GetService("HttpService")
    local okR, body = pcall(function() return readfile(BFILE) end)
    if not okR or type(body) ~= "string" or body == "" then return end
    local okD, d = pcall(function() return HS:JSONDecode(body) end)
    if not okD or type(d) ~= "table" then return end
    for i = 1, NS do
        local c = d.cfgs and d.cfgs[i]
        if type(c) == "table" then
            cfgs[i].name = tostring(c.name or cfgs[i].name):sub(1, 22)
            cfgs[i].data = type(c.data) == "table" and c.data or nil
            cfgs[i].link = tonumber(c.link)
            cfgs[i].auto = c.auto and true or false
        end
        local t = d.tags and d.tags[i]
        if type(t) == "table" then
            tags[i].name = tostring(t.name or tags[i].name):sub(1, 22)
            tags[i].code = (type(t.code) == "string" and t.code ~= "") and t.code or nil
        end
    end
end
loadB()

------------------------------------------------------------ WINDOW
local CE = Instance.new("Frame", Gui)
CE.Name = "ConfigEditor"
CE.Size = UDim2.new(0, 900, 0, 620)
CE.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -450, 0.5, -310)
CE.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg
CE.BorderSizePixel = 0
CE.Visible = false
CE.Active = true
CE.ZIndex = 200
SilenceAPI._glass(CE, { radius = 18, transparency = 0.2, edge = 0.3,
                        glowThickness = 2 })
SilenceAPI._blurWatch(CE)

local hdr = Instance.new("Frame", CE)
hdr.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 46)
hdr.BackgroundTransparency = 1
hdr.BorderSizePixel = 0
hdr.Active = true
hdr.ZIndex = 201

local hTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel", hdr)
hTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 22)
hTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 8)
hTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
hTitle.Text = "CONFIG EDITOR"
hTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
hTitle.TextSize = 15
hTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
hTitle.ZIndex = 202
SilenceAPI._theme(hTitle, "TextColor3", "text")

local hSub = Instance.new("TextLabel", hdr)
hSub.Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 12)
hSub.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 28)
hSub.BackgroundTransparency = 1
hSub.Text = "tie a panel config to a tag design"
hSub.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
hSub.TextSize = 10
hSub.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
hSub.ZIndex = 202
SilenceAPI._theme(hSub, "TextColor3", "textDim")

local hClose = Instance.new("TextButton", hdr)
hClose.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 28)
hClose.Position = UDim2.new(1, -38, 0, 9)
hClose.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
hClose.BorderSizePixel = 0
hClose.Text = "X"
hClose.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
hClose.TextSize = 14
hClose.AutoButtonColor = false
hClose.ZIndex = 202
corner(hClose, 6); stroke(hClose, Theme.accent, 1, 0.5)
SilenceAPI._theme(hClose, "TextColor3", "text")
hClose.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() CE.Visible = false end)
makeHeaderDrag(CE, hdr)

------------------------------------------------------------ LAYOUT
-- fixed geometry, because the thread between the columns is drawn from row
-- indices rather than from measured positions
-- rows start below the toolbar rather than right under the title
local COLW, ROWH, GAP, TOP, MIDW = 350, 74, 8, 110
MIDW = 900 - 2 * COLW - 2 * 20 - 20

local function colCaption(x, text)
    local l = Instance.new("TextLabel", CE)
    l.Size = UDim2.new(0, COLW, 0, 16)
    l.Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, 88)
    l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    l.Text = text
    l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    l.TextSize = 10
    l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    l.ZIndex = 202
    SilenceAPI._theme(l, "TextColor3", "accent2")
end
colCaption(20, "PANEL CONFIGS")
colCaption(900 - 20 - COLW, "TAG DESIGNS")

-- A node canvas rather than a plain background: the grid gives the threads
-- something to read against, the same way the tag editor's preview stage
-- does for a transparent card.
local canvas = Instance.new("Frame", CE)
canvas.Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 1, -156)
canvas.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 102)
canvas.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg
canvas.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
canvas.BorderSizePixel = 0
canvas.ClipsDescendants = true
canvas.ZIndex = 199
corner(canvas, 12)
stroke(canvas, Theme.bg4, 1, 0.5, "bg4")
do
    for i = 1, 17 do
        local v = Instance.new("Frame", canvas)
        v.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 1, 0)
        v.Position = UDim2.new(i / 18, 0, 0, 0)
        v.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.accent
        v.BackgroundTransparency = 0.94
        v.BorderSizePixel = 0
        v.ZIndex = 199
    end
    for i = 1, 11 do
        local h = Instance.new("Frame", canvas)
        h.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
        h.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, i / 12, 0)
        h.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.accent
        h.BackgroundTransparency = 0.94
        h.BorderSizePixel = 0
        h.ZIndex = 199
    end
end

local thread = Instance.new("Frame", CE)
thread.Size = UDim2.new(0, MIDW + 20, 1, -150)
thread.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20 + COLW, 0, TOP)
thread.BackgroundTransparency = 1
thread.BorderSizePixel = 0
thread.ZIndex = 201

local function rowY(i) return TOP + (i - 1) * (ROWH + GAP) + ROWH / 2 end

local paintAll
local function mini(parent, label, x, y, w, cb)
    local b = Instance.new("TextButton", parent)
    b.Size = UDim2.new(0, w, 0, 22)
    b.Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, y)
    b.BorderSizePixel = 0
    b.Text = label
    b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    b.TextSize = 10
    b.AutoButtonColor = false
    b.ZIndex = 204
    corner(b, 6)
    -- No outline. The fill carries the shape and the hover, which reads far
    -- calmer once there are two dozen of these on one screen.
    b.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg3
    SilenceAPI._theme(b, "TextColor3", "textDim")
    b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(b, SilenceAPI._twFade,
            { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.accent }):Play()
        b.TextColor3 = Theme.bg
    end)
    b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(b, SilenceAPI._twFade,
            { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg3 }):Play()
        b.TextColor3 = Theme.textDim
    end)
    b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(cb)
    return b
end

local function makeRow(side, i)
    local isCfg = (side == "c")
    local x = isCfg and 20 or (900 - 20 - COLW)
    local r = Instance.new("TextButton", CE)
    r.Size = UDim2.new(0, COLW, 0, ROWH)
    r.Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, TOP + (i - 1) * (ROWH + GAP))
    r.BorderSizePixel = 0
    r.Text = ""
    r.AutoButtonColor = false
    r.ZIndex = 202
    corner(r, 10)
    SilenceAPI._theme(r, "BackgroundColor3", "bg2")
    local rst = stroke(r, Theme.bg4, 1, 0.5, "bg4")

    local nameBox = Instance.new("TextBox", r)
    nameBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -110, 0, 22)
    nameBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 8)
    nameBox.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    nameBox.Text = isCfg and cfgs[i].name or tags[i].name
    nameBox.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    nameBox.TextSize = 13
    nameBox.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    nameBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    nameBox.ZIndex = 204
    SilenceAPI._theme(nameBox, "TextColor3", "text")

    local st = Instance.new("TextLabel", r)
    st.Size = UDim2.new(0, 96, 0, 22)
    st.Position = UDim2.new(1, -104, 0, 8)
    st.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    st.Font = Enum.Font.Code
    st.TextSize = 10
    st.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
    st.ZIndex = 204
    SilenceAPI._theme(st, "TextColor3", "textDim")

    nameBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        local t = tostring(nameBox.Text):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
        if t == "" then t = (isCfg and "Config " or "Design ") .. i end
        t = t:sub(1, 22)
        if isCfg then cfgs[i].name = t else tags[i].name = t end
        nameBox.Text = t
        saveB(); paintAll()
    end)

    r.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if isCfg then selC = i else selT = i end
        paintAll()
    end)

    if isCfg then
        mini(r, "SAVE", 12, 42, 66, function()
            if not canB() then
                notify("Config", "This executor has no writefile.", 4); return
            end
            if SilenceAPI._buildConfig then
                local ok, c = pcall(SilenceAPI._buildConfig)
                if ok then cfgs[i].data = c end
            end
            saveB(); paintAll()
            notify("Config", "Saved into " .. cfgs[i].name, 3)
        end)
        mini(r, "LOAD", 84, 42, 66, function()
            local b = cfgs[i]
            if not b.data then notify("Config", b.name .. " is empty.", 3) return end
            local done = {}
            if SilenceAPI._applyConfigTable
               and pcall(SilenceAPI._applyConfigTable, b.data) then
                done[#done+1] = "config"
            end
            -- the thread is the point: loading a config brings its design
            local t = b.link and tags[b.link]
            if t and t.code and SilenceAPI._applyTagCode
               and pcall(SilenceAPI._applyTagCode, t.code) then
                done[#done+1] = t.name
            end
            for _, fn in ipairs(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers) do pcall(fn) end
            if SilenceAPI._pushStyle then SilenceAPI._pushStyle() end
            notify("Config", b.name .. " -> " .. table.concat(done, " + "), 4)
        end)
        mini(r, "AUTO", 156, 42, 62, function()
            local want = not cfgs[i].auto
            -- only one, or two of them fight over the panel on inject
            for k = 1, NS do cfgs[k].auto = false end
            cfgs[i].auto = want
            saveB(); paintAll()
            notify("Config", want and (cfgs[i].name .. " loads on inject")
                                  or "Auto-load off", 4)
        end)
        mini(r, "CLEAR", 224, 42, 62, function()
            cfgs[i].data, cfgs[i].auto, cfgs[i].link = nil, false, nil
            saveB(); paintAll()
        end)
    else
        mini(r, "SAVE", 12, 42, 66, function()
            if not SilenceAPI._tagCode then return end
            local ok, c = pcall(SilenceAPI._tagCode)
            if ok and type(c) == "string" and c ~= "" then
                tags[i].code = c
                saveB(); paintAll()
                notify("Config", "Tag stored in " .. tags[i].name, 3)
            end
        end)
        mini(r, "LOAD", 84, 42, 66, function()
            if not tags[i].code then notify("Config", "Empty.", 3) return end
            if SilenceAPI._applyTagCode
               and pcall(SilenceAPI._applyTagCode, tags[i].code) then
                for _, fn in ipairs(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers) do pcall(fn) end
                if SilenceAPI._pushStyle then SilenceAPI._pushStyle() end
                notify("Config", tags[i].name .. " applied.", 3)
            end
        end)
        mini(r, "CLEAR", 156, 42, 62, function()
            tags[i].code = nil
            for k = 1, NS do if cfgs[k].link == i then cfgs[k].link = nil end end
            saveB(); paintAll()
        end)
    end

    -- The pin. Configs carry theirs on the right edge, designs on the left,
    -- so a thread always runs outward from one into the other.
    local pin = Instance.new("TextButton", r)
    pin.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
    pin.Position = isCfg and UDim2.new(1, -9, 0.5, -8) or UDim2.new(0, -7, 0.5, -8)
    pin.BorderSizePixel = 0
    pin.Text = ""
    pin.AutoButtonColor = false
    pin.ZIndex = 206
    local pc = Instance.new("UICorner", pin); pc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    local pst = Instance.new("UIStroke", pin)
    pst.Thickness = 2
    pst.Color = Theme.accent

    return { frame = r, stroke = rst, status = st, box = nameBox,
             pin = pin, pinStroke = pst }
end

------------------------------------------------------------ THE THREAD
local lines = {}
local function clearLines()
    for _, l in ipairs(lines) do pcall(function() l:Destroy() end) end
    lines = {}
end

-- One line drawn between two points inside `thread`. Rotation plus a
-- computed length is the only way to get a diagonal out of a Frame.
local function segment(parent, x1, y1, x2, y2, strong, keep)
    local dx, dy = x2 - x1, y2 - y1
    local len = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
    local l = Instance.new("Frame", parent)
    l.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    l.Size = UDim2.new(0, len, 0, strong and 3 or 2)
    l.Position = UDim2.new(0, (x1 + x2) / 2, 0, (y1 + y2) / 2)
    l.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(dy, dx))
    l.BorderSizePixel = 0
    l.BackgroundColor3 = strong and Theme.accent or Theme.bg4
    l.BackgroundTransparency = strong and 0.15 or 0.6
    l.ZIndex = 201
    corner(l, 2)
    if not keep then lines[#lines + 1] = l end
    return l
end

local function threadW()
    return thread.AbsoluteSize.X > 0 and thread.AbsoluteSize.X or (MIDW + 20)
end

local function drawLine(y1, y2, strong)
    return segment(thread, 0, y1 - TOP, threadW(), y2 - TOP, strong)
end

-- Hold a pin, drag across, let go on the other one. The live line follows
-- the cursor so it is obvious what is about to be tied to what.
-- Mouse position and AbsolutePosition are NOT in the same coordinate space:
-- GetMouseLocation is measured against the viewport, AbsolutePosition against
-- a ScreenGui that sits below the topbar. Mixing them left the line end a
-- fixed inset away from the cursor.
-- Rather than guess the offset, only DELTAS between two mouse readings are
-- used - those are in the same space whatever it is, so the maths is exact.
local dragFrom, hoverTo, liveLine = nil, nil, nil
local dragOrigin, dragAnchor = nil, nil

local function endDrag()
    if liveLine then pcall(function() liveLine:Destroy() end); liveLine = nil end
    if dragFrom and hoverTo then
        cfgs[dragFrom].link = hoverTo
        saveB()
        notify("Config", cfgs[dragFrom].name .. "  ->  " .. tags[hoverTo].name, 4)
    end
    dragFrom, hoverTo, dragOrigin, dragAnchor = nil, nil, nil, nil
    paintAll()
end

local rowsC, rowsT = {}, {}
for i = 1, NS do rowsC[i] = makeRow("c", i) end
for i = 1, NS do rowsT[i] = makeRow("t", i) end

for i = 1, NS do
    rowsC[i].pin.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
        dragFrom = i
        dragOrigin = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
        dragAnchor = Vector2.new(0, rowY(i) - TOP)
        if liveLine then pcall(function() liveLine:Destroy() end) end
        liveLine = segment(thread, dragAnchor.X, dragAnchor.Y,
                                   dragAnchor.X, dragAnchor.Y, true, true)
    end)
    rowsT[i].pin.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if dragFrom then hoverTo = i end end)
    rowsT[i].pin.MouseLeave:Connect(function() if hoverTo == i then hoverTo = nil end end)
    -- dropping on the row itself counts too; a 16px circle is a small target
    rowsT[i].frame.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if dragFrom then hoverTo = i end end)
end

SilenceAPI._onDrag(function(inp)
    if not dragFrom or not liveLine or not dragOrigin then return end
    local m = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
    -- how far the cursor has moved since the pin was pressed, which is all
    -- that is needed once the start point is known in canvas coordinates
    local ex = dragAnchor.X + (m.X - dragOrigin.X)
    local ey = dragAnchor.Y + (m.Y - dragOrigin.Y)
    local dx, dy = ex - dragAnchor.X, ey - dragAnchor.Y
    local len = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
    pcall(function()
        liveLine.Size = UDim2.new(0, len, 0, 3)
        liveLine.Position = UDim2.new(0, (dragAnchor.X + ex) / 2,
                                      0, (dragAnchor.Y + ey) / 2)
        liveLine.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(dy, dx))
    end)
end)

track(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(inp)
    if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
       or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        if dragFrom then endDrag() end
    end
end))

paintAll = function()
    for i = 1, NS do
        local c, t = cfgs[i], tags[i]
        rowsC[i].status.Text = (c.data and "saved" or "empty")
            .. (c.auto and "  AUTO" or "")
        rowsT[i].status.Text = t.code and "saved" or "empty"

        local onC = (selC == i)
        rowsC[i].stroke.Color = c.auto and Theme.accent
                                or (onC and Theme.accent or Theme.bg4)
        rowsC[i].stroke.Transparency = (onC or c.auto) and 0.1 or 0.55
        rowsC[i].frame.BackgroundColor3 = onC and Theme.bg3 or Theme.bg2

        local onT = (selT == i)
        rowsT[i].stroke.Color = onT and Theme.accent or Theme.bg4
        rowsT[i].stroke.Transparency = onT and 0.1 or 0.55
        rowsT[i].frame.BackgroundColor3 = onT and Theme.bg3 or Theme.bg2

        -- a filled pin means a thread is attached to it
        local cLinked = c.link ~= nil
        rowsC[i].pin.BackgroundColor3 = cLinked and Theme.accent or Theme.bg
        rowsC[i].pinStroke.Transparency = cLinked and 0 or 0.4
        local tLinked = false
        for k = 1, NS do if cfgs[k].link == i then tLinked = true break end end
        rowsT[i].pin.BackgroundColor3 = tLinked and Theme.accent or Theme.bg
        rowsT[i].pinStroke.Transparency = tLinked and 0 or 0.4
    end

    clearLines()
    for i = 1, NS do
        local j = cfgs[i].link
        if j and tags[j] then
            drawLine(rowY(i), rowY(j), (i == selC))
        end
    end
end

------------------------------------------------------------ FOOTER
-- A toolbar under the title instead of a row of framed buttons along the
-- bottom: the actions are what you reach for first, and outlines on every
-- one of them made the window read as a grid of boxes.
local function footBtn(label, x, w, cb)
    local b = Instance.new("TextButton", CE)
    b.Size = UDim2.new(0, w, 0, 28)
    b.Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, 52)
    b.BorderSizePixel = 0
    b.Text = label
    b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    b.TextSize = 11
    b.AutoButtonColor = false
    b.ZIndex = 203
    corner(b, 8)
    b.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg2
    SilenceAPI._theme(b, "TextColor3", "textDim")
    b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(b, SilenceAPI._twFade,
            { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.accent }):Play()
        b.TextColor3 = Theme.bg
    end)
    b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(b, SilenceAPI._twFade,
            { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.bg2 }):Play()
        b.TextColor3 = Theme.textDim
    end)
    b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(cb)
    return b
end

footBtn("LINK SELECTED", 20, 150, function()
    cfgs[selC].link = selT
    saveB(); paintAll()
    notify("Config", cfgs[selC].name .. "  ->  " .. tags[selT].name, 4)
end)
footBtn("UNLINK", 178, 100, function()
    cfgs[selC].link = nil
    saveB(); paintAll()
end)
footBtn("SAVE BOTH FROM CURRENT", 286, 200, function()
    if SilenceAPI._buildConfig then
        local ok, c = pcall(SilenceAPI._buildConfig)
        if ok then cfgs[selC].data = c end
    end
    if SilenceAPI._tagCode then
        local ok, t = pcall(SilenceAPI._tagCode)
        if ok and type(t) == "string" and t ~= "" then tags[selT].code = t end
    end
    cfgs[selC].link = selT
    saveB(); paintAll()
    notify("Config", "Stored and tied together.", 4)
end)

local foot = Instance.new("TextLabel", CE)
foot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 300, 0, 28)
foot.Position = UDim2.new(1, -320, 0, 52)
foot.BackgroundTransparency = 1
foot.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
foot.TextSize = 10
foot.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
foot.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
foot.TextWrapped = true
foot.Text = "Drag from a config's right pin onto a design's left pin to tie"
         .. " them. Loading a config also applies the design it is tied to."
foot.ZIndex = 203
SilenceAPI._theme(foot, "TextColor3", "textDim")

paintAll()
-- the thread is drawn from AbsoluteSize, which is zero until the window has
-- been laid out once
CE:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
    if CE.Visible then task.defer(paintAll) end
end)

SilenceAPI._openConfigEditor = function() CE.Visible = not CE.Visible end
SilenceAPI._modalWatch(CE)

task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(4)
    for i = 1, NS do
        local c = cfgs[i]
        if c.auto then
            if c.data and SilenceAPI._applyConfigTable then
                pcall(SilenceAPI._applyConfigTable, c.data)
            end
            local t = c.link and tags[c.link]
            if t and t.code and SilenceAPI._applyTagCode then
                pcall(SilenceAPI._applyTagCode, t.code)
            end
            for _, fn in ipairs(SilenceAPI._toggleRefreshers) do pcall(fn) end
            notify("Config", c.name .. " loaded automatically.", 5)
            break
        end
    end
end)
end)()


----------------------------------------------------------------
-- CLEANUP
----------------------------------------------------------------
function SilenceAPI.cleanup()
    if SilenceAPI._leave then pcall(SilenceAPI._leave) end
    if SilenceAPI._skyCleanup then pcall(SilenceAPI._skyCleanup) end
    pcall(stopFly)
    State.silentAim = false; State.magicBullets = false
    pcall(setEsp,false); pcall(setChams,false); pcall(setTracers,false)
    pcall(setFullbright,false); pcall(setDisco,false)
    pcall(setFreecam,false); pcall(setAntiAfk,false)
    pcall(setHitbox,nil)
    pcall(setSpectate,nil); pcall(setAimbot,false)
    pcall(setTrail,false); pcall(setBigHead,nil)
    pcall(setCrashFx,false); pcall(setGod,false)
    pcall(SilenceAPI._setHeadless,false); pcall(SilenceAPI._setAccessories,false)
    if SilenceAPI._slotsOff then pcall(SilenceAPI._slotsOff) end
    pcall(setSlowmo,1); pcall(stopEmote)
    pcall(stopTrolling); pcall(setHeadstand,false); pcall(setSpin,false)
    pcall(resetSkin); pcall(restoreFpsBoost)
    pcall(setVcBypass,false)   -- else its 1.5s Heartbeat loop survives unload
    if SilenceAPI._larpCleanup     then pcall(SilenceAPI._larpCleanup)     end
    if SilenceAPI._worldCleanup    then pcall(SilenceAPI._worldCleanup)    end
    if SilenceAPI._setPlayerLog    then pcall(SilenceAPI._setPlayerLog, false) end
    if SilenceAPI._destroyTags     then pcall(SilenceAPI._destroyTags)     end
    if SilenceAPI._disconnectWatch then pcall(SilenceAPI._disconnectWatch) end
    Workspace.Gravity=origGravity
    -- the blur lives in Lighting, not under Gui, so destroying the ScreenGui
    -- would leave the whole world blurred after an unload
    if SilenceAPI._blur then pcall(function() SilenceAPI._blur:Destroy() end) end
    for _,c in ipairs(Connections) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    if Gui then pcall(function() Gui:Destroy() end) end
end

notify("Silence", SilenceAPI._version .. " loaded - RightShift toggles GUI", 5)
print("[Silence Admin Panel] loaded - re-execute to reload.")
print("[Silence] === ALL READY === GUI should be visible now")
